## Response:
7/13/2026, 4:30:18 AM

Saya akan susun ini sebagai peta kemampuan praktis untuk microservice Jersey/JAX-RS, bukan sekadar teori security. Saya akan cek referensi resmi/otoritatif dulu supaya istilah seperti OAuth/OIDC/JWT/ACL/permission model tidak outdated.

## Introduction

Core pertanyaannya: **bagaimana memetakan skill authentication, identity, authorization, permission, ACL, dan policy model secara praktis untuk microservice berbasis Jersey/JAX-RS**.

Dalam konteks Jersey/JAX-RS, security sebaiknya dipahami sebagai **request pipeline + identity context + authorization decision + audit trail**, bukan hanya “cek token”. JAX-RS menyediakan titik masuk teknis seperti `ContainerRequestFilter`, filter ordering, `abortWith`, dan `SecurityContext`; sedangkan identity provider, token issuer, policy engine, permission store, dan audit biasanya hidup sebagai komponen eksternal atau service pendukung. Jakarta REST mendokumentasikan bahwa `ContainerRequestFilter` dapat berjalan global, name-bound, pre-match, atau post-match, dan bisa menghentikan request dengan `abortWith`; `ContainerRequestContext` juga dapat diisi dengan `SecurityContext` untuk request saat ini. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.1/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/container/containerrequestfilter))

Secara standar modern, OAuth/OIDC/JWT masih menjadi jalur utama untuk API authentication/authorization. OAuth 2.0 Security Best Current Practice sudah dipublikasikan sebagai **RFC 9700 pada Januari 2025**, memperbarui praktik keamanan OAuth 2.0 dan mendepresiasi mode operasi yang dianggap kurang aman. OIDC Core 1.0 juga mendapat pengakuan sebagai ITU-T X.1285 pada Oktober 2025, yang memperkuat posisinya sebagai standar identity layer di atas OAuth 2.0. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/rfc9700/))

OWASP juga menekankan bahwa REST service aman harus hanya menyediakan endpoint HTTPS, non-public REST service perlu access control di setiap endpoint, user authentication sebaiknya terpusat di Identity Provider, dan JWT harus divalidasi integritasnya serta tidak menerima `alg=none`. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/REST_Security_Cheat_Sheet.html))

## Decomposition Map - Practical Categories / Domain / Skills

| Area | Skill / Domain | Fokus Praktis di Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice | Output yang Harus Dikuasai |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | AuthN vs AuthZ mental model | Pisahkan “siapa dia?” dari “boleh melakukan apa?” Authentication memverifikasi identitas; authorization memutuskan akses terhadap action/resource. OWASP menegaskan user yang sudah authenticated belum tentu authorized untuk semua resource/action. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html)) | Bisa menjelaskan alur `401` vs `403`, principal, role, scope, permission, ACL |
| 2 | Identity model | Memahami `subject`, user, service account, tenant, organization, group, role, department, delegation, impersonation | Model identity canonical untuk request context |
| 3 | Authentication protocols | OAuth 2.0, OIDC, SAML legacy, API key, mTLS, basic auth internal-only, session cookie | Bisa memilih skema auth sesuai channel: user-facing, service-to-service, partner API |
| 4 | OAuth 2.0 / OAuth BCP | Authorization Code + PKCE, client credentials, refresh token rotation, token replay prevention, deprecating unsafe flows | Tidak memakai implicit flow untuk sistem baru; tahu kapan pakai access token vs ID token |
| 5 | OpenID Connect | Login/authentication layer: ID token, access token, claims, issuer, audience, nonce, discovery metadata | Bisa membedakan ID token untuk client vs access token untuk API |
| 6 | JWT validation | Validasi signature, issuer, audience, expiry, not-before, algorithm allowlist, `kid`, JWKS rotation, clock skew | JWT verifier yang fail-closed dan tidak menerima token unsigned |
| 7 | Opaque token / introspection | Token tidak self-contained; service perlu call authorization server/introspection endpoint atau gateway | Bisa menentukan kapan JWT lokal cukup vs introspection dibutuhkan |
| 8 | Jersey authentication filter | Implementasi `ContainerRequestFilter` untuk membaca header, validasi credential, set `SecurityContext`, abort request | Filter auth reusable, priority jelas, error response konsisten |
| 9 | SecurityContext | Mapping principal, roles, scheme, secure flag ke `jakarta.ws.rs.core.SecurityContext` | Resource method bisa akses principal tanpa parsing token ulang |
| 10 | Endpoint-level authorization | Guard berdasarkan route/method: `GET /cases`, `POST /orders`, `DELETE /users/{id}` | Annotation/filter/interceptor policy untuk scope/role/permission |
| 11 | Method/resource annotation | Custom annotation seperti `@RequiresPermission("case:approve")`, `@TenantScoped`, `@InternalOnly` | Authorization declaration dekat dengan resource method |
| 12 | RBAC | Role sebagai grouping permission: admin, reviewer, maker, checker, supervisor | Role matrix dan mapping role-to-permission |
| 13 | Permission model | Permission granular berbasis action/resource: `case.read`, `case.assign`, `case.approve`, `order.cancel` | Permission catalog yang stabil dan versioned |
| 14 | Scope model | Scope OAuth untuk membatasi authority token, misalnya `case:read`, `case:write` | Token scope tidak lebih luas dari kebutuhan caller |
| 15 | ABAC | Attribute-based rule: subject, object, action, environment. NIST mendefinisikan ABAC sebagai model yang mengevaluasi atribut subject, object, operation, dan kondisi environment terhadap policy/rules/relationships. ([NIST Computer Security Resource Center](https://csrc.nist.gov/pubs/sp/800/162/upd2/final)) | Policy berbasis tenant, risk level, department, time, channel, data classification |
| 16 | ACL | Access control list per resource/object: user X boleh read, group Y boleh approve | Desain tabel ACL, inheritance, lookup cepat, revoke aman |
| 17 | ReBAC / relationship authorization | Authorization berdasarkan relasi: owner, member, manager-of, assigned-to, shared-with. Google Zanzibar adalah contoh sistem authorization global untuk menyimpan/evaluasi ACL dengan model data dan configuration language seragam pada banyak service. ([Google Research](https://research.google/pubs/zanzibar-googles-consistent-global-authorization-system/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Bisa modelkan authorization berbasis graph/relationship |
| 18 | Object-level authorization / BOLA prevention | Cek akses terhadap object konkret, bukan hanya endpoint. Contoh: user boleh `GET /cases/{id}` hanya jika punya hubungan dengan case itu | Tidak ada “ambil ID orang lain lalu lolos” |
| 19 | Tenant isolation | Tenant claim, tenant resolver, tenant-aware query, tenant boundary di service dan DB | Semua query MyBatis membawa tenant constraint |
| 20 | Service-to-service identity | Client credentials, mTLS, SPIFFE/SPIRE-style identity, workload identity cloud, internal service token | Bisa membedakan user delegation vs service authority |
| 21 | Delegation & impersonation | “User A acting via service B”, admin impersonation, maker-checker, support access | Audit chain: real actor, effective actor, reason, approval |
| 22 | Policy Decision Point / PDP | Komponen yang memutuskan allow/deny. Bisa custom service, OPA, DB-backed entitlement, atau library lokal | Authorization tidak tersebar hardcoded di semua resource |
| 23 | Policy Enforcement Point / PEP | Titik enforcement: gateway, Jersey filter, resource method, domain service, repository/query layer | Tahu enforcement mana yang cukup dan mana yang wajib dekat data |
| 24 | OPA / external policy engine | OPA memisahkan policy dari service dan memungkinkan policy context-aware dikelola terpisah dari aplikasi. ([Open Policy Agent](https://openpolicyagent.org/docs/philosophy?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Bisa desain input JSON policy dan handle latency/failure PDP |
| 25 | Business-state authorization | Permission tergantung status domain: draft, submitted, approved, rejected, escalated, closed | Guard state transition: siapa boleh approve ketika case di status tertentu |
| 26 | Workflow authorization | Integrasi dengan Camunda/BPMN: task candidate group, assignee, transition guard, escalation privilege | Authorization sinkron antara API, task, dan process state |
| 27 | Data-level authorization | Row filtering, column masking, classification, ownership, regional boundary | Query MyBatis/PostgreSQL tidak mengembalikan data yang tidak boleh dilihat |
| 28 | MyBatis authorization integration | Dynamic SQL dengan tenant/user/permission filter, mapper boundary, plugin/interceptor bila perlu | Tidak mengandalkan filtering di memory setelah data terlanjur keluar |
| 29 | PostgreSQL support | ACL table, role table, permission table, materialized permission view, row-level security bila cocok | Schema authorization yang bisa diaudit dan dioptimasi |
| 30 | Secrets & key management | JWKS, key rotation, private key protection, API key hashing, KMS/HSM, secret injection di Kubernetes | Tidak menyimpan secret plaintext di config/repo |
| 31 | Session, logout, revocation | Token expiry, refresh token lifecycle, back-channel logout, denylist jika perlu, revocation event | Tahu batas JWT stateless vs kebutuhan revocation real-time |
| 32 | Error semantics | `401 Unauthorized` untuk belum/invalid authenticated, `403 Forbidden` untuk authenticated tapi tidak authorized | Error response aman, tidak membocorkan policy internal |
| 33 | Audit trail | Log allow/deny, actor, tenant, resource, action, reason, policy version, correlation ID | Bisa investigasi “siapa melakukan apa, kapan, atas dasar izin apa” |
| 34 | Observability security | Metrics auth failure, token expired, forbidden spike, PDP latency, JWKS refresh failure | Alert untuk brute force, misconfiguration, atau outage IdP/PDP |
| 35 | Testing strategy | Unit test policy, integration test filter, negative test BOLA, contract test scope, mutation/fuzz auth rules | Test tidak hanya happy path |
| 36 | Threat modeling | Token theft, replay, confused deputy, privilege escalation, broken object-level authorization, tenant breakout | Security design review sebelum implementasi |
| 37 | CI/CD guardrails | Secret scanning, dependency scanning, policy tests, OpenAPI security scheme validation | Build gagal jika auth contract/security policy rusak |
| 38 | Operational fallback | IdP down, JWKS unavailable, PDP timeout, cache stale, partial outage | Fail-closed untuk sensitive operation; cache policy dengan TTL terkontrol |
| 39 | Migration & compatibility | Legacy session/API key ke OAuth/OIDC, role lama ke permission baru, ACL migration | Evolusi security tanpa breaking semua client |
| 40 | Governance | Ownership permission catalog, policy review, emergency access, least privilege review | Authorization menjadi sistem yang bisa dikelola, bukan ad-hoc code |

## Struktur Layer yang Disarankan

Untuk Jersey/JAX-RS microservice, desain yang sehat biasanya seperti ini:

```text
HTTP Request
  ↓
Transport Security
  - HTTPS
  - mTLS optional untuk internal/high-privilege service
  ↓
Authentication Filter
  - parse Authorization header
  - validate token/API key/mTLS identity
  - build principal + claims
  - set SecurityContext
  ↓
Coarse Authorization
  - endpoint permission
  - scope/role check
  - internal/public/admin boundary
  ↓
Resource Method
  - parse request
  - call application service
  ↓
Fine Authorization
  - object-level permission
  - tenant boundary
  - workflow/business-state guard
  - ownership/ACL/ReBAC/ABAC
  ↓
Repository / MyBatis
  - tenant-aware query
  - permission-aware query when needed
  ↓
Audit + Observability
```

Prinsip pentingnya: **authorization tidak cukup hanya di controller/resource method**. Untuk sistem case management, order management, enforcement lifecycle, atau workflow-heavy platform, authorization perlu muncul di beberapa titik: endpoint, object, state transition, data query, dan audit.

---

## Permission Model Praktis

Contoh struktur permission yang maintainable:

```text
<domain>.<resource>.<action>

case.read
case.create
case.update
case.submit
case.assign
case.approve
case.reject
case.close

order.read
order.create
order.cancel
order.retry_provisioning

user.read
user.invite
user.disable

workflow.task.claim
workflow.task.complete
workflow.task.reassign
```

Role sebaiknya dianggap **bundle permission**, bukan sumber kebenaran utama:

```text
Role: CASE_REVIEWER
  - case.read
  - case.update
  - case.submit
  - workflow.task.claim
  - workflow.task.complete

Role: CASE_SUPERVISOR
  - case.read
  - case.assign
  - case.approve
  - case.reject
  - workflow.task.reassign
```

ACL/relationship dipakai ketika permission global tidak cukup:

```text
User has case.read globally?              → mungkin tidak
User assigned to case 123?                → boleh read/update
User is supervisor of assigned officer?   → boleh review
User belongs to tenant A?                 → tidak boleh akses tenant B
Case status = CLOSED?                     → tidak boleh update kecuali reopen permission
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 4:31:46 AM

Kita mulai dari implementasi lapisan paling dekat dengan aplikasi: request filter, validasi token, pembentukan security context, lalu authorization di endpoint. Saya akan jadikan ini sebagai cheatsheet praktis yang bisa langsung dipakai saat membaca/menulis kode Jersey/JAX-RS.

# Cheatsheet - Authentication & Authorization Implementation Pattern di Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Mental Model

Di microservice berbasis Jersey/JAX-RS, security request biasanya diproses seperti ini:

```mermaid id="r49sfc"
sequenceDiagram
    participant C as Client
    participant G as Gateway / Ingress
    participant F as JAX-RS ContainerRequestFilter
    participant V as Token Validator
    participant SC as SecurityContext
    participant R as Resource Method
    participant A as Authorization Guard
    participant S as Domain Service
    participant DB as PostgreSQL/MyBatis

    C->>G: HTTP request + Authorization: Bearer token
    G->>F: Forward request
    F->>F: Extract credential
    F->>V: Validate token / API key / mTLS identity
    V-->>F: Principal + claims + scopes + tenant
    F->>SC: setSecurityContext(...)
    F->>R: Continue request
    R->>A: Check permission/scope/role/object access
    A-->>R: allow / deny
    R->>S: Execute use case
    S->>DB: Tenant-aware / permission-aware query
    DB-->>S: Authorized data only
    S-->>C: Response
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="ypdoep"
Authentication answers: who is calling?
Authorization answers: what is this caller allowed to do?
Audit answers: why was this action allowed or denied?
```

JAX-RS menyediakan `ContainerRequestFilter` untuk memproses request sebelum resource method, dan `ContainerRequestContext#setSecurityContext(...)` untuk mengganti security context request saat ini. Filter juga bisa menghentikan request dengan response tertentu melalui `abortWith(...)`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/platform/10/apidocs/jakarta/ws/rs/container/containerrequestcontext?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

```text id="rlrt2w"
    └── SecurityAuditLogger.java
```

Pembagian ini menjaga agar parsing token, identity context, authorization decision, dan audit tidak tercampur di resource method.

# 3. Authentication Filter

## Tujuan

Authentication filter bertugas:

| Tugas | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Extract credential | Ambil `Authorization: Bearer <token>`, API key, atau identity dari mTLS |
| Validate credential | Validasi signature, issuer, audience, expiry, revocation/introspection jika perlu |
| Build principal | Bentuk object identity internal |
| Set `SecurityContext` | Supaya resource method bisa akses caller identity |
| Fail fast | Return `401` jika credential hilang/invalid |

Bearer token dikirim melalui HTTP `Authorization` header dengan skema `Bearer`; RFC 6750 mendefinisikan access token sebagai abstraksi authorization yang bisa dipakai resource server tanpa memahami semua credential asli user. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6750?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Skeleton Code

```java id="m9zdvi"
package com.company.security.authn;

import com.company.security.context.RequestSecurityContext;
import jakarta.annotation.Priority;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Priorities;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.HttpHeaders;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.io.IOException;

@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class AuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private static final String BEARER_PREFIX = "Bearer ";

    private final TokenAuthenticator tokenAuthenticator;

    public AuthenticationFilter(TokenAuthenticator tokenAuthenticator) {
        this.tokenAuthenticator = tokenAuthenticator;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) throws IOException {
        String authorization = requestContext.getHeaderString(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);

        if (authorization == null || authorization.isBlank()) {
            abort401(requestContext, "missing_token");
            return;
        }

        if (!authorization.startsWith(BEARER_PREFIX)) {
            abort401(requestContext, "invalid_authorization_scheme");
            return;
        }

        String token = authorization.substring(BEARER_PREFIX.length()).trim();

        AuthenticationResult result = tokenAuthenticator.authenticate(token);

        if (!result.authenticated()) {
            abort401(requestContext, result.errorCode());
            return;
        }

        RequestSecurityContext securityContext = new RequestSecurityContext(
                result.principal(),
                requestContext.getSecurityContext().isSecure(),
                "Bearer"
        );

        requestContext.setSecurityContext(securityContext);
    }

    private void abort401(ContainerRequestContext ctx, String errorCode) {
        ctx.abortWith(Response.status(Response.Status.UNAUTHORIZED)
                .header(HttpHeaders.WWW_AUTHENTICATE,
                        "Bearer realm=\"api\", error=\"invalid_token\"")
                .entity(new SecurityErrorResponse("UNAUTHORIZED", errorCode))
                .build());
    }
}
```

Catatan penting: gunakan `@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)` supaya filter authentication berjalan sebelum filter authorization.

# 4. Authentication Result

```java id="c4isn8"
package com.company.security.authn;

import com.company.security.context.AuthenticatedPrincipal;

public record AuthenticationResult(
        boolean authenticated,
        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal,
        String errorCode
) {
    public static AuthenticationResult success(AuthenticatedPrincipal principal) {
        return new AuthenticationResult(true, principal, null);
    }

    public static AuthenticationResult failure(String errorCode) {
        return new AuthenticationResult(false, null, errorCode);
    }
}
```

Gunakan object hasil authentication yang eksplisit supaya tidak menyebarkan `null`, exception acak, atau parsing claims di banyak tempat.

---

# 5. Principal Internal

## Kenapa tidak langsung pakai raw JWT claims?

Karena raw claims sering terlalu vendor-specific. Misalnya Keycloak, Auth0, Azure Entra ID, Cognito, atau internal IdP bisa punya struktur claims berbeda. Microservice sebaiknya punya bentuk identity internal yang stabil.

```java id="ypo33h"
package com.company.security.context;

import java.security.Principal;
import java.util.Set;

public record AuthenticatedPrincipal(
        String subject,
        String username,
        String tenantId,
        Set<String> roles,
        Set<String> scopes,
        Set<String> permissions,
        String tokenId,
        String issuer,
        String audience
) implements Principal {

    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return username != null ? username : subject;
    }

    public boolean hasRole(String role) {
        return roles != null && roles.contains(role);
    }

    public boolean hasScope(String scope) {
        return scopes != null && scopes.contains(scope);
    }

    public boolean hasPermission(String permission) {
        return permissions != null && permissions.contains(permission);
    }
}
```

**Prinsip desain:** semua resource method membaca `AuthenticatedPrincipal`, bukan membaca JWT langsung.

---

# 6. Custom SecurityContext

```java id="exrxl3"
package com.company.security.context;

import jakarta.ws.rs.core.SecurityContext;

import java.security.Principal;

public class RequestSecurityContext implements SecurityContext {

    private final AuthenticatedPrincipal principal;
    private final boolean secure;
    private final String authenticationScheme;

    public RequestSecurityContext(
            AuthenticatedPrincipal principal,
            boolean secure,
            String authenticationScheme
    ) {
        this.principal = principal;
        this.secure = secure;
        this.authenticationScheme = authenticationScheme;
    }

    @Override
    public Principal getUserPrincipal() {
        return principal;
    }

    public AuthenticatedPrincipal getAuthenticatedPrincipal() {
        return principal;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isUserInRole(String role) {
        return principal != null && principal.hasRole(role);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSecure() {
        return secure;
    }

    @Override
    public String getAuthenticationScheme() {
        return authenticationScheme;
    }
}
```

Setelah ini, resource method dapat memakai:

```java id="yucrmk"
@Context
SecurityContext securityContext;
```

atau cast ke principal internal:

```java id="y5ib1z"
AuthenticatedPrincipal principal =
        (AuthenticatedPrincipal) securityContext.getUserPrincipal();
```

---

# 7. JWT Validation Checklist

JWT validation tidak boleh sekadar “decode base64 lalu ambil claims”. OWASP REST Security Cheat Sheet menekankan bahwa JWT harus integrity-protected dengan signature atau MAC, dan tidak boleh menerima unsecured JWT seperti `{"alg":"none"}`. RFC 8725 juga berisi praktik terbaik untuk implementasi/deployment JWT secara aman. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/REST_Security_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Check | Wajib? | Penjelasan |
|---|---:|---|
| Signature valid | Ya | Verifikasi dengan public key/JWKS atau shared secret yang benar |
| Algorithm allowlist | Ya | Jangan percaya `alg` dari token secara buta |
| `iss` valid | Ya | Issuer harus sama dengan trusted authorization server |
| `aud` valid | Ya | Token harus memang ditujukan untuk API ini |
| `exp` belum lewat | Ya | Expired token harus ditolak |
| `nbf` valid | Ya | Token belum aktif harus ditolak |
| `iat` reasonable | Disarankan | Deteksi token terlalu lama/aneh |
| `kid` aman | Ya | Gunakan untuk lookup key, jangan jadi path/file injection |
| JWKS cache TTL | Ya | Cache key tapi tetap support rotation |
| Clock skew kecil | Ya | Misalnya 30-120 detik, jangan terlalu longgar |
| Scope/permission ada | Tergantung | Untuk endpoint protected |
| Token type benar | Ya | Jangan salah pakai ID token sebagai access token |

OIDC mendefinisikan ID Token sebagai security token yang berisi claims authentication end-user oleh Authorization Server; untuk API, yang umumnya dipakai sebagai bukti akses adalah **access token**, bukan ID token. ([OpenID Foundation](https://openid.net/specs/openid-connect-core-1_0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 8. Token Authenticator Contract

```java id="rvpnh2"
package com.company.security.authn;

public interface TokenAuthenticator {
    AuthenticationResult authenticate(String token);
}
```

Contoh implementasi konseptual:

```java id="y4guto"
package com.company.security.authn;

import com.company.security.context.AuthenticatedPrincipal;

import java.util.Set;

public class JwtTokenAuthenticator implements TokenAuthenticator {

    private final JwtVerifier jwtVerifier;

    public JwtTokenAuthenticator(JwtVerifier jwtVerifier) {
        this.jwtVerifier = jwtVerifier;
    }

    @Override
    public AuthenticationResult authenticate(String token) {
        try {
            VerifiedJwt jwt = jwtVerifier.verify(token);

            AuthenticatedPrincipal principal = new AuthenticatedPrincipal(
                    jwt.subject(),
                    jwt.username(),
                    jwt.tenantId(),
                    jwt.roles(),
                    jwt.scopes(),
                    jwt.permissions(),
                    jwt.tokenId(),
                    jwt.issuer(),
                    jwt.audience()
            );

            return AuthenticationResult.success(principal);

        } catch (TokenExpiredException e) {
            return AuthenticationResult.failure("token_expired");

        } catch (InvalidTokenException e) {
            return AuthenticationResult.failure("invalid_token");
        }
    }
}
```

`JwtVerifier` sengaja dijadikan abstraction. Implementasi real bisa memakai library seperti Nimbus JOSE + JWT, jose4j, atau library resmi dari IdP, tapi jangan membuat validator kriptografi sendiri.

---

# 9. Authorization Annotation

Authentication filter menjawab “siapa caller-nya”. Authorization filter menjawab “apakah caller ini boleh mengakses endpoint ini”.

```java id="un78fh"
package com.company.security.authz;

import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

import static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.METHOD;
import static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.TYPE;
import static java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME;

@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({TYPE, METHOD})
public @interface RequiresPermission {
    String[] value();
    MatchMode mode() default MatchMode.ALL;

    enum MatchMode {
        ALL,
        ANY
    }
}
```

Contoh pemakaian:

```java id="dcb8o3"
@Path("/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    @RequiresPermission("case.read")
    public Response getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        // object-level authorization tetap harus dicek di service layer
        return Response.ok().build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{caseId}/approve")
    @RequiresPermission("case.approve")
    public Response approveCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        return Response.noContent().build();
    }
}
```

---

# 10. Authorization Filter

```java id="oyfvw6"
package com.company.security.authz;

import com.company.security.context.AuthenticatedPrincipal;
import jakarta.annotation.Priority;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ForbiddenException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Priorities;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ResourceInfo;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Context;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.SecurityContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.Arrays;

@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public class AuthorizationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Context
    private ResourceInfo resourceInfo;

    private final AuthorizationService authorizationService;

    public AuthorizationFilter(AuthorizationService authorizationService) {
        this.authorizationService = authorizationService;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) throws IOException {
        Method method = resourceInfo.getResourceMethod();
        Class<?> resourceClass = resourceInfo.getResourceClass();

        RequiresPermission permission = resolveAnnotation(method, resourceClass);

        if (permission == null) {
            return;
        }

        SecurityContext securityContext = requestContext.getSecurityContext();

        if (securityContext == null || securityContext.getUserPrincipal() == null) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Authenticated principal is required");
        }

        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal =
                (AuthenticatedPrincipal) securityContext.getUserPrincipal();

        boolean allowed = authorizationService.hasPermissions(
                principal,
                Arrays.asList(permission.value()),
                permission.mode()
        );

        if (!allowed) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Insufficient permission");
        }
    }

    private RequiresPermission resolveAnnotation(Method method, Class<?> resourceClass) {
        RequiresPermission methodAnnotation = method.getAnnotation(RequiresPermission.class);
        if (methodAnnotation != null) {
            return methodAnnotation;
        }

        return resourceClass.getAnnotation(RequiresPermission.class);
    }
}
```

**Catatan:** endpoint-level authorization seperti ini belum cukup untuk object-level access. Misalnya user punya `case.read`, tetapi belum tentu boleh membaca `caseId` tertentu.

---

# 11. Authorization Service

```java id="pr5k31"
package com.company.security.authz;

import com.company.security.context.AuthenticatedPrincipal;

import java.util.Collection;

public class AuthorizationService {

    public boolean hasPermissions(
            AuthenticatedPrincipal principal,
            Collection<String> requiredPermissions,
            RequiresPermission.MatchMode mode
    ) {
        if (principal == null) {
            return false;
        }

        if (requiredPermissions == null || requiredPermissions.isEmpty()) {
            return true;
        }

        if (mode == RequiresPermission.MatchMode.ALL) {
            return requiredPermissions.stream().allMatch(principal::hasPermission);
        }

        return requiredPermissions.stream().anyMatch(principal::hasPermission);
    }
}
```

Ini cocok untuk coarse-grained authorization. Untuk domain sensitif, lanjutkan ke object-level authorization di service layer.

---

# 12. Object-Level Authorization

Masalah umum di API adalah **BOLA/IDOR**: caller berhasil mengakses object hanya dengan mengganti ID di URL. OWASP Authorization Cheat Sheet menekankan bahwa authorization perlu divalidasi pada setiap request dan harus diterapkan secara konsisten, bukan hanya bergantung pada status authenticated. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/REST_Security_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh pattern:

```java id="utq1v4"
public class CaseApplicationService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final ObjectAccessEvaluator objectAccessEvaluator;

    public CaseDto getCase(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor, String caseId) {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findById(caseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Case not found"));

        if (!objectAccessEvaluator.canReadCase(actor, caseRecord)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("No access to this case");
        }

        return CaseDto.from(caseRecord);
    }
}
```

Evaluator:

```java id="pvqyn6"
public class ObjectAccessEvaluator {

    public boolean canReadCase(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor, CaseRecord caseRecord) {
        if (!actor.tenantId().equals(caseRecord.tenantId())) {
            return false;
        }

        if (actor.hasPermission("case.read.all")) {
            return true;
        }

        if (actor.hasPermission("case.read.assigned")
                && caseRecord.assignedUserId().equals(actor.subject())) {
            return true;
        }

        if (actor.hasPermission("case.read.own")
                && caseRecord.createdBy().equals(actor.subject())) {
            return true;
        }

        return false;
    }

    public boolean canApproveCase(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor, CaseRecord caseRecord) {
        if (!actor.tenantId().equals(caseRecord.tenantId())) {
            return false;
        }

        if (!actor.hasPermission("case.approve")) {
            return false;
        }

        if (!"SUBMITTED".equals(caseRecord.status())) {
            return false;
        }

        if (caseRecord.createdBy().equals(actor.subject())) {
            return false; // maker-checker separation
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

Ini lebih defensible daripada hanya:

```java id="kb68sw"
@RequiresPermission("case.approve")
```

Karena approval biasanya tergantung tenant, status, ownership, assignment, dan separation-of-duty.

---

# 13. 401 vs 403

| Kondisi | Response | Contoh |
|---|---:|---|
| Tidak ada token | `401 Unauthorized` | Missing `Authorization` header |
| Token invalid/expired | `401 Unauthorized` | Signature invalid, expired token |
| Token valid tapi scope kurang | `403 Forbidden` | Tidak punya `case.approve` |
| Token valid tapi object bukan miliknya | `403 Forbidden` atau `404 Not Found` | Akses case tenant lain |
| Endpoint tidak ditemukan | `404 Not Found` | Route tidak ada |

RFC 6750 mendefinisikan penggunaan `WWW-Authenticate` response header untuk error bearer token; untuk insufficient scope, resource server biasanya memakai `403 Forbidden` dan dapat menyertakan scope yang dibutuhkan. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6750?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh response `401`:

```http id="mhm90o"
HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized
WWW-Authenticate: Bearer realm="api", error="invalid_token"
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "code": "UNAUTHORIZED",
  "message": "Invalid or expired access token"
}
```

Contoh response `403`:

```http id="z6xcce"
HTTP/1.1 403 Forbidden
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "code": "FORBIDDEN",
  "message": "Insufficient permission"
}
```

---

# 14. Security Error Response

```java id="vcwkbg"
package com.company.security.error;

import java.time.Instant;

public record SecurityErrorResponse(
        String code,
        String message,
        Instant timestamp
) {
    public SecurityErrorResponse(String code, String message) {
        this(code, message, Instant.now());
    }
}
```

Untuk production, jangan expose detail seperti:

```text id="zjglgq"
signature validation failed with key kid=abc using RS256
```

Lebih aman:

```text id="vt36na"
Invalid or expired access token
```

Detail teknis cukup masuk log internal.

# 15. Resource Method Pattern

## Jangan

```java id="edco59"
@GET
@Path("/{caseId}")
public Response getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
    String token = request.getHeader("Authorization");
    Map<String, Object> claims = parseJwt(token);
    // business logic bercampur security logic
}
```

## Sebaiknya

```java id="d1m1pi"
@GET
@Path("/{caseId}")
@RequiresPermission("case.read")
public Response getCase(
        @Context SecurityContext securityContext,
        @PathParam("caseId") String caseId
) {
    AuthenticatedPrincipal actor =
            (AuthenticatedPrincipal) securityContext.getUserPrincipal();

    CaseDto result = caseApplicationService.getCase(actor, caseId);

    return Response.ok(result).build();
}
```

Resource method hanya melakukan orchestration tipis. Authorization object-level tetap di application/domain service.

---

# 16. Permission Naming Convention

Gunakan permission yang stabil, domain-oriented, dan mudah diaudit.

```text id="php99n"
<domain>.<resource>.<action>
```

Contoh:

```text id="ll6hpn"
case.read
case.create
case.update
case.submit
case.assign
case.approve
case.reject
case.close

workflow.task.claim
workflow.task.complete
workflow.task.reassign

order.read
order.create
order.cancel
order.retry_provisioning

admin.user.read
admin.user.disable
admin.role.assign
```

Hindari permission yang terlalu UI-oriented:

```text id="kzje1f"
button_approve_visible
show_admin_menu
can_click_submit
```

Permission harus merepresentasikan **business capability**, bukan widget UI.

---

# 17. Scope vs Role vs Permission

| Konsep | Dipakai Untuk | Contoh | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Scope | Batas authority token/API client | `case:read`, `case:write` | Cocok di OAuth access token |
| Role | Bundle permission untuk user/group | `CASE_REVIEWER` | Jangan hardcode role terlalu banyak |
| Permission | Capability granular | `case.approve` | Bagus untuk authorization internal |
| ACL | Akses per object/resource | user A boleh read case 123 | Cocok untuk sharing/assignment |
| ABAC | Rule berbasis atribut | tenant, region, status, risk | Cocok untuk policy kompleks |
| ReBAC | Rule berbasis relasi | owner, manager, assignee | Cocok untuk graph relationship |

Praktik yang rapi:

```text id="jkw5g3"
OAuth scope membatasi token
Role mengelompokkan permission
Permission mengontrol action
ACL/ReBAC/ABAC mengontrol object dan context
```

---

# 18. MyBatis/PostgreSQL Enforcement Pattern

Untuk multi-tenant atau object-level sensitive data, jangan hanya filter di Java setelah query. Query harus membawa boundary.

## Buruk

```java id="ywo0dr"
List<CaseRecord> cases = caseMapper.findAll();
return cases.stream()
        .filter(c -> c.tenantId().equals(actor.tenantId()))
        .toList();
```

Masalah: data tenant lain sudah keluar dari DB ke aplikasi.

## Lebih baik

```java id="pj16yz"
List<CaseRecord> cases = caseMapper.findVisibleCases(
        actor.tenantId(),
        actor.subject(),
        actor.permissions()
);
```

Mapper:

```xml id="zc3tfj"
<select id="findVisibleCases" resultMap="CaseRecordMap">
    SELECT
        c.id,
        c.tenant_id,
        c.status,
        c.assigned_user_id,
        c.created_by,
        c.created_at
    FROM cases c
    WHERE c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND (
            #{permissions.contains('case.read.all')}
            OR c.assigned_user_id = #{subject}
            OR c.created_by = #{subject}
      )
</select>
```

Namun hati-hati: ekspresi seperti `#{permissions.contains(...)}` tidak selalu valid langsung di MyBatis XML tergantung cara parameter dikirim. Pattern yang lebih bersih adalah precompute boolean di service:

```java id="vvlvkp"
boolean canReadAll = actor.hasPermission("case.read.all");

caseMapper.findVisibleCases(
        actor.tenantId(),
        actor.subject(),
        canReadAll
);
```

Mapper:

```xml id="n0zd2d"
<select id="findVisibleCases" resultMap="CaseRecordMap">
    SELECT
        c.id,
        c.tenant_id,
        c.status,
        c.assigned_user_id,
        c.created_by,
        c.created_at
    FROM cases c
    WHERE c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND (
            #{canReadAll} = true
            OR c.assigned_user_id = #{subject}
            OR c.created_by = #{subject}
      )
</select>
```

---

# 19. Audit Event

Authorization yang baik harus bisa dijelaskan setelah kejadian.

```java id="fj263k"
public record SecurityAuditEvent(
        String eventType,
        String actorSubject,
        String tenantId,
        String resourceType,
        String resourceId,
        String action,
        String decision,
        String reason,
        String correlationId,
        String policyVersion
) {}
```

Contoh log:

```json id="y0vsjb"
{
  "eventType": "AUTHORIZATION_DECISION",
  "actorSubject": "user-123",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "case-789",
  "action": "case.approve",
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "maker_checker_violation",
  "correlationId": "req-abc",
  "policyVersion": "case-policy-v3"
}
```

Untuk regulatory/case-management system, audit bukan tambahan kosmetik. Audit adalah bukti bahwa sistem melakukan enforcement sesuai policy.

---

# 20. Failure Mode yang Harus Dipikirkan

| Failure | Risiko | Default Aman |
|---|---|---|
| IdP down | Tidak bisa introspect token | Fail-closed untuk endpoint sensitif |
| JWKS endpoint down | Tidak bisa refresh key | Pakai cached key sampai TTL, lalu fail-closed |
| Token expired | Replay token lama | Reject `401` |
| Clock skew besar | Token belum valid/expired bisa lolos | Skew kecil dan eksplisit |
| PDP/authorization service timeout | Allow/deny tidak bisa diputuskan | Fail-closed kecuali endpoint non-sensitive |
| Permission cache stale | User yang sudah dicabut masih bisa akses | TTL pendek + event invalidation |
| Tenant claim hilang | Query bisa bocor cross-tenant | Reject request |
| Missing object-level check | BOLA/IDOR | Service-layer guard wajib |
| Role rename | Authorization rusak | Permission lebih stabil daripada role |
| Debug log token | Token leakage | Masking/redaction wajib |

---

# 21. Checklist Code Review

Gunakan checklist ini saat review PR:

```text id="nyl7bp"
[ ] Endpoint protected by authentication filter
[ ] Public endpoint explicitly marked public
[ ] Token signature validated
[ ] iss validated
[ ] aud validated
[ ] exp / nbf validated
[ ] alg allowlisted
[ ] SecurityContext set exactly once
[ ] Resource method does not parse JWT manually
[ ] Endpoint-level permission declared
[ ] Object-level authorization exists for resource ID access
[ ] Tenant boundary enforced before DB query or inside DB query
[ ] MyBatis query includes tenant/user/permission constraint
[ ] 401 and 403 are semantically correct
[ ] Error response does not leak sensitive internals
[ ] Authorization decision is audited
[ ] Negative tests cover unauthorized access
[ ] Negative tests cover cross-tenant access
[ ] Negative tests cover object ID tampering
[ ] Permission names are stable and domain-oriented
```

---

# 22. Testing Matrix

| Test Type | Example |
|---|---|
| Missing token | Request without `Authorization` returns `401` |
| Invalid token | Malformed/unsigned token returns `401` |
| Expired token | Expired JWT returns `401` |
| Wrong issuer | Token from unknown IdP returns `401` |
| Wrong audience | Token for another API returns `401` |
| Missing permission | Valid token without `case.approve` returns `403` |
| Cross-tenant access | User tenant A reads tenant B case returns `403` or `404` |
| Object ownership | User changes `{caseId}` to another user’s case and is denied |
| State guard | User cannot approve case in `DRAFT` or `CLOSED` |
| Maker-checker | Creator cannot approve own case |
| Audit | Deny event is logged with reason and correlation ID |

---

# 23. Minimal Production Invariants

Kalau harus diringkas menjadi aturan keras:

```text id="mf751e"
1. Jangan parse JWT di resource method.
2. Jangan trust JWT tanpa signature, issuer, audience, expiry, dan algorithm validation.
3. Jangan gunakan ID token sebagai access token untuk API.
4. Jangan anggap role global cukup untuk object-level authorization.
5. Jangan query data lintas tenant lalu difilter di memory.
6. Jangan return detail cryptographic failure ke client.
7. Jangan membuat endpoint public secara implisit.
8. Jangan melewati audit untuk action sensitif.
9. Jangan fail-open saat policy/token validation gagal.
10. Jangan hardcode authorization rule tersebar tanpa ownership yang jelas.
```

# 24. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `ContainerRequestFilter` + `SecurityContext` | Bisa membangun identity context request |
| 2 | JWT validation | Bisa memvalidasi access token secara aman |
| 3 | `401` vs `403` | Error semantics benar |
| 4 | Custom annotation authorization | Endpoint-level permission rapi |
| 5 | Object-level authorization | Mencegah IDOR/BOLA |
| 6 | Tenant-aware MyBatis query | Data isolation tidak bocor |
| 7 | Audit authorization decision | Keputusan security bisa dijelaskan |
| 8 | Security test matrix | Negative path tertutup |
| 9 | Permission catalog | Permission stabil dan tidak UI-driven |
| 10 | Policy architecture | Siap masuk ACL, ABAC, ReBAC, PDP/PEP |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 4:34:08 AM

Kita lanjut ke JWT/OIDC access token validation. Fokusnya bukan membuat login, tetapi bagaimana microservice Jersey/JAX-RS memutuskan token yang datang benar-benar valid, ditujukan untuk API ini, dan aman dipakai sebagai identity context.

# Cheatsheet - JWT / OIDC Access Token Validation di Java/Jersey

## 1. Core Intent

Di microservice Jersey/JAX-RS, validasi JWT/OIDC bukan cuma “decode token lalu ambil username”. Tujuannya adalah memastikan:

```text id="ks49j1"
1. Token benar-benar diterbitkan oleh issuer yang dipercaya.
2. Token belum expired dan sudah valid waktunya.
3. Token memang ditujukan untuk API/resource server ini.
4. Signature token valid terhadap key yang benar.
5. Algorithm token sesuai allowlist.
6. Claims token cukup untuk membentuk identity context.
7. Scope/permission token tidak melebihi authority yang seharusnya.
8. Failure mode aman: reject, audit, jangan fail-open.
```

JWT didefinisikan RFC 7519 sebagai format token compact dan URL-safe untuk merepresentasikan claims antar pihak. Namun RFC 8725 memperbarui RFC 7519 dengan best current practices karena implementasi JWT sering rawan jika algorithm, key, audience, issuer, dan token type tidak divalidasi dengan benar. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7519?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Access Token vs ID Token

Ini salah satu sumber bug paling umum.

| Token | Dipakai oleh | Tujuan | Boleh dipakai sebagai Bearer token ke API? |
|---|---|---|---|
| **ID Token** | Client / frontend / relying party | Membuktikan user sudah diautentikasi oleh OpenID Provider | **Jangan** jadikan authorization token API |
| **Access Token** | Resource server / API | Membawa authority untuk mengakses protected resource | **Ya**, jika memang diterbitkan untuk API tersebut |
| **Refresh Token** | Client terpercaya | Mendapat access token baru | **Tidak** dikirim ke API bisnis |

OpenID Connect mendefinisikan ID Token sebagai security token yang berisi claims tentang authentication end-user oleh Authorization Server. OIDC adalah identity layer di atas OAuth 2.0, sedangkan access token adalah token yang dipakai untuk mengakses protected resource/API. ([OpenID Foundation](https://openid.net/specs/openid-connect-core-1_0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

**Invariant praktis:**

```text id="wetruj"
Jersey API harus memvalidasi access token.
Jangan menerima ID token sebagai authorization credential untuk API bisnis.
```

---

## 3. Diagram Validasi Token

```mermaid id="g4v8r0"
flowchart TD
    A[HTTP Request] --> B[Authorization Header]
    B --> C{Bearer token exists?}
    C -->|No| X[401 missing_token]
    C -->|Yes| D[Parse JWT Header]

    D --> E{alg allowed?}
    E -->|No| X2[401 invalid_algorithm]
    E -->|Yes| F[Read kid]

    F --> G[Resolve JWK from JWKS cache]
    G --> H{Key found?}
    H -->|No| G2[Refresh JWKS once]
    G2 --> H2{Key found after refresh?}
    H2 -->|No| X3[401 unknown_key]
    H2 -->|Yes| I[Verify Signature]
    H -->|Yes| I

    I --> J{Signature valid?}
    J -->|No| X4[401 invalid_signature]
    J -->|Yes| K[Validate Claims]

    K --> L{iss valid?}
    L -->|No| X5[401 invalid_issuer]
    L -->|Yes| M{aud valid?}

    M -->|No| X6[401 invalid_audience]
    M -->|Yes| N{exp / nbf valid?}

    N -->|No| X7[401 token_time_invalid]
    N -->|Yes| O{token_use / typ valid?}

    O -->|No| X8[401 wrong_token_type]
    O -->|Yes| P[Extract subject, tenant, roles, scopes, permissions]

    P --> Q[Build AuthenticatedPrincipal]
    Q --> R[Set SecurityContext]
    R --> S[Continue to Authorization Filter / Resource Method]
```

---

## 4. Validation Checklist

| Check | Wajib | Kenapa |
|---|---:|---|
| Parse sebagai signed JWT | Ya | Jangan menerima plain/unsecured JWT |
| `alg` allowlist | Ya | Mencegah downgrade/algorithm confusion |
| Signature valid | Ya | Membuktikan token tidak dimodifikasi |
| `kid` resolve ke trusted key | Ya | Mendukung key rotation dan key selection |
| `iss` valid | Ya | Token harus dari trusted issuer |
| `aud` valid | Ya | Token harus ditujukan untuk API ini |
| `exp` valid | Ya | Expired token harus ditolak |
| `nbf` valid | Ya | Token yang belum aktif harus ditolak |
| `iat` reasonable | Disarankan | Deteksi token terlalu tua/aneh |
| `jti` | Opsional | Berguna untuk replay detection / audit / denylist |
| `sub` ada | Ya | Principal identity harus jelas |
| `scope` / `scp` valid | Tergantung | Untuk OAuth scope authorization |
| `roles` / `permissions` valid | Tergantung | Untuk authorization internal |
| `tenant_id` valid | Jika multi-tenant | Boundary query dan object access |
| `typ` / token type | Disarankan kuat | Mencegah cross-JWT confusion |
| clock skew kecil | Ya | Toleransi waktu realistis tanpa melonggarkan terlalu jauh |

RFC 8725 secara eksplisit menekankan algorithm verification, validasi cryptographic operations, validasi cryptographic inputs, dan mitigasi cross-JWT confusion. RFC 7517 mendefinisikan JSON Web Key dan menjelaskan bahwa `kid` digunakan untuk memilih key tertentu dalam JWK Set, termasuk saat key rollover. ([RFC Editor](https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc8725/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. Discovery Metadata dan JWKS

Dalam OIDC, konfigurasi provider biasanya ditemukan lewat:

```text id="fxxehz"
https://<issuer>/.well-known/openid-configuration
```

Dari metadata itu, resource server/client dapat menemukan `issuer`, `jwks_uri`, supported algorithms, endpoint, dan informasi lain. OpenID Connect Discovery mendefinisikan mekanisme discovery provider, sedangkan RFC 8414 mendefinisikan OAuth 2.0 Authorization Server Metadata. ([OpenID Foundation](https://openid.net/specs/openid-connect-discovery-1_0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh metadata konseptual:

```json id="z2qv02"
{
  "issuer": "https://idp.company.com/realms/acme",
  "authorization_endpoint": "https://idp.company.com/realms/acme/protocol/openid-connect/auth",
  "token_endpoint": "https://idp.company.com/realms/acme/protocol/openid-connect/token",
  "jwks_uri": "https://idp.company.com/realms/acme/protocol/openid-connect/certs",
  "id_token_signing_alg_values_supported": ["RS256", "ES256"]
}
```

**Rule penting:**

```text id="b91ws3"
Issuer dari metadata harus sama dengan issuer yang dikonfigurasi.
Jangan menerima metadata dari URL dinamis yang dikirim client.
JWKS URI harus berasal dari issuer terpercaya, bukan dari token.
```

---

## 6. Jersey Filter Placement

JAX-RS `ContainerRequestFilter` berjalan sebelum resource method, dapat mengubah request context, dapat mengatur `SecurityContext`, dan dapat menghentikan request dengan `abortWith(...)`. Filter juga diurutkan berdasarkan `@Priority`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/platform/9/apidocs/jakarta/ws/rs/container/containerrequestcontext?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text id="nyswkk"
AuthenticationFilter
  @Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
  - extract Bearer token
  - validate JWT
  - create AuthenticatedPrincipal
  - set SecurityContext

AuthorizationFilter
  @Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
  - read SecurityContext
  - check @RequiresPermission / @RequiresScope
  - allow or throw 403
```

---

## 7. Configuration Object

Jangan hardcode issuer, audience, dan algorithm di filter.

```java id="tey3p8"
package com.company.security.jwt;

import java.net.URI;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Set;

public record JwtValidationConfig(
        URI issuer,
        URI jwksUri,
        Set<String> allowedAudiences,
        Set<String> allowedAlgorithms,
        Duration clockSkew,
        Duration jwksCacheTtl,
        boolean requireTenant,
        String expectedTokenType
) {}
```

Contoh config:

```yaml id="wvdl2k"
security:
  jwt:
    issuer: "https://idp.company.com/realms/acme"
    jwksUri: "https://idp.company.com/realms/acme/protocol/openid-connect/certs"
    allowedAudiences:
      - "case-management-api"
      - "order-management-api"
    allowedAlgorithms:
      - "RS256"
    clockSkew: "PT60S"
    jwksCacheTtl: "PT10M"
    requireTenant: true
    expectedTokenType: "access_token"
```

**Production invariant:**

```text id="ii8rdt"
API menentukan issuer/audience/algorithms yang dipercaya.
Token tidak boleh menentukan sendiri siapa issuer/key/audience yang akan dipercaya.
```

---

## 8. Token Validation Flow - Pseudocode

```java id="tsmql5"
public final class JwtAccessTokenValidator {

    private final JwtValidationConfig config;
    private final JwksKeyResolver keyResolver;

    public VerifiedAccessToken validate(String rawToken) {
        ParsedJwt parsed = JwtParser.parse(rawToken);

        validateHeader(parsed.header());
        PublicKey key = keyResolver.resolve(parsed.header().kid(), parsed.header().alg());

        verifySignature(parsed, key);
        validateClaims(parsed.claims());

        return toVerifiedAccessToken(parsed.claims());
    }

    private void validateHeader(JwtHeader header) {
        if (header.alg() == null || !config.allowedAlgorithms().contains(header.alg())) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException("invalid_algorithm");
        }

        if ("none".equalsIgnoreCase(header.alg())) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException("unsigned_token_not_allowed");
        }

        if (header.kid() == null || header.kid().isBlank()) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException("missing_key_id");
        }
    }

    private void validateClaims(JwtClaims claims) {
        if (!config.issuer().toString().equals(claims.issuer())) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException("invalid_issuer");
        }

        if (claims.audience().stream().noneMatch(config.allowedAudiences()::contains)) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException("invalid_audience");
        }

        if (claims.isExpired(config.clockSkew())) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException("token_expired");
        }

        if (claims.isNotYetValid(config.clockSkew())) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException("token_not_yet_valid");
        }

        if (claims.subject() == null || claims.subject().isBlank()) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException("missing_subject");
        }

        if (config.requireTenant()
                && (claims.tenantId() == null || claims.tenantId().isBlank())) {
            throw new InvalidTokenException("missing_tenant");
        }
    }
}
```

Ini sengaja pseudocode agar tidak mengikat pada satu library. Untuk production, gunakan library matang seperti Nimbus JOSE + JWT, jose4j, atau library resmi platform identity provider; jangan membuat implementasi signature verification sendiri.

---

## 9. Contoh dengan Nimbus JOSE + JWT - Struktur Praktis

Nimbus JOSE + JWT menyediakan framework untuk parsing dan validasi JWT access token, termasuk signed JWT dan key retrieval dari JWK Set. Dokumentasi Nimbus juga menyediakan contoh validasi bearer JWT access token dan fasilitas JWK retrieval dengan caching/rate limiting/retry. ([Connect2id](https://connect2id.com/products/nimbus-jose-jwt/examples/validating-jwt-access-tokens?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Maven Dependency

Cek versi terbaru di repository resmi/internal perusahaan sebelum pinning.

```xml id="xbjdmj"
<dependency>
    <groupId>com.nimbusds</groupId>
    <artifactId>nimbus-jose-jwt</artifactId>
    <version>${nimbus-jose-jwt.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

### Interface Validator

```java id="spk389"
package com.company.security.jwt;

public interface AccessTokenValidator {
    VerifiedAccessToken validate(String rawToken);
}
```

### Verified Token

```java id="cpogp5"
package com.company.security.jwt;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Set;

public record VerifiedAccessToken(
        String subject,
        String username,
        String tenantId,
        String issuer,
        Set<String> audiences,
        Set<String> scopes,
        Set<String> roles,
        Set<String> permissions,
        String tokenId,
        Instant expiresAt,
        Instant issuedAt
) {}
```

### Claim Mapping Helper

```java id="xhfgp4"
package com.company.security.jwt;

import java.text.ParseException;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

public final class JwtClaimMapper {

    private JwtClaimMapper() {}

    public static Set<String> stringSet(Object value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return Set.of();
        }

        if (value instanceof String s) {
            if (s.isBlank()) {
                return Set.of();
            }
            return Set.of(s.split("\\s+"));
        }

        if (value instanceof List<?> list) {
            Set<String> result = new HashSet<>();
            for (Object item : list) {
                if (item != null) {
                    result.add(item.toString());
                }
            }
            return Set.copyOf(result);
        }

        return Set.of(value.toString());
    }

    public static Instant toInstant(java.util.Date date) {
        return date == null ? null : date.toInstant();
    }
}
```

---

## 10. Claim Normalization

Different IdP sering punya claim berbeda.

| Meaning | Common Claim Variants |
|---|---|
| Subject | `sub` |
| Username | `preferred_username`, `username`, `email` |
| Scope | `scope`, `scp` |
| Roles | `roles`, `realm_access.roles`, `resource_access.<client>.roles`, `groups` |
| Permissions | `permissions`, `authorities`, custom claim |
| Tenant | `tenant_id`, `tid`, `org_id`, `organization` |
| Token ID | `jti` |
| Token type | `typ`, `token_use`, custom claim |

**Rule:** normalisasi claims di satu tempat, jangan tersebar di resource method.

```java id="v2k9v1"
public final class AccessTokenClaimsMapper {

    public VerifiedAccessToken map(ValidatedClaims claims) {
        return new VerifiedAccessToken(
                claims.subject(),
                claims.preferredUsername(),
                claims.tenantId(),
                claims.issuer(),
                claims.audiences(),
                claims.scopes(),
                claims.roles(),
                claims.permissions(),
                claims.jwtId(),
                claims.expiresAt(),
                claims.issuedAt()
        );
    }
}
```

---

## 11. AuthenticationFilter dengan Validator

```java id="s6hgp6"
package com.company.security.authn;

import com.company.security.context.AuthenticatedPrincipal;
import com.company.security.context.RequestSecurityContext;
import com.company.security.jwt.AccessTokenValidator;
import com.company.security.jwt.VerifiedAccessToken;
import jakarta.annotation.Priority;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Priorities;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.HttpHeaders;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.io.IOException;

@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class AuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private static final String BEARER = "Bearer ";

    private final AccessTokenValidator accessTokenValidator;

    public AuthenticationFilter(AccessTokenValidator accessTokenValidator) {
        this.accessTokenValidator = accessTokenValidator;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) throws IOException {
        String authorization = ctx.getHeaderString(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);

        if (authorization == null || !authorization.startsWith(BEARER)) {
            abort401(ctx, "missing_or_invalid_authorization_header");
            return;
        }

        String rawToken = authorization.substring(BEARER.length()).trim();

        try {
            VerifiedAccessToken token = accessTokenValidator.validate(rawToken);

            AuthenticatedPrincipal principal = new AuthenticatedPrincipal(
                    token.subject(),
                    token.username(),
                    token.tenantId(),
                    token.roles(),
                    token.scopes(),
                    token.permissions(),
                    token.tokenId(),
                    token.issuer(),
                    String.join(" ", token.audiences())
            );

            ctx.setSecurityContext(new RequestSecurityContext(
                    principal,
                    ctx.getSecurityContext() != null && ctx.getSecurityContext().isSecure(),
                    "Bearer"
            ));

        } catch (InvalidAccessTokenException e) {
            abort401(ctx, e.errorCode());
        }
    }

    private void abort401(ContainerRequestContext ctx, String errorCode) {
        ctx.abortWith(Response.status(Response.Status.UNAUTHORIZED)
                .header(HttpHeaders.WWW_AUTHENTICATE,
                        "Bearer realm=\"api\", error=\"invalid_token\"")
                .entity(new SecurityErrorResponse("UNAUTHORIZED", errorCode))
                .build());
    }
}
```

---

## 12. `401` Error Mapping untuk Bearer Token

RFC 6750 mendefinisikan bearer token usage untuk OAuth 2.0 protected resources dan penggunaan `WWW-Authenticate` pada error bearer token. Resource server dapat mengembalikan error seperti `invalid_request`, `invalid_token`, atau `insufficient_scope`. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7519?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```http id="x6l5s7"
HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized
WWW-Authenticate: Bearer realm="case-api", error="invalid_token"
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "code": "UNAUTHORIZED",
  "message": "Invalid or expired access token"
}
```

Untuk scope kurang:

```http id="f0tah5"
HTTP/1.1 403 Forbidden
WWW-Authenticate: Bearer realm="case-api", error="insufficient_scope", scope="case.approve"
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "code": "FORBIDDEN",
  "message": "Insufficient permission"
}
```

---

## 13. JWKS Cache dan Key Rotation

JWKS dipakai agar resource server bisa mendapatkan public key untuk memverifikasi signature JWT. `kid` membantu memilih key yang tepat dalam JWK Set, terutama saat key rollover. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7517?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Pattern aman:

```text id="v67141"
1. Cache JWKS dari trusted jwks_uri.
2. Saat kid tidak ditemukan, refresh JWKS sekali.
3. Jika masih tidak ditemukan, reject token.
4. Jangan fetch JWKS dari URL di token.
5. Jangan jadikan kid sebagai file path, SQL input, atau URL.
6. Gunakan timeout pendek saat fetch JWKS.
7. Audit unknown kid spike, karena bisa menandakan attack atau rotation problem.
```

Pseudo-implementation:

```java id="zqw0m4"
public final class CachingJwksKeyResolver {

    private final URI jwksUri;
    private final Duration cacheTtl;
    private volatile CachedJwks cached;

    public PublicKey resolve(String kid, String alg) {
        CachedJwks current = getOrRefreshIfExpired();

        return current.findKey(kid, alg)
                .orElseGet(() -> {
                    CachedJwks refreshed = forceRefresh();
                    return refreshed.findKey(kid, alg)
                            .orElseThrow(() -> new InvalidTokenException("unknown_key_id"));
                });
    }

    private CachedJwks getOrRefreshIfExpired() {
        CachedJwks current = cached;
        if (current == null || current.isExpired()) {
            return forceRefresh();
        }
        return current;
    }

    private synchronized CachedJwks forceRefresh() {
        // HTTP GET trusted jwksUri with timeout, retry budget, TLS verification
        // parse JWK Set
        // cache until now + cacheTtl or HTTP cache header, depending policy
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("implementation depends on chosen JOSE library");
    }
}
```

---

## 14. Algorithm Allowlist

Jangan menerima algorithm dari JWT header sebagai keputusan final. Header hanya memberi sinyal; server tetap menentukan algorithm yang boleh.

```yaml id="irrn5i"
security:
  jwt:
    allowedAlgorithms:
      - RS256
```

Contoh validasi:

```java id="r7j3er"
if (!allowedAlgorithms.contains(jwtHeader.alg())) {
    throw new InvalidTokenException("invalid_algorithm");
}
```

**Hindari:**

```java id="wf9fd8"
Algorithm algorithm = Algorithm.from(jwtHeader.alg());
verifyWith(algorithm);
```

Masalahnya: caller dapat mencoba memengaruhi cara server memverifikasi token. RFC 8725 secara khusus menempatkan algorithm verification dan cryptographic validation sebagai best practices utama. ([RFC Editor](https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc8725/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15. Audience Validation

Audience adalah salah satu guard paling penting untuk mencegah token yang diterbitkan untuk service lain dipakai ke service ini.

```text id="kjrx9b"
Token untuk billing-api tidak boleh diterima oleh case-api.
Token untuk frontend client tidak otomatis valid untuk backend API.
```

Contoh:

```java id="mgpmbd"
private void validateAudience(Set<String> tokenAudiences, Set<String> allowedAudiences) {
    boolean matched = tokenAudiences.stream().anyMatch(allowedAudiences::contains);

    if (!matched) {
        throw new InvalidTokenException("invalid_audience");
    }
}
```

Model config:

```yaml id="tmqiyn"
security:
  jwt:
    allowedAudiences:
      - "case-api"
      - "case-management-service"
```

---

## 16. Issuer Validation

Issuer harus exact match dengan trusted issuer.

```java id="j4tpoa"
private void validateIssuer(String tokenIssuer, String expectedIssuer) {
    if (!expectedIssuer.equals(tokenIssuer)) {
        throw new InvalidTokenException("invalid_issuer");
    }
}
```

Jangan melakukan matching longgar seperti:

```java id="kpgvjm"
if (tokenIssuer.contains("company.com")) {
    allow();
}
```

Untuk multi-issuer system, buat registry eksplisit:

```yaml id="hc5v1g"
security:
  issuers:
    - issuer: "https://idp.company.com/realms/internal"
      jwksUri: "https://idp.company.com/realms/internal/protocol/openid-connect/certs"
      audiences: ["case-api"]

    - issuer: "https://partner-idp.example.com/oauth2"
      jwksUri: "https://partner-idp.example.com/oauth2/jwks"
      audiences: ["partner-case-api"]
```

---

## 17. Time Validation

JWT registered claims meliputi `exp` untuk expiration time, `nbf` untuk not-before, `iat` untuk issued-at, `iss`, `sub`, `aud`, dan `jti`. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7519?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="dl9js3"
private void validateTime(Instant now, Instant exp, Instant nbf, Duration clockSkew) {
    if (exp == null) {
        throw new InvalidTokenException("missing_exp");
    }

    if (now.minus(clockSkew).isAfter(exp)) {
        throw new InvalidTokenException("token_expired");
    }

    if (nbf != null && now.plus(clockSkew).isBefore(nbf)) {
        throw new InvalidTokenException("token_not_yet_valid");
    }
}
```

**Clock skew rekomendasi praktis:**

| Environment | Skew |
|---|---:|
| Same cluster, NTP sehat | 30-60 detik |
| Hybrid/on-prem dengan clock drift | 60-120 detik |
| Lebih dari itu | Curigai masalah time sync |

Jangan menjadikan clock skew terlalu besar karena itu memperpanjang masa token yang seharusnya sudah invalid.

---

## 18. Scope Extraction

OIDC/OAuth provider bisa memakai claim `scope` sebagai string space-separated, atau `scp` sebagai array.

```java id="qjjlpy"
public Set<String> extractScopes(Map<String, Object> claims) {
    Object scope = claims.get("scope");
    if (scope instanceof String s) {
        return Set.of(s.split("\\s+"));
    }

    Object scp = claims.get("scp");
    if (scp instanceof Collection<?> c) {
        return c.stream()
                .map(Object::toString)
                .collect(Collectors.toUnmodifiableSet());
    }

    return Set.of();
}
```

Scope cocok untuk coarse-grained API boundary:

```text id="ww3nzi"
case:read
case:write
order:read
order:write
```

Permission internal bisa lebih domain-granular:

```text id="yeqnv0"
case.approve
case.assign
case.reopen
workflow.task.reassign
```

---

## 19. Tenant Validation

Untuk multi-tenant system, tenant harus masuk ke identity context sebelum akses DB.

```java id="c9ej7k"
private String requireTenant(Map<String, Object> claims) {
    Object tenant = claims.get("tenant_id");

    if (tenant == null || tenant.toString().isBlank()) {
        throw new InvalidTokenException("missing_tenant");
    }

    return tenant.toString();
}
```

**Invariant:**

```text id="wch91h"
Tidak ada tenant context berarti request tidak boleh mencapai MyBatis query.
```

Untuk partner/system token, tenant bisa resolved dari client registration, mTLS identity, atau mapping service account, tetapi hasil akhirnya tetap harus eksplisit di request context.

---

## 20. Token Type / Cross-JWT Confusion

Cross-JWT confusion terjadi saat token untuk satu konteks diterima di konteks lain, misalnya ID token diterima sebagai access token, atau token untuk API A diterima oleh API B. RFC 8725 membahas cross-JWT confusion sebagai salah satu kelas ancaman JWT. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc8725?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Mitigasi praktis:

```text id="ykytcp"
1. Validate audience.
2. Validate issuer.
3. Validate token type if provider supplies it.
4. Validate expected claims shape.
5. Separate validator for access token vs ID token.
6. Do not use one generic "accept any valid JWT" validator.
```

Contoh:

```java id="auyj1y"
private void validateTokenUse(Map<String, Object> claims) {
    Object tokenUse = claims.get("token_use");

    if (tokenUse != null && !"access".equals(tokenUse.toString())) {
        throw new InvalidTokenException("wrong_token_type");
    }
}
```

Nama claim berbeda antar provider. Contoh di atas hanya pattern, bukan standar universal.

---

## 21. Opaque Token vs JWT

Tidak semua access token harus JWT. OAuth 2.0 membiarkan format access token sebagai pilihan implementasi, dan RFC 7662 mendefinisikan token introspection agar protected resource dapat menanyakan active state dan metadata token ke authorization server. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7662?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Model | Kelebihan | Kekurangan | Cocok Untuk |
|---|---|---|---|
| JWT self-contained | Cepat, tidak perlu call IdP per request | Revocation sulit real-time, claim bisa stale | High-throughput internal APIs |
| Opaque token + introspection | Revocation/status real-time | Latency & dependency ke IdP | High-risk APIs, partner APIs |
| JWT + short TTL | Balance umum | Perlu refresh flow benar | Microservice umum |
| JWT + introspection selective | Bisa cek real-time untuk action sensitif | Lebih kompleks | Approval/payment/admin operation |

Pattern hybrid:

```text id="vvvgz6"
GET/list/read biasa:
  validate JWT locally

Approve/delete/admin/high-risk:
  validate JWT locally + optional introspection / permission freshness check
```

---

## 22. Revocation Strategy

JWT lokal tidak otomatis tahu kalau user sudah disable, role dicabut, atau session dicabut setelah token diterbitkan.

| Strategy | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|
| Short-lived access token | Default paling umum |
| Refresh token rotation | Client flow modern |
| Introspection | Butuh active-state real-time |
| Denylist by `jti` | Incident response / logout tertentu |
| Permission version claim | Detect permission stale |
| Session version claim | Detect forced logout |
| User status cache | Disable user cepat propagate |

OAuth 2.0 Security Best Current Practice RFC 9700 adalah BCP terbaru untuk OAuth 2.0 security dan mencakup mitigasi terhadap kelas serangan modern pada OAuth flow dan token handling. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/rfc9700/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 23. Safe Logging

Jangan log token mentah.

```java id="xj2lgg"
log.warn("JWT validation failed: reason={}, issuer={}, kid={}, correlationId={}",
        reason,
        safeIssuer,
        safeKid,
        correlationId);
```

Jangan:

```java id="be2t8g"
log.warn("Invalid token: {}", rawJwt);
```

Log yang berguna:

```json id="qy7r1v"
{
  "event": "JWT_VALIDATION_FAILED",
  "reason": "invalid_audience",
  "issuer": "https://idp.company.com/realms/acme",
  "kid": "2026-07-key-1",
  "audience": ["wrong-api"],
  "correlationId": "req-123"
}
```

---

## 24. Audit Event untuk Token Validation

```java id="e31oc0"
public record TokenValidationAuditEvent(
        String eventType,
        String decision,
        String reason,
        String issuer,
        String subject,
        String tenantId,
        String tokenId,
        String keyId,
        String algorithm,
        String correlationId
) {}
```

Contoh deny:

```json id="gzlckz"
{
  "eventType": "TOKEN_VALIDATION",
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "invalid_audience",
  "issuer": "https://idp.company.com/realms/acme",
  "subject": null,
  "tenantId": null,
  "tokenId": null,
  "keyId": "kid-2026-01",
  "algorithm": "RS256",
  "correlationId": "req-8f91"
}
```

---

## 25. Failure Mode Matrix

| Failure | Response | Log Internal | Retry? |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Missing header | `401` | `missing_authorization` | Tidak |
| Non-Bearer scheme | `401` | `invalid_scheme` | Tidak |
| Malformed JWT | `401` | `malformed_token` | Tidak |
| `alg=none` | `401` | `unsigned_token_not_allowed` | Tidak |
| Unknown `kid` | `401` | `unknown_key`, refresh JWKS attempted | Refresh JWKS sekali |
| JWKS endpoint timeout | `401` / `503` tergantung policy | `jwks_fetch_failed` | Internal retry terbatas |
| Invalid signature | `401` | `invalid_signature` | Tidak |
| Expired token | `401` | `token_expired` | Client refresh |
| Wrong issuer | `401` | `invalid_issuer` | Tidak |
| Wrong audience | `401` | `invalid_audience` | Tidak |
| Valid token, missing scope | `403` | `insufficient_scope` | Tidak |
| Valid token, object denied | `403` / `404` | `object_access_denied` | Tidak |

Untuk endpoint sensitif, default yang lebih aman adalah **fail-closed**. Jangan mengizinkan request hanya karena JWKS/PDP/IdP sedang error.

---

## 26. Unit Test Matrix

```text id="rmj4g2"
[ ] valid token accepted
[ ] missing Authorization header rejected
[ ] Basic token rejected
[ ] malformed JWT rejected
[ ] unsigned JWT rejected
[ ] disallowed alg rejected
[ ] unknown kid triggers one JWKS refresh
[ ] unknown kid after refresh rejected
[ ] invalid signature rejected
[ ] wrong issuer rejected
[ ] wrong audience rejected
[ ] expired token rejected
[ ] nbf in future rejected
[ ] missing subject rejected
[ ] missing tenant rejected for tenant-required API
[ ] ID token rejected as API access token
[ ] scope claim string parsed correctly
[ ] scp claim array parsed correctly
[ ] roles claim normalized correctly
[ ] token raw value never appears in logs
```

---

## 27. Integration Test di Jersey

Contoh target test:

```java id="dan0wq"
@Test
void shouldRejectExpiredToken() {
    String expiredToken = tokenFactory.createToken(builder -> builder
            .issuer(ISSUER)
            .audience("case-api")
            .subject("user-123")
            .expired());

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-1")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + expiredToken)
            .get();

    assertEquals(401, response.getStatus());
}
```

Contoh valid token:

```java id="s9cc1k"
@Test
void shouldAllowValidTokenWithCorrectAudience() {
    String token = tokenFactory.createToken(builder -> builder
            .issuer(ISSUER)
            .audience("case-api")
            .subject("user-123")
            .claim("tenant_id", "tenant-a")
            .claim("scope", "case:read"));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-1")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + token)
            .get();

    assertNotEquals(401, response.getStatus());
}
```

---

## 28. Production Checklist

```text id="awlr20"
[ ] Issuer configured explicitly
[ ] Audience configured explicitly per API
[ ] JWKS URI comes from trusted config/discovery, not token
[ ] Allowed algorithms configured explicitly
[ ] "none" algorithm rejected
[ ] Signature verified before trusting claims
[ ] exp required and validated
[ ] nbf validated if present
[ ] subject required
[ ] tenant required for multi-tenant API
[ ] ID token rejected as API access token
[ ] JWKS cache has TTL and forced refresh on unknown kid
[ ] JWKS HTTP client has timeout
[ ] Unknown kid refresh happens once, not infinite loop
[ ] Raw token never logged
[ ] 401/403 semantics consistent
[ ] Negative tests exist
[ ] Security audit event emitted for deny
[ ] Metrics exist for invalid_token, expired_token, invalid_audience, unknown_kid
```

---

## 29. Minimal Implementation Rules

```text id="w0iay1"
1. Decode is not validation.
2. Signature validation alone is not enough.
3. Valid issuer alone is not enough.
4. Valid audience alone is not enough.
5. A token valid for another API is invalid here.
6. A valid ID token is still not an API access token.
7. A JWT header is untrusted input.
8. kid selects among trusted keys; kid does not define trust.
9. Token claims become internal principal only after all validation passes.
10. Resource methods should never manually parse JWT.
```

---

## 30. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | JWT structure | Header, payload, signature, registered claims |
| 2 | OIDC/OAuth token roles | ID token vs access token vs refresh token |
| 3 | Issuer/audience validation | Prevent token substitution |
| 4 | JWKS/key rotation | Validate signature reliably |
| 5 | Claim normalization | Build stable `AuthenticatedPrincipal` |
| 6 | Jersey filter integration | Set `SecurityContext` correctly |
| 7 | Scope/permission extraction | Prepare authorization layer |
| 8 | Failure handling | Correct `401`, safe logs, audit |
| 9 | Tests | Negative-path coverage |
| 10 | Operations | JWKS cache, metrics, alerting |
