## Response:
7/13/2026, 4:36:19 AM

Kita masuk ke desain authorization model: permission catalog, role bundling, OAuth scope boundary, ACL per object, ABAC/ReBAC untuk rule yang lebih kaya, dan bagaimana menaruhnya di Jersey + MyBatis/PostgreSQL tanpa bocor lintas tenant.

# Cheatsheet - Permission Model, RBAC, Scope, ACL, ABAC, dan ReBAC di Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan

Cheatsheet ini fokus ke **desain authorization model**, bukan validasi JWT lagi.

Masalah yang ingin diselesaikan:

```text
User/service sudah authenticated.
Sekarang sistem harus menjawab:
- boleh akses endpoint ini?
- boleh melakukan action ini?
- boleh melihat object/data ini?
- boleh melakukan transisi workflow ini?
- boleh lintas tenant?
- keputusan allow/deny ini bisa diaudit?
```

Prinsip dasarnya: authorization harus **deny by default**, mengikuti **least privilege**, dan divalidasi pada setiap request, bukan hanya saat login. OWASP Authorization Cheat Sheet secara eksplisit menekankan least privilege, deny by default, dan validasi permission pada setiap request. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Diagram Besar Authorization Model

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[AuthenticatedPrincipal] --> B[Authorization Request]

    B --> C[Subject]
    B --> D[Action]
    B --> E[Resource/Object]
    B --> F[Context]

    C --> C1[User / Service Account / Partner / Admin]
    D --> D1[read / create / update / approve / assign / delete]
    E --> E1[Case / Order / Task / User / Tenant / File]
    F --> F1[Tenant / Status / Channel / Time / Risk / Workflow State]

    B --> G{Authorization Model}

    G --> H[Scope]
    G --> I[RBAC]
    G --> J[Permission]
    G --> K[ACL]
    G --> L[ABAC]
    G --> M[ReBAC]

    H --> N[Token Boundary]
    I --> O[Role Bundling]
    J --> P[Business Capability]
    K --> Q[Object-Specific Grant]
    L --> R[Attribute-Based Rule]
    M --> S[Relationship-Based Rule]

    N --> T[Decision: Allow / Deny]
    O --> T
    P --> T
    Q --> T
    R --> T
    S --> T

    T --> U[Jersey Resource / Domain Service / MyBatis Query]
    T --> V[Audit Log]
```

---

# 3. Mental Model

Authorization request sebaiknya selalu bisa dinyatakan seperti ini:

```text
Can <subject> perform <action> on <resource> under <context>?
```

Contoh:

```text
Can user-123 approve case-789 under tenant-A while case status = SUBMITTED?
Can service-order-api cancel order-555 under tenant-B?
Can supervisor-777 reassign workflow-task-999?
Can partner-client-X read customer-profile-111 through public API?
```

Kalau authorization hanya dicek seperti ini:

```text
Does user have ADMIN role?
```

itu terlalu kasar untuk aplikasi regulatory, case management, order management, atau workflow-heavy platform.

---

# 4. Perbedaan Scope, Role, Permission, ACL, ABAC, ReBAC

| Model | Menjawab Pertanyaan | Cocok Untuk | Contoh |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Scope** | Token ini boleh memanggil area API apa? | OAuth/API boundary | `case:read`, `case:write` |
| **Role** | User ini punya paket capability apa? | Administrasi user/group | `CASE_REVIEWER`, `SUPERVISOR` |
| **Permission** | Capability bisnis apa yang dimiliki actor? | Authorization internal aplikasi | `case.approve`, `task.reassign` |
| **ACL** | Siapa boleh akses object spesifik ini? | Sharing, assignment, object-level access | user A boleh read case 123 |
| **ABAC** | Apakah atribut subject/object/context memenuhi rule? | Policy kompleks | department, risk, region, status |
| **ReBAC** | Apakah relasi actor terhadap object membolehkan akses? | Ownership, hierarchy, graph relationship | user is assignee of case |

Ringkasan praktis:

```text
Scope  = batas token/API client
Role   = bundle permission
Permission = business capability
ACL    = grant per object
ABAC   = rule berbasis atribut
ReBAC  = rule berbasis relasi
```

---

# 5. Authorization Layering yang Direkomendasikan

Jangan memilih hanya satu model. Dalam microservice nyata, biasanya beberapa model dipakai berlapis.

```text
Request masuk
  ↓
1. Scope check
   - Apakah token boleh masuk ke API/domain ini?
  ↓
2. Endpoint permission check
   - Apakah actor punya capability untuk action ini?
  ↓
3. Object-level authorization
   - Apakah actor boleh menyentuh object ID ini?
  ↓
4. State/workflow authorization
   - Apakah action valid untuk status saat ini?
  ↓
5. Data-level enforcement
   - Apakah query hanya mengembalikan data yang boleh dilihat?
  ↓
6. Audit decision
   - Bisa dijelaskan kenapa allow/deny?
```

Contoh untuk approve case:

```text
Token scope:
  case:write

Permission:
  case.approve

Object check:
  actor tenant == case tenant

Relationship:
  actor is assigned reviewer or supervisor

State:
  case.status == SUBMITTED

Separation of duty:
  actor != case.created_by

Decision:
  allow / deny with reason
```

---

# 6. Permission Catalog

Permission adalah unit capability internal. Ini sebaiknya lebih stabil daripada role.

## Naming Convention

```text
<domain>.<resource>.<action>
```

Contoh:

```text
case.case.read
case.case.create
case.case.update
case.case.submit
case.case.assign
case.case.approve
case.case.reject
case.case.close

workflow.task.claim
workflow.task.complete
workflow.task.reassign
workflow.task.escalate

order.order.read
order.order.create
order.order.cancel
order.order.retry_provisioning

admin.user.read
admin.user.invite
admin.user.disable
admin.role.assign
```

Kalau ingin lebih ringkas, domain dan resource bisa digabung bila domainnya jelas:

```text
case.read
case.submit
case.approve
task.claim
task.complete
```

## Hindari Permission UI-Oriented

Jangan:

```text
show_approve_button
can_click_submit
display_admin_menu
```

Sebaiknya:

```text
case.approve
case.submit
admin.user.manage
```

Alasannya: permission harus merepresentasikan **business capability**, bukan tampilan UI.

---

# 7. Permission Granularity

| Granularity | Contoh | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Terlalu kasar | `admin` | Privilege terlalu luas |
| Sedang | `case.approve` | Umumnya ideal |
| Sangat granular | `case.approve.high_risk.regional.level_2` | Sulit dikelola |
| Contextual | `case.approve` + ABAC rule | Biasanya paling fleksibel |

Pattern yang sehat:

```text
Permission menentukan capability utama.
ABAC/ReBAC/Object check menentukan kondisi detailnya.
```

Contoh:

```text
Permission:
  case.approve

ABAC:
  case.risk_level <= actor.approval_limit
  case.region in actor.allowed_regions

ReBAC:
  actor is supervisor of assigned reviewer
```

---

# 8. Role-Based Access Control / RBAC

Role cocok untuk administrasi user karena manusia lebih mudah mengelola role daripada ratusan permission individual.

## Role sebagai Bundle Permission

```text
Role: CASE_REVIEWER
  - case.read
  - case.update
  - case.submit
  - workflow.task.claim
  - workflow.task.complete

Role: CASE_SUPERVISOR
  - case.read
  - case.assign
  - case.approve
  - case.reject
  - workflow.task.reassign

Role: CASE_ADMIN
  - case.read
  - case.assign
  - case.close
  - admin.user.read
```

## Jangan Hardcode Role di Business Logic

Buruk:

```java
if (principal.hasRole("ADMIN")) {
    approveCase();
}
```

Lebih baik:

```java
if (principal.hasPermission("case.approve")) {
    approveCase();
}
```

Kenapa? Karena role sering berubah mengikuti organisasi, sedangkan permission lebih stabil sebagai capability sistem.

---

# 9. Scope Model

Scope biasanya berasal dari OAuth access token. Scope cocok sebagai **API boundary**, bukan sebagai satu-satunya authorization model.

Contoh scope:

```text
case:read
case:write
order:read
order:write
admin:manage
```

Mapping praktis:

| Scope | Arti | Permission Internal yang Mungkin |
|---|---|---|
| `case:read` | Token boleh membaca case API | `case.read` |
| `case:write` | Token boleh melakukan perubahan case | `case.create`, `case.update`, `case.submit` |
| `case:approve` | Token boleh masuk ke approval endpoint | `case.approve` |
| `workflow:write` | Token boleh mutate workflow task | `workflow.task.claim`, `workflow.task.complete` |

Pattern:

```text
Scope check:
  coarse API boundary

Permission check:
  business capability

Object/ABAC/ReBAC check:
  actual access decision
```

---

# 10. ACL - Access Control List

ACL cocok ketika akses ditentukan per object.

Contoh:

```text
case-123:
  user-1: read
  user-2: read, update
  group-reviewer-a: read, approve
```

Google Zanzibar paper menggambarkan authorization sebagai sistem yang menyimpan dan mengevaluasi ACL dengan model data dan configuration language seragam untuk banyak service. Paper tersebut mencontohkan bentuk sederhana seperti “user U has relation R to object O”, dan digunakan untuk skala besar di produk seperti Calendar, Drive, Cloud, Photos, dan YouTube. ([Google Research](https://research.google/pubs/zanzibar-googles-consistent-global-authorization-system/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Schema ACL Sederhana di PostgreSQL

```sql
CREATE TABLE resource_acl (
    id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_type   TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_id     TEXT NOT NULL,
    subject_type    TEXT NOT NULL, -- USER, GROUP, ROLE, SERVICE
    subject_id      TEXT NOT NULL,
    permission      TEXT NOT NULL, -- read, update, approve
    granted_by      TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL
);

CREATE INDEX idx_resource_acl_lookup
ON resource_acl (
    tenant_id,
    resource_type,
    resource_id,
    subject_type,
    subject_id,
    permission
);
```

## ACL Lookup

```sql
SELECT 1
FROM resource_acl acl
WHERE acl.tenant_id = :tenantId
  AND acl.resource_type = 'CASE'
  AND acl.resource_id = :caseId
  AND acl.subject_type = 'USER'
  AND acl.subject_id = :actorId
  AND acl.permission = 'read'
  AND (acl.expires_at IS NULL OR acl.expires_at > now())
LIMIT 1;
```

## Kapan ACL Cocok?

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| Object sharing | Permission global sederhana |
| Assignment per case/order/task | Semua user role sama |
| Temporary grant | Policy sangat attribute-driven |
| Delegated access | Hierarki relasi kompleks tanpa graph model |

---

# 11. ABAC - Attribute-Based Access Control

ABAC menggunakan atribut subject, object, operation, dan environment untuk memutuskan akses. NIST SP 800-162 mendefinisikan ABAC sebagai model yang mengevaluasi aturan terhadap atribut subject/object, operation, dan kondisi environment yang relevan untuk grant/deny access. ([NIST Publications](https://nvlpubs.nist.gov/nistpubs/specialpublications/nist.sp.800-162.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Bentuk Authorization Request ABAC

```json
{
  "subject": {
    "id": "user-123",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a",
    "department": "compliance",
    "level": 3,
    "regions": ["ID-JK", "ID-BD"]
  },
  "action": "case.approve",
  "object": {
    "type": "case",
    "id": "case-789",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a",
    "status": "SUBMITTED",
    "riskLevel": 2,
    "region": "ID-JK",
    "createdBy": "user-999"
  },
  "environment": {
    "channel": "internal-web",
    "ipRisk": "LOW",
    "time": "2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00"
  }
}
```

## Contoh Rule ABAC

```text
Allow case.approve when:
  subject.tenantId == object.tenantId
  AND subject.department == "compliance"
  AND subject.level >= object.riskLevel
  AND object.status == "SUBMITTED"
  AND object.createdBy != subject.id
  AND object.region in subject.regions
```

## Implementasi Java Sederhana

```java
public final class CaseApprovalPolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision canApprove(
            AuthenticatedPrincipal actor,
            CaseRecord caseRecord,
            RequestEnvironment environment
    ) {
        if (!actor.tenantId().equals(caseRecord.tenantId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("tenant_mismatch");
        }

        if (!actor.hasPermission("case.approve")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_permission");
        }

        if (!"SUBMITTED".equals(caseRecord.status())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("invalid_case_status");
        }

        if (caseRecord.createdBy().equals(actor.subject())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("maker_checker_violation");
        }

        if (!actor.allowedRegions().contains(caseRecord.region())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("region_not_allowed");
        }

        if (actor.approvalLevel() < caseRecord.riskLevel()) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("approval_level_too_low");
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.allow("case_approval_policy_matched");
    }
}
```

ABAC bagus untuk policy kaya, tetapi bisa sulit diaudit bila rule tersebar di banyak class tanpa catalog/versioning.

---

# 12. ReBAC - Relationship-Based Access Control

ReBAC menentukan akses berdasarkan relasi antar subject dan object.

Contoh relasi:

```text
user-123 is owner of case-789
user-456 is assignee of case-789
user-777 is supervisor of user-456
case-789 belongs to tenant-a
case-789 has parent account-555
user-123 is member of group-reviewer-a
group-reviewer-a can approve case-789
```

OpenFGA mendeskripsikan ReBAC sebagai authorization yang bergantung pada relasi antara user dan object, serta antar object; misalnya user bisa melihat dokumen bila ia punya akses ke parent folder. OpenFGA juga menyediakan modeling language dan API untuk fine-grained authorization. ([OpenFGA](https://openfga.dev/docs/authorization-concepts?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Tuple Model

```text
<object>#<relation>@<subject>
```

Contoh:

```text
case:case-789#owner@user:user-123
case:case-789#assignee@user:user-456
case:case-789#viewer@group:reviewer-a
group:reviewer-a#member@user:user-888
tenant:tenant-a#member@user:user-123
```

## Model Konseptual

```text
type user

type group
  relations
    define member: [user]

type tenant
  relations
    define member: [user, group#member]
    define admin: [user]

type case
  relations
    define tenant: [tenant]
    define owner: [user]
    define assignee: [user]
    define reviewer: [user, group#member]
    define viewer: owner or assignee or reviewer or tenant->admin
    define approver: reviewer or tenant->admin
```

## Kapan ReBAC Cocok?

| Cocok | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Ownership | creator/owner bisa manage object |
| Assignment | assigned officer bisa update case |
| Hierarchy | manager bisa melihat subordinate cases |
| Sharing | group diberi akses ke resource |
| Parent-child access | akses folder memberi akses dokumen |
| Multi-tenant relationship | tenant admin bisa manage tenant resources |

---

# 13. Authorization Decision Object

Selalu buat decision eksplisit, jangan hanya boolean.

```java
public record AuthorizationDecision(
        boolean allowed,
        String reason,
        String policy,
        String policyVersion
) {
    public static AuthorizationDecision allow(String reason) {
        return new AuthorizationDecision(true, reason, "case-policy", "v1");
    }

    public static AuthorizationDecision deny(String reason) {
        return new AuthorizationDecision(false, reason, "case-policy", "v1");
    }
}
```

Kenapa?

```text
Boolean hanya menjawab allow/deny.
Decision object menjawab allow/deny + alasan + policy version.
```

Ini penting untuk audit, troubleshooting, dan regulatory defensibility.

---

# 14. Authorization Request Object

```java
public record AuthorizationRequest(
        String subjectId,
        String tenantId,
        String action,
        String resourceType,
        String resourceId,
        Map<String, Object> subjectAttributes,
        Map<String, Object> resourceAttributes,
        Map<String, Object> environmentAttributes
) {}
```

Contoh builder:

```java
AuthorizationRequest request = new AuthorizationRequest(
        actor.subject(),
        actor.tenantId(),
        "case.approve",
        "case",
        caseRecord.id(),
        Map.of(
                "permissions", actor.permissions(),
                "roles", actor.roles(),
                "approvalLevel", actor.approvalLevel()
        ),
        Map.of(
                "tenantId", caseRecord.tenantId(),
                "status", caseRecord.status(),
                "riskLevel", caseRecord.riskLevel(),
                "createdBy", caseRecord.createdBy()
        ),
        Map.of(
                "channel", "internal-web",
                "correlationId", correlationId
        )
);
```

---

# 15. Jersey Endpoint Pattern

Endpoint-level permission tetap berguna sebagai coarse guard.

```java
@Path("/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseApplicationService caseApplicationService;

    @POST
    @Path("/{caseId}/approve")
    @RequiresPermission("case.approve")
    public Response approveCase(
            @Context SecurityContext securityContext,
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId
    ) {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor =
                (AuthenticatedPrincipal) securityContext.getUserPrincipal();

        caseApplicationService.approveCase(actor, caseId);

        return Response.noContent().build();
    }
}
```

Tapi object/state authorization tetap di service layer.

```java
public void approveCase(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor, String caseId) {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findByIdForTenant(
            caseId,
            actor.tenantId()
    ).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Case not found"));

    AuthorizationDecision decision =
            casePolicy.canApprove(actor, caseRecord);

    auditAuthorization(actor, "case.approve", caseRecord, decision);

    if (!decision.allowed()) {
        throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
    }

    caseRepository.markApproved(caseId, actor.subject());
}
```

---

# 16. MyBatis / PostgreSQL Enforcement

Authorization jangan hanya dilakukan setelah data keluar dari database.

## Pattern Aman untuk Query List

```java
public List<CaseRecord> listVisibleCases(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor) {
    CaseVisibilityCriteria criteria = new CaseVisibilityCriteria(
            actor.tenantId(),
            actor.subject(),
            actor.hasPermission("case.read.all"),
            actor.hasPermission("case.read.assigned"),
            actor.hasPermission("case.read.own")
    );

    return caseMapper.findVisibleCases(criteria);
}
```

```java
public record CaseVisibilityCriteria(
        String tenantId,
        String actorId,
        boolean canReadAll,
        boolean canReadAssigned,
        boolean canReadOwn
) {}
```

MyBatis XML:

```xml
<select id="findVisibleCases" resultMap="CaseRecordMap">
    SELECT
        c.id,
        c.tenant_id,
        c.status,
        c.assigned_user_id,
        c.created_by,
        c.created_at
    FROM cases c
    WHERE c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND (
            #{canReadAll} = true
            OR (#{canReadAssigned} = true AND c.assigned_user_id = #{actorId})
            OR (#{canReadOwn} = true AND c.created_by = #{actorId})
      )
    ORDER BY c.created_at DESC
</select>
```

## Pattern Aman untuk Single Object

```xml
<select id="findVisibleCaseById" resultMap="CaseRecordMap">
    SELECT
        c.id,
        c.tenant_id,
        c.status,
        c.assigned_user_id,
        c.created_by,
        c.created_at
    FROM cases c
    WHERE c.id = #{caseId}
      AND c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND (
            #{canReadAll} = true
            OR (#{canReadAssigned} = true AND c.assigned_user_id = #{actorId})
            OR (#{canReadOwn} = true AND c.created_by = #{actorId})
      )
</select>
```

Ini mencegah data lintas tenant/object keluar dari DB.

---

# 17. ACL Table + MyBatis Lookup

```java
public boolean hasAclPermission(
        String tenantId,
        String resourceType,
        String resourceId,
        String subjectId,
        String permission
) {
    return aclMapper.existsActiveGrant(
            tenantId,
            resourceType,
            resourceId,
            "USER",
            subjectId,
            permission
    );
}
```

```xml
<select id="existsActiveGrant" resultType="boolean">
    SELECT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM resource_acl acl
        WHERE acl.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND acl.resource_type = #{resourceType}
          AND acl.resource_id = #{resourceId}
          AND acl.subject_type = #{subjectType}
          AND acl.subject_id = #{subjectId}
          AND acl.permission = #{permission}
          AND (acl.expires_at IS NULL OR acl.expires_at > now())
    )
</select>
```

Gunakan ACL untuk grant yang memang object-specific. Jangan semua permission dijadikan ACL kalau sebenarnya global capability.

---

# 18. Policy Service Boundary

Untuk microservice kecil, policy bisa local class. Untuk enterprise/cross-service, bisa dipisahkan menjadi entitlement service atau policy decision point.

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    A[Jersey Resource] --> B[Application Service]
    B --> C[Authorization Service]
    C --> D{Policy Source}

    D --> E[Local Java Policy]
    D --> F[PostgreSQL ACL / Permission Tables]
    D --> G[External PDP]
    D --> H[OpenFGA / Zanzibar-like Service]
    D --> I[OPA / Policy Engine]

    C --> J[AuthorizationDecision]
    J --> K[Audit]
    J --> L[Allow / Deny]
```

Pattern PEP/PDP:

| Komponen | Tugas |
|---|---|
| PEP - Policy Enforcement Point | Tempat enforcement: Jersey filter, service layer, repository |
| PDP - Policy Decision Point | Tempat keputusan allow/deny |
| PIP - Policy Information Point | Sumber atribut: DB, IdP claims, HR system, tenant config |
| PAP - Policy Administration Point | Tempat policy dikonfigurasi/dikelola |

---

# 19. Data Model Permission/RBAC

## Tabel Role dan Permission

```sql
CREATE TABLE app_permission (
    code        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    description TEXT NOT NULL,
    domain      TEXT NOT NULL,
    risk_level  TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NORMAL',
    active      BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);

CREATE TABLE app_role (
    code        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    description TEXT NOT NULL,
    active      BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);

CREATE TABLE app_role_permission (
    role_code       TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app_role(code),
    permission_code TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app_permission(code),
    PRIMARY KEY (role_code, permission_code)
);

CREATE TABLE app_user_role (
    tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    user_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    role_code TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app_role(code),
    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, user_id, role_code)
);
```

## Query Effective Permissions

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT rp.permission_code
FROM app_user_role ur
JOIN app_role_permission rp
  ON rp.role_code = ur.role_code
JOIN app_permission p
  ON p.code = rp.permission_code
WHERE ur.tenant_id = :tenantId
  AND ur.user_id = :userId
  AND p.active = true;
```

---

# 20. Role Explosion Problem

Role explosion terjadi ketika role dibuat untuk setiap kombinasi kondisi.

Contoh buruk:

```text
JAKARTA_CASE_REVIEWER_LEVEL_1
JAKARTA_CASE_REVIEWER_LEVEL_2
BANDUNG_CASE_REVIEWER_LEVEL_1
BANDUNG_CASE_REVIEWER_LEVEL_2
HIGH_RISK_JAKARTA_CASE_APPROVER
LOW_RISK_BANDUNG_CASE_APPROVER
```

Lebih baik:

```text
Role:
  CASE_REVIEWER
  CASE_APPROVER

Attributes:
  region = ID-JK, ID-BD
  approvalLevel = 1, 2, 3
  department = compliance

Permission:
  case.approve

ABAC Rule:
  actor.region contains case.region
  actor.approvalLevel >= case.riskLevel
```

Dengan begitu role tetap manusiawi, rule tetap ekspresif.

---

# 21. Authorization untuk Workflow / Camunda

Untuk workflow, permission saja tidak cukup. Harus ada state dan task relationship.

Contoh task authorization:

```text
Can actor complete task?
  - actor tenant == process tenant
  - actor has workflow.task.complete
  - task is active
  - actor is assignee OR candidate user OR member of candidate group
  - task business key belongs to visible case/order
```

Contoh approval authorization:

```text
Can actor approve case?
  - has case.approve
  - case.status == SUBMITTED
  - actor != case.createdBy
  - actor is assigned approver OR supervisor
  - actor approvalLevel >= case riskLevel
```

Jangan hanya mengandalkan Camunda task claim/assignee. API bisnis tetap perlu guard sendiri, terutama bila endpoint bisa dipanggil langsung.

---

# 22. Public, Internal, Admin, dan System Endpoint

Klasifikasikan endpoint secara eksplisit.

| Endpoint Type | Contoh | Guard |
|---|---|---|
| Public | health, readiness, metadata terbatas | No auth atau minimal |
| Authenticated | profile, own tasks | Valid token |
| Domain-protected | approve case, cancel order | Permission + object check |
| Admin | user disable, role assign | Admin permission + audit |
| Internal service | retry provisioning, event replay | service identity + mTLS/scope |
| Break-glass | emergency access | approval, reason, audit ketat |

Jangan endpoint menjadi public hanya karena lupa annotation.

Pattern:

```java
@PublicEndpoint
@Path("/health")
public Response health() {}

@RequiresPermission("case.approve")
@Path("/cases/{id}/approve")
public Response approve() {}
```

Default authorization harus deny untuk endpoint yang tidak jelas.

---

# 23. Audit Authorization Decision

```java
public record AuthorizationAuditEvent(
        String eventType,
        String decision,
        String reason,
        String subjectId,
        String tenantId,
        String action,
        String resourceType,
        String resourceId,
        String policy,
        String policyVersion,
        String correlationId
) {}
```

Contoh:

```json
{
  "eventType": "AUTHORIZATION_DECISION",
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "maker_checker_violation",
  "subjectId": "user-123",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "action": "case.approve",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "case-789",
  "policy": "case-approval-policy",
  "policyVersion": "v3",
  "correlationId": "req-abc"
}
```

Audit yang bagus harus menjawab:

```text
Siapa actor-nya?
Action apa?
Resource apa?
Allow atau deny?
Kenapa?
Policy versi berapa?
Dalam tenant/context apa?
```

---

# 24. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| `if role == ADMIN` di semua tempat | Sulit evolve, privilege luas | Gunakan permission |
| Authorization hanya di UI | API tetap bisa dipanggil langsung | Enforce di backend |
| Object access tidak dicek | BOLA/IDOR | Service-layer object guard |
| Query semua data lalu filter di Java | Data sudah bocor ke app memory | Filter di SQL/MyBatis |
| Endpoint default public | Lupa annotation jadi vulnerability | Deny by default |
| Role terlalu granular | Role explosion | Role + ABAC attributes |
| Permission terlalu granular | Sulit dikelola | Permission capability + contextual rule |
| Policy tersebar di mapper/resource/service | Sulit audit | Central policy/evaluator |
| Error terlalu detail | Bocor policy internal | Generic 403, detail di audit |
| Cache permission terlalu lama | Revocation lambat | TTL pendek / invalidation event |

---

# 25. Testing Matrix

```text
[ ] User without permission cannot access endpoint
[ ] User with permission but wrong tenant cannot access object
[ ] User with global read can list tenant data only
[ ] User with assigned read can only see assigned cases
[ ] User cannot approve own case
[ ] User cannot approve case in DRAFT
[ ] User cannot approve case above approval level
[ ] User cannot reassign task unless supervisor/admin
[ ] Service token cannot access user endpoint
[ ] User token cannot call internal service endpoint
[ ] ACL grant allows specific object access
[ ] ACL expiry revokes access
[ ] Removed role removes derived permission
[ ] Permission cache invalidates correctly
[ ] Denied decision is audited with reason
[ ] Missing annotation does not accidentally expose endpoint
```

---

# 26. Decision Guide

| Situation | Recommended Model |
|---|---|
| API token boleh read/write domain tertentu | Scope |
| User punya job function tertentu | RBAC |
| Action bisnis granular | Permission |
| User hanya boleh akses object tertentu | ACL |
| Rule tergantung tenant, region, risk, status | ABAC |
| Rule tergantung owner, assignee, manager, group, hierarchy | ReBAC |
| Enterprise cross-service authorization | PDP / entitlement service |
| Workflow/task authorization | Permission + ReBAC + state guard |
| Regulatory audit-heavy action | Explicit decision object + audit |

---

# 27. Minimal Invariants

```text
1. Default decision harus deny.
2. Permission adalah capability bisnis, bukan UI behavior.
3. Role adalah bundle, bukan policy final.
4. Scope membatasi token, bukan menggantikan object authorization.
5. Object-level authorization wajib untuk endpoint dengan resource ID.
6. Tenant boundary wajib masuk ke query.
7. State transition harus punya authorization guard.
8. Sensitive action harus audit allow/deny.
9. Authorization rule harus bisa dites secara negatif.
10. Policy harus punya owner dan versi.
```

---

# 28. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Permission catalog | Bisa mendefinisikan capability bisnis |
| 2 | RBAC | Bisa bundle permission menjadi role |
| 3 | OAuth scope mapping | Bisa membatasi token/API client |
| 4 | Object-level authorization | Bisa mencegah IDOR/BOLA |
| 5 | ACL table design | Bisa grant akses per object |
| 6 | ABAC | Bisa membuat policy berbasis atribut |
| 7 | ReBAC | Bisa modeling owner/assignee/group/hierarchy |
| 8 | MyBatis enforcement | Query tidak bocor lintas tenant |
| 9 | Workflow authorization | Task/state transition aman |
| 10 | Audit & tests | Decision bisa dibuktikan dan diregresi |

---

## Response:
7/13/2026, 4:38:26 AM

Saya lanjutkan dua tingkat sekaligus: pertama object-level authorization & tenant isolation, lalu langsung masuk ke secure repository/query pattern. Fokusnya: mencegah BOLA/IDOR, cross-tenant leakage, dan authorization yang hanya “terlihat aman” di resource layer tapi bocor di query.

# Cheatsheet - Object-Level Authorization & Tenant Isolation di Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis/PostgreSQL

## 1. Core Problem

Di tahap sebelumnya, kita sudah punya:

```text id="5689at"
Authentication:
  actor sudah jelas

Endpoint authorization:
  actor punya permission untuk memanggil endpoint

Sekarang pertanyaannya:
  apakah actor boleh mengakses object/data spesifik ini?
```

Contoh:

```http id="6kvf90"
GET /cases/CASE-123
Authorization: Bearer <valid-token>
```

Token valid dan user punya `case.read`, tetapi belum tentu user boleh membaca `CASE-123`.

Ini akar masalah **BOLA / IDOR**. OWASP API Security 2023 menempatkan **Broken Object Level Authorization** sebagai API1; attacker mengeksploitasi endpoint dengan memanipulasi object ID di path, query string, header, atau body request. OWASP juga menekankan object-level authorization check harus dipertimbangkan pada setiap function yang mengakses data source menggunakan ID dari user. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/API-Security/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

Object-level authorization selalu menjawab:

```text id="6979w2"
Can <actor> perform <action> on <object> within <tenant/context/state>?
```

Contoh:

```text id="d7dkju"
Can user-123 read case-789 in tenant-A?
Can user-123 approve case-789 while status = SUBMITTED?
Can service-order-api cancel order-555 for tenant-B?
Can supervisor-777 reassign task-999 assigned to user-456?
```

Authorization yang hanya seperti ini tidak cukup:

```java id="rqql9y"
@RequiresPermission("case.read")
public Response getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
    return Response.ok(caseService.getCase(caseId)).build();
}
```

Karena `caseId` berasal dari client dan bisa dimanipulasi.

---

## 3. Diagram Enforcement Layer

```mermaid id="lbcuvk"
flowchart TD
    A[HTTP Request with object ID] --> B[Jersey Resource]
    B --> C[Endpoint Permission Check]
    C --> D{Has coarse permission?}

    D -->|No| E[403 Forbidden]
    D -->|Yes| F[Application Service]

    F --> G[Load object with tenant constraint]
    G --> H{Object exists in actor tenant?}

    H -->|No| I[404 or 403]
    H -->|Yes| J[Object-Level Authorization]

    J --> K{Relationship / ACL / ABAC / State allowed?}

    K -->|No| L[403 Forbidden + Audit Deny]
    K -->|Yes| M[Execute Use Case]

    M --> N[MyBatis Query with tenant + visibility criteria]
    N --> O[(PostgreSQL)]

    J --> P[Authorization Audit]
    M --> P
```

---

## 4. Rule Utama

```text id="vz2p6z"
Permission checks capability.
Object-level checks actual access.
Tenant isolation constrains data boundary.
State guard constrains workflow transition.
Audit explains the decision.
```

Contoh approve case:

```text id="d68e8o"
Required permission:
  case.approve

Object check:
  actor.tenantId == case.tenantId

Relationship check:
  actor is assigned reviewer OR supervisor

State check:
  case.status == SUBMITTED

Separation-of-duty:
  actor.subject != case.createdBy
```

---

# 5. Object Access Anti-Pattern

## Anti-pattern 1 - Direct find by ID

```java id="7h8r3d"
public CaseDto getCase(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor, String caseId) {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = caseMapper.findById(caseId);
    return CaseDto.from(caseRecord);
}
```

Masalah:

```text id="ji7rgp"
- Tidak ada tenant constraint.
- Tidak ada object-level authorization.
- caseId dari client bisa diganti.
- Data tenant lain bisa bocor.
```

---

## Anti-pattern 2 - Filter setelah data keluar

```java id="qsk2w8"
public List<CaseDto> listCases(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor) {
    List<CaseRecord> allCases = caseMapper.findAll();

    return allCases.stream()
            .filter(c -> c.tenantId().equals(actor.tenantId()))
            .map(CaseDto::from)
            .toList();
}
```

Masalah:

```text id="mzjhgp"
Data yang tidak boleh dilihat sudah keluar dari database.
Kalau ada bug log/debug/cache/exception, data bisa bocor.
```

---

## Anti-pattern 3 - Trust object owner dari request body

```json id="cqle09"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-789",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "ownerUserId": "user-123"
}
```

Jangan percaya `tenantId`, `ownerUserId`, atau `assignedUserId` dari body client untuk menentukan akses. Data otoritatif harus di-load dari DB atau trusted domain service.

---

# 6. Safe Pattern - Load with Tenant Boundary

## Resource

```java id="xvavvm"
@Path("/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseApplicationService caseApplicationService;

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    @RequiresPermission("case.read")
    public Response getCase(
            @Context SecurityContext securityContext,
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId
    ) {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor =
                (AuthenticatedPrincipal) securityContext.getUserPrincipal();

        CaseDto result = caseApplicationService.getCase(actor, caseId);

        return Response.ok(result).build();
    }
}
```

## Application Service

```java id="n35smd"
public class CaseApplicationService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CaseAccessPolicy caseAccessPolicy;
    private final SecurityAuditLogger auditLogger;

    public CaseDto getCase(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor, String caseId) {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findByIdForTenant(
                caseId,
                actor.tenantId()
        ).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Case not found"));

        AuthorizationDecision decision =
                caseAccessPolicy.canRead(actor, caseRecord);

        auditLogger.logAuthorizationDecision(
                actor,
                "case.read",
                "case",
                caseId,
                decision
        );

        if (!decision.allowed()) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
        }

        return CaseDto.from(caseRecord);
    }
}
```

## Repository

```java id="ze4y80"
public Optional<CaseRecord> findByIdForTenant(String caseId, String tenantId) {
    return Optional.ofNullable(caseMapper.findByIdForTenant(caseId, tenantId));
}
```

## MyBatis Mapper XML

```xml id="h3aikb"
<select id="findByIdForTenant" resultMap="CaseRecordMap">
    SELECT
        c.id,
        c.tenant_id,
        c.status,
        c.assigned_user_id,
        c.created_by,
        c.region,
        c.risk_level,
        c.created_at,
        c.updated_at
    FROM cases c
    WHERE c.id = #{caseId}
      AND c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
</select>
```

Ini minimal memastikan cross-tenant object tidak ter-load.

---

# 7. 404 vs 403 untuk Object Access

| Situation | Response | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Object tidak ada | `404` | Normal |
| Object ada tapi tenant berbeda | `404` atau `403` | `404` sering dipakai untuk tidak mengungkap existence |
| Object ada di tenant sama tapi permission kurang | `403` | Authenticated tapi tidak authorized |
| Object ada, permission ada, state tidak valid | `409` atau `403` | Tergantung apakah ini conflict bisnis atau authorization |
| Token invalid | `401` | Authentication gagal |

Pattern umum untuk object ID:

```text id="3hzgdt"
findByIdForTenant(...) returns empty:
  return 404

found but policy denies:
  return 403
```

Untuk sistem high-security, lintas tenant sebaiknya selalu tampak seperti `404`, agar tidak menjadi enumeration oracle.

---

# 8. Object-Level Policy Class

```java id="f5b0t4"
public final class CaseAccessPolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision canRead(
            AuthenticatedPrincipal actor,
            CaseRecord caseRecord
    ) {
        if (!sameTenant(actor, caseRecord)) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("tenant_mismatch");
        }

        if (actor.hasPermission("case.read.all")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow("has_case_read_all");
        }

        if (actor.hasPermission("case.read.assigned")
                && actor.subject().equals(caseRecord.assignedUserId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow("assigned_user");
        }

        if (actor.hasPermission("case.read.own")
                && actor.subject().equals(caseRecord.createdBy())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow("case_creator");
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("no_matching_case_read_rule");
    }

    public AuthorizationDecision canApprove(
            AuthenticatedPrincipal actor,
            CaseRecord caseRecord
    ) {
        if (!sameTenant(actor, caseRecord)) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("tenant_mismatch");
        }

        if (!actor.hasPermission("case.approve")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_case_approve_permission");
        }

        if (!"SUBMITTED".equals(caseRecord.status())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("case_not_submitted");
        }

        if (actor.subject().equals(caseRecord.createdBy())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("maker_checker_violation");
        }

        if (actor.approvalLevel() < caseRecord.riskLevel()) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("approval_level_too_low");
        }

        if (!actor.allowedRegions().contains(caseRecord.region())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("region_not_allowed");
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.allow("case_approval_policy_matched");
    }

    private boolean sameTenant(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor, CaseRecord caseRecord) {
        return actor.tenantId() != null
                && actor.tenantId().equals(caseRecord.tenantId());
    }
}
```

**Catatan desain:** policy class sebaiknya return `AuthorizationDecision`, bukan `boolean`, supaya alasan deny/allow bisa diaudit.

---

# 9. AuthorizationDecision

```java id="a4km9m"
public record AuthorizationDecision(
        boolean allowed,
        String reason,
        String policyName,
        String policyVersion
) {
    public static AuthorizationDecision allow(String reason) {
        return new AuthorizationDecision(
                true,
                reason,
                "case-access-policy",
                "v1"
        );
    }

    public static AuthorizationDecision deny(String reason) {
        return new AuthorizationDecision(
                false,
                reason,
                "case-access-policy",
                "v1"
        );
    }
}
```

Kenapa penting:

```text id="5yo12w"
- Audit bisa menjelaskan kenapa deny.
- Debugging lebih cepat.
- Compliance/regulatory review punya evidence.
- Test bisa assert reason, bukan hanya status code.
```

---

# 10. Tenant Isolation Model

Ada beberapa model tenancy.

| Model | Deskripsi | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| Shared DB, shared schema | Semua tenant di tabel sama, ada `tenant_id` | Simpel, murah | Wajib disiplin query |
| Shared DB, separate schema | Tenant per schema | Isolasi lebih kuat | Migration lebih kompleks |
| Separate database | Tenant per DB | Isolasi kuat | Operasional mahal |
| Hybrid | Tenant besar dipisah, tenant kecil shared | Fleksibel | Governance kompleks |

Untuk kebanyakan microservice enterprise:

```text id="uak7qg"
shared DB + shared schema + tenant_id + strict query boundary
```

cukup umum, tapi disiplin enforcement-nya harus tinggi.

---

# 11. Tenant Context

Tenant jangan disebar sebagai string acak di semua method. Buat context eksplisit.

```java id="a7y6im"
public record TenantContext(
        String tenantId,
        String source
) {
    public static TenantContext fromPrincipal(AuthenticatedPrincipal principal) {
        if (principal.tenantId() == null || principal.tenantId().isBlank()) {
            throw new UnauthorizedException("Missing tenant context");
        }

        return new TenantContext(principal.tenantId(), "TOKEN_CLAIM");
    }
}
```

Dalam service:

```java id="w49og2"
TenantContext tenant = TenantContext.fromPrincipal(actor);

CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findByIdForTenant(
        caseId,
        tenant.tenantId()
).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Case not found"));
```

---

# 12. Secure Repository Pattern

Repository harus memaksa tenant boundary.

## Buruk

```java id="p3ez7k"
public interface CaseRepository {
    Optional<CaseRecord> findById(String caseId);
    List<CaseRecord> findAll();
}
```

## Lebih aman

```java id="om9pib"
public interface CaseRepository {
    Optional<CaseRecord> findByIdForTenant(String caseId, String tenantId);

    List<CaseRecord> findVisibleCases(CaseVisibilityCriteria criteria);

    int updateStatusForTenant(
            String caseId,
            String tenantId,
            String expectedStatus,
            String newStatus,
            String updatedBy
    );
}
```

Dengan begitu, developer sulit “lupa” tenant constraint.

---

# 13. Visibility Criteria

Jangan kirim seluruh principal ke MyBatis. Kirim criteria yang spesifik.

```java id="cav3pg"
public record CaseVisibilityCriteria(
        String tenantId,
        String actorId,
        boolean canReadAll,
        boolean canReadAssigned,
        boolean canReadOwn,
        Set<String> allowedRegions,
        Integer maxRiskLevel
) {}
```

Builder:

```java id="evsprv"
public CaseVisibilityCriteria toVisibilityCriteria(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor) {
    return new CaseVisibilityCriteria(
            actor.tenantId(),
            actor.subject(),
            actor.hasPermission("case.read.all"),
            actor.hasPermission("case.read.assigned"),
            actor.hasPermission("case.read.own"),
            actor.allowedRegions(),
            actor.maxRiskLevel()
    );
}
```

---

# 14. MyBatis Dynamic SQL untuk Visibility

MyBatis mendukung dynamic SQL dengan elemen seperti `if`, `choose`, `when`, `otherwise`, `trim`, `where`, `set`, dan `foreach`; fitur ini memang dirancang untuk membangun SQL kondisional secara aman dari XML mapper dibanding menyusun string SQL manual. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/dynamic-sql.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```xml id="2rr6qs"
<select id="findVisibleCases" resultMap="CaseRecordMap">
    SELECT
        c.id,
        c.tenant_id,
        c.status,
        c.assigned_user_id,
        c.created_by,
        c.region,
        c.risk_level,
        c.created_at
    FROM cases c
    <where>
        c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}

        AND (
            #{canReadAll} = true

            <if test="canReadAssigned">
                OR c.assigned_user_id = #{actorId}
            </if>

            <if test="canReadOwn">
                OR c.created_by = #{actorId}
            </if>
        )

        <if test="allowedRegions != null and allowedRegions.size() > 0">
            AND c.region IN
            <foreach collection="allowedRegions"
                     item="region"
                     open="("
                     separator=","
                     close=")">
                #{region}
            </foreach>
        </if>

        <if test="maxRiskLevel != null">
            AND c.risk_level &lt;= #{maxRiskLevel}
        </if>
    </where>
    ORDER BY c.created_at DESC
</select>
```

**Caveat:** Pastikan kondisi `AND (...)` tidak menjadi selalu false/true secara tak sengaja. Untuk rule kompleks, lebih aman precompute mode visibility di Java.

---

# 15. Safer Visibility Mode

Daripada banyak boolean liar:

```java id="bnr5by"
public enum CaseVisibilityMode {
    ALL,
    ASSIGNED,
    OWN,
    ASSIGNED_OR_OWN,
    NONE
}
```

Criteria:

```java id="f4ux32"
public record CaseVisibilityCriteria(
        String tenantId,
        String actorId,
        CaseVisibilityMode visibilityMode,
        Set<String> allowedRegions,
        Integer maxRiskLevel
) {}
```

Mapper:

```xml id="s3jwui"
<select id="findVisibleCases" resultMap="CaseRecordMap">
    SELECT
        c.id,
        c.tenant_id,
        c.status,
        c.assigned_user_id,
        c.created_by,
        c.region,
        c.risk_level,
        c.created_at
    FROM cases c
    <where>
        c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}

        <choose>
            <when test="visibilityMode.name() == 'ALL'">
                AND 1 = 1
            </when>

            <when test="visibilityMode.name() == 'ASSIGNED'">
                AND c.assigned_user_id = #{actorId}
            </when>

            <when test="visibilityMode.name() == 'OWN'">
                AND c.created_by = #{actorId}
            </when>

            <when test="visibilityMode.name() == 'ASSIGNED_OR_OWN'">
                AND (
                    c.assigned_user_id = #{actorId}
                    OR c.created_by = #{actorId}
                )
            </when>

            <otherwise>
                AND 1 = 0
            </otherwise>
        </choose>

        <if test="allowedRegions != null and allowedRegions.size() > 0">
            AND c.region IN
            <foreach collection="allowedRegions"
                     item="region"
                     open="("
                     separator=","
                     close=")">
                #{region}
            </foreach>
        </if>

        <if test="maxRiskLevel != null">
            AND c.risk_level &lt;= #{maxRiskLevel}
        </if>
    </where>
    ORDER BY c.created_at DESC
</select>
```

Ini lebih mudah diaudit karena setiap mode punya bentuk SQL jelas.

---

# 16. Tenant-Aware Update Pattern

Authorization bukan hanya read. Update juga harus tenant-aware dan state-aware.

## Buruk

```sql id="t7aipq"
UPDATE cases
SET status = 'APPROVED'
WHERE id = :caseId;
```

## Lebih aman

```xml id="thlb72"
<update id="approveCase">
    UPDATE cases
    SET
        status = 'APPROVED',
        approved_by = #{actorId},
        approved_at = now(),
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = #{caseId}
      AND tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
      AND created_by &lt;&gt; #{actorId}
</update>
```

Java:

```java id="bfuhg0"
int updated = caseMapper.approveCase(
        caseId,
        actor.tenantId(),
        actor.subject()
);

if (updated == 0) {
    throw new ConflictException("Case cannot be approved");
}
```

Kenapa `UPDATE ... WHERE tenant_id AND status` penting?

```text id="6l7pw3"
- Mencegah cross-tenant mutation.
- Mencegah race condition status berubah setelah policy check.
- Mencegah maker-checker violation di level SQL.
```

Untuk action sensitif, lakukan policy check di Java dan tetap pertahankan guard di SQL sebagai defense-in-depth.

---

# 17. Race Condition: Check-Then-Act

Anti-pattern:

```java id="mpwcum"
CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findByIdForTenant(caseId, actor.tenantId());

if (policy.canApprove(actor, caseRecord).allowed()) {
    caseMapper.updateStatus(caseId, "APPROVED");
}
```

Masalah:

```text id="bwg0hz"
Antara read dan update, status bisa berubah.
Actor assignment bisa berubah.
Case bisa dipindah/closed.
```

Pattern lebih aman:

```java id="p7homl"
CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findByIdForTenant(caseId, actor.tenantId())
        .orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Case not found"));

AuthorizationDecision decision = casePolicy.canApprove(actor, caseRecord);

if (!decision.allowed()) {
    auditDeny(...);
    throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
}

int updated = caseRepository.approveIfStillEligible(
        caseId,
        actor.tenantId(),
        actor.subject(),
        caseRecord.version()
);

if (updated == 0) {
    throw new ConflictException("Case state changed");
}
```

SQL optimistic locking:

```xml id="byo51f"
<update id="approveIfStillEligible">
    UPDATE cases
    SET
        status = 'APPROVED',
        approved_by = #{actorId},
        approved_at = now(),
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = #{caseId}
      AND tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
      AND created_by &lt;&gt; #{actorId}
      AND version = #{expectedVersion}
</update>
```

---

# 18. ACL Lookup Pattern

ACL dipakai saat akses ditentukan per object.

```sql id="0lctfe"
CREATE TABLE resource_acl (
    id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_type   TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_id     TEXT NOT NULL,
    subject_type    TEXT NOT NULL,
    subject_id      TEXT NOT NULL,
    permission      TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_by      TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL
);

CREATE INDEX idx_resource_acl_lookup
ON resource_acl (
    tenant_id,
    resource_type,
    resource_id,
    subject_type,
    subject_id,
    permission
);
```

Mapper:

```xml id="m5f45b"
<select id="existsActiveGrant" resultType="boolean">
    SELECT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM resource_acl acl
        WHERE acl.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND acl.resource_type = #{resourceType}
          AND acl.resource_id = #{resourceId}
          AND acl.subject_type = #{subjectType}
          AND acl.subject_id = #{subjectId}
          AND acl.permission = #{permission}
          AND (acl.expires_at IS NULL OR acl.expires_at > now())
    )
</select>
```

Policy usage:

```java id="5stvg9"
public AuthorizationDecision canReadWithAcl(
        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor,
        CaseRecord caseRecord
) {
    if (!actor.tenantId().equals(caseRecord.tenantId())) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("tenant_mismatch");
    }

    if (actor.hasPermission("case.read.all")) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.allow("global_read_permission");
    }

    boolean hasAcl = aclRepository.existsActiveGrant(
            actor.tenantId(),
            "CASE",
            caseRecord.id(),
            "USER",
            actor.subject(),
            "read"
    );

    if (hasAcl) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.allow("acl_read_grant");
    }

    return AuthorizationDecision.deny("no_acl_grant");
}
```

---

# 19. Group ACL

Sering ACL tidak hanya user-specific, tetapi group/role-specific.

```text id="3pfcjr"
CASE-123:
  USER:user-1 -> read
  GROUP:reviewer-team-a -> read, update
  ROLE:tenant-supervisor -> approve
```

Jangan query group satu per satu. Precompute subject references:

```java id="xbnkfb"
public record SubjectReference(
        String subjectType,
        String subjectId
) {}
```

```java id="xwq620"
Set<SubjectReference> subjectRefs = Set.of(
        new SubjectReference("USER", actor.subject()),
        new SubjectReference("GROUP", "reviewer-team-a"),
        new SubjectReference("ROLE", "CASE_SUPERVISOR")
);
```

Mapper approach:

```xml id="yjdxqb"
<select id="existsAnyActiveGrant" resultType="boolean">
    SELECT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM resource_acl acl
        WHERE acl.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND acl.resource_type = #{resourceType}
          AND acl.resource_id = #{resourceId}
          AND acl.permission = #{permission}
          AND (acl.expires_at IS NULL OR acl.expires_at > now())
          AND (
            <foreach collection="subjects"
                     item="s"
                     separator=" OR ">
                (acl.subject_type = #{s.subjectType}
                 AND acl.subject_id = #{s.subjectId})
            </foreach>
          )
    )
</select>
```

Jika subject set bisa besar, masukkan ke temporary table, use `VALUES`, atau gunakan join terhadap tabel membership.

---

# 20. Row-Level Security di PostgreSQL

PostgreSQL punya **Row Security Policies** yang dapat membatasi row mana yang dikembalikan atau dimodifikasi oleh query normal. Jika row security di-enable pada tabel, semua akses normal harus memenuhi policy; bila tidak ada policy, default-nya deny-all. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-rowsecurity.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh RLS konseptual:

```sql id="efzyfv"
ALTER TABLE cases ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY tenant_isolation_policy
ON cases
USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id'));
```

Set session variable per request/transaction:

```sql id="i6v729"
SET LOCAL app.tenant_id = 'tenant-a';
```

## Kapan RLS Cocok?

| Cocok | Kurang Cocok |
|---|---|
| Defense-in-depth tenant isolation | Aplikasi belum siap manage DB session state |
| Banyak query raw/reporting | Connection pooling tidak disiplin |
| Ingin DB ikut enforce row boundary | Policy butuh banyak external context |
| Multi-tenant strict isolation | Debugging SQL policy belum matang |

**Caveat penting:** Dengan connection pool, `SET LOCAL` harus berada dalam transaction boundary yang jelas. Jangan memakai `SET` global lalu connection dikembalikan ke pool karena tenant context bisa bocor ke request berikutnya.

---

# 21. App-Layer vs DB-Layer Enforcement

| Layer | Kelebihan | Kekurangan |
|---|---|---|
| Jersey filter | Konsisten untuk endpoint | Tidak tahu object/data detail |
| Application service | Paham use case/state | Bisa terlewat jika developer bypass |
| Repository/MyBatis | Dekat dengan query | Rule bisa tersebar di SQL |
| PostgreSQL RLS | Defense-in-depth kuat | Perlu discipline connection/session |
| External PDP | Policy terpusat | Latency, dependency, complexity |

Pattern realistis:

```text id="k3ox0p"
Endpoint permission:
  Jersey filter

Object/state policy:
  Application service

Tenant/data visibility:
  Repository/MyBatis SQL

High assurance:
  Optional PostgreSQL RLS
```

---

# 22. Pagination dan Count Leakage

Object/data leak bukan hanya isi data. Count juga bisa bocor.

Contoh leak:

```http id="92dgnh"
GET /cases?status=SUBMITTED
```

Response:

```json id="6pbjk1"
{
  "total": 542,
  "items": []
}
```

Kalau `items` difilter setelah query tapi `total` dihitung sebelum authorization, user bisa tahu jumlah case yang tidak boleh dia lihat.

Pattern aman:

```text id="2y2nme"
count query dan data query harus memakai visibility criteria yang sama.
```

Mapper:

```xml id="lsn5yw"
<select id="countVisibleCases" resultType="long">
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM cases c
    <where>
        c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}

        <choose>
            <when test="visibilityMode.name() == 'ALL'">
                AND 1 = 1
            </when>
            <when test="visibilityMode.name() == 'ASSIGNED'">
                AND c.assigned_user_id = #{actorId}
            </when>
            <when test="visibilityMode.name() == 'OWN'">
                AND c.created_by = #{actorId}
            </when>
            <when test="visibilityMode.name() == 'ASSIGNED_OR_OWN'">
                AND (
                    c.assigned_user_id = #{actorId}
                    OR c.created_by = #{actorId}
                )
            </when>
            <otherwise>
                AND 1 = 0
            </otherwise>
        </choose>
    </where>
</select>
```

---

# 23. Search Endpoint Leakage

Search endpoints rawan karena filter kompleks.

```http id="5z0fd6"
GET /cases/search?keyword=fraud&status=SUBMITTED&region=ID-JK
```

Safe rule:

```text id="vsjox8"
User filters are additive.
Security filters are mandatory.
User filters must never replace security filters.
```

SQL structure:

```sql id="nfpq93"
WHERE
  -- mandatory security predicates
  c.tenant_id = :tenantId
  AND <visibility predicate>

  -- user-controlled filters
  AND (:status IS NULL OR c.status = :status)
  AND (:keyword IS NULL OR c.title ILIKE '%' || :keyword || '%')
```

Jangan membangun query sehingga user filter bisa menghilangkan tenant predicate.

---

# 24. Bulk Operation Authorization

Bulk operation tidak boleh hanya check permission sekali lalu update semua ID.

Anti-pattern:

```java id="eis5qy"
if (actor.hasPermission("case.close")) {
    caseMapper.bulkClose(caseIds);
}
```

Lebih aman:

```java id="tnk55e"
public BulkCloseResult bulkClose(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor, List<String> caseIds) {
    List<CaseRecord> visibleCases =
            caseRepository.findClosableCases(actor.tenantId(), actor.subject(), caseIds);

    Map<String, AuthorizationDecision> decisions = new HashMap<>();

    for (CaseRecord caseRecord : visibleCases) {
        decisions.put(
                caseRecord.id(),
                casePolicy.canClose(actor, caseRecord)
        );
    }

    List<String> allowedIds = decisions.entrySet().stream()
            .filter(e -> e.getValue().allowed())
            .map(Map.Entry::getKey)
            .toList();

    int updated = caseRepository.closeAllowedCases(
            actor.tenantId(),
            allowedIds,
            actor.subject()
    );

    auditBulkDecision(actor, caseIds, decisions);

    return new BulkCloseResult(updated, decisions);
}
```

SQL tetap tenant-aware:

```xml id="ladzsn"
<update id="closeAllowedCases">
    UPDATE cases
    SET
        status = 'CLOSED',
        closed_by = #{actorId},
        closed_at = now(),
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND status IN ('APPROVED', 'RESOLVED')
      AND id IN
      <foreach collection="caseIds"
               item="caseId"
               open="("
               separator=","
               close=")">
          #{caseId}
      </foreach>
</update>
```

---

# 25. Object ID Design

Object ID format tidak menggantikan authorization.

| ID Type | Security Note |
|---|---|
| Sequential integer | Mudah ditebak, butuh authz ketat |
| UUID | Sulit ditebak, tetap butuh authz |
| ULID | Sortable, tetap butuh authz |
| Business key | Bisa bocor makna domain |
| Encoded global ID | Obfuscation, bukan authorization |

Rule:

```text id="8u0wmv"
Non-guessable ID mengurangi enumeration.
Object-level authorization tetap wajib.
```

OWASP menegaskan BOLA tetap relevan terlepas dari apakah object ID berupa integer sequential, UUID, atau string generik karena object ID sering terlihat di path, query, header, atau payload. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/API-Security/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 26. Cache dan Authorization

Cache bisa membuat authorization bocor.

## Risky cache key

```text id="4z0pl8"
cache key:
  case:{caseId}
```

Kalau case cached tanpa tenant/visibility boundary, user lain bisa mendapatkan object yang sama dari cache.

## Safer cache key

```text id="c2x47e"
case:{tenantId}:{caseId}
visibleCases:{tenantId}:{actorId}:{visibilityHash}:{queryHash}
```

Guideline:

```text id="76vbw5"
- Cache raw object hanya jika tenant-safe.
- Cache response user-specific dengan actor/tenant/permission hash.
- Invalidate cache saat ACL/permission/assignment berubah.
- Jangan cache deny/allow terlalu lama untuk action sensitif.
```

---

# 27. Audit for Object Authorization

```java id="r82lqq"
public record ObjectAuthorizationAuditEvent(
        String eventType,
        String decision,
        String reason,
        String subjectId,
        String tenantId,
        String action,
        String resourceType,
        String resourceId,
        String objectTenantId,
        String policyName,
        String policyVersion,
        String correlationId
) {}
```

Contoh:

```json id="ixyqrl"
{
  "eventType": "OBJECT_AUTHORIZATION",
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "tenant_mismatch",
  "subjectId": "user-123",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "action": "case.read",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "CASE-789",
  "objectTenantId": "tenant-b",
  "policyName": "case-access-policy",
  "policyVersion": "v1",
  "correlationId": "req-99"
}
```

Untuk cross-tenant deny, hati-hati jangan expose `objectTenantId` ke client. Simpan hanya di internal audit log.

---

# 28. Test Matrix - Object-Level Authorization

```text id="1akwut"
[ ] User can read own case
[ ] User cannot read another user's case in same tenant without grant
[ ] User cannot read case from another tenant
[ ] User with case.read.all can read all cases in same tenant only
[ ] User with case.read.assigned sees only assigned cases
[ ] User with case.read.own sees only created cases
[ ] User cannot approve own case
[ ] User cannot approve case in DRAFT
[ ] User cannot approve case above approval level
[ ] User cannot update case after status CLOSED
[ ] List endpoint returns only visible cases
[ ] Count endpoint counts only visible cases
[ ] Search endpoint applies tenant predicate regardless of filters
[ ] Bulk operation partially denies unauthorized IDs
[ ] ACL grant allows access
[ ] Expired ACL grant denies access
[ ] Removed assignment invalidates access
[ ] Cache does not leak cross-tenant object
[ ] Audit logs deny reason
```

---

# 29. Code Review Checklist

```text id="n6py8u"
[ ] Endpoint with object ID has object-level authorization
[ ] Repository method requires tenantId
[ ] List query includes mandatory tenant predicate
[ ] Count query uses same visibility predicate as data query
[ ] Search query cannot override security predicates
[ ] Update/delete includes tenant_id in WHERE clause
[ ] State-changing update includes expected current status
[ ] Maker-checker/separation-of-duty enforced when required
[ ] Bulk operation evaluates each object
[ ] ACL lookup includes tenant_id, resource_type, resource_id, subject, permission
[ ] Cache key includes tenant/actor/visibility when needed
[ ] Error does not reveal cross-tenant existence
[ ] Authorization decision is audited
[ ] Negative tests cover ID tampering
```

---

# 30. Practical Decision Guide

| Scenario | Recommended Pattern |
|---|---|
| `GET /cases/{id}` | `findByIdForTenant` + `canRead(actor, case)` |
| `GET /cases` | `findVisibleCases(criteria)` |
| `GET /cases/count` | `countVisibleCases(criteria)` |
| `POST /cases/{id}/approve` | endpoint permission + object policy + SQL state guard |
| `DELETE /files/{id}` | owner/ACL check + tenant-aware delete |
| `POST /cases/bulk-close` | per-object decision + tenant-aware bulk update |
| `GET /admin/users/{id}` | admin permission + tenant/org boundary |
| `POST /tasks/{id}/complete` | task relation + workflow state + business object access |
| Background job | service identity + explicit tenant/job scope |
| Partner API | client identity + partner-tenant mapping + strict object check |

---

# 31. Minimal Production Invariants

```text id="hffn76"
1. Every object ID from client is untrusted.
2. Every object access must include tenant boundary.
3. Permission does not imply access to every object.
4. List/count/search must use the same visibility rules.
5. Update/delete must include tenant_id in WHERE.
6. State-changing action must guard expected current state.
7. Bulk operation must authorize per object.
8. Cache key must include tenant/actor/visibility where relevant.
9. Cross-tenant existence should not leak to clients.
10. Authorization decision must be auditable.
```

---

# 32. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | BOLA/IDOR mental model | Paham kenapa object ID selalu untrusted |
| 2 | Tenant-aware repository | Semua query punya tenant boundary |
| 3 | Object access policy | Read/update/approve punya decision eksplisit |
| 4 | Visibility criteria | List/search/count tidak bocor |
| 5 | SQL guard untuk mutation | Update/delete aman dari race dan cross-tenant |
| 6 | ACL lookup | Grant per object bisa dievaluasi |
| 7 | Bulk authorization | Tidak ada bypass lewat batch endpoint |
| 8 | Cache safety | Cache tidak bocor lintas actor/tenant |
| 9 | Audit | Deny/allow punya reason |
| 10 | Negative tests | ID tampering dan cross-tenant test wajib |
