## Response:
7/13/2026, 4:40:18 AM

Kita lanjut ke arsitektur policy: kapan authorization cukup sebagai Java class lokal, kapan perlu entitlement service/PDP, dan bagaimana failure handling supaya sistem tidak fail-open saat policy layer bermasalah.

# Cheatsheet - Policy Architecture: PEP/PDP/PIP/PAP, Entitlement Service, OPA/OpenFGA-style Authorization, dan Failure Handling di Jersey/JAX-RS Microservices

## 1. Core Problem

Sampai titik ini, kita sudah punya:

```text
Authentication:
  siapa caller-nya?

Endpoint authorization:
  apakah caller boleh masuk endpoint?

Object-level authorization:
  apakah caller boleh akses object tertentu?

Tenant isolation:
  apakah data tetap berada dalam boundary tenant?
```

Sekarang naik level ke pertanyaan arsitektur:

```text
Di mana policy authorization sebaiknya ditempatkan?

Apakah cukup di Java class lokal?
Perlu entitlement service?
Perlu OPA?
Perlu OpenFGA / Zanzibar-style ReBAC?
Bagaimana kalau policy service down?
Bagaimana supaya sistem tidak fail-open?
```

NIST mendefinisikan **Policy Enforcement Point / PEP** sebagai komponen yang menegakkan keputusan policy saat subject meminta akses ke protected object, sedangkan keputusan akses dibuat oleh **Policy Decision Point / PDP**. NIST juga mendefinisikan **PDP** sebagai entitas sistem yang membuat authorization decision untuk dirinya sendiri atau untuk entitas lain. ([NIST Computer Security Resource Center](https://csrc.nist.gov/glossary/term/policy_enforcement_point?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Diagram Besar Policy Architecture

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Client Request] --> B[Ingress / API Gateway]
    B --> C[Jersey / JAX-RS Resource]

    C --> D[PEP: Authentication Filter]
    D --> E[PEP: Authorization Filter]
    E --> F[Application Service PEP]
    F --> G[Repository / Query PEP]

    E --> H[PDP: Local Policy Engine]
    F --> H

    H --> I[PIP: Identity Claims]
    H --> J[PIP: Resource Attributes]
    H --> K[PIP: Tenant Config]
    H --> L[PIP: Workflow State]
    H --> M[PIP: ACL / Relationship Store]

    H --> N[PAP: Policy Admin / Config]
    N --> O[Permission Catalog]
    N --> P[Role Matrix]
    N --> Q[ABAC Rules]
    N --> R[ReBAC Model]
    N --> S[Policy Versioning]

    H --> T[Authorization Decision]
    T --> U{Allow?}
    U -->|Yes| V[Execute Use Case]
    U -->|No| W[403 / 404 / 409]

    T --> X[Audit Log]
    V --> Y[(PostgreSQL)]
```

---

# 3. Komponen Utama

| Komponen | Nama | Tugas | Contoh di Jersey/JAX-RS |
|---|---|---|---|
| **PEP** | Policy Enforcement Point | Tempat enforcement keputusan allow/deny | `ContainerRequestFilter`, resource method, service method, MyBatis query |
| **PDP** | Policy Decision Point | Tempat membuat keputusan authorization | Java policy class, entitlement service, OPA, OpenFGA |
| **PIP** | Policy Information Point | Sumber data atribut untuk policy | JWT claims, DB object, tenant config, ACL table, workflow task |
| **PAP** | Policy Administration Point | Tempat policy dikelola | Admin UI, config repo, permission catalog, policy bundle |
| **Audit** | Evidence layer | Mencatat allow/deny decision | structured security audit log |

Model ini penting karena authorization enterprise biasanya tidak gagal karena “tidak ada if-check”, tetapi karena **policy tersebar, tidak konsisten, tidak bisa diaudit, dan failure handling-nya tidak jelas**.

---

# 4. Layer Enforcement di Jersey/JAX-RS

Authorization sebaiknya tidak hanya ada di satu tempat.

```text
1. API Gateway / Ingress
   - coarse protection
   - public/internal boundary
   - mTLS/client certificate
   - rate limit

2. Jersey Authentication Filter
   - validate token
   - build principal
   - set SecurityContext

3. Jersey Authorization Filter
   - endpoint permission/scope
   - annotation-based guard

4. Application Service
   - object-level authorization
   - workflow/state authorization
   - maker-checker rule
   - tenant-specific domain rule

5. Repository / MyBatis
   - tenant-aware query
   - visibility-aware query
   - update/delete WHERE guard

6. PostgreSQL
   - optional RLS
   - constraints
   - audit trigger if required
```

**Key invariant:**

```text
Gateway-level authorization is never enough for object-level business authorization.
```

Gateway bisa tahu endpoint dan token, tetapi biasanya tidak tahu `case.status`, `created_by`, `assigned_user_id`, `risk_level`, atau Camunda task state.

---

# 5. Local Policy vs External PDP

## Option A - Local Java Policy

```text
Jersey Service
  -> Java Policy Class
  -> AuthorizationDecision
```

Contoh:

```java
public final class CaseApprovalPolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision canApprove(
            AuthenticatedPrincipal actor,
            CaseRecord caseRecord
    ) {
        if (!actor.tenantId().equals(caseRecord.tenantId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("tenant_mismatch");
        }

        if (!actor.hasPermission("case.approve")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_permission");
        }

        if (!"SUBMITTED".equals(caseRecord.status())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("invalid_status");
        }

        if (actor.subject().equals(caseRecord.createdBy())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("maker_checker_violation");
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.allow("case_approval_allowed");
    }
}
```

### Cocok Bila

| Cocok | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Policy domain-specific | Rule sangat dekat dengan business logic |
| Service masih sedikit | Belum perlu central PDP |
| Latency harus sangat rendah | Tidak ada network call |
| Policy butuh object rich | Sudah punya aggregate/domain object |
| Team ingin simple dulu | Lebih mudah debug/test |

### Risiko

| Risiko | Mitigasi |
|---|---|
| Rule tersebar di banyak service | Buat package policy khusus |
| Policy sulit diubah tanpa deploy | Externalize sebagian config |
| Audit tidak konsisten | Pakai `AuthorizationDecision` standar |
| Cross-service inconsistency | Permission catalog/versioning bersama |

---

## Option B - Entitlement Service

```text
Jersey Service
  -> Entitlement Service
  -> Permission/ACL/Role Store
  -> Decision
```

Entitlement service adalah service internal yang mengelola effective permission, role, grant, ACL, tenant entitlement, atau assignment tertentu.

### Cocok Bila

| Cocok | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Banyak microservice perlu permission yang sama | Hindari duplicate rule |
| Role/permission sering berubah | Central management |
| Butuh admin UI | Role, grant, ACL dikelola user admin |
| Butuh audit terpusat | Semua decision tercatat seragam |
| Butuh revocation cepat | Bisa invalidasi permission dari pusat |

### Risiko

| Risiko | Mitigasi |
|---|---|
| Latency tambahan | Cache short TTL |
| Single point of failure | HA, fallback policy, circuit breaker |
| Coupling antar service | Stable authorization API |
| PDP timeout | Fail-closed untuk sensitive action |
| Terlalu generic | Tetap sisakan domain guard di service |

---

## Option C - OPA-style Policy Engine

OPA adalah general-purpose policy engine open source yang dirancang untuk menyatukan policy enforcement lintas stack, dan dokumentasi resminya memposisikannya sebagai engine policy yang dapat digunakan di berbagai layer sistem. ([Open Policy Agent](https://openpolicyagent.org/docs?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
Jersey Service
  -> OPA sidecar/service/library
  -> Rego policy bundle
  -> allow/deny + reason
```

### Cocok Bila

| Cocok | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Policy declarative | Rule bisa diekspresikan sebagai policy |
| Banyak stack berbeda | Java, Kubernetes, gateway, CI/CD |
| Security team ingin policy ownership | Policy tidak seluruhnya di Java code |
| Butuh policy bundle/versioning | Policy bisa dikelola seperti artifact |
| Butuh admission/control-plane policy juga | OPA sering dipakai lintas stack |

### Risiko

| Risiko | Mitigasi |
|---|---|
| Developer harus belajar Rego/policy language | Mulai dari use case terbatas |
| Debugging berbeda dari Java | Simpan input/output decision |
| Butuh data input lengkap | Desain authorization input contract |
| Network/sidecar failure | Timeout + fail-closed |
| Policy terlalu jauh dari domain | Jangan pindahkan semua business rule secara membabi buta |

---

## Option D - OpenFGA / Zanzibar-style ReBAC

OpenFGA mendokumentasikan ReBAC sebagai authorization yang membuat access rule bergantung pada hubungan antara user dan object, serta antara object dan object; misalnya user dapat melihat dokumen jika punya akses ke parent folder. ([OpenFGA](https://openfga.dev/docs/authorization-concepts?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Google Zanzibar paper menjelaskan sistem global untuk menyimpan dan mengevaluasi ACL, dengan model data dan configuration language seragam untuk banyak service Google seperti Calendar, Drive, Cloud, Photos, dan YouTube. ([Google Research](https://research.google/pubs/zanzibar-googles-consistent-global-authorization-system/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
Jersey Service
  -> OpenFGA / Zanzibar-like PDP
  -> Relationship Tuples
  -> check(user, relation, object)
```

### Cocok Bila

| Cocok | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Authorization berbasis relasi | owner, assignee, member, parent folder, group |
| Object sharing kompleks | User/group/role bisa diberi akses object |
| Hierarchy penting | manager-of, parent-child, organization tree |
| Banyak service pakai relationship yang sama | Central graph authorization |
| ACL mulai tidak manageable | ReBAC lebih ekspresif |

### Risiko

| Risiko | Mitigasi |
|---|---|
| Model relasi salah | Desain tuple/model dengan hati-hati |
| Tidak semua rule cocok jadi relasi | Gabungkan dengan ABAC/local rule |
| Latency check per object | Batch check/cache |
| Data relationship stale | Event-driven sync + consistency policy |
| Debugging graph decision kompleks | Simpan trace/reason/context |

---

# 6. Decision Guide: Pilih Arsitektur Mana?

| Situasi | Pilihan Awal yang Masuk Akal |
|---|---|
| Service baru, policy masih sederhana | Local Java policy |
| Permission/role dikelola admin lintas service | Entitlement service |
| Policy perlu dikelola security/platform team | OPA-style PDP |
| Banyak ownership, sharing, hierarchy, group membership | OpenFGA/Zanzibar-style ReBAC |
| Query list/search harus tenant/visibility-aware | MyBatis/PostgreSQL enforcement tetap wajib |
| Workflow approval/state-heavy | Local domain policy + optional entitlement/ReBAC |
| Regulatory audit-heavy | Explicit decision object + centralized audit |
| Latency sangat ketat | Local policy + cached entitlement |
| Revocation harus cepat | Entitlement/PDP + cache invalidation |

**Rule praktis:**

```text
Mulai dari local policy yang rapi.
Naik ke entitlement/PDP ketika duplication, governance, atau cross-service consistency menjadi masalah nyata.
```

Jangan langsung membangun authorization platform besar kalau problem saat ini masih bisa diselesaikan dengan policy class, permission catalog, dan repository guard yang disiplin.

---

# 7. Recommended Hybrid Architecture

Untuk microservice Jersey/JAX-RS enterprise, kombinasi yang realistis:

```text
JWT / OIDC:
  Authentication + coarse scopes

Local Java Policy:
  Domain-specific rule, workflow state, maker-checker

Entitlement Service:
  Effective permissions, role mapping, tenant-level grants

ACL / ReBAC Store:
  Object relationship, assignment, sharing, group access

MyBatis/PostgreSQL:
  Tenant-aware and visibility-aware query

Audit Service:
  Allow/deny decisions, reason, policy version
```

Diagram:

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Jersey Resource] --> B[Authentication Filter]
    B --> C[Authorization Filter]

    C --> D[Scope / Permission Check]
    D --> E[Application Service]

    E --> F[Load Resource with Tenant Boundary]
    F --> G[Local Domain Policy]

    G --> H[Entitlement Client]
    G --> I[Relationship / ACL Client]
    G --> J[Workflow State]

    H --> K[Entitlement Service]
    I --> L[OpenFGA / ACL Store]
    J --> M[Camunda / Domain DB]

    G --> N[AuthorizationDecision]
    N --> O{Allowed?}

    O -->|No| P[403 / 404 / 409]
    O -->|Yes| Q[MyBatis Repository]

    Q --> R[(PostgreSQL)]

    N --> S[Audit Logger]
```

---

# 8. Authorization Decision Contract

Jangan kembalikan boolean dari PDP/service. Boolean terlalu miskin untuk audit.

```java
public record AuthorizationDecision(
        boolean allowed,
        String reason,
        String policyName,
        String policyVersion,
        String decisionSource,
        Map<String, Object> evidence
) {
    public static AuthorizationDecision allow(
            String reason,
            String policyName,
            String policyVersion,
            String decisionSource,
            Map<String, Object> evidence
    ) {
        return new AuthorizationDecision(
                true,
                reason,
                policyName,
                policyVersion,
                decisionSource,
                evidence
        );
    }

    public static AuthorizationDecision deny(
            String reason,
            String policyName,
            String policyVersion,
            String decisionSource,
            Map<String, Object> evidence
    ) {
        return new AuthorizationDecision(
                false,
                reason,
                policyName,
                policyVersion,
                decisionSource,
                evidence
        );
    }
}
```

Contoh decision:

```json
{
  "allowed": false,
  "reason": "maker_checker_violation",
  "policyName": "case-approval-policy",
  "policyVersion": "2026.07.1",
  "decisionSource": "LOCAL_DOMAIN_POLICY",
  "evidence": {
    "actorId": "user-123",
    "caseCreatedBy": "user-123",
    "caseStatus": "SUBMITTED",
    "tenantMatched": true
  }
}
```

---

# 9. Authorization Request Contract

Untuk external PDP, input harus stabil dan explicit.

```java
public record AuthorizationRequest(
        Subject subject,
        Action action,
        Resource resource,
        Environment environment
) {
    public record Subject(
            String id,
            String type,
            String tenantId,
            Set<String> roles,
            Set<String> permissions,
            Set<String> scopes,
            Map<String, Object> attributes
    ) {}

    public record Action(
            String code
    ) {}

    public record Resource(
            String type,
            String id,
            String tenantId,
            Map<String, Object> attributes
    ) {}

    public record Environment(
            String channel,
            String requestIp,
            String correlationId,
            Instant requestTime,
            Map<String, Object> attributes
    ) {}
}
```

Contoh JSON input:

```json
{
  "subject": {
    "id": "user-123",
    "type": "USER",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a",
    "roles": ["CASE_REVIEWER"],
    "permissions": ["case.approve"],
    "scopes": ["case:write"],
    "attributes": {
      "department": "compliance",
      "approvalLevel": 2,
      "regions": ["ID-JK"]
    }
  },
  "action": {
    "code": "case.approve"
  },
  "resource": {
    "type": "case",
    "id": "case-789",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a",
    "attributes": {
      "status": "SUBMITTED",
      "riskLevel": 2,
      "region": "ID-JK",
      "createdBy": "user-999"
    }
  },
  "environment": {
    "channel": "internal-web",
    "requestIp": "10.1.2.3",
    "correlationId": "req-abc",
    "requestTime": "2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00",
    "attributes": {}
  }
}
```

**Invariant:**

```text
PDP input harus berisi semua data yang dibutuhkan untuk mengambil keputusan.
PDP tidak boleh diam-diam mengandalkan implicit context yang tidak diaudit.
```

---

# 10. PEP di Jersey Authorization Filter

PEP pertama biasanya authorization filter untuk coarse endpoint guard.

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public class AuthorizationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Context
    private ResourceInfo resourceInfo;

    private final AuthorizationService authorizationService;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        RequiresPermission required = resolvePermission(resourceInfo);

        if (required == null) {
            return;
        }

        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor =
                (AuthenticatedPrincipal) ctx.getSecurityContext().getUserPrincipal();

        AuthorizationDecision decision =
                authorizationService.checkEndpointPermission(actor, required.value());

        if (!decision.allowed()) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
        }
    }
}
```

Ini hanya **coarse-grained PEP**. Untuk object access, PEP tetap perlu ada di application service.

---

# 11. PEP di Application Service

```java
public final class CaseApplicationService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CasePolicyDecisionService policyDecisionService;
    private final SecurityAuditLogger auditLogger;

    public void approveCase(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor, String caseId) {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findByIdForTenant(
                caseId,
                actor.tenantId()
        ).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Case not found"));

        AuthorizationDecision decision =
                policyDecisionService.canApproveCase(actor, caseRecord);

        auditLogger.logAuthorizationDecision(
                actor,
                "case.approve",
                "case",
                caseId,
                decision
        );

        if (!decision.allowed()) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
        }

        int updated = caseRepository.approveIfStillEligible(
                caseId,
                actor.tenantId(),
                actor.subject(),
                caseRecord.version()
        );

        if (updated == 0) {
            throw new ConflictException("Case state changed");
        }
    }
}
```

**Kenapa PEP perlu di service layer?**

Karena resource filter tidak tahu domain object yang aktual. Filter hanya tahu path, method, annotation, dan principal.

---

# 12. PEP di Repository/MyBatis

Authorization decision di Java tetap harus diperkuat query guard.

```xml
<update id="approveIfStillEligible">
    UPDATE cases
    SET
        status = 'APPROVED',
        approved_by = #{actorId},
        approved_at = now(),
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = #{caseId}
      AND tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
      AND created_by &lt;&gt; #{actorId}
      AND version = #{expectedVersion}
</update>
```

Ini bukan pengganti PDP. Ini adalah **defense-in-depth** untuk mencegah race condition dan cross-tenant mutation.

---

# 13. Local + External PDP Composition

Policy decision bisa menggabungkan beberapa sumber.

```java
public final class CasePolicyDecisionService {

    private final LocalCasePolicy localCasePolicy;
    private final EntitlementClient entitlementClient;
    private final RelationshipClient relationshipClient;

    public AuthorizationDecision canApproveCase(
            AuthenticatedPrincipal actor,
            CaseRecord caseRecord
    ) {
        AuthorizationDecision localDecision =
                localCasePolicy.preCheckApprove(actor, caseRecord);

        if (!localDecision.allowed()) {
            return localDecision;
        }

        AuthorizationDecision entitlementDecision =
                entitlementClient.check(actor, "case.approve", caseRecord);

        if (!entitlementDecision.allowed()) {
            return entitlementDecision;
        }

        AuthorizationDecision relationshipDecision =
                relationshipClient.check(
                        actor.subject(),
                        "approver",
                        "case:" + caseRecord.id()
                );

        if (!relationshipDecision.allowed()) {
            return relationshipDecision;
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.allow(
                "all_policy_layers_allowed",
                "case-approval-composite-policy",
                "2026.07.1",
                "COMPOSITE",
                Map.of(
                        "local", localDecision.reason(),
                        "entitlement", entitlementDecision.reason(),
                        "relationship", relationshipDecision.reason()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

**Composition rule yang aman:**

```text
Untuk sensitive action:
  semua mandatory layer harus allow.

Jika salah satu mandatory layer deny:
  deny.

Jika mandatory layer unavailable:
  fail-closed.
```

---

# 14. Policy Evaluation Order

Urutan evaluasi yang disarankan:

```text
1. Cheap local reject
   - tenant mismatch
   - missing permission
   - invalid status
   - maker-checker violation

2. Entitlement check
   - effective permission
   - tenant grant
   - license/subscription entitlement
   - admin delegation

3. Relationship / ACL check
   - owner
   - assignee
   - group member
   - supervisor
   - shared-with

4. Expensive/risk-aware check
   - risk score
   - step-up auth requirement
   - external approval requirement

5. Query mutation guard
   - expected state
   - tenant
   - version
```

Tujuannya:

```text
Reject cepat untuk kasus jelas.
External call hanya dilakukan saat memang perlu.
Sensitive mutation tetap dijaga di SQL.
```

---

# 15. Entitlement Service API Design

## Request

```http
POST /v1/authorization/check
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json
{
  "subject": {
    "id": "user-123",
    "type": "USER",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a"
  },
  "action": "case.approve",
  "resource": {
    "type": "case",
    "id": "case-789",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a"
  },
  "context": {
    "correlationId": "req-abc",
    "channel": "internal-web"
  }
}
```

## Response

```json
{
  "allowed": false,
  "reason": "missing_entitlement",
  "policyName": "tenant-entitlement-policy",
  "policyVersion": "2026.07.1",
  "decisionId": "dec-123"
}
```

## Batch Check

Untuk list/bulk operation, hindari N+1 PDP calls.

```http
POST /v1/authorization/batch-check
```

```json
{
  "subject": {
    "id": "user-123",
    "type": "USER",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a"
  },
  "checks": [
    {
      "action": "case.read",
      "resource": {
        "type": "case",
        "id": "CASE-1"
      }
    },
    {
      "action": "case.read",
      "resource": {
        "type": "case",
        "id": "CASE-2"
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

---

# 16. OPA-style Integration Pattern

OPA cocok untuk policy yang ingin dibuat declarative dan bisa dipakai lintas stack. OPA sendiri diposisikan sebagai general-purpose policy engine untuk menyatukan enforcement lintas stack. ([Open Policy Agent](https://openpolicyagent.org/docs?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Input

```json
{
  "subject": {
    "id": "user-123",
    "tenant_id": "tenant-a",
    "permissions": ["case.approve"],
    "approval_level": 2,
    "regions": ["ID-JK"]
  },
  "action": "case.approve",
  "resource": {
    "type": "case",
    "id": "case-789",
    "tenant_id": "tenant-a",
    "status": "SUBMITTED",
    "risk_level": 2,
    "region": "ID-JK",
    "created_by": "user-999"
  }
}
```

## Policy Conceptual

```rego
package authz.case

default allow := false

allow if {
  input.subject.tenant_id == input.resource.tenant_id
  "case.approve" in input.subject.permissions
  input.resource.status == "SUBMITTED"
  input.resource.created_by != input.subject.id
  input.resource.risk_level <= input.subject.approval_level
  input.resource.region in input.subject.regions
}
```

## Java Client Boundary

```java
public interface PolicyEngineClient {
    AuthorizationDecision evaluate(AuthorizationRequest request);
}
```

**Prinsip:** service Java tidak perlu tahu detail Rego. Service hanya tahu contract `AuthorizationRequest -> AuthorizationDecision`.

---

# 17. OpenFGA / ReBAC Integration Pattern

ReBAC cocok ketika rule utama adalah relasi. OpenFGA menjelaskan ReBAC sebagai akses yang bergantung pada relasi user-object dan object-object. ([OpenFGA](https://openfga.dev/docs/authorization-concepts?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Check Concept

```text
Can user:user-123 have relation approver on case:case-789?
```

## Tuple Examples

```text
case:case-789#owner@user:user-999
case:case-789#assignee@user:user-456
case:case-789#approver@group:reviewer-team-a#member
group:reviewer-team-a#member@user:user-123
tenant:tenant-a#admin@user:user-777
```

## Java Boundary

```java
public interface RelationshipAuthorizationClient {

    AuthorizationDecision check(
            String subject,
            String relation,
            String object
    );

    Map<String, AuthorizationDecision> batchCheck(
            String subject,
            String relation,
            Collection<String> objects
    );
}
```

## Usage

```java
AuthorizationDecision relationshipDecision =
        relationshipAuthorizationClient.check(
                "user:" + actor.subject(),
                "approver",
                "case:" + caseRecord.id()
        );
```

**Caveat:** ReBAC tidak otomatis menggantikan semua ABAC rule. Rule seperti `case.status == SUBMITTED`, `riskLevel <= approvalLevel`, atau `createdBy != actor` sering tetap lebih natural di local domain policy atau OPA.

---

# 18. Policy Information Point / PIP Design

PIP adalah sumber atribut/data untuk policy.

| PIP | Data | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| JWT claims | subject, tenant, scope, roles | Claim stale |
| Entitlement DB | effective permission, tenant grant | Cache stale |
| Domain DB | case status, owner, risk, region | Query latency |
| Camunda | task assignee, candidate group, process state | State race |
| HR/Org service | manager, department, level | Availability |
| Tenant config | feature/region/license | Config drift |
| ACL/ReBAC store | relationship tuples | Event sync lag |

**Rule:**

```text
Jangan masukkan data PIP yang tidak dipercaya ke PDP tanpa validasi.
Client request body bukan PIP tepercaya.
```

Misalnya `tenantId` dari request body tidak boleh dipakai sebagai tenant otoritatif. Tenant harus berasal dari validated token, service account mapping, atau resource yang di-load dari DB.

---

# 19. Policy Administration Point / PAP

PAP adalah tempat policy dikelola.

Contoh PAP:

```text
- permission catalog table
- role management admin UI
- ACL management screen
- OPA policy bundle repository
- OpenFGA model + tuple writer
- tenant entitlement configuration
- emergency access workflow
```

PAP harus punya audit sendiri:

```text
Who changed policy?
What changed?
Which tenant/resource?
Old value?
New value?
Approval reference?
Effective time?
```

Contoh audit policy change:

```json
{
  "eventType": "POLICY_CHANGE",
  "changedBy": "admin-123",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "changeType": "ROLE_PERMISSION_ADDED",
  "role": "CASE_SUPERVISOR",
  "permission": "case.approve",
  "oldValue": null,
  "newValue": "case.approve",
  "approvedBy": "admin-777",
  "effectiveAt": "2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00"
}
```

---

# 20. Failure Handling Matrix

Ini bagian yang sering dilupakan.

| Failure | Contoh | Default Aman |
|---|---|---|
| PDP timeout | Entitlement service lambat | Fail-closed untuk sensitive action |
| PDP 5xx | Policy service error | Fail-closed, log incident |
| PDP unreachable | Network issue | Fail-closed kecuali endpoint low-risk dengan cached allow |
| PIP unavailable | HR/org service down | Fail-closed jika atribut wajib |
| Relationship store stale | Tuple belum sync | Gunakan consistency policy dan audit |
| Permission cache stale | Role dicabut tapi masih cached | TTL pendek + invalidation |
| Policy bundle invalid | OPA policy gagal load | Jangan deploy / fail startup |
| Unknown policy version | Client/service mismatch | Reject atau fallback ke previous verified version |
| Partial batch failure | Beberapa object gagal dicek | Deny failed items |
| Circuit breaker open | PDP unavailable berkepanjangan | Deny sensitive, degrade read-only bila disetujui policy |

**Invariant:**

```text
Untuk action sensitif, authorization failure harus gagal sebagai deny, bukan allow.
```

---

# 21. Fail-Open vs Fail-Closed

| Mode | Arti | Kapan Boleh |
|---|---|---|
| Fail-closed | Jika policy tidak bisa dievaluasi, request ditolak | Default untuk write/sensitive/admin |
| Fail-open | Jika policy gagal, request tetap diizinkan | Hampir tidak boleh untuk authorization |
| Fail-degraded | Fitur dibatasi, misalnya read-only | Bisa untuk low-risk internal dashboard |
| Fail-cached | Pakai cached decision/entitlement | Hanya dengan TTL pendek dan action terbatas |

Pattern:

```java
public AuthorizationDecision safeEvaluate(AuthorizationRequest request) {
    try {
        return pdpClient.evaluate(request);

    } catch (PdpTimeoutException e) {
        if (request.action().isSensitive()) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny(
                    "pdp_timeout_fail_closed",
                    "pdp-failure-policy",
                    "v1",
                    "FAILURE_HANDLER",
                    Map.of("failure", "timeout")
            );
        }

        return cachedDecisionStore.findFreshDecision(request)
                .orElseGet(() -> AuthorizationDecision.deny(
                        "no_fresh_cached_decision",
                        "pdp-failure-policy",
                        "v1",
                        "FAILURE_HANDLER",
                        Map.of()
                ));
    }
}
```

---

# 22. Caching Strategy

Caching authorization bisa membantu latency, tetapi rawan stale permission.

## Apa yang Bisa Di-cache?

| Data | Cache? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| JWKS public keys | Ya | TTL dan refresh on unknown `kid` |
| Permission catalog | Ya | Versioned |
| Effective permissions | Ya | TTL pendek / invalidation |
| Tenant config | Ya | Versioned |
| ACL/ReBAC decision | Hati-hati | Tergantung action |
| Deny decision | Hati-hati | Bisa berubah setelah grant |
| Allow decision untuk write | Sangat hati-hati | TTL sangat pendek atau avoid |
| Object attributes | Tergantung | Must not bypass fresh state |

## Cache Key

```text
authz:{tenantId}:{subjectId}:{action}:{resourceType}:{resourceId}:{policyVersion}:{permissionVersion}
```

Untuk list/search:

```text
visibility:{tenantId}:{subjectId}:{queryHash}:{policyVersion}:{permissionVersion}
```

**Jangan cache hanya berdasarkan resource ID:**

```text
case:CASE-123
```

Itu bisa bocor lintas tenant/actor.

---

# 23. Consistency Model

Authorization punya problem consistency.

| Data | Butuh Strong? | Contoh |
|---|---:|---|
| Token validity | Medium/high | expired token harus ditolak |
| Role revocation | High untuk admin/sensitive | user dicabut permission approve |
| ACL grant | Medium/high | object share baru |
| Assignment | High untuk task/workflow | task reassigned |
| Tenant config | Medium | feature disabled |
| Relationship hierarchy | Medium | manager berubah |
| Audit | High | decision harus tercatat |

Praktik yang aman:

```text
Read/list:
  boleh eventual consistency terbatas

Sensitive write:
  butuh fresh check atau short TTL

Admin/break-glass:
  fresh check + audit wajib
```

---

# 24. Observability untuk Policy Layer

Metrics penting:

```text
authz_decision_total{decision="allow|deny", action, resource_type}
authz_decision_latency_ms{source="local|entitlement|opa|openfga"}
authz_pdp_timeout_total
authz_pdp_error_total
authz_cache_hit_total
authz_cache_stale_total
authz_unknown_policy_version_total
authz_denied_by_reason_total{reason}
authz_cross_tenant_denied_total
```

Logs penting:

```json
{
  "eventType": "AUTHORIZATION_DECISION",
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "pdp_timeout_fail_closed",
  "subjectId": "user-123",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "action": "case.approve",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "case-789",
  "policyName": "case-approval-composite-policy",
  "policyVersion": "2026.07.1",
  "decisionSource": "FAILURE_HANDLER",
  "correlationId": "req-abc"
}
```

Alert yang layak:

```text
- PDP timeout spike
- deny spike by reason
- unknown kid / invalid token spike
- cross-tenant deny spike
- policy version mismatch
- cache stale spike
- entitlement service unavailable
```

# 25. Testing Strategy

## Unit Test

```text
[ ] Local policy allows valid actor/resource/context
[ ] Local policy denies tenant mismatch
[ ] Local policy denies missing permission
[ ] Local policy denies invalid workflow state
[ ] Local policy denies maker-checker violation
[ ] Decision contains reason and policy version
```

## Contract Test PDP

```text
[ ] AuthorizationRequest schema compatible
[ ] PDP response schema compatible
[ ] Unknown action returns deny
[ ] Unknown resource type returns deny
[ ] Missing subject returns deny
[ ] Missing tenant returns deny
```

## Failure Test

```text
[ ] PDP timeout fails closed for write action
[ ] PDP 5xx fails closed
[ ] Cached allow expires after TTL
[ ] Stale permission does not allow sensitive action
[ ] Batch check partial failure denies failed objects
[ ] Circuit breaker open denies sensitive action
```

## Integration Test

```text
[ ] Jersey filter invokes endpoint PEP
[ ] Application service invokes object PEP
[ ] MyBatis update includes tenant/status/version guard
[ ] Audit event emitted on allow
[ ] Audit event emitted on deny
[ ] Cross-tenant access does not leak existence
```

---

# 26. Policy Versioning

Policy harus punya versi.

```text
case-access-policy: 2026.07.1
case-approval-policy: 2026.07.1
tenant-entitlement-policy: 2026.07.1
relationship-model: 2026.07.1
```

Kenapa?

```text
- Audit bisa menjelaskan decision berdasarkan policy saat itu.
- Rollback lebih aman.
- Test bisa mengunci behavior policy.
- Incident review bisa tahu rule mana yang berlaku.
```

Contoh audit:

```json
{
  "decision": "ALLOW",
  "reason": "all_policy_layers_allowed",
  "policyName": "case-approval-composite-policy",
  "policyVersion": "2026.07.1",
  "relationshipModelVersion": "2026.07.1",
  "permissionCatalogVersion": "2026.06.4"
}
```

---

# 27. Migration Strategy

Kalau sistem awalnya role-hardcoded, jangan langsung rewrite semua.

## Tahap 1 - Inventory

```text
Cari semua:
- hasRole(...)
- if ADMIN
- hardcoded permission string
- endpoint tanpa annotation
- query tanpa tenant_id
- update/delete tanpa tenant_id
```

## Tahap 2 - Introduce Permission Catalog

```text
Role lama:
  ADMIN
  REVIEWER
  SUPERVISOR

Mapping baru:
  ADMIN -> admin.user.manage, case.read.all, case.assign
  REVIEWER -> case.read.assigned, case.update, task.complete
  SUPERVISOR -> case.approve, task.reassign
```

## Tahap 3 - Wrap Policy

```java
public boolean legacyCanApprove(User user, CaseRecord caseRecord) {
    return caseApprovalPolicy.canApprove(toPrincipal(user), caseRecord).allowed();
}
```

## Tahap 4 - Add Audit

```text
Jangan tunggu policy sempurna.
Tambahkan audit decision secepat mungkin.
```

## Tahap 5 - Externalize Jika Perlu

```text
Jika policy sudah berulang lintas service:
  move to entitlement service / PDP

Jika relasi object makin kompleks:
  consider OpenFGA/Zanzibar-style model

Jika policy deklaratif lintas platform:
  consider OPA-style model
```

---

# 28. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Semua authorization di gateway | Object-level access bocor | Service-layer PEP |
| Semua rule hardcoded di resource | Sulit audit/evolve | Policy class/service |
| PDP return boolean saja | Tidak ada reason | Return decision object |
| PDP dipanggil per row list | N+1 latency | Query visibility / batch check |
| Fail-open saat PDP down | Privilege escalation | Fail-closed |
| Cache allow terlalu lama | Revocation lambat | TTL pendek/invalidation |
| Client mengirim policy context | Manipulasi atribut | Load context dari trusted source |
| Role dianggap policy final | Role explosion/overprivilege | Permission + ABAC/ReBAC |
| Query tidak pakai tenant_id | Cross-tenant leak | Tenant-aware repository |
| Policy change tanpa audit | Tidak defensible | PAP audit trail |

---

# 29. Practical Reference Architecture

```text
com.company.security
├── authn
│   └── AuthenticationFilter
│
├── authz
│   ├── RequiresPermission
│   ├── AuthorizationFilter
│   ├── AuthorizationDecision
│   ├── AuthorizationRequest
│   └── AuthorizationDecisionService
│
├── policy
│   ├── CaseAccessPolicy
│   ├── CaseApprovalPolicy
│   ├── WorkflowTaskPolicy
│   └── TenantPolicy
│
├── entitlement
│   ├── EntitlementClient
│   ├── EntitlementDecision
│   └── CachedEntitlementClient
│
├── relationship
│   ├── RelationshipAuthorizationClient
│   └── RelationshipDecision
│
├── failure
│   ├── PdpFailureHandler
│   ├── AuthorizationCircuitBreaker
│   └── CachedDecisionStore
│
└── audit
    ├── AuthorizationAuditEvent
    └── SecurityAuditLogger
```

---

# 30. Minimal Production Invariants

```text
1. PEP harus ada di filter, service, dan query layer sesuai kebutuhan.
2. PDP harus return decision object, bukan boolean saja.
3. PDP input harus explicit dan auditable.
4. Client-supplied context tidak boleh dipercaya sebagai policy truth.
5. Sensitive action harus fail-closed saat PDP/PIP gagal.
6. External PDP tidak menggantikan tenant-aware SQL.
7. Gateway authorization tidak menggantikan object-level authorization.
8. Policy harus versioned.
9. Policy change harus audited.
10. Authorization decision harus observable melalui logs/metrics.
```

---

# 31. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | PEP/PDP/PIP/PAP mental model | Bisa memisahkan enforcement, decision, data, admin |
| 2 | Local policy class | Rule domain bisa dites dan diaudit |
| 3 | AuthorizationDecision contract | Decision punya reason/evidence/version |
| 4 | Entitlement service | Permission/role/grant bisa centralized |
| 5 | OPA-style PDP | Policy declarative lintas stack |
| 6 | OpenFGA/ReBAC | Relationship/object sharing bisa dimodelkan |
| 7 | Failure handling | PDP down tidak menjadi fail-open |
| 8 | Caching & consistency | Latency turun tanpa revocation risk besar |
| 9 | Observability | Deny spike/timeout/stale cache terlihat |
| 10 | Migration | Hardcoded role bisa dipindahkan bertahap |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 4:42:38 AM

Kita lanjut ke testing/regression suite. Saya akan susun dari sudut pandang senior engineer: bukan hanya “test 401/403”, tapi memastikan authorization tidak regresi saat endpoint, mapper, permission, workflow, atau PDP berubah.

# Cheatsheet - Authorization Testing & Security Regression Suite di Jersey/JAX-RS

## 1. Tujuan

Testing authorization bukan cuma memastikan endpoint mengembalikan `401` atau `403`.

Yang ingin dijaga adalah:

```text
1. Token invalid tidak bisa masuk.
2. Token valid tapi permission kurang tetap ditolak.
3. User tidak bisa akses object milik user/tenant lain.
4. List/search/count tidak membocorkan data.
5. Update/delete tidak bypass tenant/status/version guard.
6. PDP/entitlement/ACL failure tidak berubah menjadi allow.
7. Audit allow/deny tetap tercatat.
8. Perubahan role/permission/policy tidak membuat regresi diam-diam.
```

OWASP API Security 2023 menempatkan **Broken Object Level Authorization / BOLA** sebagai API1, dan menjelaskan bahwa attacker dapat mengeksploitasi endpoint dengan memanipulasi object ID yang dikirim lewat path, query string, header, atau payload. OWASP juga menekankan bahwa object-level authorization check perlu dipertimbangkan pada setiap function yang mengakses data source menggunakan ID dari user. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/API-Security/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Testing Pyramid untuk Authorization

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Authorization Regression Suite] --> B[Unit Tests]
    A --> C[Integration Tests]
    A --> D[Contract Tests]
    A --> E[Database / Mapper Tests]
    A --> F[Security Scenario Tests]
    A --> G[Failure Mode Tests]
    A --> H[Audit Verification]
    A --> I[CI/CD Guardrails]

    B --> B1[Policy class]
    B --> B2[Permission evaluator]
    B --> B3[JWT claim mapper]
    B --> B4[AuthorizationDecision reason]

    C --> C1[Jersey filters]
    C --> C2[SecurityContext]
    C --> C3[Resource methods]
    C --> C4[401 / 403 mapping]

    D --> D1[PDP API contract]
    D --> D2[Entitlement service]
    D --> D3[OpenFGA / OPA adapter]
    D --> D4[Failure response contract]

    E --> E1[MyBatis visibility query]
    E --> E2[Tenant predicate]
    E --> E3[Update/delete guard]
    E --> E4[Count/search consistency]

    F --> F1[BOLA / IDOR]
    F --> F2[Cross-tenant access]
    F --> F3[Horizontal privilege escalation]
    F --> F4[Vertical privilege escalation]

    G --> G1[PDP timeout]
    G --> G2[JWKS failure]
    G --> G3[Cache stale]
    G --> G4[Partial batch failure]

    H --> H1[Allow audit]
    H --> H2[Deny audit]
    H --> H3[Policy version]
    H --> H4[Correlation ID]
```

---

# 3. Test Categories

| Category | Fokus | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Authentication test | Token/credential valid atau tidak | missing token, expired token, wrong audience |
| Endpoint authorization test | Scope/permission terhadap endpoint | user tanpa `case.approve` ditolak |
| Object-level authorization test | Akses terhadap object spesifik | user A tidak bisa baca case user B |
| Tenant isolation test | Boundary tenant | tenant A tidak bisa akses tenant B |
| Data visibility test | List/search/count aman | list hanya berisi visible cases |
| Mutation guard test | Update/delete aman | update wajib `tenant_id`, `status`, `version` |
| Workflow authorization test | State/task/assignee valid | task hanya bisa complete oleh assignee/candidate |
| PDP/entitlement failure test | External policy failure aman | PDP timeout fail-closed |
| Cache authorization test | Cache tidak stale/bocor | permission revoked tidak tetap allow |
| Audit test | Decision tercatat | deny punya reason/policy/correlation ID |

---

# 4. Fundamental Test Matrix

```text
[ ] Missing token returns 401
[ ] Malformed token returns 401
[ ] Expired token returns 401
[ ] Token with wrong issuer returns 401
[ ] Token with wrong audience returns 401
[ ] Valid token without required scope returns 403
[ ] Valid token without required permission returns 403
[ ] Valid permission but wrong object returns 403 or 404
[ ] Valid permission but wrong tenant returns 404 or 403
[ ] Valid permission but invalid workflow state returns 403 or 409
[ ] Valid permission but maker-checker violation returns 403
[ ] List endpoint returns only visible data
[ ] Count endpoint counts only visible data
[ ] Search endpoint cannot bypass tenant predicate
[ ] Bulk endpoint authorizes per object
[ ] Update/delete includes tenant/status/version guard
[ ] PDP timeout does not allow sensitive action
[ ] Audit is emitted for allow and deny
```

OWASP Authorization Cheat Sheet recommends validating permissions on every request and warns that even a single missed access-control check can compromise confidentiality or integrity. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Test Fixture Model

Buat dataset kecil tapi kaya skenario.

```text
Tenant A
  user_alice
    roles: CASE_REVIEWER
    permissions:
      - case.read.assigned
      - case.update
      - workflow.task.complete

  user_bob
    roles: CASE_SUPERVISOR

      - case.read.all

  case_A_own_by_alice
    tenant_id: tenant-a

    assigned_user_id: alice
    status: DRAFT
    risk_level: 1

  case_A_submitted_by_alice
    tenant_id: tenant-a
    created_by: alice
    assigned_user_id: bob
    status: SUBMITTED
    risk_level: 2

Tenant B
  user_charlie
    roles: CASE_REVIEWER
    permissions:
      - case.read.assigned

  case_B_assigned_to_charlie
    tenant_id: tenant-b
    created_by: charlie
    assigned_user_id: charlie
    status: SUBMITTED
    risk_level: 1
```

Minimal fixtures ini bisa menguji:

```text
- own vs assigned
- same tenant vs cross tenant
- reviewer vs supervisor
- draft vs submitted
- maker-checker
- read.all vs read.assigned
```

# 6. Naming Convention Test

Gunakan nama test yang menjelaskan policy, bukan implementasi internal.

## Baik

```java
shouldDenyApproveWhenActorCreatedTheCase()
shouldDenyReadWhenCaseBelongsToDifferentTenant()
shouldAllowReadWhenActorIsAssignedAndHasReadAssignedPermission()
shouldReturnOnlyVisibleCasesInListEndpoint()
shouldFailClosedWhenEntitlementServiceTimesOut()
```

## Kurang baik

```java
testCase1()
test403()
testAuthz()
testFilter()
```

Authorization test harus menjadi dokumentasi policy.

---

# 7. Unit Test - Policy Class

Policy class adalah tempat paling murah untuk menguji kombinasi rule.

## Policy

```java
public final class CaseAccessPolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision canApprove(
            AuthenticatedPrincipal actor,
            CaseRecord caseRecord
    ) {
        if (!actor.tenantId().equals(caseRecord.tenantId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("tenant_mismatch");
        }

        if (!actor.hasPermission("case.approve")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_case_approve_permission");
        }

        if (!"SUBMITTED".equals(caseRecord.status())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("case_not_submitted");
        }

        if (actor.subject().equals(caseRecord.createdBy())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("maker_checker_violation");
        }

        if (actor.approvalLevel() < caseRecord.riskLevel()) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("approval_level_too_low");
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.allow("case_approval_allowed");
    }
}
```

## Tests

```java
class CaseAccessPolicyTest {

    private final CaseAccessPolicy policy = new CaseAccessPolicy();

    @Test
    void shouldAllowApproveWhenActorHasPermissionAndCaseIsSubmitted() {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor = PrincipalFixture.supervisor()
                .tenantId("tenant-a")
                .subject("bob")
                .permission("case.approve")
                .approvalLevel(3)
                .build();

        CaseRecord caseRecord = CaseFixture.submittedCase()
                .tenantId("tenant-a")
                .createdBy("alice")
                .riskLevel(2)
                .build();

        AuthorizationDecision decision = policy.canApprove(actor, caseRecord);

        assertTrue(decision.allowed());
        assertEquals("case_approval_allowed", decision.reason());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldDenyApproveWhenTenantMismatch() {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor = PrincipalFixture.supervisor()
                .tenantId("tenant-a")
                .permission("case.approve")
                .approvalLevel(3)
                .build();

        CaseRecord caseRecord = CaseFixture.submittedCase()
                .tenantId("tenant-b")
                .createdBy("alice")
                .riskLevel(1)
                .build();

        AuthorizationDecision decision = policy.canApprove(actor, caseRecord);

        assertFalse(decision.allowed());
        assertEquals("tenant_mismatch", decision.reason());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldDenyApproveWhenActorCreatedTheCase() {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor = PrincipalFixture.supervisor()
                .tenantId("tenant-a")
                .subject("alice")
                .permission("case.approve")
                .approvalLevel(3)
                .build();

        CaseRecord caseRecord = CaseFixture.submittedCase()
                .tenantId("tenant-a")
                .createdBy("alice")
                .riskLevel(1)
                .build();

        AuthorizationDecision decision = policy.canApprove(actor, caseRecord);

        assertFalse(decision.allowed());
        assertEquals("maker_checker_violation", decision.reason());
    }
}
```

**Target unit test:** semua branch policy punya test allow dan deny.

---

# 8. Unit Test - Permission Evaluator

```java
class PermissionEvaluatorTest {

    private final PermissionEvaluator evaluator = new PermissionEvaluator();

    @Test
    void shouldAllowWhenAllRequiredPermissionsExist() {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor = PrincipalFixture.user()
                .permissions(Set.of("case.read", "case.update"))
                .build();

        boolean allowed = evaluator.hasAll(actor, Set.of("case.read", "case.update"));

        assertTrue(allowed);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldDenyWhenAnyRequiredPermissionMissing() {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor = PrincipalFixture.user()
                .permissions(Set.of("case.read"))
                .build();

        boolean allowed = evaluator.hasAll(actor, Set.of("case.read", "case.approve"));

        assertFalse(allowed);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldDenyNullPrincipal() {
        boolean allowed = evaluator.hasAll(null, Set.of("case.read"));

        assertFalse(allowed);
    }
}
```

---

# 9. Unit Test - Claim Mapping

JWT validation sendiri biasanya diuji dengan library/tooling khusus, tetapi claim normalization wajib diuji karena provider berbeda bisa memakai claim berbeda.

```java
class AccessTokenClaimsMapperTest {

    private final AccessTokenClaimsMapper mapper = new AccessTokenClaimsMapper();

    @Test
    void shouldParseScopeString() {
        Map<String, Object> claims = Map.of(
                "sub", "user-123",
                "tenant_id", "tenant-a",
                "scope", "case:read case:write"
        );

        VerifiedAccessToken token = mapper.map(claims);

        assertEquals(Set.of("case:read", "case:write"), token.scopes());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldParseScpArray() {
        Map<String, Object> claims = Map.of(
                "sub", "user-123",
                "tenant_id", "tenant-a",
                "scp", List.of("case:read", "case:write")
        );

        VerifiedAccessToken token = mapper.map(claims);

        assertEquals(Set.of("case:read", "case:write"), token.scopes());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectMissingTenantWhenTenantRequired() {
        Map<String, Object> claims = Map.of(
                "sub", "user-123",
                "scope", "case:read"
        );

        assertThrows(InvalidAccessTokenException.class, () -> mapper.map(claims));
    }
}
```

---

# 10. Integration Test - Jersey Authentication Filter

JAX-RS `ContainerRequestFilter` dapat memproses request sebelum resource method dan dapat menghentikan request dengan `abortWith`; `ContainerRequestContext` juga menyediakan `setSecurityContext(...)` untuk mengatur security context request saat ini. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.1/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/container/containerrequestfilter?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Test Cases

```text
[ ] Missing Authorization header -> 401
[ ] Non-Bearer Authorization header -> 401
[ ] Invalid JWT -> 401
[ ] Valid JWT -> SecurityContext contains AuthenticatedPrincipal
[ ] Token subject mapped correctly
[ ] Tenant claim mapped correctly
[ ] Roles/scopes/permissions mapped correctly
```

## Example

```java
class AuthenticationFilterIT extends JerseyTest {

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        return new ResourceConfig()
                .register(new AuthenticationFilter(fakeAccessTokenValidator()))
                .register(TestIdentityResource.class);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldReturn401WhenAuthorizationHeaderMissing() {
        Response response = target("/test/me")
                .request()
                .get();

        assertEquals(401, response.getStatus());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldSetSecurityContextWhenTokenValid() {
        Response response = target("/test/me")
                .request()
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer valid-token")
                .get();

        assertEquals(200, response.getStatus());

        IdentityResponse body = response.readEntity(IdentityResponse.class);
        assertEquals("user-123", body.subject());
        assertEquals("tenant-a", body.tenantId());
    }
}
```

Test resource:

```java
@Path("/test")
public class TestIdentityResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/me")
    public IdentityResponse me(@Context SecurityContext securityContext) {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal =
                (AuthenticatedPrincipal) securityContext.getUserPrincipal();

        return new IdentityResponse(
                principal.subject(),
                principal.tenantId()
        );
    }
}
```

# 11. Integration Test - Authorization Filter

```java
class AuthorizationFilterIT extends JerseyTest {

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        return new ResourceConfig()
                .register(fakeAuthenticationFilterWithPrincipal())
                .register(new AuthorizationFilter(new AuthorizationService()))
                .register(SecuredCaseResource.class);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldAllowWhenRequiredPermissionExists() {
        Response response = target("/cases/CASE-1")
                .request()
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer token-with-case-read")
                .get();

        assertEquals(200, response.getStatus());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldDenyWhenRequiredPermissionMissing() {
        Response response = target("/cases/CASE-1")
                .request()
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer token-without-case-read")
                .get();

        assertEquals(403, response.getStatus());
    }
}
```

Resource:

```java
@Path("/cases")
public class SecuredCaseResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    @RequiresPermission("case.read")
    public Response getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        return Response.ok().build();
    }
}
```

# 12. Object-Level Authorization Test

Endpoint permission test tidak cukup. Harus ada test yang mengganti object ID.

OWASP Web Security Testing Guide menjelaskan bahwa IDOR terjadi ketika aplikasi memberi akses langsung ke object berdasarkan input user, sehingga attacker dapat memodifikasi parameter yang menunjuk object dan bypass authorization. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/www-project-web-security-testing-guide/latest/4-Web_Application_Security_Testing/05-Authorization_Testing/04-Testing_for_Insecure_Direct_Object_References?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Scenario

```text
Given:
  Alice is in tenant-a
  Alice has case.read.assigned
  CASE-1 is assigned to Alice
  CASE-2 is assigned to Bob
  CASE-3 belongs to tenant-b

Expected:
  Alice can read CASE-1
  Alice cannot read CASE-2
  Alice cannot read CASE-3
```

## Test

```java
@Test
void shouldDenyReadingAnotherUsersCaseInSameTenant() {
    AuthenticatedPrincipal alice = PrincipalFixture.alice()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .permission("case.read.assigned")
            .build();

    String bobCaseId = seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .assignedUserId("bob")
            .createdBy("bob")
            .build()
            .id();

    Response response = target("/cases/" + bobCaseId)
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, tokenFor(alice))
            .get();

    assertEquals(403, response.getStatus());
}

@Test
void shouldNotExposeCrossTenantCaseExistence() {
    AuthenticatedPrincipal alice = PrincipalFixture.alice()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .permission("case.read.assigned")
            .build();

    String tenantBCaseId = seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-b")
            .assignedUserId("charlie")
            .createdBy("charlie")
            .build()
            .id();

    Response response = target("/cases/" + tenantBCaseId)
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, tokenFor(alice))
            .get();

    assertEquals(404, response.getStatus());
}
```

# 13. Tenant Isolation Test

## Repository Contract

Repository method harus memaksa tenant.

```java
interface CaseRepository {
    Optional<CaseRecord> findByIdForTenant(String caseId, String tenantId);
}
```

## Test

```java
@Test
void findByIdForTenantShouldNotReturnCaseFromDifferentTenant() {
    String caseId = seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-b")
            .build()
            .id();

    Optional<CaseRecord> result =
            caseRepository.findByIdForTenant(caseId, "tenant-a");

    assertTrue(result.isEmpty());
}
```

## Mutation Test

```java
@Test
void approveShouldNotUpdateCaseFromDifferentTenant() {
    String caseId = seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-b")
            .status("SUBMITTED")
            .createdBy("charlie")
            .build()
            .id();

    int updated = caseMapper.approveCase(
            caseId,
            "tenant-a",
            "alice"
    );

    assertEquals(0, updated);
}
```

# 14. MyBatis Mapper Test - Visibility Query

Gunakan database test yang mendekati PostgreSQL asli. Untuk query authorization, jangan terlalu bergantung pada in-memory DB yang behavior SQL-nya berbeda.

## Criteria

```java
CaseVisibilityCriteria criteria = new CaseVisibilityCriteria(
        "tenant-a",
        "alice",
        CaseVisibilityMode.ASSIGNED_OR_OWN,
        Set.of("ID-JK"),
        2
);
```

## Test

```java
@Test
void findVisibleCasesShouldReturnOnlyAssignedOrOwnCasesWithinTenantAndRegion() {
    seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .assignedUserId("alice")
            .region("ID-JK")
            .riskLevel(1)
            .build();

    seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .assignedUserId("bob")
            .createdBy("bob")
            .region("ID-JK")
            .riskLevel(1)
            .build();

    seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-b")
            .assignedUserId("alice")
            .region("ID-JK")
            .riskLevel(1)
            .build();

    List<CaseRecord> result = caseMapper.findVisibleCases(criteria);

    assertEquals(1, result.size());
    assertTrue(result.stream().allMatch(c -> c.tenantId().equals("tenant-a")));
    assertTrue(result.stream().allMatch(c ->
            c.assignedUserId().equals("alice") || c.createdBy().equals("alice")));
}
```

# 15. Count/Search Consistency Test

Bug umum: `items` aman, tapi `total` bocor.

## Test

```java
@Test
void countVisibleCasesShouldUseSameVisibilityCriteriaAsList() {
    CaseVisibilityCriteria criteria = new CaseVisibilityCriteria(
            "tenant-a",
            "alice",
            CaseVisibilityMode.ASSIGNED,
            Set.of(),
            null
    );

    seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .assignedUserId("alice")
            .build();

    seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .assignedUserId("bob")
            .build();

    seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-b")
            .assignedUserId("alice")
            .build();

    List<CaseRecord> cases = caseMapper.findVisibleCases(criteria);
    long count = caseMapper.countVisibleCases(criteria);

    assertEquals(cases.size(), count);
    assertEquals(1, count);
}
```

@Test
void searchShouldNotAllowUserFiltersToBypassTenantPredicate() {
    CaseSearchCriteria criteria = new CaseSearchCriteria(
            "tenant-a",
            "alice",
            CaseVisibilityMode.ASSIGNED,
            "SUBMITTED",

    );

            .tenantId("tenant-b")
            .assignedUserId("alice")
            .status("SUBMITTED")
            .title("fraud investigation")

# 16. State-Changing Mutation Test

Authorization check di Java harus diperkuat SQL guard.

## Use Case

Approve case only if:
  tenant_id = actor.tenantId
  status = SUBMITTED
  created_by <> actor.subject
  version = expectedVersion

@Test
void approveShouldFailWhenCaseStatusChangedAfterRead() {

    int updated = caseMapper.approveIfStillEligible(
            caseRecord.id(),
            "tenant-a",
            "bob",
            1
    );

    assertEquals(0, updated);
}

# 17. Bulk Operation Test

Bulk endpoint rawan karena developer sering mengecek permission sekali lalu update banyak object.

## Scenario

```text
Alice can close CASE-1.
Alice cannot close CASE-2.
CASE-3 belongs to tenant-b.

Expected:
  only CASE-1 closed
  CASE-2 denied
  CASE-3 not touched
  audit has per-object decision
```

## Test

```java
@Test
void bulkCloseShouldAuthorizeEachObjectIndividually() {
    AuthenticatedPrincipal alice = PrincipalFixture.alice()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .permission("case.close")
            .build();

    String allowedCase = seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .assignedUserId("alice")
            .status("RESOLVED")
            .build()
            .id();

    String deniedCase = seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .assignedUserId("bob")
            .status("RESOLVED")
            .build()
            .id();

    String crossTenantCase = seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-b")
            .assignedUserId("alice")
            .status("RESOLVED")
            .build()
            .id();

    BulkCloseResult result = caseService.bulkClose(
            alice,
            List.of(allowedCase, deniedCase, crossTenantCase)
    );

    assertTrue(result.closedIds().contains(allowedCase));
    assertFalse(result.closedIds().contains(deniedCase));
    assertFalse(result.closedIds().contains(crossTenantCase));

    assertEquals("CLOSED", caseMapper.findByIdForTenant(allowedCase, "tenant-a").status());
    assertEquals("RESOLVED", caseMapper.findByIdForTenant(deniedCase, "tenant-a").status());
    assertTrue(caseMapper.findByIdForTenant(crossTenantCase, "tenant-a").isEmpty());
}
```

# 18. Workflow / Camunda Authorization Test

Untuk workflow-heavy app, test authorization harus menggabungkan:

```text
- permission
- tenant
- task assignee/candidate group
- process business key
- domain object status
- maker-checker
```

## Scenario

```text
User can complete task only if:
  has workflow.task.complete
  task is active
  user is assignee OR candidate user/group
  task business object is visible to user
```

## Test

```java
@Test
void shouldDenyTaskCompletionWhenActorIsNotAssigneeOrCandidate() {
    AuthenticatedPrincipal alice = PrincipalFixture.alice()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .permission("workflow.task.complete")
            .build();

    WorkflowTask task = TaskFixture.activeTask()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .businessKey("CASE-1")
            .assignee("bob")
            .candidateGroups(Set.of("supervisors"))
            .build();

    AuthorizationDecision decision =
            workflowTaskPolicy.canCompleteTask(alice, task);

    assertFalse(decision.allowed());
    assertEquals("actor_not_task_assignee_or_candidate", decision.reason());
}
```

# 19. PDP / Entitlement Contract Test

Kalau ada external PDP/entitlement service, contract harus stabil.

## Request Contract

```json
{
  "subject": {
    "id": "user-123",
    "type": "USER",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a"
  },
  "action": "case.approve",
  "resource": {
    "type": "case",
    "id": "case-789",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a"
  },
  "context": {
    "correlationId": "req-abc"
  }
}
```

## Response Contract

```json
{
  "allowed": false,
  "reason": "missing_entitlement",
  "policyName": "tenant-entitlement-policy",
  "policyVersion": "2026.07.1",
  "decisionId": "dec-123"
}
```

## Tests

```text
[ ] Unknown subject returns deny
[ ] Unknown action returns deny
[ ] Unknown resource type returns deny
[ ] Missing tenant returns deny
[ ] Response always contains allowed
[ ] Response always contains reason
[ ] Response always contains policyName
[ ] Response always contains policyVersion
[ ] Timeout behavior is handled by client
[ ] 5xx behavior is handled by client
```

---

# 20. PDP Failure Mode Test

Sensitive action must fail-closed.

```java
@Test
void shouldFailClosedWhenPdpTimesOutForSensitiveAction() {
    PdpClient pdpClient = request -> {
        throw new PdpTimeoutException("timeout");
    };

    AuthorizationDecisionService service =
            new AuthorizationDecisionService(
                    pdpClient,
                    new FailClosedPdpFailureHandler()
            );

    AuthorizationRequest request = AuthorizationRequestFixture
            .sensitiveAction("case.approve")
            .build();

    AuthorizationDecision decision = service.evaluate(request);

    assertFalse(decision.allowed());
    assertEquals("pdp_timeout_fail_closed", decision.reason());
}
```

Partial batch failure:

```java
@Test
void batchCheckShouldDenyItemsWithFailedPdpDecision() {
    BatchAuthorizationResult result = authorizationService.batchCheck(
            BatchAuthorizationRequestFixture.withThreeObjects()
                    .pdpFailsFor("CASE-2")
                    .build()
    );

    assertTrue(result.decisionFor("CASE-1").allowed());
    assertFalse(result.decisionFor("CASE-2").allowed());
    assertEquals("pdp_partial_failure_fail_closed", result.decisionFor("CASE-2").reason());
}
```

# 21. Cache Regression Test

Authorization cache bisa menyebabkan stale allow.

## Permission Revocation Test

```java
@Test
void shouldNotAllowSensitiveActionAfterPermissionRevoked() {
    AuthenticatedPrincipal bob = PrincipalFixture.bob()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .permission("case.approve")
            .build();

    String caseId = seed.submittedCase("tenant-a").createdBy("alice").build().id();

    assertTrue(caseService.canApprove(bob, caseId).allowed());

    entitlementStore.revokePermission("tenant-a", "bob", "case.approve");
    authorizationCache.invalidateSubject("tenant-a", "bob");

    AuthorizationDecision decision = caseService.canApprove(bob.withoutPermission("case.approve"), caseId);

    assertFalse(decision.allowed());
    assertEquals("missing_case_approve_permission", decision.reason());
}
```

## Cache Key Test

```java
@Test
void cachedCaseResponseShouldNotBeSharedAcrossTenants() {
    String caseId = "CASE-123";

    cache.put("case:tenant-a:" + caseId, CaseDtoFixture.tenantA());
    cache.put("case:tenant-b:" + caseId, CaseDtoFixture.tenantB());

    assertEquals("tenant-a", cache.get("case:tenant-a:" + caseId).tenantId());
    assertEquals("tenant-b", cache.get("case:tenant-b:" + caseId).tenantId());
}
```

**Anti-test smell:** cache key hanya `case:{caseId}`.

# 22. Audit Verification Test

Audit bukan optional untuk sensitive action.

## Allow Audit

```java
@Test
void shouldAuditAllowDecisionForApproveCase() {
    AuthenticatedPrincipal bob = PrincipalFixture.supervisor()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .subject("bob")
            .permission("case.approve")
            .build();

    String caseId = seed.submittedCase("tenant-a")
            .createdBy("alice")
            .build()
            .id();

    caseService.approveCase(bob, caseId);

    AuthorizationAuditEvent event = auditSink.lastEvent();

    assertEquals("AUTHORIZATION_DECISION", event.eventType());
    assertEquals("ALLOW", event.decision());
    assertEquals("case.approve", event.action());
    assertEquals(caseId, event.resourceId());
    assertEquals("bob", event.subjectId());
    assertEquals("tenant-a", event.tenantId());
    assertNotNull(event.policyName());
    assertNotNull(event.policyVersion());
    assertNotNull(event.correlationId());
}
```

## Deny Audit

```java
@Test
void shouldAuditDenyDecisionWithReason() {
    AuthenticatedPrincipal alice = PrincipalFixture.reviewer()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .subject("alice")
            .permission("case.approve")
            .build();

    String caseId = seed.submittedCase("tenant-a")
            .createdBy("alice")
            .build()
            .id();

    assertThrows(ForbiddenException.class, () -> caseService.approveCase(alice, caseId));

    AuthorizationAuditEvent event = auditSink.lastEvent();

    assertEquals("DENY", event.decision());
    assertEquals("maker_checker_violation", event.reason());
    assertEquals("case.approve", event.action());
}
```

---

# 23. OpenAPI / Route Coverage Test

Bug besar: endpoint baru lupa diberi annotation security.

## Rule

```text
Every non-public resource method must have one of:
  - @RequiresPermission
  - @RequiresScope
  - @InternalEndpoint
  - @PublicEndpoint
```

## Reflection Test

```java
@Test
void everyResourceMethodShouldDeclareSecurityClassification() {
    Set<Class<?>> resources = Set.of(
            CaseResource.class,
            OrderResource.class,
            UserAdminResource.class
    );

    List<String> violations = new ArrayList<>();

    for (Class<?> resource : resources) {
        for (Method method : resource.getDeclaredMethods()) {
            if (!isJaxRsEndpoint(method)) {
                continue;
            }

            boolean classified =
                    method.isAnnotationPresent(RequiresPermission.class)
                    || method.isAnnotationPresent(RequiresScope.class)
                    || method.isAnnotationPresent(PublicEndpoint.class)
                    || method.isAnnotationPresent(InternalEndpoint.class)
                    || resource.isAnnotationPresent(RequiresPermission.class);

            if (!classified) {
                violations.add(resource.getSimpleName() + "#" + method.getName());
            }
        }
    }

    assertTrue(
            violations.isEmpty(),
            "Unclassified endpoints: " + violations
    );
}
```

Ini simple tapi sangat efektif untuk mencegah “endpoint public karena lupa”.

---

# 24. Static Query Guard Test

Untuk MyBatis XML, bisa buat test sederhana yang mencari query mutasi tanpa `tenant_id`.

```java
@Test
void updateAndDeleteStatementsShouldContainTenantPredicate() throws IOException {
    List<Path> mapperFiles = Files.walk(Path.of("src/main/resources/mappers"))
            .filter(path -> path.toString().endsWith(".xml"))
            .toList();

    List<String> violations = new ArrayList<>();

    for (Path file : mapperFiles) {
        String xml = Files.readString(file);

        List<String> statements = extractUpdateAndDeleteStatements(xml);

        for (String statement : statements) {
            if (!statement.contains("tenant_id")) {
                violations.add(file + " statement missing tenant_id");
            }
        }
    }

    assertTrue(violations.isEmpty(), String.join("\n", violations));
}
```

Ini bukan pengganti test SQL sebenarnya, tapi bagus sebagai guardrail CI.

---

# 25. Property-Based / Matrix Testing

Authorization rule sering punya kombinasi banyak atribut.

```text
tenant match: yes/no
permission: yes/no
status: DRAFT/SUBMITTED/CLOSED
actor is creator: yes/no
actor approval level >= risk: yes/no
region allowed: yes/no
```

Daripada tulis manual semua, gunakan parameterized test.

```java
@ParameterizedTest
@MethodSource("approvalCases")
void shouldEvaluateApprovalPolicyCorrectly(
        ApprovalPolicyScenario scenario
) {
    AuthorizationDecision decision = policy.canApprove(
            scenario.actor(),
            scenario.caseRecord()
    );

    assertEquals(scenario.expectedAllowed(), decision.allowed());
    assertEquals(scenario.expectedReason(), decision.reason());
}
```

Provider:

```java
static Stream<ApprovalPolicyScenario> approvalCases() {
    return Stream.of(
            scenario("tenant mismatch", false, "tenant_mismatch")
                    .actorTenant("tenant-a")
                    .caseTenant("tenant-b"),

            scenario("missing permission", false, "missing_case_approve_permission")
                    .sameTenant()
                    .withoutPermission("case.approve"),

            scenario("draft case", false, "case_not_submitted")
                    .sameTenant()
                    .permission("case.approve")
                    .caseStatus("DRAFT"),

            scenario("maker checker", false, "maker_checker_violation")
                    .sameTenant()
                    .permission("case.approve")
                    .caseStatus("SUBMITTED")
                    .actorIsCreator(),

            scenario("valid approval", true, "case_approval_allowed")
                    .sameTenant()
                    .permission("case.approve")
                    .caseStatus("SUBMITTED")
                    .actorNotCreator()
    );
}
```

---

# 26. CI/CD Security Regression Gate

Minimal CI gate:

```text
Pull Request must pass:
  - unit policy tests
  - Jersey auth filter integration tests
  - MyBatis tenant/visibility tests
  - route classification test
  - mutation query tenant guard test
  - BOLA/IDOR negative tests
  - PDP failure tests
  - audit verification tests
```

Opsional tapi bagus:

```text
- dependency vulnerability scan
- secret scan
- OpenAPI security scheme validation
- container image scan
- policy bundle validation
- ReBAC model validation
- migration test for permission schema
```

---

# 27. Regression Checklist per PR

Gunakan checklist ini saat review PR yang menyentuh endpoint/resource/mapper/security.

```text
[ ] Endpoint baru punya security classification
[ ] Endpoint dengan object ID punya object-level authorization
[ ] List/search/count pakai visibility criteria
[ ] Mapper SELECT punya tenant predicate bila data tenant-scoped
[ ] UPDATE/DELETE punya tenant_id di WHERE
[ ] State mutation punya expected status/version guard
[ ] Bulk operation authorize per object
[ ] Permission baru masuk permission catalog
[ ] Role mapping diperbarui bila perlu
[ ] Audit event ditambahkan untuk sensitive action
[ ] Negative tests ditambahkan
[ ] PDP/entitlement failure behavior jelas
[ ] Error response tidak membocorkan existence lintas tenant
```

---

# 28. Test Data Builder Pattern

Agar test authorization mudah dibaca, gunakan fixture builder.

```java
public final class PrincipalFixture {

    public static PrincipalBuilder alice() {
        return new PrincipalBuilder()
                .subject("alice")
                .username("alice")
                .tenantId("tenant-a");
    }

    public static PrincipalBuilder supervisor() {
        return new PrincipalBuilder()
                .subject("bob")
                .username("bob")
                .tenantId("tenant-a")
                .permission("case.read.all")
                .permission("case.approve")
                .approvalLevel(3);
    }
}
```

```java
public final class CaseFixture {

    public static CaseBuilder submittedCase() {
        return new CaseBuilder()
                .id(UUID.randomUUID().toString())
                .tenantId("tenant-a")
                .status("SUBMITTED")
                .riskLevel(1)
                .createdBy("alice");
    }

    public static CaseBuilder draftCase() {
        return new CaseBuilder()
                .id(UUID.randomUUID().toString())
                .tenantId("tenant-a")
                .status("DRAFT")
                .riskLevel(1)
                .createdBy("alice");
    }
}
```

Test yang baik harus terbaca seperti skenario bisnis, bukan seperti setup teknis panjang.

---

# 29. Red-Team Style Scenarios

Tambahkan test yang meniru attacker.

## ID Tampering

```text
Login as Alice.
Call GET /cases/{aliceCaseId}.
Then replace path ID with Bob's case ID.
Expected: 403 or 404.
```

## Tenant Tampering

```text
Login as tenant-a user.
Send request body with tenantId = tenant-b.
Expected: ignored or rejected.
```

## Query Filter Tampering

```text
GET /cases?tenantId=tenant-b
Expected:
  server ignores tenantId from query or rejects request.
  tenant comes from principal, not client.
```

## Bulk Tampering

```text
POST /cases/bulk-close with mixed authorized and unauthorized IDs.
Expected:
  only authorized IDs updated.
  unauthorized IDs denied or omitted safely.
```

## Vertical Privilege

```text
Reviewer calls supervisor/admin endpoint.
Expected: 403.
```

## Workflow Tampering

```text
User completes task ID not assigned to them.
Expected: 403.
```

---

# 30. What Not to Test as Primary Signal

| Weak Test | Problem | Better Test |
|---|---|---|
| Only test happy path | Tidak membuktikan security | Add negative matrix |
| Only test role ADMIN | Tidak cover real policy | Test permission/object/state |
| Only test UI visibility | API bisa dipanggil langsung | Test backend endpoint |
| Only mock repository | Query leak tidak terdeteksi | Mapper/integration DB test |
| Only test `403` | Tidak tahu reason | Assert audit reason |
| Only test one tenant | Cross-tenant bug tidak terlihat | Fixture minimal 2 tenants |
| Only test single object | Bulk/list leak tidak terlihat | Test list/count/search/bulk |

---

# 31. Minimal Production Test Suite

Kalau harus ringkas, minimal wajib punya:

```text
1. AuthenticationFilterIT
   - missing/invalid/valid token

2. AuthorizationFilterIT
   - missing permission
   - required permission

3. CaseAccessPolicyTest
   - allow/deny by tenant, permission, status, maker-checker

4. CaseRepositoryTenantIsolationTest
   - find/update cannot cross tenant

5. CaseVisibilityMapperTest
   - list/count/search only visible cases

6. CaseObjectAuthorizationIT
   - ID tampering denied

7. CaseMutationGuardTest
   - status/version/tenant guard

8. EntitlementFailureTest
   - PDP timeout fail-closed

9. AuthorizationAuditTest
   - allow/deny audit emitted

10. ResourceSecurityClassificationTest
   - endpoint cannot be accidentally public
```

---

---

# 33. Minimal Invariants

```text
1. Every endpoint is explicitly classified.
2. Every object ID from client is tested as hostile input.
3. Every tenant-scoped query is tested against cross-tenant data.
4. Every list/search/count test checks unauthorized data is absent.
5. Every mutation test checks tenant/status/version guard.
6. Every sensitive action has allow and deny audit tests.
7. Every PDP dependency has timeout/5xx tests.
8. Every role/permission policy has negative tests.
9. Every bulk endpoint has mixed-authority tests.
10. Every authorization regression must fail CI before release.
```

---

# 34. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Unit policy tests | Semua branch allow/deny tertutup |
| 2 | Jersey filter tests | `SecurityContext`, `401`, `403` benar |
| 3 | Object ID tampering tests | BOLA/IDOR tertutup |
| 4 | Tenant isolation tests | Cross-tenant query/mutation gagal |
| 5 | Visibility mapper tests | List/search/count konsisten |
| 6 | Mutation guard tests | SQL guard status/version/tenant aman |
| 7 | PDP failure tests | Sensitive action fail-closed |
| 8 | Audit tests | Allow/deny punya evidence |
| 9 | Route classification test | Endpoint tidak public tanpa sengaja |
| 10 | CI gate | Regression suite wajib jalan di PR |
