## Response:
7/13/2026, 4:49:24 AM

Kita lanjut ke audit logging dan forensic traceability. Fokusnya: bagaimana mencatat authentication/authorization decision agar berguna untuk investigasi, compliance, dan debugging tanpa membocorkan token/PII berlebihan.

# Cheatsheet - Security Audit Logging & Forensic Traceability untuk Authentication/Authorization di Jersey/JAX-RS Microservices

## 1. Tujuan

Audit logging untuk authentication/authorization bukan sekadar “log error”. Tujuannya adalah membuat sistem bisa menjawab pertanyaan investigatif:

```text
Siapa actor-nya?
Datang dari tenant mana?
Action apa yang diminta?
Resource apa yang disentuh?
Decision-nya allow atau deny?
Rule/policy versi berapa yang dipakai?
Kenapa diizinkan atau ditolak?
Request ini terkait trace/correlation ID apa?
Apakah ada perubahan permission/role/ACL sebelumnya?
```

OWASP Logging Cheat Sheet menekankan bahwa application logging penting untuk security monitoring, incident response, dan forensic analysis, tetapi juga memperingatkan agar log tidak menjadi sumber kebocoran data sensitif baru. OWASP Top 10 2021 A09 juga menyebut login, failed login, dan high-value transactions sebagai event yang perlu diaudit; NIST SP 800-92 memberi panduan praktis untuk log management enterprise. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Logging_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Diagram Audit Traceability

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[HTTP Request] --> B[Correlation ID Filter]
    B --> C[Authentication Filter]
    C --> D{AuthN Decision}

    D -->|Deny| E[AUTHN_DENY Audit]
    D -->|Allow| F[SecurityContext Created]

    F --> G[Authorization Filter]
    G --> H{Endpoint Permission Decision}

    H -->|Deny| I[AUTHZ_DENY Audit]
    H -->|Allow| J[Application Service]

    J --> K[Load Resource with Tenant Boundary]
    K --> L[Object / State / ACL / ABAC Policy]
    L --> M{Object AuthZ Decision}

    M -->|Deny| N[OBJECT_AUTHZ_DENY Audit]
    M -->|Allow| O[Execute Use Case]

    O --> P[Mutation / Query / Workflow Action]
    P --> Q[BUSINESS_ACTION Audit]

    E --> R[(Central Log / SIEM)]
    I --> R
    N --> R
    Q --> R

    R --> S[Investigation / Alerting / Compliance Evidence]
```

---

## 3. Event yang Wajib Diaudit

| Event | Kapan | Level | Wajib Field |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `AUTHENTICATION_SUCCESS` | Token valid dan principal terbentuk | INFO | subject, tenant, issuer, client/app, correlation ID |
| `AUTHENTICATION_FAILURE` | Token missing/invalid/expired/wrong audience | WARN | reason, issuer jika aman, kid jika aman, correlation ID |
| `AUTHORIZATION_ALLOW` | Endpoint/object/action diizinkan | INFO | subject, tenant, action, resource, policy, reason |
| `AUTHORIZATION_DENY` | Permission/object/state/tenant policy menolak | WARN | subject, tenant, action, resource, reason |
| `CROSS_TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED` | Actor mencoba object tenant lain | WARN/HIGH | actor tenant, resource ID, reason, no client disclosure |
| `POLICY_DECISION_FAILURE` | PDP/entitlement/OPA/OpenFGA timeout/error | ERROR | dependency, timeout/error, fallback decision |
| `PERMISSION_CHANGE` | Role/permission/grant berubah | INFO/HIGH | changedBy, target subject, old/new, approval |
| `ACL_CHANGE` | Grant/revoke object ACL | INFO/HIGH | resource, subject, permission, changedBy |
| `ADMIN_ACTION` | User disable, role assign, config change | INFO/HIGH | admin actor, target, reason, approval |
| `HIGH_VALUE_BUSINESS_ACTION` | Approve, reject, close, cancel, delete | INFO/HIGH | actor, object, old/new state, policy decision |
| `BREAK_GLASS_ACCESS` | Emergency access | HIGH/CRITICAL | actor, reason, approval, expiry, accessed resource |

**Rule utama:** log allow/deny untuk sensitive action harus bisa menjadi evidence, bukan hanya debug text.

---

## 4. Audit Event Schema

Gunakan structured logging, bukan string bebas.

```java
public record SecurityAuditEvent(
        String eventType,
        String eventId,
        Instant eventTime,

        String correlationId,
        String traceId,
        String spanId,

        Actor actor,
        Client client,
        RequestInfo request,
        ResourceRef resource,

        String action,
        String decision,
        String reason,

        String policyName,
        String policyVersion,
        String decisionSource,

        Map<String, Object> evidence,
        Map<String, Object> labels
) {
    public record Actor(
            String subject,
            String username,
            String tenantId,
            String actorType,
            String effectiveSubject,
            String impersonatedBy
    ) {}

    public record Client(
            String clientId,
            String clientType,
            String issuer,
            String audience,
            String tokenId
    ) {}

    public record RequestInfo(
            String method,
            String path,
            String sourceIp,
            String userAgent,
            String authScheme
    ) {}

    public record ResourceRef(
            String type,
            String id,
            String tenantId
    ) {}
}
```

Contoh JSON:

```json
{
  "eventType": "AUTHORIZATION_DECISION",
  "eventId": "evt-01J...",
  "eventTime": "2026-07-13T10:15:30.123+07:00",
  "correlationId": "req-8f91",
  "traceId": "4bf92f3577b34da6a3ce929d0e0e4736",
  "actor": {
    "subject": "user-123",
    "username": "alice",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a",
    "actorType": "USER",
    "effectiveSubject": "user-123",
    "impersonatedBy": null
  },
  "client": {
    "clientId": "case-web",
    "clientType": "OIDC_CLIENT",
    "issuer": "https://idp.company.com/realms/internal",
    "audience": "case-api",
    "tokenId": "jti-abc"
  },
  "request": {
    "method": "POST",
    "path": "/cases/CASE-789/approve",
    "sourceIp": "10.1.2.3",
    "userAgent": "Mozilla/5.0",
    "authScheme": "Bearer"
  },
  "resource": {
    "type": "case",
    "id": "CASE-789",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a"
  },
  "action": "case.approve",
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "maker_checker_violation",
  "policyName": "case-approval-policy",
  "policyVersion": "2026.07.1",
  "decisionSource": "LOCAL_DOMAIN_POLICY",
  "evidence": {
    "caseStatus": "SUBMITTED",
    "actorIsCreator": true,
    "tenantMatched": true
  },
  "labels": {
    "risk": "HIGH",
    "domain": "case-management"
  }
}
```

---

## 5. Apa yang Tidak Boleh Dilog

OWASP Logging Cheat Sheet menekankan bahwa data sensitif harus tidak dicatat, dihapus, dimasking, disanitasi, di-hash, atau dienkripsi sesuai kebutuhan; Application Logging Vocabulary Cheat Sheet juga mengingatkan agar log tidak menjadi secondary sensitive-data risk. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Logging_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Jangan Log | Kenapa | Alternatif Aman |
|---|---|---|
| Raw JWT / bearer token | Credential leakage | `jti`, token hash, issuer, kid |
| Refresh token | Credential sangat sensitif | Jangan log |
| Password / OTP / recovery code | Secret | Jangan log |
| API key plaintext | Credential leakage | Prefix + hash |
| Full request body | Bisa berisi PII/secret | Field allowlist |
| Full response body | Bisa berisi PII/data bisnis | Metadata saja |
| Full card/account number | Regulated data | Masked / tokenized ID |
| Full URL dengan query sensitive | Token bisa ada di query | Path + sanitized query keys |
| Stack trace ke client | Info leak | Internal error ID |
| Authorization header | Credential | `authScheme=Bearer` saja |

Pattern aman:

```text
Log identifiers and decisions.
Do not log credentials and unnecessary payload.
```

---

## 6. Correlation ID Filter

Setiap request harus punya correlation ID. Kalau client tidak mengirim, generate di edge/service.

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR)
public class CorrelationIdFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    public static final String HEADER = "X-Correlation-ID";
    public static final String PROPERTY = "correlationId";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        String correlationId = requestContext.getHeaderString(HEADER);

        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        requestContext.setProperty(PROPERTY, correlationId);
        MDC.put("correlationId", correlationId);
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
            ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) {
        Object correlationId = requestContext.getProperty(PROPERTY);

        if (correlationId != null) {
            responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle(HEADER, correlationId.toString());
        }

        MDC.remove("correlationId");
    }
}
```

**Invariant:** semua audit event harus membawa `correlationId`.

---

## 7. Authentication Audit

Authentication filter harus mencatat success/failure secara aman.

```java
public final class AuthenticationAuditLogger {

    private final SecurityAuditSink sink;

    public void logSuccess(
            ContainerRequestContext ctx,
            AuthenticatedPrincipal principal,
            VerifiedAccessToken token
    ) {
        sink.emit(SecurityAuditEventBuilder.authenticationSuccess()
                .correlationId(correlationId(ctx))
                .actorSubject(principal.subject())
                .tenantId(principal.tenantId())
                .issuer(token.issuer())
                .audience(String.join(" ", token.audiences()))
                .tokenId(token.tokenId())
                .authScheme("Bearer")
                .requestMethod(ctx.getMethod())
                .requestPath(ctx.getUriInfo().getPath())
                .build());
    }

    public void logFailure(
            ContainerRequestContext ctx,
            String reason,
            String safeIssuer,
            String safeKid
    ) {
        sink.emit(SecurityAuditEventBuilder.authenticationFailure()
                .correlationId(correlationId(ctx))
                .decision("DENY")
                .reason(reason)
                .issuer(safeIssuer)
                .keyId(safeKid)
                .authScheme("Bearer")
                .requestMethod(ctx.getMethod())
                .requestPath(ctx.getUriInfo().getPath())
                .build());
    }

    private String correlationId(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        Object value = ctx.getProperty(CorrelationIdFilter.PROPERTY);
        return value == null ? null : value.toString();
    }
}
```

Failure reasons yang baik:

```text
missing_authorization_header
invalid_authorization_scheme
malformed_token
token_expired
invalid_signature
invalid_issuer
invalid_audience
unknown_key_id
missing_tenant
wrong_token_type
```

Jangan log raw token untuk membedakan error.

---

## 8. Authorization Audit di Filter

Endpoint-level deny perlu audit.

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public class AuthorizationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Context
    private ResourceInfo resourceInfo;

    private final AuthorizationService authorizationService;
    private final SecurityAuditLogger auditLogger;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        RequiresPermission required = resolvePermission(resourceInfo);

        if (required == null) {
            return;
        }

        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor =
                (AuthenticatedPrincipal) ctx.getSecurityContext().getUserPrincipal();

        AuthorizationDecision decision =
                authorizationService.hasPermission(actor, required.value());

        auditLogger.logEndpointDecision(
                ctx,
                actor,
                required.value(),
                decision
        );

        if (!decision.allowed()) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
        }
    }
}
```

**Catatan:** untuk high-volume read endpoint, audit allow bisa sampled atau diturunkan levelnya, tetapi deny dan sensitive actions jangan di-skip.

---

## 9. Authorization Audit di Application Service

Object-level decision lebih penting daripada endpoint decision karena berisi konteks domain.

```java
public void approveCase(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor, String caseId) {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findByIdForTenant(
            caseId,
            actor.tenantId()
    ).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Case not found"));

    AuthorizationDecision decision =
            casePolicy.canApprove(actor, caseRecord);

    auditLogger.logObjectAuthorizationDecision(
            actor,
            "case.approve",
            "case",
            caseId,
            caseRecord.tenantId(),
            decision,
            Map.of(
                    "caseStatus", caseRecord.status(),
                    "riskLevel", caseRecord.riskLevel(),
                    "createdBy", hashOrInternalId(caseRecord.createdBy()),
                    "actorIsCreator", actor.subject().equals(caseRecord.createdBy())
            )
    );

    if (!decision.allowed()) {
        throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
    }

    int updated = caseRepository.approveIfStillEligible(
            caseId,
            actor.tenantId(),
            actor.subject(),
            caseRecord.version()
    );

    if (updated == 0) {
        throw new ConflictException("Case state changed");
    }

    auditLogger.logBusinessAction(
            actor,
            "case.approved",
            "case",
            caseId,
            Map.of(
                    "oldStatus", "SUBMITTED",
                    "newStatus", "APPROVED"
            )
    );
}
```

---

## 10. Audit Decision vs Business Action

Pisahkan dua jenis event:

| Event | Arti | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Authorization decision | Sistem memutuskan allow/deny | `AUTHORIZATION_DECISION allow case.approve` |
| Business action | Aksi benar-benar terjadi | `CASE_APPROVED` |

Kenapa dipisah?

```text
Authorization allow tidak selalu berarti action berhasil.
Action bisa gagal karena conflict, DB error, timeout, atau optimistic lock.
```

Contoh urutan:

```text
AUTHORIZATION_DECISION ALLOW case.approve
BUSINESS_ACTION CASE_APPROVED
```

Atau:

```text
AUTHORIZATION_DECISION ALLOW case.approve
BUSINESS_ACTION_FAILED CASE_APPROVE_CONFLICT
```

---

## 11. Audit untuk 401 vs 403 vs 404

| Response | Audit Event | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `401` | `AUTHENTICATION_FAILURE` | Credential/token gagal |
| `403` | `AUTHORIZATION_DENY` | Authenticated tapi tidak authorized |
| `404` karena object tidak ada | `RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND` opsional | Jangan noisy |
| `404` untuk cross-tenant concealment | `CROSS_TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED` internal | Jangan bocorkan ke client |
| `409` state conflict | `BUSINESS_CONFLICT` | Bisa jadi race/state issue |

Pattern cross-tenant concealment:

```java
Optional<CaseRecord> caseRecord = caseRepository.findById(caseId);

if (caseRecord.isPresent()
        && !caseRecord.get().tenantId().equals(actor.tenantId())) {
    auditLogger.logCrossTenantAccessDenied(actor, "case", caseId);
    throw new NotFoundException("Case not found");
}
```

**Caveat:** query `findById` tanpa tenant hanya boleh dipakai bila memang diperlukan untuk audit/internal check dan dijaga ketat. Pattern lebih umum adalah `findByIdForTenant` lalu return `404`.

---

## 12. Permission / Role / ACL Change Audit

Authorization decision tidak cukup. Perubahan policy juga harus diaudit.

```java
public record PolicyChangeAuditEvent(
        String eventType,
        String changedBy,
        String tenantId,
        String targetSubjectType,
        String targetSubjectId,
        String changeType,
        String resourceType,
        String resourceId,
        String permission,
        Object oldValue,
        Object newValue,
        String reason,
        String approvalReference,
        Instant effectiveAt,
        String correlationId
) {}
```

Contoh:

```json
{
  "eventType": "ACL_GRANT_CREATED",
  "changedBy": "admin-777",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "targetSubjectType": "USER",
  "targetSubjectId": "user-123",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "CASE-789",
  "permission": "read",
  "oldValue": null,
  "newValue": "GRANTED",
  "reason": "temporary investigation support",
  "approvalReference": "APPROVAL-456",
  "effectiveAt": "2026-07-13T10:30:00+07:00",
  "correlationId": "req-admin-1"
}
```

Policy change audit harus bisa menjawab:

```text
Kenapa user ini tiba-tiba bisa akses object itu?
Siapa yang memberi akses?
Kapan akses diberikan?
Kapan akses dicabut?
Apakah ada approval?
```

## 13. Impersonation dan Delegation Audit

Untuk support/admin operation, bedakan **real actor** dan **effective actor**.

```json
{
  "eventType": "AUTHORIZATION_DECISION",
  "actor": {
    "subject": "support-admin-1",
    "effectiveSubject": "user-123",
    "impersonatedBy": "support-admin-1",
    "tenantId": "tenant-a",
    "actorType": "SUPPORT_ADMIN"
  },
  "action": "case.read",
  "resource": {
    "type": "case",
    "id": "CASE-789"
  },
  "decision": "ALLOW",
  "reason": "approved_support_impersonation",
  "evidence": {
    "ticketId": "SUP-12345",
    "approvalId": "APP-777",
    "expiresAt": "2026-07-13T11:00:00+07:00"
  }
}
```

**Invariant:**

```text
Jangan overwrite actor asli dengan effective user.
Audit harus menyimpan keduanya.
```

## 14. Break-Glass Audit

Emergency access harus sangat ketat.

Minimum fields:

```text
actorSubject
tenantId
reason
ticketId / incidentId
approvalReference
resourceType/resourceId
accessStart
accessEnd
actionsPerformed
reviewRequired
reviewedBy
reviewedAt
```

Contoh:

```json
{
  "eventType": "BREAK_GLASS_ACCESS_USED",
  "decision": "ALLOW",
  "actorSubject": "sre-999",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "action": "case.read.sensitive",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "CASE-789",
  "reason": "production incident investigation",
  "incidentId": "INC-2026-0713-001",
  "approvalReference": "APP-2026-0713-009",
  "expiresAt": "2026-07-13T12:00:00+07:00",
  "reviewRequired": true
}
```

Break-glass tanpa audit hampir selalu menjadi compliance risk.

---

## 15. Log Level Strategy

| Level | Dipakai Untuk |
|---|---|
| DEBUG | Local development, detail non-sensitive |
| INFO | Auth success, authz allow, business action |
| WARN | Auth failure, authz deny, suspicious access |
| ERROR | PDP failure, audit sink failure, unexpected security exception |
| CRITICAL/ALERT | Break-glass abuse, repeated cross-tenant attempts, policy bypass suspicion |

Jangan menjadikan semua deny sebagai ERROR. Banyak deny normal, misalnya user tanpa permission. Namun repeated deny, cross-tenant attempt, atau sensitive action deny bisa dinaikkan severity.

---

## 16. Audit Sink Design

```java
public interface SecurityAuditSink {
    void emit(SecurityAuditEvent event);
}
```

Implementasi:

```text
Console/logback JSON appender
Kafka topic
Database audit table
SIEM collector
Cloud logging
```

Pattern aman:

```java
public final class ResilientSecurityAuditLogger {

    private final SecurityAuditSink sink;
    private final Logger fallbackLogger;

    public void emit(SecurityAuditEvent event) {
        try {
            sink.emit(event);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            fallbackLogger.error(
                    "SECURITY_AUDIT_EMIT_FAILED eventType={} eventId={} correlationId={}",
                    event.eventType(),
                    event.eventId(),
                    event.correlationId(),
                    e
            );

            if (isSensitiveMandatoryEvent(event)) {
                // Depending on policy, either fail request or trigger local fallback queue.
                throw new AuditWriteFailedException(event.eventId());
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Untuk high-assurance systems, aksi sensitif bisa dianggap gagal bila audit mandatory tidak bisa ditulis. Untuk sistem lain, gunakan local durable queue/outbox agar request tidak langsung gagal tetapi evidence tetap tidak hilang.

---

## 17. Audit Outbox Pattern

Untuk memastikan audit tidak hilang saat sink eksternal down:

```sql
CREATE TABLE security_audit_outbox (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    event_type      TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_time      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       TEXT NULL,
    subject_id      TEXT NULL,
    resource_type   TEXT NULL,
    resource_id     TEXT NULL,
    payload         JSONB NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    retry_count     INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    next_retry_at   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_security_audit_outbox_pending
ON security_audit_outbox (status, next_retry_at);
```

Flow:

```text
Sensitive business transaction
  ↓
Write domain change + audit outbox in same DB transaction
  ↓
Background publisher sends to Kafka/SIEM
  ↓
Mark as SENT
```

Cocok untuk action seperti approve/reject/close/cancel.

---

## 18. PostgreSQL Audit Table

Kalau audit perlu query investigatif langsung:

```sql
CREATE TABLE security_audit_event (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    event_time      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    event_type      TEXT NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       TEXT NULL,
    subject_id      TEXT NULL,
    actor_type      TEXT NULL,
    action          TEXT NULL,
    resource_type   TEXT NULL,
    resource_id     TEXT NULL,
    decision        TEXT NULL,
    reason          TEXT NULL,
    policy_name     TEXT NULL,
    policy_version  TEXT NULL,
    correlation_id  TEXT NULL,
    trace_id        TEXT NULL,
    source_ip_hash  TEXT NULL,
    payload         JSONB NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX idx_audit_tenant_time
ON security_audit_event (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_audit_subject_time
ON security_audit_event (subject_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_audit_resource_time
ON security_audit_event (resource_type, resource_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_audit_correlation
ON security_audit_event (correlation_id);
```

**Catatan:** untuk PII seperti IP address, pertimbangkan hashing/masking sesuai kebijakan perusahaan dan regulasi.

---

## 19. Structured Logging dengan Logback

Contoh log JSON konseptual:

```json
{
  "timestamp": "2026-07-13T10:15:30.123+07:00",
  "level": "WARN",
  "logger": "security.audit",
  "eventType": "AUTHORIZATION_DECISION",
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "missing_case_approve_permission",
  "subjectId": "user-123",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "action": "case.approve",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "CASE-789",
  "policyName": "case-approval-policy",
  "policyVersion": "2026.07.1",
  "correlationId": "req-abc"
}
```

Di Java:

```java
private static final Logger SECURITY_AUDIT =
        LoggerFactory.getLogger("security.audit");

public void logAuthorizationDecision(SecurityAuditEvent event) {
    SECURITY_AUDIT.info(
            "eventType={} decision={} reason={} subjectId={} tenantId={} action={} resourceType={} resourceId={} policyName={} policyVersion={} correlationId={}",
            event.eventType(),
            event.decision(),
            event.reason(),
            event.actor().subject(),
            event.actor().tenantId(),
            event.action(),
            event.resource().type(),
            event.resource().id(),
            event.policyName(),
            event.policyVersion(),
            event.correlationId()
    );
}
```

Lebih ideal: gunakan JSON encoder agar field menjadi structured, bukan string parsing.

---

## 20. Log Injection Defense

Log injection terjadi saat input user memasukkan newline/control characters sehingga log terlihat seperti event palsu.

Sanitizer sederhana:

```java
public final class LogSanitizer {

    private LogSanitizer() {}

    public static String safe(String value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }

        return value
                .replace("\r", "\\r")
                .replace("\n", "\\n")
                .replace("\t", "\\t");
    }
}
```

Untuk structured logging JSON, encoder biasanya membantu escaping, tetapi tetap hindari memasukkan payload mentah dari user ke message string.

---

## 21. PII Minimization

Audit perlu cukup detail untuk investigasi, tetapi tidak perlu menyimpan semua data.

| Data | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| `subjectId` internal | Boleh |
| username/email | Hati-hati, mask bila perlu |
| IP address | Hash/mask jika policy privacy menuntut |
| user agent | Boleh tapi panjang dibatasi |
| request body | Jangan log penuh |
| resource ID internal | Boleh |
| business payload | Field allowlist |
| token claims lengkap | Jangan |
| permission list lengkap | Hati-hati; log effective permission hanya bila perlu |
| denial reason | Boleh di audit internal, jangan selalu expose ke client |

Prinsip:

```text
Collect enough evidence.
Avoid collecting everything.
```

---

## 22. Audit untuk MyBatis / Repository Mutation

Untuk state-changing SQL, audit sebelum dan sesudah mutation.

```java
public void cancelOrder(AuthenticatedPrincipal actor, String orderId) {
    OrderRecord before = orderRepository.findByIdForTenant(
            orderId,
            actor.tenantId()
    ).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Order not found"));

    AuthorizationDecision decision =
            orderPolicy.canCancel(actor, before);

    auditLogger.logAuthorizationDecision(actor, "order.cancel", before, decision);

    if (!decision.allowed()) {
        throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
    }

    int updated = orderRepository.cancelIfStillCancellable(
            orderId,
            actor.tenantId(),
            before.version(),
            actor.subject()
    );

    if (updated == 0) {
        auditLogger.logBusinessActionFailed(
                actor,
                "order.cancel",
                "order",
                orderId,
                "state_changed_or_conflict"
        );
        throw new ConflictException("Order state changed");
    }

    auditLogger.logBusinessAction(
            actor,
            "order.cancelled",
            "order",
            orderId,
            Map.of(
                    "oldStatus", before.status(),
                    "newStatus", "CANCELLED"
            )
    );
}
```

Audit harus membedakan:

```text
Denied karena policy
Failed karena conflict
Failed karena DB/system error
Succeeded
```

---

## 23. Audit untuk PDP/OPA/OpenFGA Failure

```json
{
  "eventType": "POLICY_DECISION_FAILURE",
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "pdp_timeout_fail_closed",
  "decisionSource": "ENTITLEMENT_SERVICE",
  "action": "case.approve",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "CASE-789",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "subjectId": "user-123",
  "evidence": {
    "dependency": "entitlement-service",
    "timeoutMs": 500,
    "fallbackMode": "FAIL_CLOSED",
    "cacheUsed": false
  },
  "correlationId": "req-abc"
}
```

Wajib dicatat karena PDP failure bisa terlihat seperti “user tidak boleh”, padahal root cause-nya dependency outage.

---

## 24. Metrics dari Audit

Audit log untuk forensic, metrics untuk operasi harian.

```text
security_authn_success_total
security_authn_failure_total{reason}
security_authz_allow_total{action,resource_type}
security_authz_deny_total{action,resource_type,reason}
security_cross_tenant_denied_total
security_policy_failure_total{source,reason}
security_break_glass_used_total
security_admin_policy_change_total{change_type}
security_audit_emit_failure_total
```

Alert contoh:

```text
invalid_audience spike
unknown_kid spike
cross_tenant_denied_total > baseline
pdp_timeout_fail_closed spike
break_glass_used_total > 0
audit_emit_failure_total > 0
admin role assignment outside business hours
```

---

## 25. Forensic Query Examples

### Siapa mengakses case tertentu?

```sql
SELECT
    event_time,
    event_type,
    subject_id,
    action,
    decision,
    reason,
    policy_name,
    policy_version,
    correlation_id
FROM security_audit_event
WHERE resource_type = 'case'
  AND resource_id = 'CASE-789'
ORDER BY event_time DESC;
```

### Kenapa user ditolak?

```sql
SELECT
    event_time,
    action,
    resource_type,
    resource_id,
    decision,
    reason,
    payload -> 'evidence' AS evidence
FROM security_audit_event
WHERE subject_id = 'user-123'
  AND tenant_id = 'tenant-a'
  AND decision = 'DENY'
ORDER BY event_time DESC;
```

### Permission apa yang berubah sebelum incident?

```sql
SELECT
    event_time,
    event_type,
    subject_id,
    action,
    payload
FROM security_audit_event
WHERE event_type IN (
    'ROLE_ASSIGNED',
    'ROLE_REMOVED',
    'PERMISSION_GRANTED',
    'PERMISSION_REVOKED',
    'ACL_GRANT_CREATED',
    'ACL_GRANT_REVOKED'
)
  AND tenant_id = 'tenant-a'
  AND event_time BETWEEN :incidentStart - interval '24 hours'
                      AND :incidentEnd
ORDER BY event_time;
```

### Ada cross-tenant attempt?

```sql
SELECT
    event_time,
    subject_id,
    tenant_id,
    resource_type,
    resource_id,
    reason,
    correlation_id
FROM security_audit_event
WHERE event_type = 'CROSS_TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED'
ORDER BY event_time DESC;
```

---

## 26. Retention dan Tamper Resistance

NIST SP 800-92 menekankan pentingnya log management lifecycle: generation, transmission, storage, analysis, dan disposal. Untuk audit authorization, retention harus disesuaikan dengan regulasi, risiko bisnis, dan kebutuhan investigasi. ([NIST Computer Security Resource Center](https://csrc.nist.gov/pubs/sp/800/92/final?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Concern | Praktik |
|---|---|
| Retention | Sesuaikan regulasi/internal policy |
| Integrity | Append-only storage, WORM, object lock, hash chain bila perlu |
| Access control | Audit log hanya bisa diakses role tertentu |
| Segregation of duties | Admin aplikasi tidak otomatis bisa menghapus audit |
| Time sync | NTP/clock sync wajib |
| Export | SIEM/Kafka/log platform |
| Deletion | Sesuai legal/privacy retention policy |
| Encryption | At rest dan in transit |

Untuk sistem regulatory/high-assurance, audit log harus diperlakukan sebagai data sensitif juga.

---

## 27. Testing Audit

Audit harus dites, bukan diasumsikan.

```text
[ ] Authentication failure emits audit event
[ ] Authentication success emits audit event
[ ] Authorization deny emits reason
[ ] Authorization allow for sensitive action emits audit event
[ ] Business action success emits old/new state
[ ] Business action conflict emits failed event
[ ] PDP timeout emits policy failure event
[ ] Cross-tenant access emits internal audit
[ ] Raw token never appears in audit payload
[ ] Correlation ID exists in every audit event
[ ] Policy version exists in every authz event
[ ] Impersonation stores real actor and effective actor
[ ] Audit sink failure is handled according to policy
```

Contoh test:

```java
@Test
void shouldNotLogRawBearerTokenOnAuthenticationFailure() {
    String rawToken = "eyJhbGciOiJSUzI1NiJ9.secret.payload";

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-1")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + rawToken)
            .get();

    assertEquals(401, response.getStatus());

    SecurityAuditEvent event = auditSink.lastEvent();

    assertEquals("AUTHENTICATION_FAILURE", event.eventType());
    assertFalse(event.toString().contains(rawToken));
}
```

---

## 28. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Log raw JWT | Credential leakage | Log `jti`, `kid`, issuer |
| Log full request body | PII/secret leakage | Field allowlist |
| Audit hanya deny, tidak allow | Tidak tahu aksi legitimate | Audit allow untuk sensitive action |
| Audit tanpa reason | Tidak berguna untuk forensic | `reason` wajib |
| Audit tanpa policy version | Tidak tahu rule yang berlaku | `policyVersion` wajib |
| Audit hanya di gateway | Object decision hilang | Audit di service layer |
| Log string bebas | Sulit query/alert | Structured logging |
| Semua audit async tanpa fallback | Event bisa hilang | Outbox/fallback |
| Tidak log policy change | Tidak tahu siapa memberi akses | Audit role/ACL/permission change |
| Tidak test audit | Regressions diam-diam | Audit verification tests |

---

## 29. Minimal Production Invariants

```text
1. Every security audit event has correlationId.
2. Every authz decision has actor, tenant, action, resource, decision, reason.
3. Sensitive allow and all deny decisions are audited.
4. Raw token, password, API key, and refresh token are never logged.
5. Cross-tenant denies are logged internally but not exposed to clients.
6. Policy changes are audited with old/new value and changedBy.
7. Impersonation stores real actor and effective actor.
8. PDP failure logs fallback mode and final decision.
9. Audit logs are structured and queryable.
10. Audit sink failure behavior is explicitly defined.
```

---

## 30. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Structured audit event schema | Field standar untuk authn/authz/business action |
| 2 | Correlation ID | Semua event bisa ditrace lintas service |
| 3 | Authentication audit | Success/failure aman tanpa raw token |
| 4 | Authorization audit | Allow/deny punya reason dan policy version |
| 5 | Business action audit | Old/new state dan actor tercatat |
| 6 | Policy change audit | Role/permission/ACL changes defensible |
| 7 | Impersonation/break-glass audit | Real actor dan effective actor tidak hilang |
| 8 | Audit sink/outbox | Event tidak hilang saat sink down |
| 9 | Metrics & alerting | Deny spike, PDP failure, cross-tenant attempt terlihat |
| 10 | Audit tests | Regression tidak lolos CI |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 4:52:35 AM

# Cheatsheet - Public/Internal/Admin Endpoint Classification & Security Boundary di Jersey/JAX-RS

## 1. Tujuan

Cheatsheet ini fokus ke **klasifikasi endpoint dan boundary enforcement** dalam microservice Jersey/JAX-RS.

Masalah yang ingin dicegah:

```text
- Endpoint baru tidak sengaja public.
- Endpoint internal bisa dipanggil dari internet.
- Endpoint admin hanya dilindungi role kasar.
- Service-to-service endpoint menerima user token biasa.
- Gateway dianggap cukup, padahal service layer tetap bisa dipanggil dari network internal.
- Health/readiness/metrics terlalu banyak membocorkan informasi.
```

OWASP REST Security Cheat Sheet menegaskan bahwa REST service yang aman harus hanya menyediakan endpoint HTTPS, dan non-public REST service harus menjalankan access control pada setiap endpoint. OWASP juga menyarankan mutual client-side certificates untuk web service yang highly privileged. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/REST_Security_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Endpoint Taxonomy

Endpoint harus diklasifikasikan eksplisit. Jangan biarkan default-nya “siapa saja bisa akses”.

| Endpoint Type | Contoh | Caller | Security Boundary |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Public** | `/health/live`, public metadata terbatas | Load balancer, public client | No auth atau minimal auth |
| **Authenticated User** | `/me`, `/my/tasks` | User login | Valid user access token |
| **Domain Protected** | `/cases/{id}`, `/orders/{id}` | User/service dengan business permission | Scope + permission + object auth |
| **Admin** | `/admin/users/{id}/disable`, `/admin/roles` | Admin/operator | Admin permission + MFA/step-up + audit |
| **Internal Service** | `/internal/retry`, `/internal/reindex` | Service account | Service identity + mTLS/scope/network policy |
| **Partner API** | `/partner/orders`, `/partner/cases` | External client/app | Client credential, mTLS/API gateway, tenant mapping |
| **Operations** | `/metrics`, `/debug`, `/actuator` | SRE/platform | Network restricted + auth |
| **Break-glass** | `/emergency/access` | Approved emergency operator | Strong approval + reason + expiry + audit |
| **Webhook Callback** | `/webhooks/payment`, `/callbacks/*` | External system | Signature verification + replay protection |

---

# 3. Diagram Security Boundary

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Internet / Client / Partner] --> B[Ingress / API Gateway / WAF]
    B --> C{Route Classification}

    C -->|Public| D[Public Endpoint Guard]
    C -->|User API| E[JWT/OIDC AuthN]
    C -->|Partner API| F[Client Credential / mTLS / Signature]
    C -->|Internal| G[Network Policy + mTLS + Service Token]
    C -->|Admin| H[Admin AuthZ + Step-up + Audit]
    C -->|Ops| I[Restricted Network + Auth]

    D --> J[Jersey Resource]
    E --> K[Jersey Authentication Filter]
    F --> K
    G --> K
    H --> K
    I --> K

    K --> L[Jersey Authorization Filter]
    L --> M[Application Service Policy]
    M --> N[Object / Tenant / Workflow Authorization]
    N --> O[MyBatis / PostgreSQL Guard]
    O --> P[(Database)]

    L --> Q[Security Audit]
    M --> Q
    N --> Q
```

---

# 4. Core Rule

```text
Every endpoint must be classified.

Allowed classifications:
  - @PublicEndpoint
  - @RequiresAuthentication
  - @RequiresPermission
  - @RequiresScope
  - @InternalEndpoint
  - @AdminEndpoint
  - @PartnerEndpoint
  - @OperationsEndpoint

No classification = build/test failure.
```

Jakarta REST menyediakan `ContainerRequestFilter` untuk memproses request sebelum resource method; filter dapat berjalan pre-match atau post-match dan bisa menghentikan request dengan `abortWith`. Ini membuat filter cocok sebagai enforcement point untuk authentication, route classification, dan authorization coarse-grained di Jersey/JAX-RS. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.1/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/container/containerrequestfilter?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Annotation Strategy

## Endpoint Classification Annotation

```java
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({TYPE, METHOD})
public @interface PublicEndpoint {
    String reason();
}
```

```java
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({TYPE, METHOD})
public @interface InternalEndpoint {
    String[] allowedServices() default {};
    boolean requireMtls() default true;
}
```

```java
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({TYPE, METHOD})
public @interface AdminEndpoint {
    String[] permissions();
    boolean requireStepUp() default true;
    String risk() default "HIGH";
}
```

```java
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({TYPE, METHOD})
public @interface PartnerEndpoint {
    String[] allowedClients() default {};
    boolean requireClientCredential() default true;
    boolean requireReplayProtection() default true;
}
```

```java
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({TYPE, METHOD})
public @interface OperationsEndpoint {
    String[] allowedRoles() default {"SRE", "PLATFORM_ADMIN"};
    boolean internalNetworkOnly() default true;
}
```

---

# 6. Example Resource Classification

```java
@Path("/health")
@PublicEndpoint(reason = "Kubernetes liveness/readiness probes")
public class HealthResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/live")
    public Response live() {
        return Response.ok(Map.of("status", "UP")).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/ready")
    public Response ready() {
        return Response.ok(Map.of("status", "READY")).build();
    }
}
```

```java
@Path("/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    @RequiresPermission("case.read")
    public Response getCase(
            @Context SecurityContext securityContext,
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId
    ) {
        return Response.ok().build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{caseId}/approve")
    @RequiresPermission("case.approve")
    public Response approveCase(
            @Context SecurityContext securityContext,
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId
    ) {
        return Response.noContent().build();
    }
}
```

```java
@Path("/internal/cases")
@InternalEndpoint(
        allowedServices = {"case-worker-service", "reconciliation-service"},
        requireMtls = true
)
public class InternalCaseResource {

    @POST
    @Path("/{caseId}/reindex")
    public Response reindexCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        return Response.accepted().build();
    }
}
```

```java
@Path("/admin/users")
@AdminEndpoint(
        permissions = {"admin.user.manage"},
        requireStepUp = true,
        risk = "HIGH"
)
public class UserAdminResource {

    @POST
    @Path("/{userId}/disable")
    public Response disableUser(@PathParam("userId") String userId) {
        return Response.noContent().build();
    }
}
```

---

# 7. Classification Enforcement Filter

Filter ini mencegah endpoint tanpa klasifikasi.

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION - 100)
public class EndpointClassificationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Context
    private ResourceInfo resourceInfo;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        Method method = resourceInfo.getResourceMethod();
        Class<?> clazz = resourceInfo.getResourceClass();

        EndpointClassification classification =
                EndpointClassificationResolver.resolve(clazz, method);

        if (classification == EndpointClassification.UNCLASSIFIED) {
            ctx.abortWith(Response.status(Response.Status.FORBIDDEN)
                    .entity(Map.of(
                            "code", "ENDPOINT_UNCLASSIFIED",
                            "message", "Endpoint is not classified"
                    ))
                    .build());
        }

        ctx.setProperty("endpointClassification", classification.name());
    }
}
```

Resolver:

```java
public final class EndpointClassificationResolver {

    private EndpointClassificationResolver() {}

    public static EndpointClassification resolve(Class<?> clazz, Method method) {
        if (has(method, PublicEndpoint.class) || has(clazz, PublicEndpoint.class)) {
            return EndpointClassification.PUBLIC;
        }

        if (has(method, InternalEndpoint.class) || has(clazz, InternalEndpoint.class)) {
            return EndpointClassification.INTERNAL;
        }

        if (has(method, AdminEndpoint.class) || has(clazz, AdminEndpoint.class)) {
            return EndpointClassification.ADMIN;
        }

        if (has(method, PartnerEndpoint.class) || has(clazz, PartnerEndpoint.class)) {
            return EndpointClassification.PARTNER;
        }

        if (has(method, OperationsEndpoint.class) || has(clazz, OperationsEndpoint.class)) {
            return EndpointClassification.OPERATIONS;
        }

        if (has(method, RequiresPermission.class) || has(clazz, RequiresPermission.class)) {
            return EndpointClassification.DOMAIN_PROTECTED;
        }

        if (has(method, RequiresScope.class) || has(clazz, RequiresScope.class)) {
            return EndpointClassification.AUTHENTICATED;
        }

        return EndpointClassification.UNCLASSIFIED;
    }

    private static boolean has(AnnotatedElement element, Class<? extends Annotation> annotation) {
        return element.isAnnotationPresent(annotation);
    }
}
```

Enum:

```java
public enum EndpointClassification {
    PUBLIC,
    AUTHENTICATED,
    DOMAIN_PROTECTED,
    ADMIN,
    INTERNAL,
    PARTNER,
    OPERATIONS,
    BREAK_GLASS,
    UNCLASSIFIED
}
```

---

# 8. Public Endpoint Rules

Public endpoint bukan berarti bebas menampilkan detail.

| Public Endpoint | Boleh | Jangan |
|---|---|---|
| `/health/live` | `{"status":"UP"}` | DB detail, build secret, dependency list |
| `/health/ready` | `{"status":"READY"}` | Connection string, internal hostnames |
| `/version` | build version terbatas | Git token, env vars |
| `/public/metadata` | public config | tenant list, internal feature flags |
| public docs | sanitized OpenAPI | internal/admin endpoints |

Pattern:

```java
@GET
@Path("/live")
@PublicEndpoint(reason = "Liveness probe")
public Response live() {
    return Response.ok(Map.of("status", "UP")).build();
}
```

Jangan:

```json
{
  "status": "UP",
  "database": "postgres://user:password@db.internal:5432/app",
  "jwtIssuer": "https://idp.internal/realms/prod",
  "activeProfiles": ["prod", "debug-auth"]
}
```

---

# 9. Authenticated User Endpoint

Authenticated endpoint hanya butuh valid identity, tetapi belum tentu permission granular.

Contoh:

```java
@Path("/me")
public class MeResource {

    @GET
    @RequiresScope("profile:read")
    public Response me(@Context SecurityContext securityContext) {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor =
                (AuthenticatedPrincipal) securityContext.getUserPrincipal();

        return Response.ok(Map.of(
                "subject", actor.subject(),
                "username", actor.username(),
                "tenantId", actor.tenantId()
        )).build();
    }
}
```

Gunakan untuk endpoint seperti:

```text
/me
/me/preferences
/me/sessions
/me/tasks
/me/notifications
```

Tapi hati-hati: `/me/tasks/{taskId}` tetap butuh object-level authorization.

---

# 10. Domain-Protected Endpoint

Endpoint bisnis umumnya butuh:

```text
1. Valid token.
2. Correct OAuth scope.
3. Required permission.
4. Tenant boundary.
5. Object-level authorization.
6. State/workflow guard.
7. Audit.
```

Contoh:

```java
@POST
@Path("/cases/{caseId}/approve")
@RequiresPermission("case.approve")
public Response approve(
        @Context SecurityContext securityContext,
        @PathParam("caseId") String caseId
) {
    AuthenticatedPrincipal actor =
            (AuthenticatedPrincipal) securityContext.getUserPrincipal();

    caseService.approveCase(actor, caseId);

    return Response.noContent().build();
}
```

Service layer tetap wajib mengecek:

```text
actor.tenantId == case.tenantId
case.status == SUBMITTED
actor != case.createdBy
actor has sufficient approval level
actor has relationship/assignment/grant if required
```

---

# 11. Admin Endpoint

Admin endpoint adalah high-risk boundary.

Admin endpoint sebaiknya butuh:

```text
- dedicated admin permission
- strong authentication
- step-up/MFA marker bila tersedia
- tenant/org boundary
- approval untuk perubahan berisiko
- audit allow/deny
- audit business change old/new value
```

Contoh:

```java
@Path("/admin/roles")
@AdminEndpoint(
        permissions = {"admin.role.manage"},
        requireStepUp = true,
        risk = "HIGH"
)
public class RoleAdminResource {

    @POST
    @Path("/{roleCode}/permissions/{permissionCode}")
    public Response addPermissionToRole(
            @Context SecurityContext securityContext,
            @PathParam("roleCode") String roleCode,
            @PathParam("permissionCode") String permissionCode
    ) {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal actor =
                (AuthenticatedPrincipal) securityContext.getUserPrincipal();

        roleAdminService.addPermission(actor, roleCode, permissionCode);

        return Response.noContent().build();
    }
}
```

Anti-pattern:

```java
if (principal.hasRole("ADMIN")) {
    allow();
}
```

Lebih aman:

```java
principal.hasPermission("admin.role.manage")
&& principal.hasStepUpAuthentication()
&& adminPolicy.canManageRole(actor, targetRole)
```

---

# 12. Internal Service Endpoint

Internal endpoint bukan “tanpa auth karena internal network”.

Internal endpoint harus mempertimbangkan:

```text
- service identity
- mTLS or workload identity
- internal-only ingress/network policy
- allowed caller service list
- service token audience
- audit
```

RFC 8705 mendefinisikan OAuth 2.0 mutual-TLS client authentication dan certificate-bound access tokens, yaitu mekanisme OAuth client authentication menggunakan mTLS dengan X.509 certificates. Ini relevan untuk service-to-service atau highly privileged clients yang butuh client authentication lebih kuat daripada bearer-only token. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc8705?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh policy:

```java
public final class InternalEndpointPolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision canCallInternalEndpoint(
            AuthenticatedPrincipal actor,
            InternalEndpoint endpoint
    ) {
        if (!"SERVICE".equals(actor.actorType())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("actor_is_not_service_account");
        }

        if (endpoint.requireMtls() && !actor.mtlsVerified()) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("mtls_required");
        }

        if (endpoint.allowedServices().length > 0
                && !Set.of(endpoint.allowedServices()).contains(actor.clientId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("service_not_allowed");
        }

        if (!actor.hasScope("internal:write")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_internal_scope");
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.allow("internal_service_allowed");
    }
}
```

---

# 13. Service-to-Service Token Rules

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| User token ≠ service token | Jangan izinkan user token memanggil endpoint internal |
| Audience harus spesifik | Token untuk `service-a` tidak valid untuk `service-b` |
| Client ID harus jelas | `client_id`, `azp`, atau service identity harus dipetakan |
| Scope internal khusus | Misalnya `internal:case-reindex`, bukan `case:write` umum |
| mTLS untuk high privilege | Service identity diverifikasi di transport layer |
| Audit service caller | Log `clientId`, `serviceName`, `tenantContext`, action |

Contoh service principal:

```java
public record AuthenticatedPrincipal(
        String subject,
        String actorType,      // USER, SERVICE, PARTNER, ADMIN
        String clientId,
        String serviceName,
        String tenantId,
        Set<String> scopes,
        Set<String> permissions,
        boolean mtlsVerified
) implements Principal {
    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return subject;
    }
}
```

---

# 14. Partner API Endpoint

Partner API berbeda dari internal endpoint.

Partner endpoint biasanya butuh:

```text
- client credential / private_key_jwt / mTLS
- partner-to-tenant mapping
- rate limit
- replay protection
- idempotency key
- strict payload validation
- per-partner permission/scope
- audit by partner client ID
```

Contoh:

```java
@Path("/partner/orders")
@PartnerEndpoint(
        allowedClients = {"partner-acme", "partner-telco-x"},
        requireClientCredential = true,
        requireReplayProtection = true
)
public class PartnerOrderResource {

    @POST
    public Response createOrder(
            @Context SecurityContext securityContext,
            CreatePartnerOrderRequest request
    ) {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal partner =
                (AuthenticatedPrincipal) securityContext.getUserPrincipal();

        PartnerTenantContext tenantContext =
                partnerTenantResolver.resolve(partner.clientId(), request.externalTenantRef());

        orderPartnerService.createOrder(partner, tenantContext, request);

        return Response.accepted().build();
    }
}
```

Jangan percaya tenant dari body partner tanpa mapping resmi.

```text
partner client ID + external tenant ref
  -> trusted mapping table
  -> internal tenant ID
```

---

# 15. Webhook Endpoint

Webhook sering public dari sisi network, tetapi harus punya verification.

Minimal guard:

```text
- verify signature
- verify timestamp tolerance
- reject replayed event ID
- idempotency
- strict source allowlist jika mungkin
- schema validation
- audit accepted/rejected event
```

Contoh konseptual:

```java
@Path("/webhooks/payment")
@PublicEndpoint(reason = "External payment provider callback with signature verification")
public class PaymentWebhookResource {

    @POST
    public Response handleWebhook(
            @HeaderParam("X-Signature") String signature,
            @HeaderParam("X-Timestamp") String timestamp,
            String body
    ) {
        webhookVerifier.verify(signature, timestamp, body);
        paymentWebhookService.process(body);
        return Response.ok().build();
    }
}
```

Public network reachability tidak berarti tanpa authentication; webhook authentication biasanya berbasis signature, bukan user JWT.

---

# 16. Operations Endpoint

Operations endpoint seperti metrics/debug/actuator bisa sangat sensitif.

| Endpoint | Risk | Guard |
|---|---|---|
| `/metrics` | Exposes internals/cardinality | internal network + auth |
| `/debug/*` | High info leak | disabled in prod or admin-only |
| `/config` | Secret leak risk | never expose secrets |
| `/threads` | Runtime info leak | SRE-only |
| `/heapdump` | Critical PII/secret leak | avoid/disable |
| `/ready` | Low/medium | sanitized output |

Kubernetes NetworkPolicy adalah mekanisme application-centric untuk mengatur bagaimana Pod boleh berkomunikasi dengan entitas jaringan lain; NetworkPolicy dapat mendeskripsikan traffic yang diizinkan ke/dari pod. Ini relevan untuk membatasi endpoint internal/ops hanya dari namespace/pod tertentu, tetapi tetap bukan pengganti application-level authorization. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/services-networking/network-policies/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 17. Gateway vs Service Enforcement

| Layer | Bisa Melakukan | Tidak Cukup Untuk |
|---|---|---|
| API Gateway | TLS termination, WAF, JWT coarse validation, routing, rate limit | Object-level domain authorization |
| Ingress | Host/path routing, TLS, basic network boundary | Permission/state checks |
| Service Mesh | mTLS, workload identity, traffic policy | Business authorization |
| Jersey Filter | Authentication, endpoint classification, coarse permission | Deep object state unless loaded |
| Application Service | Object/state/workflow policy | Network boundary |
| MyBatis/PostgreSQL | Tenant/data guard | Token validation |

Rule:

```text
Gateway protects the edge.
Service protects the domain.
Repository protects the data boundary.
```

---

# 18. Route Boundary Matrix

| Endpoint Type | Gateway | Jersey Filter | Service Layer | DB/Mapper |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Public health | allow route | classification check | minimal | none |
| Authenticated user | JWT validation optional/coarse | full authn | user context | tenant where needed |
| Domain protected | JWT + rate limit | permission/scope | object/state auth | tenant/visibility query |
| Admin | admin route + WAF | admin permission/step-up | admin policy | audit old/new |
| Internal | internal route only | service identity/mTLS | job/action policy | tenant/status guard |
| Partner | partner route + rate limit | client auth/scope | partner tenant mapping | tenant boundary |
| Ops | internal route | SRE/platform auth | minimal | none or restricted |
| Break-glass | highly restricted | emergency auth | approval/expiry policy | full audit |

---

# 19. Network Boundary di Kubernetes

Untuk internal endpoint, gunakan kombinasi:

```text
- separate path prefix: /internal/*
- API gateway route not exposed publicly
- Kubernetes NetworkPolicy
- namespace/service account isolation
- mTLS/service mesh if available
- application-level service identity check
```

Contoh konseptual NetworkPolicy:

```yaml
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: allow-case-worker-to-case-api
  namespace: case-management
spec:
  podSelector:
    matchLabels:
      app: case-api
  policyTypes:
    - Ingress
  ingress:
    - from:
        - podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              app: case-worker
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: 8080
```

NetworkPolicy membatasi network path, tetapi jika attacker sudah berada di pod yang diizinkan atau terjadi salah label, application-level auth tetap diperlukan.

---

# 20. On-Prem / VM Boundary

Jika deploy on-prem atau VM tanpa Kubernetes:

```text
- reverse proxy route split
- firewall/security group
- internal DNS only
- mTLS between services
- separate admin listener/port if needed
- VPN/bastion for ops endpoints
- service account token validation
```

Pattern:

```text
Public listener:
  /api/*
  /partner/*
  /health/live

Internal listener:
  /internal/*
  /metrics
  /admin/* if admin network separated
```

Namun tetap gunakan classification annotation agar boundary tidak hanya bergantung konfigurasi infra.

---

# 21. Admin Step-Up Authentication

Step-up berarti user sudah login, tetapi untuk action berisiko tinggi perlu bukti authentication yang lebih baru/kuat.

Contoh claim yang mungkin digunakan tergantung IdP:

```text
acr
amr
auth_time
```

Policy konseptual:

```java
public final class StepUpPolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision requireRecentStrongAuth(
            AuthenticatedPrincipal actor,
            Duration maxAge
    ) {
        if (!actor.authenticationMethods().contains("mfa")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("mfa_required");
        }

        if (actor.authTime() == null) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_auth_time");
        }

        if (actor.authTime().isBefore(Instant.now().minus(maxAge))) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("step_up_auth_too_old");
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.allow("step_up_satisfied");
    }
}
```

Gunakan untuk:

```text
- assign admin role
- disable user
- rotate secret
- approve high-risk case
- break-glass access
- export sensitive data
```

---

# 22. Internal Endpoint Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| `/internal/*` tanpa auth | Lateral movement | Service identity + mTLS |
| Menganggap VPC aman | Insider/compromised pod risk | Zero-trust style checks |
| User token bisa call internal endpoint | Privilege escalation | Require actorType=SERVICE |
| Internal endpoint exposed di gateway | Internet attack surface | Gateway route deny |
| Health endpoint full detail | Info leak | Sanitized health |
| Metrics public | Sensitive operational leak | Internal network + auth |
| Admin pakai role `ADMIN` saja | Overprivileged | Specific permission + step-up |
| Partner tenant dari body | Tenant spoofing | Trusted mapping table |
| Webhook tanpa replay check | Replay attack | timestamp + event ID store |

---

# 23. Route Classification Test

Wajib ada test refleksi untuk mencegah endpoint tanpa annotation.

```java
@Test
void everyJaxRsEndpointMustBeClassified() {
    Set<Class<?>> resources = Set.of(
            HealthResource.class,
            CaseResource.class,
            InternalCaseResource.class,
            UserAdminResource.class,
            PartnerOrderResource.class
    );

    List<String> violations = new ArrayList<>();

    for (Class<?> resource : resources) {
        for (Method method : resource.getDeclaredMethods()) {
            if (!isJaxRsMethod(method)) {
                continue;
            }

            EndpointClassification classification =
                    EndpointClassificationResolver.resolve(resource, method);

            if (classification == EndpointClassification.UNCLASSIFIED) {
                violations.add(resource.getSimpleName() + "#" + method.getName());
            }
        }
    }

    assertTrue(
            violations.isEmpty(),
            "Unclassified endpoints: " + violations
    );
}
```

Helper:

```java
private boolean isJaxRsMethod(Method method) {
    return method.isAnnotationPresent(GET.class)
            || method.isAnnotationPresent(POST.class)
            || method.isAnnotationPresent(PUT.class)
            || method.isAnnotationPresent(PATCH.class)
            || method.isAnnotationPresent(DELETE.class);
}
```

---

# 24. Internal Endpoint Test

```java
@Test
void internalEndpointShouldRejectUserToken() {
    String userToken = tokenFactory.userToken(builder -> builder
            .subject("alice")
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .scope("internal:write"));

    Response response = target("/internal/cases/CASE-1/reindex")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + userToken)
            .post(Entity.json("{}"));

    assertEquals(403, response.getStatus());
}
```

```java
@Test
void internalEndpointShouldAllowApprovedServiceTokenWithMtls() {
    String serviceToken = tokenFactory.serviceToken(builder -> builder
            .subject("service:case-worker-service")
            .clientId("case-worker-service")
            .scope("internal:write")
            .audience("case-api"));

    Response response = target("/internal/cases/CASE-1/reindex")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + serviceToken)
            .header("X-MTLS-Verified", "true")
            .post(Entity.json("{}"));

    assertEquals(202, response.getStatus());
}
```

Catatan: header seperti `X-MTLS-Verified` hanya boleh dipercaya jika di-set oleh trusted proxy/service mesh dan di-strip dari traffic eksternal. Jangan menerima header ini langsung dari internet.

---

# 25. Admin Endpoint Test

```java
@Test
void adminEndpointShouldRejectAdminWithoutStepUp() {
    String token = tokenFactory.userToken(builder -> builder
            .subject("admin-1")
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .permission("admin.user.manage")
            .claim("amr", List.of("pwd"))
            .claim("auth_time", Instant.now().minus(Duration.ofHours(8))));

    Response response = target("/admin/users/user-123/disable")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + token)
            .post(Entity.json("{}"));

    assertEquals(403, response.getStatus());
}
```

```java
@Test
void adminEndpointShouldAuditRoleChange() {
    String token = tokenFactory.userToken(builder -> builder
            .subject("admin-1")
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .permission("admin.role.manage")
            .claim("amr", List.of("pwd", "mfa"))
            .claim("auth_time", Instant.now()));

    Response response = target("/admin/roles/CASE_SUPERVISOR/permissions/case.approve")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + token)
            .post(Entity.json("{}"));

    assertEquals(204, response.getStatus());

    SecurityAuditEvent event = auditSink.lastEvent();
    assertEquals("POLICY_CHANGE", event.eventType());
    assertEquals("admin-1", event.actor().subject());
    assertEquals("admin.role.manage", event.action());
}
```

# 26. Partner Endpoint Test

```java
@Test
void partnerEndpointShouldRejectUnknownClient() {
    String token = tokenFactory.clientCredentialToken(builder -> builder
            .clientId("unknown-partner")
            .audience("partner-api")
            .scope("partner:order:create"));

    Response response = target("/partner/orders")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + token)
            .post(Entity.json(validPartnerOrder()));

    assertEquals(403, response.getStatus());
}
```

```java
@Test
void partnerEndpointShouldResolveTenantFromTrustedMapping() {
    String token = tokenFactory.clientCredentialToken(builder -> builder
            .clientId("partner-acme")
            .audience("partner-api")
            .scope("partner:order:create"));

    CreatePartnerOrderRequest body = new CreatePartnerOrderRequest(
            "external-tenant-001",
            "ORDER-123"
    );

    Response response = target("/partner/orders")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + token)
            .post(Entity.json(body));

    assertEquals(202, response.getStatus());

    assertEquals(
            "tenant-a",
            partnerTenantMappingRepository
                    .resolve("partner-acme", "external-tenant-001")
                    .internalTenantId()
    );
}
```

# 27. Public Endpoint Test

```java
@Test
void publicHealthEndpointShouldNotExposeInternalDetails() {
    Response response = target("/health/ready")
            .request()
            .get();

    assertEquals(200, response.getStatus());

    String body = response.readEntity(String.class);

    assertFalse(body.contains("jdbc:"));
    assertFalse(body.contains("password"));
    assertFalse(body.contains("issuer"));
    assertFalse(body.contains("internal"));
}
```

Public endpoint boleh public, tapi tetap harus punya output contract yang aman.

---

# 28. OpenAPI Security Contract

Pastikan OpenAPI juga mencerminkan klasifikasi security.

Contoh:

```yaml
paths:
  /health/live:
    get:
      security: []
      x-endpoint-classification: PUBLIC

  /cases/{caseId}:
    get:
      security:
        - bearerAuth: []
      x-endpoint-classification: DOMAIN_PROTECTED
      x-required-permissions:
        - case.read

  /internal/cases/{caseId}/reindex:
    post:
      security:
        - serviceBearerAuth: []
        - mtlsAuth: []
      x-endpoint-classification: INTERNAL
      x-allowed-services:
        - case-worker-service

  /admin/users/{userId}/disable:
    post:
      security:
        - bearerAuth: []
      x-endpoint-classification: ADMIN
      x-required-permissions:
        - admin.user.manage
      x-require-step-up: true
```

OpenAPI bukan enforcement final, tapi berguna untuk review, gateway config, client generation, dan security scan.

---

# 30. Code Review Checklist

```text
[ ] Endpoint baru punya explicit classification.
[ ] Public endpoint punya reason.
[ ] Public endpoint tidak expose internal details.
[ ] Domain endpoint punya permission/scope.
[ ] Endpoint dengan object ID punya object-level authorization.
[ ] Admin endpoint punya specific admin permission, bukan ADMIN role saja.
[ ] Admin endpoint high-risk punya step-up/MFA marker jika tersedia.
[ ] Internal endpoint reject user token.
[ ] Internal endpoint require service identity.
[ ] Internal endpoint tidak exposed via public gateway route.
[ ] Partner endpoint resolve tenant dari trusted mapping.
[ ] Webhook endpoint verify signature and replay.
[ ] Ops endpoint tidak public.
[ ] Metrics/debug endpoint restricted.
[ ] Gateway policy dan application policy konsisten.
[ ] Route classification test diperbarui.
[ ] OpenAPI security metadata diperbarui.
[ ] Audit event untuk sensitive action tersedia.
```

---

# 31. Practical Decision Guide

| Pertanyaan | Jawaban Desain |
|---|---|
| Endpoint boleh public? | Hanya kalau memang tidak butuh identity dan output sanitized |
| Endpoint butuh user login? | `@RequiresScope` atau authenticated classification |
| Endpoint menjalankan business action? | `@RequiresPermission` + service policy |
| Endpoint hanya untuk service internal? | `@InternalEndpoint` + service token + mTLS/network boundary |
| Endpoint untuk admin? | `@AdminEndpoint` + permission spesifik + step-up + audit |
| Endpoint menerima partner call? | `@PartnerEndpoint` + client auth + tenant mapping |
| Endpoint menerima webhook? | Public route + signature + replay protection |
| Endpoint operasi? | Internal network + SRE/platform permission |
| Endpoint emergency? | Break-glass flow + approval + expiry + audit |

---

# 32. Minimal Production Invariants

```text
1. No endpoint is unclassified.
2. Public endpoint must be intentionally public and sanitized.
3. Internal endpoint must reject user token.
4. Admin endpoint must not rely only on role ADMIN.
5. Partner endpoint must not trust tenant from request body.
6. Webhook endpoint must verify signature and replay.
7. Gateway boundary must be backed by service-level enforcement.
8. Operations endpoint must not be internet exposed.
9. mTLS/network policy is defense-in-depth, not replacement for app auth.
10. Sensitive endpoint must produce security audit event.
```

---

# 33. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Endpoint taxonomy | Bisa membedakan public/user/domain/admin/internal/partner/ops |
| 2 | Annotation strategy | Semua endpoint diklasifikasi eksplisit |
| 3 | Classification filter | Endpoint unclassified ditolak |
| 4 | Public endpoint hardening | Health/version/docs tidak bocor |
| 5 | Admin endpoint guard | Permission spesifik + step-up + audit |
| 6 | Internal endpoint guard | Service identity + mTLS/network policy |
| 7 | Partner/webhook guard | Client auth, tenant mapping, signature, replay |
| 8 | Gateway vs service boundary | Tidak bergantung pada satu layer |
| 9 | Route/OpenAPI tests | Endpoint baru tidak lolos tanpa security metadata |
| 10 | Audit integration | Sensitive boundary punya evidence |

7/13/2026, 4:55:01 AM

Kita lanjut ke service-to-service security. Fokusnya: membedakan user token vs service token, client credentials, mTLS, workload identity, internal scopes, replay protection, dan authorization antar microservice.

# Cheatsheet - Service-to-Service Authentication & Authorization di Jersey/JAX-RS Microservices

## 1. Tujuan

Service-to-service security menjawab pertanyaan:

```text id="2s2g7w"
Service apa yang memanggil?
Apakah service itu benar-benar service yang diklaim?
Apakah token/credential-nya ditujukan untuk API ini?
Apakah service tersebut boleh melakukan action ini?
Apakah request ini boleh dijalankan untuk tenant/resource tertentu?
Apakah bisa dicegah replay, lateral movement, dan confused deputy?
```

Ini berbeda dari user authentication. Dalam user flow, actor utama adalah manusia. Dalam service-to-service flow, actor utama adalah **workload / service account / client application**.

OAuth 2.0 mTLS di RFC 8705 mendefinisikan client authentication dan certificate-bound access tokens menggunakan mutual TLS dengan X.509 certificate. OAuth 2.0 Security Best Current Practice terbaru, RFC 9700, juga memperbarui threat model dan rekomendasi security OAuth 2.0 berdasarkan pengalaman implementasi modern. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc8705?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model

```text id="56h5ii"
User token:
  "Alice is calling case-api"

Service token:
  "case-worker-service is calling case-api"

Delegated token:
  "case-worker-service is calling case-api on behalf of Alice"

Partner token:
  "partner-acme is calling partner-api for mapped tenant X"

Workload identity:
  "this running workload/pod/service is cryptographically identified as service Y"
```

Jangan campur semua menjadi satu tipe principal.

```java id="8j5ex8"
public enum ActorType {
    USER,
    SERVICE,
    PARTNER,
    JOB,
    ADMIN,
    SYSTEM
}
```

---

# 3. Diagram Service-to-Service Flow

```mermaid id="4sue3s"
sequenceDiagram
    participant S as Calling Service
    participant AS as Authorization Server / IdP
    participant GW as Gateway / Mesh / Ingress
    participant API as Jersey API
    participant F as AuthN Filter
    participant P as Service AuthZ Policy
    participant DB as PostgreSQL

    S->>AS: client_credentials / workload token exchange
    AS-->>S: access_token for target API

    S->>GW: HTTPS/mTLS + Bearer access_token
    GW->>API: Forward request with verified transport context

    API->>F: ContainerRequestFilter
    F->>F: Validate token issuer/audience/expiry/signature
    F->>F: Extract client_id/service identity/scopes
    F->>API: set SecurityContext(service principal)

    API->>P: Check service permission/internal scope
    P->>P: Check allowed caller service + tenant/resource/action
    P-->>API: allow / deny

    API->>DB: tenant/status guarded operation
    DB-->>API: result
    API-->>S: response
```

---

# 4. Authentication Mechanism Options

| Mechanism | Cocok Untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko / Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| OAuth2 Client Credentials | Service-to-service API | Standard, mudah dipakai dengan scopes/audience | Bearer token bisa dipakai ulang jika bocor |
| mTLS | Internal/high-privilege service | Client dan server saling autentikasi | PKI/cert lifecycle kompleks |
| Certificate-bound access token | High assurance OAuth | Token terikat ke certificate | Butuh AS/resource server support |
| Kubernetes ServiceAccount Token | Pod identity ke Kubernetes/API tertentu | Native Kubernetes workload identity | Jangan dipakai mentah sebagai universal API auth tanpa validasi |
| Cloud Workload Identity | Pod/workload akses cloud API | Hindari static cloud keys | Vendor-specific |
| SPIFFE/SPIRE | Cross-platform workload identity | Identity portable untuk workload heterogen | Perlu control plane dan operational maturity |
| HMAC request signature | Webhook/partner/internal event | Replay-safe jika benar | Key rotation/signature canonicalization kompleks |
| Static API key | Legacy/simple partner | Mudah | Lemah jika tidak hashed, scoped, rotated |

Kubernetes mendefinisikan ServiceAccount sebagai non-human account yang memberi identity berbeda untuk workload di cluster; Pod, komponen sistem, dan entity lain dapat memakai kredensial ServiceAccount untuk mengidentifikasi diri. SPIFFE mendefinisikan framework untuk menerbitkan identity workload lintas environment, dan SPIFFE ID adalah URI yang secara unik mengidentifikasi workload. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/security/service-accounts/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Recommended Layering

Untuk service-to-service endpoint internal, jangan pilih hanya satu guard.

```text id="8dhqtv"
Layer 1 - Network boundary
  Kubernetes NetworkPolicy / firewall / private route

Layer 2 - Transport identity
  mTLS / service mesh / trusted proxy identity

Layer 3 - Token validation
  OAuth2 access token, issuer, audience, expiry, signature

Layer 4 - Service principal mapping
  client_id / SPIFFE ID / service account -> internal service identity

Layer 5 - Service authorization
  allowed caller service + internal scopes + service permissions

Layer 6 - Domain guard
  tenant/resource/state/object authorization

Layer 7 - Audit
  caller service, target service, action, tenant, decision, reason
```

**Rule:** network “internal” tidak sama dengan trusted. Compromised pod/service masih bisa bergerak lateral bila internal endpoint hanya bergantung pada network.

---

# 6. Service Principal Model

Jangan pakai `AuthenticatedPrincipal` yang hanya user-centric.

```java id="zdk6vm"
public record AuthenticatedPrincipal(
        String subject,
        ActorType actorType,

        String username,
        String tenantId,

        String clientId,
        String serviceName,
        String workloadId,
        String spiffeId,

        Set<String> scopes,
        Set<String> permissions,
        Set<String> allowedTenants,

        boolean mtlsVerified,
        String certificateThumbprint,

        String issuer,
        Set<String> audiences,
        String tokenId
) implements Principal {

    @Override
    public String getName() {
        if (actorType == ActorType.SERVICE) {
            return serviceName != null ? serviceName : clientId;
        }
        return username != null ? username : subject;
    }

    public boolean isService() {
        return actorType == ActorType.SERVICE;
    }

    public boolean hasScope(String scope) {
        return scopes != null && scopes.contains(scope);
    }

    public boolean hasPermission(String permission) {
        return permissions != null && permissions.contains(permission);
    }
}
```

Service identity minimal harus punya:

```text id="r4xb78"
actorType = SERVICE
clientId / serviceName
issuer
audience
scopes
optional mTLS/SPIFFE certificate binding
allowed tenant/resource boundary
```

---

# 7. Client Credentials Flow

Client Credentials cocok ketika service memanggil service lain atas nama dirinya sendiri, bukan atas nama user.

```text id="d9vyss"
case-worker-service
  -> Authorization Server
  -> access_token with:
       sub = service account
       client_id = case-worker-service
       aud = case-api
       scope = internal:case-reindex
  -> case-api
```

Resource server wajib validasi:

```text id="rf52g7"
- issuer trusted
- audience == target API
- expiry valid
- signature valid
- actorType/service claim valid
- client_id allowed
- internal scope present
```

Contoh claim konseptual:

```json id="0a8zk9"
{
  "iss": "https://idp.company.com/realms/internal",
  "sub": "service-account-case-worker",
  "client_id": "case-worker-service",
  "aud": "case-api",
  "scope": "internal:case-reindex internal:case-read",
  "azp": "case-worker-service",
  "exp": 1783920000,
  "iat": 1783919700,
  "jti": "token-123",
  "actor_type": "SERVICE"
}
```

---

# 8. Service Token Validation di Jersey Filter

```java id="r6fzws"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class ServiceAwareAuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private static final String BEARER = "Bearer ";

    private final AccessTokenValidator tokenValidator;
    private final ServicePrincipalMapper servicePrincipalMapper;
    private final UserPrincipalMapper userPrincipalMapper;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String authorization = ctx.getHeaderString(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);

        if (authorization == null || !authorization.startsWith(BEARER)) {
            abort401(ctx, "missing_or_invalid_authorization_header");
            return;
        }

        String rawToken = authorization.substring(BEARER.length()).trim();

        try {
            VerifiedAccessToken token = tokenValidator.validate(rawToken);

            AuthenticatedPrincipal principal =
                    token.isServiceToken()
                            ? servicePrincipalMapper.map(token, ctx)
                            : userPrincipalMapper.map(token, ctx);

            ctx.setSecurityContext(new RequestSecurityContext(
                    principal,
                    ctx.getSecurityContext() != null && ctx.getSecurityContext().isSecure(),
                    "Bearer"
            ));

        } catch (InvalidAccessTokenException e) {
            abort401(ctx, e.errorCode());
        }
    }

    private void abort401(ContainerRequestContext ctx, String reason) {
        ctx.abortWith(Response.status(Response.Status.UNAUTHORIZED)
                .header(HttpHeaders.WWW_AUTHENTICATE,
                        "Bearer realm=\"api\", error=\"invalid_token\"")
                .entity(Map.of("code", "UNAUTHORIZED", "reason", reason))
                .build());
    }
}
```

---

# 9. Mapping Service Principal

```java id="dtpum5"
public final class ServicePrincipalMapper {

    private final ServiceRegistry serviceRegistry;
    private final MtlsContextExtractor mtlsContextExtractor;

    public AuthenticatedPrincipal map(
            VerifiedAccessToken token,
            ContainerRequestContext ctx
    ) {
        String clientId = firstNonBlank(
                token.claim("client_id"),
                token.claim("azp"),
                token.subject()
        );

        ServiceRegistration registration =
                serviceRegistry.findByClientId(clientId)
                        .orElseThrow(() ->
                                new InvalidAccessTokenException("unknown_service_client"));

        MtlsContext mtls = mtlsContextExtractor.extract(ctx);

        return new AuthenticatedPrincipal(
                token.subject(),
                ActorType.SERVICE,

                null,
                null,

                clientId,
                registration.serviceName(),
                registration.workloadId(),
                mtls.spiffeId(),

                token.scopes(),
                registration.permissions(),
                registration.allowedTenants(),

                mtls.verified(),
                mtls.certificateThumbprint(),

                token.issuer(),
                token.audiences(),
                token.tokenId()
        );
    }

    private String firstNonBlank(String... values) {
        for (String value : values) {
            if (value != null && !value.isBlank()) {
                return value;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}
```

**Catatan:** claim name berbeda antar IdP. `client_id`, `azp`, `appid`, `client_id` custom, atau service account subject harus dinormalisasi di satu tempat.
