# 17. Principal Mapper Strategy

Pisahkan validator dan mapper.

```text id="u7kbfr"
AccessTokenValidator:
  Apakah token valid secara kriptografis dan semantik?

PrincipalMapper:
  Token valid ini menjadi principal internal seperti apa?
```

Interface:

```java id="7tqwsh"
public interface PrincipalMapper {
    boolean supports(VerifiedCredential credential);

    SecurityActor map(
            VerifiedCredential credential,
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext
    );
}
```

Implementasi:

```text id="imvzlf"
UserPrincipalMapper
ServicePrincipalMapper
PartnerPrincipalMapper
DelegatedPrincipalMapper
ImpersonationPrincipalMapper
SystemJobPrincipalMapper
```

Router:

```java id="y0cqos"
public final class CompositePrincipalMapper {

    private final List<PrincipalMapper> mappers;

    public SecurityActor map(
            VerifiedCredential credential,
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext
    ) {
        return mappers.stream()
                .filter(mapper -> mapper.supports(credential))
                .findFirst()
                .orElseThrow(() -> new InvalidAccessTokenException("unsupported_principal_type"))
                .map(credential, requestContext);
    }
}
```

---

# 18. User Principal Mapper

```java id="s6hs8m"
public final class UserPrincipalMapper implements PrincipalMapper {

    @Override
    public boolean supports(VerifiedCredential credential) {
        return "USER".equals(credential.claimAsString("actor_type"))
                || credential.hasClaim("preferred_username");
    }

    @Override
    public SecurityActor map(
            VerifiedCredential token,
            ContainerRequestContext ctx
    ) {
        ActorIdentity user = new ActorIdentity(
                token.subject(),
                ActorType.USER,
                token.claimAsString("preferred_username"),
                token.claimAsString("tenant_id"),
                token.claimAsString("org_id"),
                token.claimAsString("department"),
                token.claimAsString("client_id"),
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                Map.of()
        );

        TenantContext tenant = TenantContext.singleTenant(
                user.tenantId(),
                TenantSource.TOKEN_CLAIM
        );

        TokenContext tokenContext = token.toTokenContext();
        TransportContext transport = TransportContextFactory.from(ctx);

        AuditActorChain actorChain = new AuditActorChain(
                user,
                user,
                user,
                null,
                DelegationMode.NONE,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null
        );

        return new DefaultSecurityPrincipal(
                user,
                user,
                null,
                DelegationMode.NONE,
                tenant,
                token.scopes(),
                token.roles(),
                token.permissions(),
                tokenContext,
                transport,
                actorChain
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 19. Service Principal Mapper

```java id="dvckvk"
public final class ServicePrincipalMapper implements PrincipalMapper {

    private final ServiceRegistry serviceRegistry;

    @Override
    public boolean supports(VerifiedCredential credential) {
        return "SERVICE".equals(credential.claimAsString("actor_type"))
                || credential.hasClaim("client_id");
    }

    @Override
    public SecurityActor map(
            VerifiedCredential token,
            ContainerRequestContext ctx
    ) {
        String clientId = token.claimAsString("client_id");

        ServiceRegistration registration =
                serviceRegistry.findActiveByClientId(clientId)
                        .orElseThrow(() -> new InvalidAccessTokenException("unknown_service_client"));

        ActorIdentity service = new ActorIdentity(
                token.subject(),
                ActorType.SERVICE,
                registration.serviceName(),
                null,
                null,
                null,
                clientId,
                registration.serviceName(),
                null,
                registration.workloadId(),
                token.claimAsString("spiffe_id"),
                Map.of("serviceTier", registration.tier())
        );

        TenantContext tenant = new TenantContext(
                null,
                TenantSource.SERVICE_REGISTRY,
                registration.allowedTenants()
        );

        AuditActorChain actorChain = new AuditActorChain(
                service,
                service,
                null,
                service,
                DelegationMode.NONE,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null
        );

        return new DefaultSecurityPrincipal(
                service,
                null,
                service,
                DelegationMode.NONE,
                tenant,
                token.scopes(),
                Set.of(),
                registration.permissions(),
                token.toTokenContext(),
                TransportContextFactory.from(ctx),
                actorChain
        );
    }
}
```

Service principal bisa tidak punya `tenantId` tunggal, tetapi punya `allowedTenants`.

---

# 20. Partner Principal Mapper

```java id="fs32ml"
public final class PartnerPrincipalMapper implements PrincipalMapper {

    private final PartnerRegistry partnerRegistry;

    @Override
    public boolean supports(VerifiedCredential credential) {
        return "PARTNER".equals(credential.claimAsString("actor_type"));
    }

    @Override
    public SecurityActor map(
            VerifiedCredential token,
            ContainerRequestContext ctx
    ) {
        String clientId = token.claimAsString("client_id");

        PartnerRegistration partner =
                partnerRegistry.findActiveByClientId(clientId)
                        .orElseThrow(() -> new InvalidAccessTokenException("unknown_partner_client"));

        ActorIdentity partnerActor = new ActorIdentity(
                token.subject(),
                ActorType.PARTNER,
                partner.displayName(),
                null,
                null,
                null,
                clientId,
                null,
                partner.partnerId(),
                null,
                null,
                Map.of("partnerType", partner.type())
        );

        TenantContext tenant = new TenantContext(
                null,
                TenantSource.PARTNER_MAPPING,
                partner.allowedTenantIds()
        );

        AuditActorChain actorChain = new AuditActorChain(
                partnerActor,
                partnerActor,
                null,
                null,
                DelegationMode.PARTNER_ACTING_FOR_TENANT,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null
        );

        return new DefaultSecurityPrincipal(
                partnerActor,
                null,
                null,
                DelegationMode.PARTNER_ACTING_FOR_TENANT,
                tenant,
                token.scopes(),
                Set.of(),
                partner.permissions(),
                token.toTokenContext(),
                TransportContextFactory.from(ctx),
                actorChain
        );
    }
}
```

Partner tenant final tetap harus di-resolve dari trusted mapping saat request membawa external tenant reference.

# 21. Delegated Principal Mapper

```java id="fhbb6y"
public final class DelegatedPrincipalMapper implements PrincipalMapper {

    @Override
    public boolean supports(VerifiedCredential credential) {
        return "DELEGATION".equals(credential.claimAsString("delegation_mode"));
    }

    @Override
    public SecurityActor map(
            VerifiedCredential token,
            ContainerRequestContext ctx
    ) {
        ActorIdentity callingService = new ActorIdentity(
                token.claimAsString("calling_service.sub"),
                ActorType.SERVICE,
                token.claimAsString("calling_service.service_name"),
                null,
                null,
                null,
                token.claimAsString("calling_service.client_id"),
                token.claimAsString("calling_service.service_name"),
                null,
                token.claimAsString("calling_service.workload_id"),
                token.claimAsString("calling_service.spiffe_id"),
                Map.of()
        );

        ActorIdentity originalUser = new ActorIdentity(
                token.claimAsString("original_actor.sub"),
                ActorType.USER,
                token.claimAsString("original_actor.username"),
                token.claimAsString("original_actor.tenant_id"),
                token.claimAsString("original_actor.org_id"),
                token.claimAsString("original_actor.department"),
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                Map.of()
        );

        TenantContext tenant = TenantContext.singleTenant(
                originalUser.tenantId(),
                TenantSource.TOKEN_CLAIM
        );

        AuditActorChain actorChain = new AuditActorChain(
                callingService,
                originalUser,
                originalUser,
                callingService,
                DelegationMode.DELEGATION,
                token.claimAsString("delegation_id"),
                null,
                token.claimAsString("delegation_reason"),
                null
        );

        return new DefaultSecurityPrincipal(
                originalUser,
                originalUser,
                callingService,
                DelegationMode.DELEGATION,
                tenant,
                token.scopes(),
                token.roles(),
                token.permissions(),
                token.toTokenContext(),
                TransportContextFactory.from(ctx),
                actorChain
        );
    }
}
```

Untuk delegated action, policy harus mengecek calling service dan original user.

# 22. Impersonation Principal Mapper

```java id="n4yekm"
public final class ImpersonationPrincipalMapper implements PrincipalMapper {

    @Override
    public boolean supports(VerifiedCredential credential) {
        return "IMPERSONATION".equals(credential.claimAsString("delegation_mode"));
    }

    @Override
    public SecurityActor map(
            VerifiedCredential token,
            ContainerRequestContext ctx
    ) {
        ActorIdentity impersonator = new ActorIdentity(
                token.claimAsString("impersonator.sub"),
                ActorType.ADMIN,
                token.claimAsString("impersonator.username"),
                token.claimAsString("impersonator.tenant_id"),
                null,
                null,
                token.claimAsString("client_id"),
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                Map.of()
        );

        ActorIdentity effectiveUser = new ActorIdentity(
                token.claimAsString("effective_actor.sub"),
                ActorType.USER,
                token.claimAsString("effective_actor.username"),
                token.claimAsString("effective_actor.tenant_id"),
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                Map.of()
        );

        TenantContext tenant = TenantContext.singleTenant(
                effectiveUser.tenantId(),
                TenantSource.TOKEN_CLAIM
        );

        AuditActorChain actorChain = new AuditActorChain(
                impersonator,
                effectiveUser,
                effectiveUser,
                null,
                DelegationMode.IMPERSONATION,
                null,
                token.claimAsString("impersonation_grant_id"),
                token.claimAsString("impersonation_reason"),
                token.claimAsString("ticket_id")
        );

        return new DefaultSecurityPrincipal(
                effectiveUser,
                effectiveUser,
                null,
                DelegationMode.IMPERSONATION,
                tenant,
                token.scopes(),
                token.roles(),
                token.permissions(),
                token.toTokenContext(),
                TransportContextFactory.from(ctx),
                actorChain
        );
    }
}
```

**Penting:** `getName()` boleh mengembalikan effective actor untuk kompatibilitas, tetapi audit harus selalu memakai `auditActorChain.realActor`.

---

# 23. Authentication Filter Integration

```java id="sxb2b1"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class AuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private static final String BEARER = "Bearer ";

    private final CredentialValidator credentialValidator;
    private final CompositePrincipalMapper principalMapper;
    private final AuthenticationAuditLogger auditLogger;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String authorization = ctx.getHeaderString(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);

        if (authorization == null || !authorization.startsWith(BEARER)) {
            auditLogger.logFailure(ctx, "missing_or_invalid_authorization_header");
            abort401(ctx, "missing_or_invalid_authorization_header");
            return;
        }

        String rawToken = authorization.substring(BEARER.length()).trim();

        try {
            VerifiedCredential credential = credentialValidator.validate(rawToken);
            SecurityActor actor = principalMapper.map(credential, ctx);

            RequestSecurityContext securityContext =
                    new RequestSecurityContext(
                            actor,
                            ctx.getSecurityContext() != null
                                    && ctx.getSecurityContext().isSecure(),
                            "Bearer"
                    );

            ctx.setSecurityContext(securityContext);
            ctx.setProperty("securityActor", actor);

            auditLogger.logSuccess(ctx, actor);

        } catch (InvalidCredentialException e) {
            auditLogger.logFailure(ctx, e.errorCode());
            abort401(ctx, e.errorCode());
        }
    }

    private void abort401(ContainerRequestContext ctx, String reason) {
        ctx.abortWith(Response.status(Response.Status.UNAUTHORIZED)
                .header(HttpHeaders.WWW_AUTHENTICATE,
                        "Bearer realm=\"api\", error=\"invalid_token\"")
                .entity(Map.of(
                        "code", "UNAUTHORIZED",
                        "reason", reason
                ))
                .build());
    }
}
```

---

# 24. Authorization Filter dengan Principal Baru

```java id="ylefpy"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public class PermissionAuthorizationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Context
    private ResourceInfo resourceInfo;

    private final PermissionAuthorizationService authorizationService;
    private final SecurityAuditLogger auditLogger;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        RequiresPermission required = resolvePermission(resourceInfo);

        if (required == null) {
            return;
        }

        SecurityActor actor = SecurityPrincipals.requireActor(ctx.getSecurityContext());

        AuthorizationDecision decision =
                authorizationService.check(actor, required.value(), required.mode());

        auditLogger.logEndpointAuthorization(ctx, actor, required.value(), decision);

        if (!decision.allowed()) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 25. Resource Method Style

## Untuk domain endpoint

```java id="q6noqe"
@GET
@Path("/cases/{caseId}")
@RequiresPermission("case.read")
public Response getCase(
        @Context SecurityContext securityContext,
        @PathParam("caseId") String caseId
) {
    SecurityActor actor = SecurityPrincipals.requireActor(securityContext);

    CaseDto result = caseApplicationService.getCase(actor, caseId);

    return Response.ok(result).build();
}
```

## Untuk internal endpoint

```java id="cmp72c"
@POST
@Path("/internal/cases/{caseId}/reindex")
@InternalEndpoint(
        allowedServices = {"case-worker-service"},
        requiredPermissions = {"internal.case.reindex"},
        requireMtls = true
)
public Response reindex(
        @Context SecurityContext securityContext,
        @PathParam("caseId") String caseId
) {
    SecurityActor service = SecurityPrincipals.requireService(securityContext);

    internalCaseService.reindex(service, caseId);

    return Response.accepted().build();
}
```

---

# 26. Application Service Style

Service layer menerima `SecurityActor`, bukan raw token, bukan `SecurityContext`.

```java id="f53rw3"
public CaseDto getCase(SecurityActor actor, String caseId) {
    TenantContext tenant = actor.tenantContext();

    CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findByIdForTenant(
            caseId,
            tenant.tenantId()
    ).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Case not found"));

    AuthorizationDecision decision =
            casePolicy.canRead(actor, caseRecord);

    auditLogger.logObjectAuthorization(actor, "case.read", caseRecord, decision);

    if (!decision.allowed()) {
        throw new ForbiddenException("Access denied");
    }

    return CaseDto.from(caseRecord);
}
```

**Rule:** `SecurityContext` adalah JAX-RS concern. Domain/application service cukup tahu `SecurityActor`.

---

# 27. Policy dengan Unified Principal

```java id="mnqj09"
public final class CaseAccessPolicy {

    public AuthorizationDecision canRead(SecurityActor actor, CaseRecord caseRecord) {
        if (!actor.tenantContext().canAccessTenant(caseRecord.tenantId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("tenant_not_allowed");
        }

        if (actor.hasPermission("case.read.all")) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow("has_case_read_all");
        }

        if (actor.hasPermission("case.read.assigned")
                && actor.effectiveActor().subject().equals(caseRecord.assignedUserId())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow("assigned_user");
        }

        if (actor.hasPermission("case.read.own")
                && actor.effectiveActor().subject().equals(caseRecord.createdBy())) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.allow("case_creator");
        }

        if (actor.delegationMode() == DelegationMode.IMPERSONATION) {
            return AuthorizationDecision.deny("impersonation_read_not_allowed_by_default");
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("no_case_read_rule_matched");
    }
}
```

Untuk impersonation, jangan otomatis memakai permission effective user tanpa grant policy tambahan.

---

# 28. Permission Source Strategy

Permission bisa berasal dari beberapa sumber:

| Source | Contoh | Caveat |
|---|---|---|
| Token claim | `permissions` claim | Bisa stale sampai token expired |
| Entitlement service | effective permission runtime | Latency/failure |
| Local registry | service permissions | Harus dikelola |
| DB role mapping | user-role-permission | Perlu cache/invalidation |
| ACL/ReBAC check | object relation | Jangan masukkan semua ke principal |
| Impersonation grant | allowed actions | Harus expiry/ticket/audit |

Saran:

```text id="kf8b30"
Principal membawa coarse/effective permissions.
Object-specific grants tidak perlu semua dimasukkan ke principal.
ACL/ReBAC dievaluasi saat object diketahui.
```

---

# 29. Principal Size Control

Jangan membuat principal terlalu besar.

Jangan masukkan:

```text id="p2c1r0"
- seluruh profile user
- seluruh ACL user
- semua group ribuan item
- raw token
- request body
- resource object
- DB connection/session
```

Masukkan yang perlu:

```text id="shki27"
- stable actor identity
- actor type
- tenant context
- scopes/roles/permissions normalized
- token metadata aman
- transport metadata aman
- actor chain untuk audit
```

Jika group/permission banyak, gunakan:

```text id="w5hwk7"
- permission version
- entitlement lookup
- cache key
- group hash
- lazy PDP check
```

---

# 30. Thread Safety

Principal harus immutable.

```text id="zu0btv"
- gunakan record atau final class
- Set.copyOf(...)
- Map.copyOf(...)
- jangan expose mutable collection
- jangan simpan request object mutable di principal
```

Kenapa:

```text id="i5ymba"
Jersey request bisa diproses concurrent/async.
Mutable principal membuat authorization unpredictable dan sulit diaudit.
```

---

# 31. Request Property vs SecurityContext

Boleh menyimpan actor juga di request property untuk filter lain:

```java id="nr1reo"
ctx.setProperty("securityActor", actor);
ctx.setSecurityContext(new RequestSecurityContext(actor, secure, "Bearer"));
```

Helper:

```java id="wz61m9"
public static SecurityActor fromRequest(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
    Object actor = ctx.getProperty("securityActor");

    if (actor instanceof SecurityActor securityActor) {
        return securityActor;
    }

    return SecurityPrincipals.requireActor(ctx.getSecurityContext());
}
```

Namun canonical source tetap `SecurityContext`.

---

# 32. Error Semantics

| Masalah Principal | Response |
|---|---:|
| Missing token | `401` |
| Invalid token | `401` |
| Token valid tapi unsupported actor type | `403` atau `401`, tergantung policy |
| Missing tenant untuk tenant-scoped endpoint | `403` atau `401` |
| User token ke internal endpoint | `403` |
| Service token ke user-only endpoint | `403` |
| Impersonation tanpa grant | `403` |
| Delegation original actor missing | `403` |

Rule:

```text id="cbij6e"
Authentication failure = tidak bisa membentuk principal valid.
Authorization failure = principal valid tapi tidak boleh untuk endpoint/action.
```

---

# 33. Audit Mapping dari SecurityActor

```java id="c4w6g3"
public final class AuditActorMapper {

    public AuditActorFields map(SecurityActor actor) {
        AuditActorChain chain = actor.auditActorChain();

        return new AuditActorFields(
                chain.realActor() != null ? chain.realActor().stableActorId() : null,
                chain.effectiveActor() != null ? chain.effectiveActor().stableActorId() : null,
                chain.originalActor() != null ? chain.originalActor().stableActorId() : null,
                chain.callingService() != null ? chain.callingService().stableActorId() : null,
                chain.mode().name(),
                actor.tenantContext().tenantId(),
                actor.tokenContext().issuer(),
                actor.tokenContext().tokenId(),
                actor.transportContext().mtlsVerified(),
                actor.transportContext().spiffeId()
        );
    }
}
```

Event audit authorization:

```json id="pqybhf"
{
  "eventType": "AUTHORIZATION_DECISION",
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "original_actor_not_authorized",
  "realActor": "SERVICE:service-account-case-orchestrator",
  "effectiveActor": "USER:user-alice",
  "originalActor": "USER:user-alice",
  "callingService": "SERVICE:service-account-case-orchestrator",
  "delegationMode": "DELEGATION",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "action": "case.approve",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "CASE-789"
}
```

---

# 34. Testing Principal Mapping

```text id="q1l03j"
[ ] User token maps to ActorType.USER
[ ] Service token maps to ActorType.SERVICE
[ ] Partner token maps to ActorType.PARTNER
[ ] Delegated token preserves calling service and original actor
[ ] Impersonation token preserves impersonator and effective user
[ ] Missing tenant fails for user token
[ ] Unknown service client fails
[ ] Raw token is not stored in principal
[ ] Principal collections are immutable
[ ] getName returns stable ID
[ ] AuditActorChain is complete
```

Example:

```java id="nadtk1"
@Test
void delegatedTokenShouldPreserveCallingServiceAndOriginalUser() {
    VerifiedCredential token = TokenFixture.delegatedToken()
            .callingService("case-orchestrator")
            .originalUser("alice")
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .audience("case-api")
            .build();

    SecurityActor actor = delegatedPrincipalMapper.map(token, requestContext);

    assertEquals(DelegationMode.DELEGATION, actor.delegationMode());
    assertEquals("alice", actor.originalActor().orElseThrow().subject());
    assertEquals("case-orchestrator", actor.callingService().orElseThrow().serviceName());
    assertEquals("tenant-a", actor.tenantContext().tenantId());
}
```

---

# 35. Testing SecurityContext

```java id="pbik6v"
@Test
void requestSecurityContextShouldExposeSecurityActorAsPrincipal() {
    SecurityActor actor = PrincipalFixture.user()
            .subject("alice")
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .permission("case.read")
            .build();

    RequestSecurityContext context =
            new RequestSecurityContext(actor, true, "Bearer");

    assertSame(actor, context.getUserPrincipal());
    assertTrue(context.isSecure());
    assertEquals("Bearer", context.getAuthenticationScheme());
}
```

---

# 36. Route-Level Actor Type Tests

```java id="i7fodm"
@Test
void internalEndpointShouldRejectUserActor() {
    SecurityActor user = PrincipalFixture.user()
            .subject("alice")
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .build();

    assertThrows(
            ForbiddenException.class,
            () -> SecurityPrincipals.requireService(
                    new RequestSecurityContext(user, true, "Bearer")
            )
    );
}
```

---

# 37. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Resource method parsing JWT | Validasi tidak konsisten | Mapper + SecurityContext |
| Principal hanya `String userId` | Tidak support service/delegation | Unified `SecurityActor` |
| Tenant optional diam-diam | Cross-tenant leak | `TenantContext` wajib untuk tenant-scoped endpoint |
| Role dipakai sebagai policy final | Role explosion/overprivilege | Permission + policy |
| Raw token disimpan di principal | Credential leak | Token metadata saja |
| Impersonation overwrite actor | Forensic loss | Actor chain |
| Service token diperlakukan user | Actor confusion | `ActorType` guard |
| Partner tenant dari body | Tenant spoofing | Partner mapping |
| Principal mutable | Race/bug | Immutable record/final |
| Audit ambil actor dari string bebas | Evidence buruk | `AuditActorChain` |

---

# 38. Code Review Checklist

```text id="vjmgkv"
[ ] Resource method tidak parsing JWT.
[ ] Resource method memakai SecurityActor/helper.
[ ] Application service menerima SecurityActor, bukan SecurityContext.
[ ] Principal membedakan USER/SERVICE/PARTNER/ADMIN/SYSTEM.
[ ] TenantContext explicit.
[ ] TokenContext tidak menyimpan raw token.
[ ] TransportContext tidak percaya spoofable header tanpa trusted proxy.
[ ] Delegation menyimpan calling service dan original actor.
[ ] Impersonation menyimpan real actor dan effective actor.
[ ] Permissions/scopes/roles sudah normalized.
[ ] Object-specific ACL tidak dipaksa masuk semua ke principal.
[ ] Principal immutable.
[ ] AuditActorChain lengkap.
[ ] Unit test principal mapper tersedia.
[ ] SecurityContext test tersedia.
```

---

# 39. Minimal Production Invariants

```text id="qbdu16"
1. Token is not principal.
2. Principal is internal normalized identity.
3. SecurityContext carries principal per request.
4. Resource methods must not parse JWT manually.
5. Actor type must be explicit.
6. Tenant context must be explicit.
7. Service/user/partner/delegation/impersonation cannot share ambiguous principal mode.
8. Raw token must never be stored in principal or audit.
9. Actor chain must preserve real, effective, original, and calling service identities.
10. Principal must be immutable and testable.
```

---

# 40. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `SecurityContext` basics | Paham carrier identity JAX-RS |
| 2 | `SecurityActor` interface | Unified principal internal |
| 3 | Actor type model | USER/SERVICE/PARTNER/ADMIN/SYSTEM jelas |
| 4 | Tenant/token/transport context | Context aman dan explicit |
| 5 | Principal mappers | Token vendor-specific dinormalisasi |
| 6 | Delegation/impersonation chain | Actor trace tidak hilang |
| 7 | Resource/service usage | Resource tipis, service pakai `SecurityActor` |
| 8 | Audit mapping | Decision evidence lengkap |
| 9 | Tests | Mapper/security context/actor mode tertutup |
| 10 | Refactor legacy | Hilangkan JWT parsing di resource/service |

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjutkan ke: **Cheatsheet Permission Catalog & Entitlement Data Model in PostgreSQL/MyBatis - roles, permissions, groups, user-role mapping, service permissions, ACL grants, effective permissions, caching, and revocation**.

---

**Sources:**

- [SecurityContext (jakarta.ws.rs-api 3.0.0 API)](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.0/apidocs/jakarta/ws/rs/core/securitycontext?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 5:04:37 AM

# Cheatsheet - Permission Catalog & Entitlement Data Model di PostgreSQL/MyBatis

## 1. Tujuan

Cheatsheet ini fokus ke **model data authorization**: bagaimana menyimpan dan menghitung permission, role, group, service permission, ACL grant, effective permission, cache, dan revocation secara rapi.

Masalah yang ingin dicegah:

```text id="tuz3x7"
- Permission tersebar sebagai hardcoded string tanpa catalog.
- Role menjadi terlalu besar dan tidak least-privilege.
- User-role mapping tidak tenant-aware.
- Service account memakai permission user biasa.
- ACL grant tidak punya expiry/revocation/audit.
- Permission revoke lambat karena cache stale.
- MyBatis query tidak punya boundary yang jelas.
- Tidak bisa menjawab “kenapa user ini punya akses?”
```

Prinsip dasarnya: **role adalah bundle**, **permission adalah capability**, **ACL adalah grant per object**, dan **entitlement adalah effective access yang dihitung dari beberapa sumber**. OWASP Authorization Cheat Sheet menekankan least privilege, deny by default, dan validasi permission pada setiap request; ini berarti model data harus mendukung grant yang eksplisit, terbatas, dapat dicabut, dan dapat diaudit. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Big Picture Data Model

```mermaid id="xlbyav"
flowchart TD
    A[User] --> B[User Role Assignment]
    C[Group] --> D[Group Membership]
    D --> B

    B --> E[Role]
    E --> F[Role Permission]
    F --> G[Permission Catalog]

    H[Service Client] --> I[Service Permission]
    I --> G

    J[Partner Client] --> K[Partner Entitlement]
    K --> G

    L[Resource Object] --> M[ACL Grant]
    A --> M
    C --> M
    E --> M
    H --> M

    G --> N[Effective Permission View / Query]
    M --> N
    I --> N
    K --> N

    N --> O[Authorization Policy]
    O --> P[Authorization Decision]
    P --> Q[Audit Log]
```

---

# 3. Core Concepts

| Concept | Meaning | Stored As |
|---|---|---|
| Permission | Atomic business capability | `app_permission` |
| Role | Bundle permission untuk user/group | `app_role`, `app_role_permission` |
| User role assignment | Role user dalam tenant/scope tertentu | `app_user_role` |
| Group | Kumpulan user | `app_group`, `app_group_member` |
| Group role assignment | Role yang diberikan ke group | `app_group_role` |
| Service permission | Capability milik service account | `service_client_permission` |
| Partner entitlement | Capability milik partner/client | `partner_entitlement` |
| ACL grant | Permission terhadap object spesifik | `resource_acl` |
| Effective permission | Permission hasil gabungan role/group/service/direct | query/view/cache |
| Revocation | Pencabutan grant/role/ACL/token | status, `revoked_at`, version increment |
| Permission version | Versi entitlement untuk cache invalidation | `subject_entitlement_version` |

---

# 4. Permission Catalog

Permission catalog adalah sumber kebenaran daftar capability.

```sql id="ath1ye"
CREATE TABLE app_permission (
    code            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    domain          TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_type   TEXT NOT NULL,
    action          TEXT NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT NOT NULL,
    risk_level      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NORMAL',
    active          BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_permission_code_format
        CHECK (code ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*(\.[a-z][a-z0-9_]*)+$'),

    CONSTRAINT chk_permission_risk_level
        CHECK (risk_level IN ('LOW', 'NORMAL', 'HIGH', 'CRITICAL'))
);
```

PostgreSQL constraints memberi kontrol terhadap data di tabel, termasuk check constraint dan foreign key, sehingga cocok untuk menjaga permission catalog tetap konsisten. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-constraints.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh data:

```sql id="i5h6sh"
INSERT INTO app_permission
(code, domain, resource_type, action, description, risk_level)
VALUES
('case.read', 'case-management', 'case', 'read', 'Read visible cases', 'NORMAL'),
('case.read.all', 'case-management', 'case', 'read_all', 'Read all cases in tenant', 'HIGH'),
('case.approve', 'case-management', 'case', 'approve', 'Approve submitted case', 'HIGH'),
('workflow.task.complete', 'workflow', 'task', 'complete', 'Complete workflow task', 'NORMAL'),
('admin.user.disable', 'admin', 'user', 'disable', 'Disable user account', 'CRITICAL');
```

---

# 5. Permission Naming Rules

```text id="q3kfe0"
Recommended:
  <resource>.<action>
  <domain>.<resource>.<action> for larger systems

Examples:
  case.read
  case.read.all
  case.approve
  workflow.task.complete
  admin.user.disable
  internal.case.reindex
```

Jangan:

```text id="zvuxkm"
show_button_approve
can_click_menu
admin
superuser
case_access
```

Permission harus menggambarkan **capability bisnis**, bukan detail UI.

---

# 6. Role Model

Role adalah bundle permission, bukan policy final.

```sql id="zbf5zq"
CREATE TABLE app_role (
    code          TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_scoped BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    description   TEXT NOT NULL,
    active        BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE app_role_permission (
    role_code       TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app_role(code),
    permission_code TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app_permission(code),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by      TEXT NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (role_code, permission_code)
);
```

Contoh:

```sql id="zlp6ga"
INSERT INTO app_role (code, description)
VALUES
('CASE_REVIEWER', 'Can review assigned cases'),
('CASE_SUPERVISOR', 'Can supervise case review and approval'),
('TENANT_ADMIN', 'Can administer tenant users and roles');

INSERT INTO app_role_permission (role_code, permission_code, created_by)
VALUES
('CASE_REVIEWER', 'case.read', 'system'),
('CASE_REVIEWER', 'workflow.task.complete', 'system'),
('CASE_SUPERVISOR', 'case.read.all', 'system'),
('CASE_SUPERVISOR', 'case.approve', 'system'),
('TENANT_ADMIN', 'admin.user.disable', 'system');
```

---

# 7. User Role Assignment

Role assignment harus tenant-aware.

```sql id="gpuyqj"
CREATE TABLE app_user_role (
    tenant_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    user_id     TEXT NOT NULL,
    role_code   TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app_role(code),
    assigned_by TEXT NOT NULL,
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    revoked_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    revoked_by  TEXT NULL,
    reason      TEXT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, user_id, role_code)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_user_role_lookup
ON app_user_role (tenant_id, user_id)
WHERE revoked_at IS NULL;
```

Kenapa `expires_at` dan `revoked_at` penting?

```text id="9qw1eu"
- Temporary access.
- Emergency/support access.
- Clean revocation.
- Audit defensibility.
- Cache invalidation trigger.
```

---

# 8. Group Model

Group berguna untuk menghindari role assignment satu per satu.

```sql id="7nceya"
CREATE TABLE app_group (
    tenant_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    group_id    TEXT NOT NULL,
    name        TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT NULL,
    active      BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, group_id)
);

CREATE TABLE app_group_member (
    tenant_id  TEXT NOT NULL,
    group_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    user_id    TEXT NOT NULL,
    added_by   TEXT NOT NULL,
    added_at   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    removed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, group_id, user_id),

    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, group_id)
        REFERENCES app_group(tenant_id, group_id)
);

CREATE TABLE app_group_role (
    tenant_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    group_id    TEXT NOT NULL,
    role_code   TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app_role(code),
    assigned_by TEXT NOT NULL,
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    revoked_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, group_id, role_code),

    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, group_id)
        REFERENCES app_group(tenant_id, group_id)
);
```

Group-effective permission:

```text id="h8xyph"
user -> group membership -> group role -> role permission -> permission
```

---

# 9. Direct User Permission Grant

Gunakan direct permission dengan hati-hati. Ini berguna untuk exception/temporary grant, tetapi bisa membuat governance sulit jika berlebihan.

```sql id="sqvk1s"
CREATE TABLE app_user_permission_grant (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    user_id         TEXT NOT NULL,
    permission_code TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app_permission(code),
    granted_by      TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    revoked_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    revoked_by      TEXT NULL,
    reason          TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX idx_user_permission_grant_active
ON app_user_permission_grant (tenant_id, user_id, permission_code)
WHERE revoked_at IS NULL;
```

Rule:

```text id="kbl1wi"
Role assignment = normal access.
Direct grant = exception access.
ACL = object-specific access.
```

---

# 10. Service Client Permission

Service permission harus dipisahkan dari user permission.

```sql id="w5249b"
CREATE TABLE service_client (
    client_id       TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    service_name    TEXT NOT NULL,
    active          BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    require_mtls    BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    allowed_tenants TEXT[] NULL,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE service_client_permission (
    client_id       TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES service_client(client_id),
    permission_code TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app_permission(code),
    granted_by      TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    revoked_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (client_id, permission_code)
);
```

Contoh permission service:

```sql id="h5enra"
INSERT INTO app_permission
(code, domain, resource_type, action, description, risk_level)
VALUES
('internal.case.reindex', 'internal', 'case', 'reindex', 'Reindex case search document', 'HIGH'),
('internal.order.reconcile', 'internal', 'order', 'reconcile', 'Run order reconciliation', 'HIGH');

INSERT INTO service_client (client_id, service_name, require_mtls, allowed_tenants)
VALUES
('case-worker-service', 'case-worker-service', true, ARRAY['tenant-a', 'tenant-b']);

INSERT INTO service_client_permission (client_id, permission_code, granted_by)
VALUES
('case-worker-service', 'internal.case.reindex', 'system');
```

---

# 11. Partner Entitlement

Partner client biasanya punya allowed tenant mapping dan permission/scope terbatas.

```sql id="jjc3p6"
CREATE TABLE partner_client (
    client_id      TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    partner_id     TEXT NOT NULL,
    display_name   TEXT NOT NULL,
    active         BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE partner_tenant_mapping (
    client_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES partner_client(client_id),
    external_tenant_ref TEXT NOT NULL,
    tenant_id           TEXT NOT NULL,
    active              BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (client_id, external_tenant_ref)
);

CREATE TABLE partner_entitlement (
    client_id       TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES partner_client(client_id),
    permission_code TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app_permission(code),
    granted_by      TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    revoked_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (client_id, permission_code)
);
```

Rule:

```text id="f1i7zr"
Partner request body may contain externalTenantRef.
Internal tenantId must come from partner_tenant_mapping.
```

---

# 12. ACL Grant Model

ACL dipakai untuk akses object-specific.

```sql id="3ad338"
CREATE TABLE resource_acl (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_type   TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_id     TEXT NOT NULL,

    subject_type    TEXT NOT NULL,
    subject_id      TEXT NOT NULL,

    permission_code TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app_permission(code),

    granted_by      TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    revoked_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    revoked_by      TEXT NULL,
    reason          TEXT NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT chk_acl_subject_type
        CHECK (subject_type IN ('USER', 'GROUP', 'ROLE', 'SERVICE', 'PARTNER'))
);

CREATE INDEX idx_resource_acl_lookup
ON resource_acl (
    tenant_id,
    resource_type,
    resource_id,
    subject_type,
    subject_id,
    permission_code
)
WHERE revoked_at IS NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_resource_acl_subject
ON resource_acl (
    tenant_id,
    subject_type,
    subject_id,
    permission_code
)
WHERE revoked_at IS NULL;
```

Contoh:

```sql id="2ed95h"
INSERT INTO resource_acl
(id, tenant_id, resource_type, resource_id, subject_type, subject_id,
 permission_code, granted_by, reason)
VALUES
(gen_random_uuid(), 'tenant-a', 'case', 'CASE-789', 'USER', 'user-123',
 'case.read', 'supervisor-1', 'temporary case review');
```

---

# 13. Effective Permissions - Query Strategy

Effective permission bisa dihitung dari:

```text id="jn63jw"
1. direct user role
2. group role
3. direct user permission grant
4. service client permission
5. partner entitlement
6. ACL object grant
```

Untuk user global/tenant permission:

```sql id="uplxxq"
SELECT DISTINCT rp.permission_code
FROM app_user_role ur
JOIN app_role_permission rp
  ON rp.role_code = ur.role_code
JOIN app_permission p
  ON p.code = rp.permission_code
WHERE ur.tenant_id = :tenantId
  AND ur.user_id = :userId
  AND ur.revoked_at IS NULL
  AND (ur.expires_at IS NULL OR ur.expires_at > now())
  AND p.active = true

UNION

SELECT DISTINCT grp.permission_code
FROM app_group_member gm
JOIN app_group_role gr
  ON gr.tenant_id = gm.tenant_id
 AND gr.group_id = gm.group_id
JOIN app_role_permission grp
  ON grp.role_code = gr.role_code
JOIN app_permission p
  ON p.code = grp.permission_code
WHERE gm.tenant_id = :tenantId
  AND gm.user_id = :userId
  AND gm.removed_at IS NULL
  AND gr.revoked_at IS NULL
  AND p.active = true

UNION

SELECT DISTINCT upg.permission_code
FROM app_user_permission_grant upg
JOIN app_permission p
  ON p.code = upg.permission_code
WHERE upg.tenant_id = :tenantId
  AND upg.user_id = :userId
  AND upg.revoked_at IS NULL
  AND (upg.expires_at IS NULL OR upg.expires_at > now())
  AND p.active = true;
```

---

# 14. Effective Permissions - MyBatis Mapper

MyBatis mapper XML cocok untuk query explicit seperti ini. MyBatis dynamic SQL menyediakan elemen seperti `if`, `choose`, `where`, `set`, `trim`, dan `foreach` untuk membangun SQL kondisional secara aman dibanding string concatenation manual. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/dynamic-sql.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Mapper interface:

```java id="806pds"
public interface EntitlementMapper {

    Set<String> findEffectiveUserPermissions(
            @Param("tenantId") String tenantId,
            @Param("userId") String userId
    );

    Set<String> findEffectiveServicePermissions(
            @Param("clientId") String clientId
    );

    boolean existsActiveAclGrant(
            AclGrantLookup lookup
    );
}
```

XML:

```xml id="wx62vo"
<select id="findEffectiveUserPermissions" resultType="string">
    SELECT DISTINCT permission_code
    FROM (
        SELECT rp.permission_code
        FROM app_user_role ur
        JOIN app_role_permission rp
          ON rp.role_code = ur.role_code
        JOIN app_permission p
          ON p.code = rp.permission_code
        WHERE ur.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND ur.user_id = #{userId}
          AND ur.revoked_at IS NULL
          AND (ur.expires_at IS NULL OR ur.expires_at > now())
          AND p.active = true

        UNION

        SELECT grp.permission_code
        FROM app_group_member gm
        JOIN app_group_role gr
          ON gr.tenant_id = gm.tenant_id
         AND gr.group_id = gm.group_id
        JOIN app_role_permission grp
          ON grp.role_code = gr.role_code
        JOIN app_permission p
          ON p.code = grp.permission_code
        WHERE gm.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND gm.user_id = #{userId}
          AND gm.removed_at IS NULL
          AND gr.revoked_at IS NULL
          AND p.active = true

        UNION

        SELECT upg.permission_code
        FROM app_user_permission_grant upg
        JOIN app_permission p
          ON p.code = upg.permission_code
        WHERE upg.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND upg.user_id = #{userId}
          AND upg.revoked_at IS NULL
          AND (upg.expires_at IS NULL OR upg.expires_at > now())
          AND p.active = true
    ) effective_permissions
</select>
```

---

# 15. ACL Lookup with Subject References

ACL lookup perlu mengecek user, group, role, service, atau partner subject.

```java id="5wktmz"
public record SubjectReference(
        String subjectType,
        String subjectId
) {}
```

```java id="meyf5c"
public record AclGrantLookup(
        String tenantId,
        String resourceType,
        String resourceId,
        String permissionCode,
        List<SubjectReference> subjects
) {}
```

Mapper:

```xml id="35w3s8"
<select id="existsActiveAclGrant" resultType="boolean">
    SELECT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM resource_acl acl
        WHERE acl.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND acl.resource_type = #{resourceType}
          AND acl.resource_id = #{resourceId}
          AND acl.permission_code = #{permissionCode}
          AND acl.revoked_at IS NULL
          AND (acl.expires_at IS NULL OR acl.expires_at > now())
          AND (
              <foreach collection="subjects"
                       item="s"
                       separator=" OR ">
                  (acl.subject_type = #{s.subjectType}
                   AND acl.subject_id = #{s.subjectId})
              </foreach>
          )
    )
</select>
```

**Caveat:** jika `subjects` bisa kosong, handle di Java sebelum query. Jangan hasilkan SQL `AND ()`.

---

# 16. Subject Reference Builder

```java id="ey01ku"
public final class SubjectReferenceBuilder {

    public List<SubjectReference> buildForUser(
            String userId,
            Set<String> groupIds,
            Set<String> roleCodes
    ) {
        List<SubjectReference> refs = new ArrayList<>();

        refs.add(new SubjectReference("USER", userId));

        for (String groupId : groupIds) {
            refs.add(new SubjectReference("GROUP", groupId));
        }

        for (String roleCode : roleCodes) {
            refs.add(new SubjectReference("ROLE", roleCode));
        }

        return List.copyOf(refs);
    }

    public List<SubjectReference> buildForService(String clientId, String serviceName) {
        return List.of(
                new SubjectReference("SERVICE", clientId),
                new SubjectReference("SERVICE", serviceName)
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 17. Entitlement Service Layer

```java id="z3djp9"
public final class EntitlementService {

    private final EntitlementMapper entitlementMapper;
    private final EntitlementCache entitlementCache;

    public Set<String> effectiveUserPermissions(String tenantId, String userId) {
        String cacheKey = "user:%s:%s".formatted(tenantId, userId);

        return entitlementCache.get(cacheKey)
                .orElseGet(() -> {
                    Set<String> permissions =
                            entitlementMapper.findEffectiveUserPermissions(tenantId, userId);

                    entitlementCache.put(cacheKey, permissions);

                    return permissions;
                });
    }

    public Set<String> effectiveServicePermissions(String clientId) {
        String cacheKey = "service:%s".formatted(clientId);

        return entitlementCache.get(cacheKey)
                .orElseGet(() -> {
                    Set<String> permissions =
                            entitlementMapper.findEffectiveServicePermissions(clientId);

                    entitlementCache.put(cacheKey, permissions);

                    return permissions;
                });
    }
}
```

**Rule:** entitlement cache harus punya invalidation strategy, bukan hanya TTL panjang.

---

# 18. Permission Versioning for Cache Invalidation

Buat versi entitlement per subject.

```sql id="jkr14n"
CREATE TABLE subject_entitlement_version (
    subject_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    tenant_id    TEXT NULL,
    subject_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    version      BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    updated_at   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (subject_type, tenant_id, subject_id)
);
```

Increment saat role/permission/group/ACL berubah:

```sql id="41r5ic"
INSERT INTO subject_entitlement_version
(subject_type, tenant_id, subject_id, version, updated_at)
VALUES
(:subjectType, :tenantId, :subjectId, 1, now())
ON CONFLICT (subject_type, tenant_id, subject_id)
DO UPDATE
SET version = subject_entitlement_version.version + 1,
    updated_at = now();
```

Cache key:

```text id="fzv7lc"
entitlement:user:{tenantId}:{userId}:v{version}
entitlement:service:{clientId}:v{version}
acl:{tenantId}:{resourceType}:{resourceId}:{subjectHash}:v{version}
```

---

# 19. Revocation Model

Revocation harus memengaruhi:

```text id="9e8e3k"
- user role assignment
- group membership
- group role assignment
- direct permission grant
- service permission
- partner entitlement
- ACL grant
- token/session if required
- cache entries
```

Soft revoke pattern:

```sql id="heezf6"
UPDATE app_user_role
SET revoked_at = now(),
    revoked_by = :revokedBy,
    reason = :reason
WHERE tenant_id = :tenantId
  AND user_id = :userId
  AND role_code = :roleCode
  AND revoked_at IS NULL;
```

Then:

```sql id="to1v73"
-- increment subject entitlement version
```

Audit:

```json id="zv00af"
{
  "eventType": "ROLE_REVOKED",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "targetSubjectType": "USER",
  "targetSubjectId": "user-123",
  "roleCode": "CASE_SUPERVISOR",
  "changedBy": "admin-777",
  "reason": "user moved department",
  "oldValue": "ACTIVE",
  "newValue": "REVOKED"
}
```

---

# 20. Expiry vs Revocation

| Mechanism | Meaning | Use Case |
|---|---|---|
| `expires_at` | Grant otomatis tidak aktif setelah waktu tertentu | temporary access |
| `revoked_at` | Grant dicabut secara eksplisit | admin revoke |
| `active=false` | Catalog/client/role disabled | deactivate role/client |
| version increment | cache invalidation | permission changed |
| token revocation | invalidate issued token | urgent security incident |

Query active grant selalu:

```sql id="ghmr1d"
revoked_at IS NULL
AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now())
```

---

# 21. Effective Permission Cache Strategy

| Data | Cache TTL | Invalidation |
|---|---:|---|
| Permission catalog | Long | version/deploy/admin change |
| Role-permission mapping | Medium | role change event |
| User effective permission | Short/medium | subject version increment |
| Service permission | Medium | service registry change |
| ACL decision | Short | ACL change/resource assignment event |
| Sensitive write allow | Avoid/very short | fresh check preferred |

Rule:

```text id="uz65n0"
Read/list can tolerate short-lived cache.
Admin/sensitive write should use fresh or version-validated entitlement.
```

---

# 22. Cache Invalidation Event

```json id="13tifg"
{
  "eventType": "ENTITLEMENT_CHANGED",
  "subjectType": "USER",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "subjectId": "user-123",
  "newVersion": 42,
  "changedBy": "admin-777",
  "changeReason": "ROLE_REVOKED",
  "changedAt": "2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00"
}
```

Consumer:

```java id="8q6qv0"
public void onEntitlementChanged(EntitlementChangedEvent event) {
    entitlementCache.evict(
            event.subjectType(),
            event.tenantId(),
            event.subjectId()
    );

    versionCache.put(
            event.subjectType(),
            event.tenantId(),
            event.subjectId(),
            event.newVersion()
    );
}
```

---

# 23. Permission Resolution at Authentication Time vs Request Time

| Timing | Pros | Cons | Suitable For |
|---|---|---|---|
| At token issuance | Fast resource server | Stale until token expiry | coarse scopes |
| At authentication filter | Principal contains permissions | Extra DB/cache call per request | medium complexity |
| At authorization decision | Fresh context | More latency | sensitive actions |
| Hybrid | Balanced | More complex | enterprise systems |

Recommended hybrid:

```text id="9xjio2"
Token:
  scopes + subject + tenant + client identity

Authentication filter:
  normalize principal

Authorization:
  load effective permissions from cache/version

Sensitive object action:
  fresh entitlement/ACL/object policy check
```

---

# 24. MyBatis Mapper for Service Permissions

```xml id="0f4ybt"
<select id="findEffectiveServicePermissions" resultType="string">
    SELECT scp.permission_code
    FROM service_client_permission scp
    JOIN service_client sc
      ON sc.client_id = scp.client_id
    JOIN app_permission p
      ON p.code = scp.permission_code
    WHERE scp.client_id = #{clientId}
      AND sc.active = true
      AND scp.revoked_at IS NULL
      AND p.active = true
</select>
```

Service client lookup:

```xml id="jwjep5"
<select id="findActiveServiceClient" resultMap="ServiceClientMap">
    SELECT
        client_id,
        service_name,
        active,
        require_mtls,
        allowed_tenants
    FROM service_client
    WHERE client_id = #{clientId}
      AND active = true
</select>
```

```xml id="z850jd"
<select id="findPartnerPermissions" resultType="string">
    SELECT pe.permission_code
    FROM partner_entitlement pe
    JOIN partner_client pc
      ON pc.client_id = pe.client_id
    JOIN app_permission p
      ON p.code = pe.permission_code
    WHERE pe.client_id = #{clientId}
      AND pc.active = true

      AND p.active = true
</select>
```

```xml id="4dr6jv"
<select id="resolvePartnerTenant" resultType="string">
    SELECT tenant_id
    FROM partner_tenant_mapping
    WHERE client_id = #{clientId}
      AND external_tenant_ref = #{externalTenantRef}
      AND active = true
</select>
```

# 26. Materialized View Option

Untuk high-volume read permission, bisa materialize effective permissions.

```sql id="eghrls"
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_effective_user_permission AS
SELECT DISTINCT
    ur.tenant_id,
    ur.user_id,
    rp.permission_code
FROM app_user_role ur
JOIN app_role_permission rp
  ON rp.role_code = ur.role_code
JOIN app_permission p
  ON p.code = rp.permission_code
WHERE ur.revoked_at IS NULL
  AND (ur.expires_at IS NULL OR ur.expires_at > now())
  AND p.active = true

UNION

SELECT DISTINCT
    gm.tenant_id,
    gm.user_id,
    grp.permission_code
FROM app_group_member gm
JOIN app_group_role gr
  ON gr.tenant_id = gm.tenant_id
 AND gr.group_id = gm.group_id
JOIN app_role_permission grp
  ON grp.role_code = gr.role_code
JOIN app_permission p
  ON p.code = grp.permission_code
WHERE gm.removed_at IS NULL
  AND gr.revoked_at IS NULL
  AND p.active = true;
```

Index:

```sql id="0x4ed4"
CREATE INDEX idx_mv_effective_user_permission_lookup
ON mv_effective_user_permission (tenant_id, user_id, permission_code);
```

**Caveat:** materialized view perlu refresh strategy. Karena `expires_at > now()` berubah seiring waktu, refresh period dan stale behavior harus jelas. Untuk sensitive action, jangan bergantung pada materialized view stale.

---

# 27. Row-Level Security Option

PostgreSQL Row Security Policies bisa membatasi row mana yang dikembalikan, dimasukkan, diubah, atau dihapus oleh query normal. Jika row security diaktifkan di tabel dan tidak ada policy, default-nya deny-all; policy dapat dibuat dengan `CREATE POLICY`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-rowsecurity.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh defense-in-depth tenant policy:

```sql id="0dqih8"
ALTER TABLE cases ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY cases_tenant_policy
ON cases
USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id'));
```

Dalam transaction:

```sql id="92x408"
SET LOCAL app.tenant_id = 'tenant-a';
```

Caveat:

```text id="k23iao"
- Connection pool harus disiplin transaction boundary.
- SET LOCAL hanya berlaku dalam transaction.
- App tetap perlu policy/service-level authorization.
- RLS bagus sebagai defense-in-depth, bukan pengganti authorization model.
```

---

# 28. Permission Catalog Migration Strategy

Permission catalog harus versioned dan migratable.

```sql id="2r5sn5"
CREATE TABLE permission_catalog_version (
    version       TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    description   TEXT NOT NULL,
    applied_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    applied_by    TEXT NOT NULL
);
```

Migration example:

```sql id="rsxahj"
BEGIN;

INSERT INTO app_permission
(code, domain, resource_type, action, description, risk_level)
VALUES
('case.reopen', 'case-management', 'case', 'reopen', 'Reopen closed case', 'HIGH');

INSERT INTO app_role_permission
(role_code, permission_code, created_by)
VALUES
('CASE_SUPERVISOR', 'case.reopen', 'migration-2026-07-13');

INSERT INTO permission_catalog_version
(version, description, applied_by)
VALUES
('2026.07.13.001', 'Add case.reopen permission', 'liquibase');

COMMIT;
```

Untuk rollback, revoke mapping dulu, jangan langsung hapus permission jika audit historis masih butuh referensi.

```sql id="47v5px"
CREATE TABLE entitlement_audit_event (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    event_time      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    event_type      TEXT NOT NULL,

    tenant_id       TEXT NULL,
    subject_type    TEXT NULL,

    resource_type   TEXT NULL,
    resource_id     TEXT NULL,

    permission_code TEXT NULL,

);

CREATE INDEX idx_entitlement_audit_subject_time
ON entitlement_audit_event (tenant_id, subject_type, subject_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_entitlement_audit_resource_time
ON entitlement_audit_event (tenant_id, resource_type, resource_id, event_time DESC);
```

Events:

```text id="8oayw0"
ROLE_ASSIGNED
ROLE_REVOKED
GROUP_MEMBER_ADDED
GROUP_MEMBER_REMOVED
GROUP_ROLE_ASSIGNED
GROUP_ROLE_REVOKED
USER_PERMISSION_GRANTED
USER_PERMISSION_REVOKED
SERVICE_PERMISSION_GRANTED
SERVICE_PERMISSION_REVOKED
ACL_GRANT_CREATED
ACL_GRANT_REVOKED
PARTNER_ENTITLEMENT_GRANTED
PARTNER_ENTITLEMENT_REVOKED
```

# 30. Grant/Revoke Service Example

```java id="gxvjin"
public final class RoleAdministrationService {

    private final RoleMapper roleMapper;
    private final EntitlementVersionService versionService;
    private final EntitlementAuditLogger auditLogger;

    public void assignRole(
            SecurityActor admin,
            String tenantId,
            String userId,
            String roleCode,
            String reason
    ) {
        requireAdminPermission(admin, "admin.role.assign");

        roleMapper.insertUserRole(
                tenantId,
                userId,
                roleCode,
                admin.effectiveActor().subject(),
                reason
        );

        versionService.increment("USER", tenantId, userId);

        auditLogger.logRoleAssigned(
                admin,
                tenantId,
                userId,
                roleCode,
                reason
        );
    }

    public void revokeRole(
            SecurityActor admin,
            String tenantId,
            String userId,
            String roleCode,
            String reason
    ) {
        requireAdminPermission(admin, "admin.role.revoke");

        int updated = roleMapper.revokeUserRole(
                tenantId,
                userId,
                roleCode,
                admin.effectiveActor().subject(),
                reason
        );

        if (updated > 0) {
            versionService.increment("USER", tenantId, userId);
            auditLogger.logRoleRevoked(admin, tenantId, userId, roleCode, reason);
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 31. Deny-by-Default Query Pattern

Entitlement query should return empty if nothing explicit exists.

```java id="04jpbs"
public boolean hasPermission(String tenantId, String userId, String permission) {
    return entitlementMapper.findEffectiveUserPermissions(tenantId, userId)
            .contains(permission);
}
```

If no rows:

```text id="zn5rwa"
no permissions
  -> deny
```

Do not create fallback like:

```java id="qddw7c"
if (permissions.isEmpty()) {
    return true; // bad
}
```

---

# 32. Performance Considerations

| Problem | Strategy |
|---|---|
| Many group memberships | index `(tenant_id, user_id)`, precompute membership |
| Many permissions per role | cache role-permission mapping |
| Heavy ACL checks | subject refs + composite indexes |
| List visibility | enforce in query, avoid per-row PDP |
| Frequent revocation | versioned cache invalidation |
| Materialized effective permission | refresh/incremental strategy |
| Large `IN` list in ACL | temp table or join table |
| Multi-tenant scale | partitioning by tenant if needed |
| Sensitive action | fresh check despite cache |

PostgreSQL indexes and constraints are part of schema design; MyBatis mapper XML keeps SQL explicit, which is useful when authorization predicates must be inspectable during code review. MyBatis Mapper XML documentation describes mapped statements as the central mechanism where SQL statements are defined. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 33. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Permission hardcoded tanpa catalog | Tidak bisa audit/governance | `app_permission` |
| Role `ADMIN` terlalu luas | Overprivilege | specific permission |
| User-role tanpa tenant | Cross-tenant privilege | `tenant_id` wajib |
| Direct grant dipakai normal | Chaos/governance sulit | role/group first |
| ACL tanpa expiry/revoked_at | Akses lama terlupakan | lifecycle fields |
| Service pakai user permission | Actor confusion | service permission namespace |
| Partner tenant dari body | Tenant spoofing | mapping table |
| Cache permission TTL panjang | Revocation lambat | version invalidation |
| Hapus permission historis | Audit rusak | deactivate instead |
| Query active grant lupa `revoked_at` | Revoked access masih aktif | active predicate standard |

---

# 34. Code Review Checklist

```text id="tbzdq4"
[ ] Permission baru masuk app_permission.
[ ] Permission name domain-oriented, bukan UI-oriented.
[ ] Role baru hanya bundle permission yang perlu.
[ ] User role assignment selalu tenant-aware.
[ ] Temporary access punya expires_at.
[ ] Revoke memakai revoked_at, bukan hard delete.
[ ] Entitlement change meng-increment subject version.
[ ] Entitlement change membuat audit event.
[ ] Service permission memakai namespace internal/system.
[ ] Partner entitlement memakai tenant mapping tepercaya.
[ ] ACL grant punya tenant/resource/subject/permission/reason.
[ ] ACL lookup mengecek revoked_at dan expires_at.
[ ] Cache key mencakup tenant/subject/version.
[ ] Sensitive action tidak hanya bergantung pada stale cache.
[ ] MyBatis query punya index yang mendukung lookup.
```

---

# 35. Testing Matrix

```text id="br4dz6"
[ ] User gets permission from direct role.
[ ] User gets permission from group role.
[ ] User gets permission from direct temporary grant.
[ ] Expired user role does not grant permission.
[ ] Revoked user role does not grant permission.
[ ] Removed group membership removes group permissions.
[ ] Disabled permission is not effective.
[ ] Service gets only service permissions.
[ ] Service cannot use user role permissions.
[ ] Partner client resolves tenant via mapping.
[ ] Partner cannot access unmapped tenant.
[ ] ACL grants object access to user.
[ ] ACL grants object access through group.
[ ] Expired ACL does not grant access.
[ ] Revoked ACL does not grant access.
[ ] Entitlement version increments after role revoke.
[ ] Cache invalidates after entitlement change.
[ ] Deny by default when no grants exist.
[ ] Audit event emitted for grant/revoke.
```

---

# 36. Minimal Production Invariants

```text id="lt4aqk"
1. Permission catalog is the source of truth.
2. Role is a bundle, not final policy.
3. User-role assignment must be tenant-aware.
4. Service permissions must be separate from user permissions.
5. ACL grants must be object-specific, expirable, revocable, and audited.
6. Revocation must update entitlement version.
7. Cache key must include tenant, subject, and version.
8. Sensitive actions should use fresh or version-validated entitlement.
9. No explicit grant means deny.
10. Entitlement changes must be auditable.
```

---

# 37. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Permission catalog | Capability bisnis terdokumentasi |
| 2 | Role-permission model | Role sebagai bundle permission |
| 3 | User/group mapping | User dan group mendapat role tenant-aware |
| 4 | Direct grant | Temporary/exception access terkendali |
| 5 | Service/partner entitlement | Non-user actor punya permission terpisah |
| 6 | ACL grant | Object-specific access bisa dikelola |
| 7 | Effective permission query | Permission bisa dihitung konsisten |
| 8 | Revocation/versioning | Cache bisa invalidated cepat |
| 9 | Audit | Grant/revoke defensible |
| 10 | Tests/performance | Query aman, cepat, dan regresi tertutup |
