## Response:
7/13/2026, 5:08:28 AM

# Cheatsheet - Authorization Cache, Revocation, dan Consistency Strategy di Jersey/JAX-RS Microservices

## 1. Tujuan

Authorization cache dipakai untuk menurunkan latency dan beban DB/PDP, tetapi cache juga bisa membuat akses yang sudah dicabut tetap aktif.

Problem utama:

```text id="cugvvu"
User permission dicabut.
Role diubah.
ACL grant expired.
Service permission direvoke.
Token masih valid.
Cache masih menyimpan old entitlement.
Request sensitive action tetap allow.
```

Prinsip dasarnya: permission harus divalidasi pada setiap request, least privilege, dan deny-by-default. OWASP Authorization Cheat Sheet menekankan tiga prinsip ini secara eksplisit; artinya cache boleh membantu performa, tetapi tidak boleh mengubah revoked/unknown access menjadi allow. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model

```text id="4n6t9e"
Authorization data has freshness requirements.

Not all authorization data needs same freshness.

Read/list:
  can tolerate short cache if risk low

Sensitive write/admin:
  needs fresh or version-validated check

Revocation:
  must be propagated quickly

PDP failure:
  must not become fail-open
```

Cache strategy harus menjawab:

```text id="xhdp4g"
Apa yang di-cache?
Berapa lama?
Bagaimana invalidasi?
Bagaimana kalau event invalidation hilang?
Bagaimana kalau cache stale?
Action mana yang wajib fresh check?
Apa fallback saat entitlement/PDP/cache down?
```

---

# 3. Diagram Besar

```mermaid id="0f2gdc"
flowchart TD
    A[Request] --> B[Authentication Filter]
    B --> C[SecurityActor]

    C --> D[Authorization Service]
    D --> E{Cache Lookup}

    E -->|Hit + Fresh| F[Use Cached Entitlement]
    E -->|Miss / Stale| G[Load Entitlement Source]

    G --> H[(PostgreSQL Entitlement Tables)]
    G --> I[External PDP / Entitlement Service]
    G --> J[ACL / ReBAC Store]

    H --> K[Computed Entitlement]
    I --> K
    J --> K

    K --> L[Store Cache with Version + TTL]
    L --> M[Policy Decision]

    F --> M
    M --> N{Sensitive Action?}

    N -->|No| O[Allow / Deny]
    N -->|Yes| P[Fresh / Version-Validated Check]

    P --> O
    O --> Q[Audit Decision]

    R[Role/ACL/Permission Change] --> S[Increment Entitlement Version]
    S --> T[Publish Invalidation Event]
    T --> U[Cache Evict]
```

---

# 4. Apa Saja yang Bisa Di-cache?

| Data | Cache? | Risiko | Strategy |
|---|---:|---|---|
| JWKS public keys | Ya | key rotation delay | TTL + refresh on unknown `kid` |
| Permission catalog | Ya | permission disabled masih aktif | versioned catalog |
| Role-permission mapping | Ya | role change stale | version/invalidation |
| Effective user permissions | Ya | revoked access masih allow | short TTL + subject version |
| Service permissions | Ya | compromised service still allowed | version + short/medium TTL |
| Partner entitlements | Ya | partner revoked but still allowed | version + short TTL |
| ACL object grant | Hati-hati | object access stale | short TTL + resource invalidation |
| Authorization allow decision | Sangat hati-hati | stale allow | avoid for sensitive writes |
| Authorization deny decision | Hati-hati | newly granted access delayed | short TTL |
| Object visibility list | Hati-hati | list/count leak | cache by actor+tenant+version+query |
| Token introspection result | Ya, pendek | revoked token still active | very short TTL / active check for sensitive |

OAuth 2.0 Token Introspection RFC 7662 mendefinisikan cara resource server menanyakan status aktif token dan metadata token ke authorization server; ini berguna untuk opaque token atau ketika freshness/revocation penting. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7662?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Cache Classification

Gunakan klasifikasi berdasarkan risiko.

```java id="zcdiar"
public enum AuthorizationFreshnessLevel {
    LOW_RISK_CACHED,
    VERSION_VALIDATED,
    FRESH_REQUIRED
}
```

Mapping action:

| Action | Freshness |
|---|---|
| `case.read` | `LOW_RISK_CACHED` atau `VERSION_VALIDATED` |
| `case.list` | `VERSION_VALIDATED` |
| `case.approve` | `FRESH_REQUIRED` |
| `admin.user.disable` | `FRESH_REQUIRED` |
| `admin.role.assign` | `FRESH_REQUIRED` |
| `internal.case.reindex` | `VERSION_VALIDATED` |
| `acl.grant.create` | `FRESH_REQUIRED` |
| `breakglass.access` | `FRESH_REQUIRED` |

Rule:

```text id="0k4y0n"
Semakin irreversible/high-risk action, semakin fresh authorization check.
```

---

# 6. TTL Strategy

| Data | Suggested TTL | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Permission catalog | 5-60 menit | version/invalidation preferred |
| Role-permission mapping | 1-10 menit | invalidate on role edit |
| User effective permission | 30 detik-5 menit | tergantung revocation SLA |
| Service permission | 1-10 menit | service changes jarang |
| Partner entitlement | 30 detik-5 menit | partner risk tinggi |
| ACL decision | 5-60 detik | object access volatile |
| Token introspection active state | 5-30 detik | sensitive action bisa no-cache |
| Negative decision | 5-30 detik | agar grant baru tidak terlalu delay |
| List/search response | sangat pendek | include query+actor+version |

**Catatan:** angka TTL harus mengikuti risk appetite, SLA revocation, dan traffic profile, bukan angka universal.

---

# 7. Versioned Entitlement Cache

TTL saja tidak cukup. Tambahkan version.

```sql id="jkr14n"
CREATE TABLE subject_entitlement_version (
    subject_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    tenant_id    TEXT NULL,
    subject_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    version      BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    updated_at   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (subject_type, tenant_id, subject_id)
);
```

Cache key:

```text id="28qev6"
entitlement:user:{tenantId}:{userId}:v{version}
entitlement:service:{clientId}:v{version}
entitlement:partner:{clientId}:v{version}
acl:{tenantId}:{resourceType}:{resourceId}:{subjectHash}:v{subjectVersion}:rv{resourceVersion}
```

Kalau version berubah, cache lama otomatis tidak dipakai.

---

# 8. Increment Version Saat Entitlement Berubah

```sql id="41r5ic"
INSERT INTO subject_entitlement_version
(subject_type, tenant_id, subject_id, version, updated_at)
VALUES
(:subjectType, :tenantId, :subjectId, 1, now())
ON CONFLICT (subject_type, tenant_id, subject_id)
DO UPDATE
SET version = subject_entitlement_version.version + 1,
    updated_at = now();
```

Event yang wajib increment version:

```text id="e8fgoq"
- role assigned/revoked
- group membership added/removed
- group role assigned/revoked
- direct user permission grant/revoke
- service permission grant/revoke
- partner entitlement grant/revoke
- ACL grant/revoke affecting subject
- user disabled/suspended
- tenant disabled
```

---

# 9. Resource Version untuk ACL/Object Access

Subject version tidak cukup untuk ACL object-specific. Tambahkan resource authorization version.

```sql id="d2oemr"
CREATE TABLE resource_authorization_version (
    tenant_id      TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_type  TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_id    TEXT NOT NULL,
    version        BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    updated_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, resource_type, resource_id)
);
```

Increment saat:

```text id="aqrj7j"
- ACL grant/revoke pada resource
- resource owner berubah
- assignee berubah
- status berubah jika status memengaruhi access
- resource tenant berubah, jika model mengizinkan
- sensitivity/risk classification berubah
```

Cache key ACL:

```text id="ez6bqj"
acl:{tenantId}:{resourceType}:{resourceId}:{subjectHash}:sv{subjectVersion}:rv{resourceVersion}
```

---

# 10. Cache Key Design

## Effective User Permission

```text id="wtnc99"
authz:effective-permission:user:{tenantId}:{userId}:v{entitlementVersion}
```

## Service Permission

```text id="up8v9s"
authz:effective-permission:service:{clientId}:v{entitlementVersion}
```

## Partner Entitlement

```text id="sngbo9"
authz:effective-permission:partner:{clientId}:v{entitlementVersion}
```

## ACL Decision

```text id="jkdusn"
authz:acl:{tenantId}:{resourceType}:{resourceId}:{permission}:{subjectHash}:sv{subjectVersion}:rv{resourceVersion}
```

## List Visibility

```text id="wd4ku9"
authz:visible-list:{tenantId}:{subjectId}:{visibilityMode}:{queryHash}:v{entitlementVersion}
```

Jangan:

```text id="m0d06b"
case:{caseId}
permission:{userId}
acl:{resourceId}
```

Karena kurang tenant, actor, action, dan version.

---

# 11. Subject Hash

Untuk ACL check yang memakai subject refs seperti user, group, role:

```java id="9og66v"
public final class SubjectHash {

    public static String compute(List<SubjectReference> subjects) {
        String canonical = subjects.stream()
                .sorted(Comparator
                        .comparing(SubjectReference::subjectType)
                        .thenComparing(SubjectReference::subjectId))
                .map(s -> s.subjectType() + ":" + s.subjectId())
                .collect(Collectors.joining("|"));

        return sha256Hex(canonical);
    }

    private static String sha256Hex(String value) {
        // gunakan library crypto standar di implementasi nyata
        return DigestUtils.sha256Hex(value);
    }
}
```

Tujuan:

```text id="ctczu9"
Cache key stabil walaupun urutan group/role refs berbeda.
```

---

# 12. Entitlement Cache Interface

```java id="t69z1i"
public interface EntitlementCache {

    Optional<CachedEntitlement> get(String key);

    void put(String key, CachedEntitlement value, Duration ttl);

    void evict(String key);

    void evictBySubject(String subjectType, String tenantId, String subjectId);

    void evictByResource(String tenantId, String resourceType, String resourceId);
}
```

Cached value:

```java id="p9f1bm"
public record CachedEntitlement(
        Set<String> permissions,
        long version,
        Instant loadedAt,
        Instant expiresAt,
        String source
) {
    public boolean isExpired(Clock clock) {
        return Instant.now(clock).isAfter(expiresAt);
    }
}
```

---

# 13. Version Lookup Cache

Agar tidak selalu query DB untuk version, cache version pendek juga.

```java id="a65rqh"
public interface EntitlementVersionProvider {

    long currentSubjectVersion(
            String subjectType,
            String tenantId,
            String subjectId
    );

    long currentResourceVersion(
            String tenantId,
            String resourceType,
            String resourceId
    );
}
```

Caveat:

```text id="e3ird3"
Version cache juga bisa stale.
Untuk sensitive action, version lookup harus fresh atau TTL sangat pendek.
```

---

# 14. Authorization Service dengan Versioned Cache

```java id="h0c9vj"
public final class CachedEntitlementService {

    private final EntitlementMapper entitlementMapper;
    private final EntitlementVersionProvider versionProvider;
    private final EntitlementCache cache;
    private final Clock clock;

    public Set<String> effectiveUserPermissions(
            String tenantId,
            String userId,
            AuthorizationFreshnessLevel freshness
    ) {
        long version = versionProvider.currentSubjectVersion(
                "USER",
                tenantId,
                userId
        );

        String key = "authz:effective-permission:user:%s:%s:v%d"
                .formatted(tenantId, userId, version);

        if (freshness != AuthorizationFreshnessLevel.FRESH_REQUIRED) {
            Optional<CachedEntitlement> cached = cache.get(key)
                    .filter(value -> !value.isExpired(clock));

            if (cached.isPresent()) {
                return cached.get().permissions();
            }
        }

        Set<String> permissions =
                entitlementMapper.findEffectiveUserPermissions(tenantId, userId);

        Duration ttl = ttlFor(freshness);

        cache.put(
                key,
                new CachedEntitlement(
                        permissions,
                        version,
                        Instant.now(clock),
                        Instant.now(clock).plus(ttl),
                        "POSTGRES"
                ),
                ttl
        );

        return permissions;
    }

    private Duration ttlFor(AuthorizationFreshnessLevel freshness) {
        return switch (freshness) {
            case LOW_RISK_CACHED -> Duration.ofMinutes(2);
            case VERSION_VALIDATED -> Duration.ofSeconds(30);
            case FRESH_REQUIRED -> Duration.ZERO;
        };
    }
}
```

Untuk `FRESH_REQUIRED`, boleh tetap menulis cache setelah load, tapi jangan membaca cache sebagai sumber keputusan.

---

# 15. Revocation Strategy

Ada beberapa level revocation.

| Level | Contoh | Efek |
|---|---|---|
| Permission revoke | remove `case.approve` dari user/role | effective permission hilang |
| ACL revoke | user tidak lagi bisa baca object tertentu | object access hilang |
| Session revoke | user harus login ulang | token/session invalid |
| Token revoke | access/refresh token tidak aktif | token ditolak |
| Service revoke | service client disabled | service ditolak |
| Tenant revoke | tenant disabled/suspended | semua actor tenant ditolak |

OAuth 2.0 Token Revocation RFC 7009 mendefinisikan endpoint revocation agar client dapat memberitahu authorization server bahwa token yang sebelumnya diterbitkan tidak lagi dibutuhkan; ini umum dipakai untuk logout atau mencabut refresh/access token sesuai dukungan provider. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7009?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 16. Revocation Flow

```mermaid id="vyo9bm"
sequenceDiagram
    participant Admin as Admin
    participant API as Admin API
    participant DB as PostgreSQL
    participant Bus as Event Bus / NOTIFY
    participant Cache as Authz Cache
    participant Svc as Jersey Service

    Admin->>API: Revoke role/permission/ACL
    API->>DB: update revoked_at
    API->>DB: increment subject/resource version
    API->>DB: write entitlement audit
    API->>Bus: publish ENTITLEMENT_CHANGED
    Bus-->>Cache: evict subject/resource keys

    Svc->>Cache: next request cache lookup
    Cache-->>Svc: miss/stale
    Svc->>DB: load fresh entitlement
    Svc-->>Svc: deny if permission gone
```

---

# 17. PostgreSQL LISTEN/NOTIFY untuk Invalidation

PostgreSQL `NOTIFY` mengirim notification event dengan optional payload ke client yang sebelumnya melakukan `LISTEN` pada channel tersebut. `LISTEN` mendaftarkan session sebagai listener pada channel. Ini bisa dipakai untuk invalidasi cache sederhana di deployment kecil/menengah. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-notify.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Trigger function:

```sql id="1r62i2"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION notify_entitlement_changed()
RETURNS trigger AS $$
DECLARE
    payload json;
BEGIN
    payload = json_build_object(
        'subjectType', NEW.subject_type,
        'tenantId', NEW.tenant_id,
        'subjectId', NEW.subject_id,
        'version', NEW.version,
        'updatedAt', NEW.updated_at
    );

    PERFORM pg_notify('entitlement_changed', payload::text);

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

Trigger:

```sql id="96r61x"
CREATE TRIGGER trg_notify_entitlement_changed
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON subject_entitlement_version
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION notify_entitlement_changed();
```

Caveat:

```text id="u5o5uc"
LISTEN/NOTIFY bukan durable message queue.
Jika listener down, event bisa hilang.
Tetap butuh TTL/version lookup sebagai safety net.
```

---

# 18. Event Bus Invalidation

Untuk multi-service/cluster lebih besar, gunakan Kafka/event bus.

Event:

```json id="8b2g47"
{
  "eventType": "ENTITLEMENT_CHANGED",
  "eventId": "evt-123",
  "subjectType": "USER",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "subjectId": "user-123",
  "newVersion": 42,
  "changeReason": "ROLE_REVOKED",
  "changedBy": "admin-777",
  "changedAt": "2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00"
}
```

Consumer behavior:

```java id="n9fibf"
public void onEntitlementChanged(EntitlementChangedEvent event) {
    cache.evictBySubject(
            event.subjectType(),
            event.tenantId(),
            event.subjectId()
    );

    versionCache.put(
            event.subjectType(),
            event.tenantId(),
            event.subjectId(),
            event.newVersion()
    );

    metrics.increment("authz_cache_invalidation_total");
}
```

Design rule:

```text id="0s5fi7"
Event invalidation speeds up revocation.
Versioned keys and TTL protect you when event is delayed/lost.
```

---

# 19. Token Revocation vs Entitlement Revocation

| Revocation Type | Menyelesaikan | Tidak Menyelesaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Token revocation | token tidak bisa dipakai lagi | permission stale dalam token jika token masih diterima offline |
| Entitlement revocation | permission/role/ACL dicabut | token masih valid untuk authentication |
| Session revocation | user harus login ulang | service token/ACL issue |
| Cache invalidation | keputusan cache fresh | stolen token tanpa revocation |
| Short token lifetime | membatasi durasi stale claim | immediate revocation tidak selalu |

Pattern aman:

```text id="60cmbw"
Authentication:
  token valid?

Authorization:
  entitlement masih valid?

Sensitive action:
  token + entitlement + object policy fresh enough?
```

---

# 20. JWT Claim Staleness

Jika permission dimasukkan ke JWT:

```json id="bw0nsl"
{
  "sub": "user-123",
  "tenant_id": "tenant-a",
  "permissions": ["case.approve"],
  "exp": 1783920000
}
```

Permission bisa stale sampai token expired.

Mitigasi:

```text id="gtobdk"
- keep access token short-lived
- avoid putting high-risk permissions directly in long-lived token
- include permission_version claim
- compare token permission_version with server-side current version
- introspect token for sensitive action if provider supports it
- revoke session/token for urgent cases
```

Example:

```json id="cncotm"
{
  "sub": "user-123",
  "tenant_id": "tenant-a",
  "permission_version": 41,
  "exp": 1783920000
}
```

Server check:

```java id="cfr14i"
long tokenVersion = token.permissionVersion();
long currentVersion = versionProvider.currentSubjectVersion(
        "USER",
        token.tenantId(),
        token.subject()
);

if (tokenVersion < currentVersion) {
    throw new InvalidAccessTokenException("permission_version_stale");
}
```

---

# 21. Introspection Strategy

Untuk opaque token atau high-risk action, introspection dapat memberi freshness lebih baik karena resource server bertanya ke authorization server tentang active state token. RFC 7662 mendefinisikan introspection response yang berisi status `active` dan metadata token. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7662?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Strategy:

| Endpoint | Introspection |
|---|---|
| Read low-risk | Tidak perlu / cached |
| List medium-risk | Optional cached |
| Sensitive write | Required atau version-validated |
| Admin action | Required/fresh |
| Break-glass | Required/fresh |
| Suspicious request | Required/fresh |

Pseudocode:

```java id="b18w22"
public TokenFreshnessDecision validateTokenFreshness(
        VerifiedAccessToken token,
        ActionRisk risk
) {
    if (risk == ActionRisk.CRITICAL) {
        IntrospectionResult result = introspectionClient.introspect(token.tokenIdOrRawReference());

        if (!result.active()) {
            return TokenFreshnessDecision.deny("token_not_active");
        }

        return TokenFreshnessDecision.allow("token_active_by_introspection");
    }

    return TokenFreshnessDecision.allow("local_token_validation_sufficient");
}
```

---

# 22. Cache-Aside Pattern

```java id="73ycg5"
public Set<String> loadEffectivePermissions(String tenantId, String userId) {
    String key = buildKey(tenantId, userId);

    Optional<Set<String>> cached = cache.get(key);

    if (cached.isPresent()) {
        return cached.get();
    }

    Set<String> permissions =
            entitlementMapper.findEffectiveUserPermissions(tenantId, userId);

    cache.put(key, permissions, Duration.ofSeconds(60));

    return permissions;
}
```

Caveat:

```text id="tao2ah"
Cache-aside alone is not enough for revocation.
Add version and event invalidation.
```

---

# 23. Read-Through with Version Check

```java id="rh8j8j"
public Set<String> loadEffectivePermissionsVersioned(
        String tenantId,
        String userId
) {
    long version = versionProvider.currentSubjectVersion("USER", tenantId, userId);

    String key = "perm:%s:%s:v%d".formatted(tenantId, userId, version);

    return cache.get(key)
            .orElseGet(() -> {
                Set<String> permissions =
                        entitlementMapper.findEffectiveUserPermissions(tenantId, userId);

                cache.put(key, permissions, Duration.ofMinutes(2));

                return permissions;
            });
}
```

---

# 24. Stale-While-Revalidate?

Untuk authorization, **stale-while-revalidate** sangat berbahaya jika stale value adalah allow.

| Use Case | Stale-While-Revalidate? |
|---|---:|
| Public metadata | Boleh |
| UI menu visibility | Boleh, hanya cosmetic |
| Read low-risk internal dashboard | Mungkin |
| Object data access | Hati-hati |
| Sensitive write/admin | Jangan |
| Break-glass | Jangan |

Rule:

```text id="hxkgmk"
Stale allow is dangerous.
Stale deny is inconvenient.
For security-critical actions, prefer stale deny over stale allow.
```

---

# 25. Negative Cache

Caching deny dapat mengurangi load, tetapi bisa membuat user yang baru diberi akses tetap ditolak sampai TTL.

```java id="zrumom"
public record CachedAuthorizationDecision(
        boolean allowed,
        String reason,
        Instant loadedAt,
        Instant expiresAt,
        long subjectVersion,
        long resourceVersion
) {}
```

Guideline:

```text id="zd331f"
- deny cache TTL pendek
- include version
- evict on grant event
- do not cache system errors as deny forever
```

Jangan cache PDP timeout sebagai normal deny dalam waktu lama. Simpan reason `pdp_timeout_fail_closed` dengan TTL sangat pendek atau tidak di-cache.

---

# 26. Distributed Cache vs Local Cache

| Cache Type | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Local in-memory | sangat cepat, sederhana | tiap instance punya stale state sendiri |
| Distributed cache | shared, easier invalidation | latency, dependency |
| DB/materialized view | durable/queryable | slower, refresh complexity |
| Hybrid local + distributed | cepat + coordinated | lebih kompleks |

Recommended enterprise pattern:

```text id="ko03wn"
local cache:
  tiny TTL for hot data

distributed cache:
  effective permission/version

event invalidation:
  evict subject/resource

DB source of truth:
  final fallback/fresh check
```

---

# 27. Fail-Closed Behavior

Kalau entitlement source/cache bermasalah, jangan otomatis allow.

| Failure | Low-risk Read | Sensitive Write/Admin |
|---|---|---|
| Cache down | query DB/PDP | query DB/PDP |
| DB entitlement timeout | deny or degraded | deny |
| PDP timeout | deny or cached deny/short cached allow only if approved | deny |
| Version lookup fails | deny or use very fresh cached data | deny |
| Invalidation event delayed | version/TTL safety | fresh check |
| Cache returns corrupt data | ignore + reload | deny if reload fails |

Pseudocode:

```java id="2k4v4x"
public AuthorizationDecision evaluateWithFailurePolicy(
        AuthorizationRequest request
) {
    try {
        return authorizationEvaluator.evaluate(request);

    } catch (EntitlementUnavailableException e) {
        if (request.action().risk() == ActionRisk.LOW_READ) {
            return fallbackForLowRiskRead(request);
        }

        return AuthorizationDecision.deny(
                "entitlement_unavailable_fail_closed",
                "authz-failure-policy",
                "2026.07.1"
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 28. Sensitive Action Fresh Check

```java id="dh2nas"
public AuthorizationDecision canApproveCase(
        SecurityActor actor,
        CaseRecord caseRecord
) {
    Set<String> freshPermissions =
            entitlementService.effectiveUserPermissions(
                    actor.tenantContext().tenantId(),
                    actor.effectiveActor().subject(),
                    AuthorizationFreshnessLevel.FRESH_REQUIRED
            );

    if (!freshPermissions.contains("case.approve")) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_fresh_case_approve_permission");
    }

    return casePolicy.canApprove(actor, caseRecord);
}
```

---

# 29. ACL Freshness

ACL tends to be more volatile than role-permission mapping.

```java id="hzewuf"
public AuthorizationDecision canReadCaseWithAcl(
        SecurityActor actor,
        CaseRecord caseRecord
) {
    if (!actor.tenantContext().canAccessTenant(caseRecord.tenantId())) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("tenant_not_allowed");
    }

    if (actor.hasPermission("case.read.all")) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.allow("has_case_read_all");
    }

    AclDecision decision = aclService.check(
            actor,
            "case",
            caseRecord.id(),
            "case.read",
            AuthorizationFreshnessLevel.VERSION_VALIDATED
    );

    if (decision.allowed()) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.allow("acl_grant_allowed");
    }

    return AuthorizationDecision.deny("no_acl_grant");
}
```

For ACL changes:

```text id="bi3ac6"
Increment subject version if subject-specific grant changed.
Increment resource version if resource ACL changed.
Evict by subject and resource.
```

---

# 30. Transactional Revocation

Revocation should be atomic with audit and version increment.

```java id="0epz4x"
@Transactional
public void revokeRole(
        SecurityActor admin,
        String tenantId,
        String userId,
        String roleCode,
        String reason
) {
    int updated = roleMapper.revokeUserRole(
            tenantId,
            userId,
            roleCode,
            admin.effectiveActor().subject(),
            reason
    );

    if (updated == 0) {
        return;
    }

    long version = entitlementVersionMapper.increment(
            "USER",
            tenantId,
            userId
    );

    entitlementAuditMapper.insertRoleRevokedEvent(
            UUID.randomUUID(),
            tenantId,
            userId,
            roleCode,
            admin.effectiveActor().subject(),
            reason,
            version
    );

    // publish after commit, or write outbox in same tx
    entitlementOutboxMapper.insertEntitlementChangedEvent(
            "USER",
            tenantId,
            userId,
            version,
            "ROLE_REVOKED"
    );
}
```

Avoid:

```text id="7aipar"
revoke DB row
then maybe audit
then maybe version
then maybe event

If crash happens in middle, cache may remain stale.
```

Use outbox for durable event publishing.

---

# 31. Outbox for Invalidation Events

```sql id="l7uecz"
CREATE TABLE entitlement_event_outbox (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    event_type      TEXT NOT NULL,
    subject_type    TEXT NULL,
    tenant_id       TEXT NULL,
    subject_id      TEXT NULL,
    resource_type   TEXT NULL,
    resource_id     TEXT NULL,
    version         BIGINT NULL,
    payload         JSONB NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    retry_count     INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    next_retry_at   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_entitlement_outbox_pending
ON entitlement_event_outbox (status, next_retry_at);
```

Flow:

```text id="3jyyno"
DB transaction:
  revoke grant
  increment version
  audit change
  insert outbox event

background publisher:
  publish event to Kafka/Redis/NOTIFY
  mark SENT
```

---

# 32. Revocation SLA

Tentukan SLA revocation per risk.

| Access Type | Example | Suggested Revocation SLA |
|---|---|---|
| UI menu visibility | button/menu | minutes acceptable |
| Normal read | case.read | < 1-5 minutes |
| Object ACL | shared case/file | < 30-60 seconds |
| Sensitive write | approve/delete/admin | near-immediate/fresh |
| Service client disabled | internal service | near-immediate |
| Break-glass | emergency access | immediate expiry/revocation |

SLA ini menentukan TTL, version lookup, introspection, dan invalidation architecture.

---

# 33. Metrics

```text id="fdaks9"
authz_cache_hit_total{cache="entitlement|acl|decision"}
authz_cache_miss_total{cache="entitlement|acl|decision"}
authz_cache_stale_total
authz_cache_evict_total{reason}
authz_entitlement_version_mismatch_total
authz_permission_revoked_total
authz_acl_revoked_total
authz_revocation_event_lag_ms
authz_fresh_check_total{action}
authz_fresh_check_latency_ms{action}
authz_fail_closed_total{reason}
authz_stale_allow_prevented_total
```

Alert:

```text id="qclnbv"
- revocation event lag above SLA
- cache stale spike
- version mismatch spike
- fail-closed spike for entitlement source
- sensitive action using cached allow
- unknown entitlement version
```

# 35. Testing Matrix

```text id="5c265s"
[ ] Effective permission cache hit returns permission.
[ ] Cache miss loads from DB.
[ ] Expired cache reloads from DB.
[ ] Version change causes old cache key to be ignored.
[ ] Role revoke increments subject entitlement version.
[ ] Group membership removal increments user entitlement version.
[ ] ACL grant increments subject/resource version.
[ ] ACL revoke evicts subject/resource cache.
[ ] Sensitive action does not use stale cached allow.
[ ] Token permission_version older than server version is rejected.
[ ] PDP timeout fails closed for sensitive action.
[ ] Negative cache expires quickly.
[ ] LISTEN/NOTIFY/event invalidation evicts cache.
[ ] Lost invalidation event is mitigated by TTL/version lookup.
[ ] Service permission revoke prevents internal endpoint call.
[ ] Partner entitlement revoke prevents partner action.
[ ] Audit event emitted for revoke and stale deny.
```

---

# 36. Example Tests

## Version Change Ignores Old Cache

```java id="7r8flr"
@Test
void shouldIgnoreOldPermissionCacheWhenVersionIncrements() {
    String tenantId = "tenant-a";
    String userId = "alice";

    versionProvider.setVersion("USER", tenantId, userId, 1);

    cache.put(
            "authz:effective-permission:user:tenant-a:alice:v1",
            new CachedEntitlement(
                    Set.of("case.approve"),
                    1,
                    now(),
                    now().plusSeconds(120),
                    "TEST"
            ),
            Duration.ofSeconds(120)
    );

    versionProvider.setVersion("USER", tenantId, userId, 2);
    entitlementMapper.stubPermissions(tenantId, userId, Set.of("case.read"));

    Set<String> permissions = entitlementService.effectiveUserPermissions(
            tenantId,
            userId,
            AuthorizationFreshnessLevel.VERSION_VALIDATED
    );

    assertFalse(permissions.contains("case.approve"));
    assertTrue(permissions.contains("case.read"));
}
```

## Sensitive Action Fresh Check

```java id="9xd8lx"
@Test
void sensitiveActionShouldNotUseStaleCachedAllow() {
    SecurityActor alice = PrincipalFixture.user()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .subject("alice")
            .permission("case.approve")
            .build();

    cache.putCurrentPermissions("tenant-a", "alice", Set.of("case.approve"));

    entitlementMapper.stubPermissions("tenant-a", "alice", Set.of("case.read"));

    AuthorizationDecision decision = casePolicy.canApproveWithFreshEntitlement(
            alice,
            CaseFixture.submittedCase().createdBy("bob").build()
    );

    assertFalse(decision.allowed());
    assertEquals("missing_fresh_case_approve_permission", decision.reason());
}
```

## Token Permission Version Stale

```java id="o8a0jm"
@Test
void shouldRejectTokenWithStalePermissionVersion() {
    VerifiedAccessToken token = TokenFixture.userToken()
            .subject("alice")
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .permissionVersion(41)
            .build();

    versionProvider.setVersion("USER", "tenant-a", "alice", 42);

    assertThrows(
            InvalidAccessTokenException.class,
            () -> permissionVersionValidator.validate(token)
    );
}
```

---

# 37. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| TTL panjang untuk effective permission | revoked access tetap allow | version + invalidation + short TTL |
| Cache allow decision untuk admin action | stale privilege | fresh check |
| Cache key tanpa tenant | cross-tenant leak | tenant in key |
| Cache key tanpa action/resource | wrong decision reuse | include action/resource |
| Invalidation event tanpa version | lost/out-of-order event bug | versioned event |
| Event only, no TTL fallback | listener down = stale forever | TTL/version lookup |
| Revocation tanpa audit | tidak defensible | audit old/new |
| Revocation tanpa cache eviction | stale allow | version increment + evict |
| JWT permission claim trusted forever | stale token authority | short token + permission version |
| PDP timeout cached as deny too long | false deny/outage hidden | short/no cache + alert |

---

# 38. Code Review Checklist

```text id="g5103d"
[ ] Cache key includes tenant, subject, action/resource where needed.
[ ] Cache key includes entitlement/resource version.
[ ] Sensitive action bypasses cached allow or validates version fresh.
[ ] Revocation increments version in same transaction.
[ ] Revocation emits audit event.
[ ] Revocation writes durable invalidation event/outbox.
[ ] ACL changes increment resource version.
[ ] Cache TTL matches risk class.
[ ] PDP/DB failure fails closed for sensitive action.
[ ] Negative cache TTL is short.
[ ] Token permission_version is checked if permissions are token-embedded.
[ ] Metrics exist for stale, hit/miss, eviction, fail-closed.
[ ] Tests cover lost invalidation event scenario.
```

---

# 39. Minimal Production Invariants

```text id="ri4n7e"
1. Cached authorization data must have TTL.
2. Effective permission cache must be versioned.
3. Revocation must increment entitlement/resource version.
4. Sensitive actions must not rely on stale cached allow.
5. Token claims containing permissions must be treated as potentially stale.
6. Lost invalidation events must be mitigated by TTL/version lookup.
7. Cache keys must include tenant and subject.
8. Object/ACL cache keys must include resource and action.
9. Authorization source failures must fail closed for sensitive actions.
10. Revocation and stale-deny decisions must be audited.
```

---

# 40. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Cache risk classification | Tahu mana boleh cache, mana wajib fresh |
| 2 | Versioned entitlement | Cache tidak stale setelah revoke |
| 3 | Resource/ACL version | Object grant changes cepat berlaku |
| 4 | Event invalidation | Revoke propagate cepat |
| 5 | TTL fallback | Lost event tidak stale selamanya |
| 6 | Token claim staleness | Permission dalam JWT tidak dipercaya selamanya |
| 7 | Introspection strategy | High-risk token freshness |
| 8 | Fail-closed policy | PDP/DB failure aman |
| 9 | Metrics/audit | Stale/fail-closed terlihat |
| 10 | Regression tests | Revocation dan cache safety tertutup |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 5:11:23 AM

Kita lanjut ke API key/HMAC/webhook/replay protection. Fokusnya: partner callback dan internal command yang tidak memakai user JWT, bagaimana signature diverifikasi, timestamp/nonce/idempotency dipakai, dan retry aman tanpa double mutation.

# Cheatsheet - API Key, HMAC Signature, Webhook Security, Idempotency, dan Replay Protection di Jersey/JAX-RS

## 1. Tujuan

Cheatsheet ini fokus ke endpoint yang **tidak selalu cocok memakai user JWT**, misalnya:

```text id="yu6o1r"
- partner API dengan API key
- webhook callback dari external provider
- internal command endpoint
- payment/order callback
- event ingestion endpoint
- batch/retry/reconciliation endpoint
```

Masalah yang ingin dicegah:

```text id="v8jjgj"
- API key bocor lalu dipakai tanpa batas.
- Webhook palsu diterima.
- Payload diubah di tengah jalan.
- Request lama di-replay ulang.
- Retry menyebabkan double charge/double order/double mutation.
- Idempotency key dipakai ulang untuk payload berbeda.
- Signature diverifikasi dari body yang sudah berubah.
- Secret hardcoded di repository.
```

OWASP REST Security Cheat Sheet menekankan REST service aman harus memakai HTTPS untuk melindungi credential seperti API key/JWT saat transit, dan non-public REST service harus melakukan access control pada setiap endpoint. Untuk service yang highly privileged, OWASP juga menyarankan mempertimbangkan mutual client-side certificates. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/REST_Security_Cheat_Sheet.html))

---

# 2. Mental Model

```text id="caiz2v"
Authentication:
  Apakah caller memang pemilik credential/signature?

Integrity:
  Apakah payload tidak berubah?

Authorization:
  Apakah caller boleh action ini?

Replay protection:
  Apakah request ini belum pernah dipakai?

Idempotency:
  Jika client retry request yang sama, apakah hasilnya aman dan tidak double-process?

Audit:
  Bisa dibuktikan siapa memanggil, payload fingerprint apa, decision apa?
```

API key/HMAC/webhook security bukan pengganti authorization. Signature hanya membuktikan request dibuat oleh pihak yang punya secret dan payload tidak berubah; sistem tetap harus mengecek permission, tenant, object, state, idempotency, dan audit.

---

# 3. Diagram Besar

```mermaid id="cd4o9n"
sequenceDiagram
    participant C as Partner/Webhook/Internal Client
    participant API as Jersey API
    participant F as Security Filter
    participant R as Replay Store
    participant I as Idempotency Store
    participant P as Policy
    participant S as Service Layer
    participant DB as PostgreSQL

    C->>API: POST + API-Key/HMAC/Timestamp/Nonce/Idempotency-Key
    API->>F: ContainerRequestFilter
    F->>F: Extract raw body safely
    F->>F: Validate API key / HMAC signature
    F->>F: Validate timestamp tolerance
    F->>R: Check nonce/eventId not used
    R-->>F: ok / replay
    F->>API: set Partner/System Principal

    API->>I: Check idempotency key + request fingerprint
    I-->>API: new / duplicate / conflict / in-progress

    API->>P: Authorization policy
    P-->>API: allow / deny

    API->>S: Execute use case
    S->>DB: transactional mutation
    S->>I: store final response/result
    API-->>C: response
```

---

# 4. API Key vs HMAC vs Webhook Signature

| Mechanism | Membuktikan | Tidak Membuktikan | Cocok Untuk |
|---|---|---|---|
| API key | Caller tahu secret/token | Payload integrity, replay safety | Simple partner/internal API |
| HMAC signature | Caller punya shared secret + payload tidak berubah | Authorization final | Webhook, partner callback |
| Webhook secret | Provider resmi mengirim payload | Object/action allowed | Provider callback |
| Timestamp + nonce | Request masih fresh dan belum dipakai | Caller identity sendiri | Replay protection |
| Idempotency key | Retry request yang sama dikenali | Caller authenticity | POST/PATCH fault tolerance |
| mTLS | Client certificate valid | Payload semantics | Highly privileged service |
| OAuth client credentials | Client identity/token audience | Payload integrity by itself | Service-to-service API |

HMAC adalah mekanisme message authentication code berbasis shared secret dan cryptographic hash function; RFC 2104 menjelaskan HMAC sebagai cara memverifikasi integritas informasi dengan secret key bersama. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc2104))

---

# 5. Recommended Header Contract

Untuk partner/internal command:

```http id="u1in7r"
POST /partner/orders HTTP/1.1
Host: api.company.com
Content-Type: application/json
X-Client-Id: partner-acme
X-Timestamp: 2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00
X-Nonce: 0190f0e5-7b5b-7d82-84fc-3d35db96b9f8
X-Content-SHA256: base64url(sha256(raw_body))
X-Signature-Algorithm: HMAC-SHA256
X-Signature: v1=base64url(hmac_sha256(secret, canonical_string))
Idempotency-Key: 0190f0e5-7b5b-7d82-84fc-3d35db96b9f8
```

Canonical string:

```text id="4n7t5d"
method + "\n" +
path + "\n" +
queryCanonical + "\n" +
timestamp + "\n" +
nonce + "\n" +
contentSha256
```

Example:

```text id="9hhocf"
POST
/partner/orders
externalTenantRef=ACME-001
2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00
0190f0e5-7b5b-7d82-84fc-3d35db96b9f8
q2w3e4r5...
```

---

# 6. API Key Storage

Jangan simpan API key plaintext.

```sql id="nqlgrr"
CREATE TABLE api_client (
    client_id        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    display_name     TEXT NOT NULL,
    client_type      TEXT NOT NULL, -- PARTNER, INTERNAL, WEBHOOK_PROVIDER
    active           BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    allowed_tenants  TEXT[] NULL,
    created_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_api_client_type
        CHECK (client_type IN ('PARTNER', 'INTERNAL', 'WEBHOOK_PROVIDER'))
);

CREATE TABLE api_client_secret (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    client_id       TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES api_client(client_id),
    secret_hash     TEXT NOT NULL,
    secret_prefix   TEXT NOT NULL,
    algorithm       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'HMAC-SHA256',
    active          BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    revoked_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL
);

CREATE INDEX idx_api_client_secret_lookup
ON api_client_secret (client_id, secret_prefix)
WHERE active = true AND revoked_at IS NULL;
```

Pola aman:

```text id="oleash"
API key shown once to client:
  live_acme_abc123.secret-long-random

Server stores:
  client_id
  secret_prefix
  hash(secret)
  metadata
```

OWASP Secrets Management Cheat Sheet menekankan secret seperti API key dan credential harus dikelola sebagai secret dan tidak disimpan sembarangan; GitHub webhook documentation juga menekankan webhook secret harus disimpan aman dan tidak di-hardcode atau dipush ke repository. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Secrets_Management_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 7. API Key Authentication Pattern

API key sederhana:

```http id="dn9q7e"
X-API-Key: live_acme_abc123.secret-long-random
```

Filter flow:

```text id="gfu844"
1. Extract X-API-Key.
2. Split prefix and secret.
3. Lookup secret metadata by prefix.
4. Hash presented secret.
5. Constant-time compare with stored hash.
6. Check active/revoked/expired.
7. Build Partner/System principal.
8. Continue to authorization policy.
```

Jangan:

```text id="35k3sb"
- simpan API key plaintext
- log API key
- terima API key di query string
- API key tanpa expiry/rotation
- API key langsung berarti full access
```

---

# 8. HMAC Signature Verification

GitHub webhook docs menekankan server harus memvalidasi signature webhook sebelum memproses delivery, dengan menghitung hash menggunakan secret token dan membandingkannya dengan signature yang dikirim, supaya hanya payload dari GitHub dan tidak dimodifikasi yang diproses. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/en/webhooks/using-webhooks/validating-webhook-deliveries))

## Java HMAC Utility

```java id="xnp3p1"
public final class HmacSigner {

    private HmacSigner() {}

    public static byte[] hmacSha256(byte[] secret, String canonicalString) {
        try {
            Mac mac = Mac.getInstance("HmacSHA256");
            mac.init(new SecretKeySpec(secret, "HmacSHA256"));
            return mac.doFinal(canonicalString.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        } catch (GeneralSecurityException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot calculate HMAC", e);
        }
    }

    public static boolean constantTimeEquals(byte[] expected, byte[] actual) {
        return MessageDigest.isEqual(expected, actual);
    }
}
```

## Signature Format

```text id="qvqmw3"
X-Signature: v1=<base64url-hmac>
```

Support multiple versions for rotation:

```text id="azwllq"
v1=old-signature,v2=new-signature
```

---

# 9. Canonicalization Rules

Signature harus dihitung dari data yang sama persis oleh client dan server.

Canonical fields:

```text id="f3q3mq"
method:
  uppercase HTTP method

path:
  raw normalized path, not full URL

query:
  sorted query parameters, percent-encoding normalized

timestamp:
  exact header string or normalized ISO-8601

nonce:
  exact header string

body hash:
  SHA-256 of raw request body bytes
```

Jangan sign object hasil JSON parse. JSON bisa berubah karena whitespace, field order, encoding, atau serializer.

```text id="tgegyf"
Sign raw bytes, not parsed JSON.
Verify before parsing business payload.
```

---

# 10. Raw Body Capture di Jersey

Masalah: request body stream biasanya hanya bisa dibaca sekali. Untuk signature verification, filter perlu membaca raw body lalu memasang ulang stream untuk resource method.

```java id="ko90sp"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class RawBodyCachingFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    public static final String RAW_BODY_PROPERTY = "rawRequestBody";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) throws IOException {
        if (!hasEntity(ctx)) {
            ctx.setProperty(RAW_BODY_PROPERTY, new byte[0]);
            return;
        }

        byte[] body = ctx.getEntityStream().readAllBytes();

        ctx.setProperty(RAW_BODY_PROPERTY, body);
        ctx.setEntityStream(new ByteArrayInputStream(body));
    }

    private boolean hasEntity(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        return ctx.hasEntity();
    }
}
```

**Caveat:** batasi ukuran body. Jangan `readAllBytes()` untuk payload besar tanpa limit.

---

# 11. Body Size Guard

```java id="rxdu47"
public final class LimitedInputStream extends FilterInputStream {

    private final long maxBytes;
    private long readBytes;

    public LimitedInputStream(InputStream in, long maxBytes) {
        super(in);
        this.maxBytes = maxBytes;
    }

    @Override
    public int read() throws IOException {
        int value = super.read();
        if (value != -1) {
            increment(1);
        }
        return value;
    }

    @Override
    public int read(byte[] b, int off, int len) throws IOException {
        int count = super.read(b, off, len);
        if (count > 0) {
            increment(count);
        }
        return count;
    }

    private void increment(long count) throws IOException {
        readBytes += count;
        if (readBytes > maxBytes) {
            throw new PayloadTooLargeException("Request body too large");
        }
    }
}
```

Gunakan limit berbeda per endpoint:

```text id="w060yn"
webhook payment:
  256 KB

partner order create:
  1 MB

bulk ingestion:
  explicit upload design, streaming signature, separate policy
```

---

# 12. Signature Verification Filter

```java id="xmg3oz"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION + 10)
public class HmacSignatureAuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final ApiClientRepository clientRepository;
    private final ReplayProtectionService replayProtectionService;
    private final Clock clock;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String clientId = requiredHeader(ctx, "X-Client-Id");
        String timestamp = requiredHeader(ctx, "X-Timestamp");
        String nonce = requiredHeader(ctx, "X-Nonce");
        String contentSha256 = requiredHeader(ctx, "X-Content-SHA256");
        String signatureHeader = requiredHeader(ctx, "X-Signature");

        byte[] rawBody = (byte[]) ctx.getProperty(RawBodyCachingFilter.RAW_BODY_PROPERTY);

        ApiClient client = clientRepository.findActiveClient(clientId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new NotAuthorizedException("Unknown client"));

        validateTimestamp(timestamp);
        validateContentDigest(rawBody, contentSha256);

        replayProtectionService.requireNotReplayed(
                clientId,
                nonce,
                Duration.ofMinutes(5)
        );

        String canonical = CanonicalRequestBuilder.build(
                ctx.getMethod(),
                ctx.getUriInfo().getPath(),
                ctx.getUriInfo().getQueryParameters(),
                timestamp,
                nonce,
                contentSha256
        );

        boolean valid = client.activeSecrets().stream()
                .anyMatch(secret -> verifySignature(secret, canonical, signatureHeader));

        if (!valid) {
            throw new NotAuthorizedException("Invalid signature");
        }

        SecurityActor actor = PartnerPrincipalFactory.fromClient(client, ctx);
        ctx.setSecurityContext(new RequestSecurityContext(actor, true, "HMAC"));
    }

    private void validateTimestamp(String timestamp) {
        Instant requestTime = Instant.parse(timestamp);
        Instant now = Instant.now(clock);

        Duration skew = Duration.between(requestTime, now).abs();

        if (skew.compareTo(Duration.ofMinutes(5)) > 0) {
            throw new NotAuthorizedException("Timestamp outside tolerance");
        }
    }

    private void validateContentDigest(byte[] rawBody, String expectedDigest) {
        String actual = Base64.getUrlEncoder()
                .withoutPadding()
                .encodeToString(MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(rawBody));

        if (!MessageDigest.isEqual(
                actual.getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII),
                expectedDigest.getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII))) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Content digest mismatch");
        }
    }

    private boolean verifySignature(
            ApiClientSecret secret,
            String canonical,
            String signatureHeader
    ) {
        byte[] expected = HmacSigner.hmacSha256(secret.rawSecretBytes(), canonical);
        byte[] actual = SignatureHeaderParser.extractV1(signatureHeader);

        return HmacSigner.constantTimeEquals(expected, actual);
    }

    private String requiredHeader(ContainerRequestContext ctx, String name) {
        String value = ctx.getHeaderString(name);
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new NotAuthorizedException("Missing header: " + name);
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

**Catatan penting:** contoh di atas konseptual. Di production, jangan menyimpan `rawSecretBytes()` plaintext di object jangka panjang; gunakan KMS/Vault atau secret material yang didekripsi sesaat di memory.

---

# 13. Timestamp Validation

Timestamp membatasi umur request.

```text id="vtsvot"
Accept if:
  abs(now - X-Timestamp) <= tolerance

Recommended tolerance:
  2-5 minutes for partner/webhook
  shorter for internal high-risk command
```

Error:

| Condition | Response |
|---|---:|
| Missing timestamp | `401` |
| Invalid timestamp format | `401` atau `400` |
| Timestamp too old | `401` |
| Timestamp too far in future | `401` |
| Clock skew too high | `401` + audit |

Audit reason:

```text id="cjnwfa"
timestamp_missing
timestamp_invalid_format
timestamp_outside_tolerance
```

---

# 14. Nonce / Event ID Replay Protection

Timestamp saja tidak cukup. Request dalam window 5 menit masih bisa di-replay.

```sql id="en2p83"
CREATE TABLE replay_protection (
    client_id      TEXT NOT NULL,
    nonce          TEXT NOT NULL,
    first_seen_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (client_id, nonce)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_replay_protection_expiry
ON replay_protection (expires_at);
```

Service:

```java id="9ltke0"
public final class ReplayProtectionService {

    private final ReplayProtectionMapper mapper;
    private final Clock clock;

    public void requireNotReplayed(
            String clientId,
            String nonce,
            Duration ttl
    ) {
        Instant now = Instant.now(clock);
        Instant expiresAt = now.plus(ttl);

        try {
            mapper.insertNonce(clientId, nonce, expiresAt);
        } catch (DuplicateKeyException e) {
            throw new ReplayDetectedException(clientId, nonce);
        }
    }
}
```

Mapper:

```xml id="xk321y"
<insert id="insertNonce">
    INSERT INTO replay_protection (
        client_id,
        nonce,
        expires_at
    )
    VALUES (
        #{clientId},
        #{nonce},
        #{expiresAt}
    )
</insert>
```

Cleanup:

```sql id="ypcmff"
DELETE FROM replay_protection
WHERE expires_at < now();
```

---

# 15. Idempotency Key

`Idempotency-Key` adalah header yang digunakan agar request non-idempotent seperti `POST` atau `PATCH` bisa lebih fault-tolerant terhadap retry; draft IETF HTTPAPI menjelaskan key ini sebagai unique value yang dipakai server untuk mengenali retry dari request yang sama. Draft tersebut juga menyebut key tidak boleh dipakai ulang untuk payload berbeda, dan server dapat memakai fingerprint payload/request untuk membedakan request. Karena dokumen ini masih Internet-Draft, perlakukan sebagai work-in-progress, tetapi pattern-nya sudah umum dipakai di API modern. ([IETF](https://www.ietf.org/archive/id/draft-ietf-httpapi-idempotency-key-header-07.html))

## Kapan Wajib?

```text id="5e8aqo"
POST /orders
POST /payments
POST /cases/{id}/approve
POST /internal/retry-provisioning
POST /partner/orders
POST /webhooks/payment-events
```

Tidak wajib untuk:

```text id="17yahb"
GET /cases/{id}
PUT /resource/{id} with natural idempotency
DELETE /resource/{id} if already idempotent by design
```

---

# 16. Idempotency Store

```sql id="fbi7tb"
CREATE TABLE idempotency_record (
    client_id         TEXT NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key   TEXT NOT NULL,

    operation         TEXT NOT NULL,
    request_hash      TEXT NOT NULL,

    status            TEXT NOT NULL,
    response_status   INT NULL,
    response_body     JSONB NULL,
    resource_type     TEXT NULL,
    resource_id       TEXT NULL,

    created_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (client_id, idempotency_key),

    CONSTRAINT chk_idempotency_status
        CHECK (status IN ('IN_PROGRESS', 'COMPLETED', 'FAILED_RETRYABLE', 'FAILED_FINAL'))
);

CREATE INDEX idx_idempotency_expiry
ON idempotency_record (expires_at);
```

---

# 17. Idempotency Flow

```mermaid id="q4qki7"
flowchart TD
    A[Request with Idempotency-Key] --> B[Compute request fingerprint]
    B --> C{Key exists?}

    C -->|No| D[Insert IN_PROGRESS]
    D --> E[Process business transaction]
    E --> F[Store response COMPLETED]
    F --> G[Return response]

    C -->|Yes, same fingerprint + COMPLETED| H[Return previous response]
    C -->|Yes, same fingerprint + IN_PROGRESS| I[409 Conflict]
    C -->|Yes, different fingerprint| J[422 Unprocessable Content]
    C -->|Expired| K[Policy: reject or allow new]
```

Draft idempotency-key menyebut duplicate request yang sudah selesai sebaiknya mendapat hasil operasi sebelumnya, request concurrent dengan key sama dapat menghasilkan conflict, dan reuse key untuk payload berbeda dapat dibalas dengan `422`. ([IETF](https://www.ietf.org/archive/id/draft-ietf-httpapi-idempotency-key-header-07.html))

---

# 18. Idempotency Service

```java id="99kp2w"
public final class IdempotencyService {

    private final IdempotencyMapper mapper;
    private final Clock clock;

    public IdempotencyDecision begin(
            String clientId,
            String operation,
            String idempotencyKey,
            String requestHash,
            Duration ttl
    ) {
        Optional<IdempotencyRecord> existing =
                mapper.find(clientId, idempotencyKey);

        if (existing.isEmpty()) {
            mapper.insert(
                    clientId,
                    idempotencyKey,
                    operation,
                    requestHash,
                    "IN_PROGRESS",
                    Instant.now(clock).plus(ttl)
            );

            return IdempotencyDecision.processNew();
        }

        IdempotencyRecord record = existing.get();

        if (!record.requestHash().equals(requestHash)) {
            return IdempotencyDecision.conflictDifferentPayload();
        }

        return switch (record.status()) {
            case "COMPLETED" -> IdempotencyDecision.returnStoredResponse(
                    record.responseStatus(),
                    record.responseBody()
            );
            case "IN_PROGRESS" -> IdempotencyDecision.inProgress();
            case "FAILED_RETRYABLE" -> IdempotencyDecision.retryAllowed();
            case "FAILED_FINAL" -> IdempotencyDecision.returnStoredResponse(
                    record.responseStatus(),
                    record.responseBody()
            );
            default -> throw new IllegalStateException("Unknown idempotency status");
        };
    }

    public void complete(
            String clientId,
            String idempotencyKey,
            int responseStatus,
            JsonNode responseBody,
            String resourceType,
            String resourceId
    ) {
        mapper.complete(
                clientId,
                idempotencyKey,
                responseStatus,
                responseBody,
                resourceType,
                resourceId
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 19. Transactional Idempotency Pattern

Untuk mutation penting, idempotency record dan business mutation sebaiknya satu transaction.

```java id="8zf2rr"
@Transactional
public CreateOrderResponse createPartnerOrder(
        SecurityActor partner,
        CreatePartnerOrderRequest request,
        String idempotencyKey,
        String requestHash
) {
    IdempotencyDecision decision = idempotencyService.begin(
            partner.effectiveActor().clientId(),
            "partner.order.create",
            idempotencyKey,
            requestHash,
            Duration.ofHours(24)
    );

    if (decision.shouldReturnStoredResponse()) {
        return decision.storedResponseAs(CreateOrderResponse.class);
    }

    if (decision.isInProgress()) {
        throw new ConflictException("Request is already being processed");
    }

    if (decision.isDifferentPayload()) {
        throw new UnprocessableEntityException("Idempotency key reused with different payload");
    }

    Order order = orderService.createOrder(partner, request);

    CreateOrderResponse response = new CreateOrderResponse(order.id(), "ACCEPTED");

    idempotencyService.complete(
            partner.effectiveActor().clientId(),
            idempotencyKey,
            202,
            json(response),
            "order",
            order.id()
    );

    return response;
}
```

---

# 20. Request Fingerprint

Fingerprint harus stabil dan aman.

Options:

```text id="3xd23r"
- SHA-256 raw body bytes
- canonical JSON hash
- selected business fields hash
- method + path + raw body hash
- request digest/signature
```

Recommended for API:

```text id="dke78j"
requestHash = sha256(
  method + "\n" +
  path + "\n" +
  canonicalTenantOrClient + "\n" +
  rawBodySha256
)
```

Jangan hanya hash idempotency key.

---

# 21. Webhook Security

Webhook endpoint biasanya reachable dari internet, tetapi harus diverifikasi.

Checklist:

```text id="tq8l4m"
[ ] HTTPS only
[ ] Verify provider signature
[ ] Use raw body bytes for verification
[ ] Validate timestamp if provider sends one
[ ] Store event/delivery ID to reject replay
[ ] Enforce payload size limit
[ ] Parse only after verification
[ ] Validate schema
[ ] Idempotently process event
[ ] Return 2xx only after safe accept/record
[ ] Audit accepted/rejected event
```

GitHub docs menyarankan memvalidasi webhook signature sebelum memproses delivery agar server hanya memproses delivery yang benar-benar dikirim provider dan tidak dimodifikasi; dokumentasinya juga menyebut secret harus random/high entropy dan disimpan aman. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/en/webhooks/using-webhooks/validating-webhook-deliveries))

---

# 22. Webhook Endpoint Example

```java id="sg7jex"
@Path("/webhooks/github")
@PublicEndpoint(reason = "GitHub webhook callback verified by HMAC signature")
public class GitHubWebhookResource {

    private final GitHubWebhookVerifier verifier;
    private final WebhookEventService eventService;

    @POST
    public Response receive(
            @HeaderParam("X-GitHub-Event") String eventType,
            @HeaderParam("X-GitHub-Delivery") String deliveryId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Hub-Signature-256") String signature,
            String body
    ) {
        verifier.verify(signature, body.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

        eventService.acceptOnce(
                "github",
                deliveryId,
                eventType,
                body
        );

        return Response.ok().build();
    }
}
```

**Caveat:** untuk signature, body sebaiknya raw bytes dari filter, bukan `String body` yang mungkin sudah melalui decoding. GitHub signature menggunakan HMAC hex digest dengan prefix `sha256=` dan payload content. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/pt/enterprise-cloud%40latest/webhooks/using-webhooks/validating-webhook-deliveries?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 23. Webhook Event Store

```sql id="feyvmj"
CREATE TABLE webhook_event (
    provider        TEXT NOT NULL,
    delivery_id     TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_type      TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload_hash    TEXT NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL,
    received_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    processed_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    error_message   TEXT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (provider, delivery_id),

    CONSTRAINT chk_webhook_event_status
        CHECK (status IN ('RECEIVED', 'PROCESSING', 'PROCESSED', 'IGNORED', 'FAILED'))
);
```

Processing flow:

```text id="hu945p"
1. Verify signature.
2. Insert webhook_event(provider, delivery_id).
3. If duplicate delivery_id:
   - return 200 if already processed/accepted
   - do not process twice
4. Process asynchronously if heavy.
5. Mark PROCESSED/FAILED.
```

---

# 24. Webhook Async Processing

Jangan lakukan heavy processing langsung di webhook request bila provider punya retry timeout singkat.

```text id="r57eeo"
Webhook HTTP handler:
  verify signature
  store event
  enqueue work
  return 2xx

Worker:
  parse schema
  apply idempotent business logic
  mark processed
```

Pattern:

```java id="d42htw"
@Transactional
public void acceptWebhook(
        String provider,
        String deliveryId,
        String eventType,
        byte[] rawBody
) {
    String payloadHash = sha256(rawBody);

    boolean inserted = webhookEventRepository.insertIfAbsent(
            provider,
            deliveryId,
            eventType,
            payloadHash,
            "RECEIVED"
    );

    if (!inserted) {
        return;
    }

    webhookOutboxRepository.enqueue(provider, deliveryId);
}
```

---

# 25. Safe Retry Behavior

| Scenario | Response |
|---|---|
| First request accepted | `202 Accepted` or result |
| Duplicate same idempotency key after completed | same previous response |
| Duplicate while in progress | `409 Conflict` |
| Same key different payload | `422 Unprocessable Content` |
| Missing key for required operation | `400 Bad Request` |
| Invalid signature | `401 Unauthorized` |
| Replay nonce detected | `401` or `409` |
| Webhook duplicate delivery | `200 OK` after safe dedupe |
| Business validation failed final | store final failure if deterministic |
| Transient downstream failure | mark retryable carefully |

For idempotency-key behavior, the current IETF draft describes returning previous result for completed duplicate, conflict for concurrent duplicate, and `400`/`422` style errors for missing or reused keys depending on the situation. ([IETF](https://www.ietf.org/archive/id/draft-ietf-httpapi-idempotency-key-header-07.html))

---

# 26. API Key + HMAC + Idempotency Filter Order

Recommended order:

```text id="hu2ec4"
1. CorrelationIdFilter
2. RawBodyCachingFilter with size limit
3. Hmac/APIKey AuthenticationFilter
4. ReplayProtectionFilter
5. EndpointClassificationFilter
6. AuthorizationFilter
7. IdempotencyFilter or service-level idempotency
8. Resource method
```

Why idempotency after authentication?

```text id="fr5epb"
Idempotency key scope should include authenticated client identity.
Unauthenticated caller must not reserve arbitrary keys.
```

---

# 27. Idempotency Filter vs Service Layer

| Location | Pros | Cons |
|---|---|---|
| Filter | Consistent across endpoint | Hard to know operation semantics |
| Resource | Knows endpoint operation | Can duplicate logic |
| Service layer | Best transaction control | Requires explicit coding |
| DB unique constraint | Strong final guard | Needs app logic for response replay |

Recommended:

```text id="z6ha4s"
Use filter for required header validation.
Use service layer for transactional idempotency.
Use DB unique constraint for final enforcement.
```

---

# 28. Partner Tenant Mapping

For partner API:

```text id="4pyure"
clientId + externalTenantRef
  -> trusted mapping table
  -> internal tenantId
```

```sql id="4ygl42"
CREATE TABLE partner_tenant_mapping (
    client_id           TEXT NOT NULL,
    external_tenant_ref TEXT NOT NULL,
    tenant_id           TEXT NOT NULL,
    active              BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,

    PRIMARY KEY (client_id, external_tenant_ref)
);
```

Never:

```text id="l1aorl"
tenantId from request body = trusted tenant
```

Always:

```java id="y9m0zm"
String tenantId = partnerTenantMapper.resolve(
        partner.clientId(),
        request.externalTenantRef()
).orElseThrow(() -> new ForbiddenException("Unknown partner tenant"));
```

---

# 29. Authorization After Authentication

After API key/HMAC is valid:

```text id="uj2a8q"
Check:
  client active
  client allowed endpoint
  client allowed operation
  client allowed tenant
  client allowed resource type
  payload allowed by schema/business policy
```

Policy example:

```java id="0q5d04"
public AuthorizationDecision canCreatePartnerOrder(
        SecurityActor partner,
        String internalTenantId
) {
    if (partner.effectiveActor().actorType() != ActorType.PARTNER) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("actor_not_partner");
    }

    if (!partner.hasPermission("partner.order.create")) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_partner_order_create");
    }

    if (!partner.tenantContext().canAccessTenant(internalTenantId)) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("partner_not_allowed_for_tenant");
    }

    return AuthorizationDecision.allow("partner_order_create_allowed");
}
```

---

# 30. Secret Rotation

Support overlapping secrets.

```text id="nfjc5r"
Client has:
  old secret active until T
  new secret active from now

Server accepts:
  signatures generated by old or new during rotation window

After T:
  revoke old secret
```

Schema fields:

```text id="7np2qm"
secret_id
secret_prefix
active
created_at
expires_at
revoked_at
algorithm
```

Audit events:

```text id="i4zj1g"
API_CLIENT_SECRET_CREATED
API_CLIENT_SECRET_ROTATED
API_CLIENT_SECRET_REVOKED
API_CLIENT_DISABLED
```

Do not rotate by changing client ID unless client identity itself changes.

---

# 31. Constant-Time Compare

Do not use plain string equality for signatures.

```java id="c7g8zm"
boolean ok = MessageDigest.isEqual(expectedBytes, actualBytes);
```

Why:

```text id="tkp03b"
Plain equality may return faster on early mismatch.
Constant-time comparison reduces timing leak risk.
```

---

# 32. Error Semantics

| Error | Status | Reason |
|---|---:|---|
| Missing API key/signature | `401` | `missing_credential` |
| Unknown client | `401` | `unknown_client` |
| Invalid signature | `401` | `invalid_signature` |
| Timestamp outside tolerance | `401` | `timestamp_outside_tolerance` |
| Replay detected | `401` or `409` | `replay_detected` |
| Client not allowed operation | `403` | `client_not_authorized` |
| Partner tenant not mapped | `403` | `tenant_not_allowed` |
| Missing idempotency key | `400` | `idempotency_key_required` |
| Same key different payload | `422` | `idempotency_key_payload_mismatch` |
| Same key in progress | `409` | `idempotency_key_in_progress` |
| Payload too large | `413` | `payload_too_large` |

Avoid telling client which part of signature was wrong:

```text id="22k9uu"
Good:
  invalid_signature

Bad:
  expected digest abc but got xyz
```

# 35. Testing Matrix

```text id="h886kg"
[ ] Missing API key rejected.
[ ] Unknown client rejected.
[ ] Revoked client rejected.
[ ] Expired secret rejected.
[ ] Valid API key accepted.
[ ] Missing signature rejected.
[ ] Invalid signature rejected.
[ ] Signature from modified body rejected.
[ ] Signature from parsed/reformatted JSON rejected if raw body differs.
[ ] Valid HMAC signature accepted.
[ ] Timestamp too old rejected.
[ ] Timestamp too far in future rejected.
[ ] Reused nonce rejected.
[ ] Same idempotency key + same payload returns stored response.
[ ] Same idempotency key + different payload returns 422.
[ ] Same idempotency key while in progress returns 409.
[ ] Partner tenant mapping enforced.
[ ] Body over max size rejected.
[ ] Raw secret/API key never appears in logs.
[ ] Webhook duplicate delivery not processed twice.
[ ] Secret rotation accepts old and new during overlap.
[ ] Secret rotation rejects old after revoke.
```

---

# 36. Test Example - Signature Body Tampering

```java id="c50ffd"
@Test
void shouldRejectSignatureWhenBodyModified() {
    String originalBody = """
            {"externalTenantRef":"ACME-001","orderId":"ORDER-1"}
            """;

    String tamperedBody = """
            {"externalTenantRef":"ACME-001","orderId":"ORDER-2"}
            """;

    SignedRequest signed = signer.sign(
            "partner-acme",
            "POST",
            "/partner/orders",
            originalBody
    );

    Response response = target("/partner/orders")
            .request()
            .header("X-Client-Id", signed.clientId())
            .header("X-Timestamp", signed.timestamp())
            .header("X-Nonce", signed.nonce())
            .header("X-Content-SHA256", signed.contentSha256())
            .header("X-Signature", signed.signature())
            .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString())
            .post(Entity.json(tamperedBody));

    assertEquals(401, response.getStatus());
}
```

---

# 37. Test Example - Idempotency Same Key Different Payload

```java id="mslbss"
@Test
void shouldRejectSameIdempotencyKeyWithDifferentPayload() {
    String key = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    CreateOrderRequest first = new CreateOrderRequest("ACME-001", "ORDER-1");
    CreateOrderRequest second = new CreateOrderRequest("ACME-001", "ORDER-2");

    Response r1 = partnerClient.createOrder(first, key);
    Response r2 = partnerClient.createOrder(second, key);

    assertEquals(202, r1.getStatus());
    assertEquals(422, r2.getStatus());
}
```

---

# 38. Test Example - Replay Nonce

```java id="rl62vk"
@Test
void shouldRejectReusedNonceWithinToleranceWindow() {
    String nonce = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    SignedRequest first = signer.signWithNonce("partner-acme", nonce, validBody());
    SignedRequest second = signer.signWithNonce("partner-acme", nonce, validBody());

    Response r1 = send(first);
    Response r2 = send(second);

    assertEquals(202, r1.getStatus());
    assertEquals(401, r2.getStatus());
}
```

---

# 39. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| API key di query string | bocor di logs/proxy/history | header only |
| API key plaintext di DB | credential leak | hash + prefix |
| Secret hardcoded | repo leak | Vault/KMS/secret manager |
| Signature atas parsed JSON | mismatch/bypass bug | raw body bytes |
| Tidak pakai timestamp | replay selamanya | timestamp tolerance |
| Timestamp tanpa nonce | replay dalam window | nonce/event ID store |
| Idempotency key tanpa request hash | key reuse payload beda | fingerprint |
| Idempotency hanya di memory | multi-instance double process | DB unique constraint |
| Duplicate webhook diproses ulang | double mutation | delivery ID dedupe |
| Signature invalid detail diexpose | attacker feedback | generic error |
| Partner tenant dari body | tenant spoofing | trusted mapping |
| Secret rotation tanpa overlap | downtime client | old+new overlap window |

---

# 40. Code Review Checklist

```text id="b2gh6i"
[ ] Endpoint memakai HTTPS only di edge.
[ ] API key tidak diterima via query string.
[ ] API key/secret tidak disimpan plaintext.
[ ] HMAC memakai raw body bytes.
[ ] Canonical string terdokumentasi.
[ ] Signature compare constant-time.
[ ] Timestamp tolerance diterapkan.
[ ] Nonce/event ID disimpan dengan TTL.
[ ] Payload size dibatasi sebelum readAllBytes.
[ ] Idempotency key wajib untuk non-idempotent POST/PATCH.
[ ] Idempotency record menyimpan request fingerprint.
[ ] Same key different payload ditolak.
[ ] Duplicate completed request mengembalikan response konsisten.
[ ] Partner tenant di-resolve dari trusted mapping.
[ ] Webhook duplicate tidak diproses dua kali.
[ ] Audit event untuk invalid signature/replay/idempotency conflict.
[ ] Secret rotation didukung.
```

---

# 41. Minimal Production Invariants

```text id="sg1h55"
1. API key is identity hint, not authorization proof.
2. HMAC must be calculated over canonical request and raw body digest.
3. Signature must be compared with constant-time comparison.
4. Timestamp must have bounded tolerance.
5. Timestamp must be paired with nonce/event ID for replay protection.
6. Non-idempotent retryable operations need idempotency key.
7. Idempotency key must be scoped by authenticated client.
8. Same idempotency key with different payload must be rejected.
9. Webhook delivery ID must be deduplicated.
10. Secrets must be hashed/encrypted, rotated, and never logged.
```

---

# 42. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | API key lifecycle | Client identity, secret storage, rotation |
| 2 | HMAC signature | Payload authenticity/integrity |
| 3 | Canonical request | Client/server signature konsisten |
| 4 | Raw body handling | Verifikasi sebelum parse |
| 5 | Timestamp/nonce | Replay protection |
| 6 | Idempotency key | Retry aman untuk POST/PATCH |
| 7 | Webhook dedupe | Duplicate event tidak double-process |
| 8 | Partner tenant mapping | Tenant spoofing dicegah |
| 9 | Audit/metrics | Invalid signature/replay terlihat |
| 10 | Regression tests | Tampering, replay, retry, rotation tertutup |
