7/13/2026, 5:14:37 AM

# Cheatsheet - Secure Error Handling & Response Semantics untuk Authentication/Authorization di Jersey/JAX-RS

## 1. Tujuan

Cheatsheet ini fokus ke **cara mapping error security menjadi HTTP response** yang benar, konsisten, dan tidak membocorkan informasi sensitif.

Masalah yang ingin dicegah:

```text id="be51mk"
- Invalid token diberi 500.
- Missing permission diberi 401 padahal user sudah authenticated.
- Cross-tenant object existence bocor lewat 403.
- Error response membocorkan stack trace, SQL, claim detail, token detail.
- Client tidak bisa membedakan validation error, auth error, conflict, dan idempotency conflict.
- Audit internal kehilangan reason karena response terlalu generic tanpa audit detail.
```

Jakarta REST menyediakan `WebApplicationException` untuk menghasilkan HTTP error response dari resource/provider, dan `ExceptionMapper` untuk memetakan exception menjadi response secara terpusat. RFC 9457 mendefinisikan format **Problem Details for HTTP APIs** dan menggantikan RFC 7807; format ini cocok untuk error response yang machine-readable. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/platform/10/apidocs/jakarta/ws/rs/webapplicationexception?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model

```text id="ijm3qo"
Client response:
  aman, stabil, tidak terlalu detail

Audit log:
  detail internal, reason spesifik, policy version, evidence

Exception:
  internal control flow / error representation

HTTP status:
  semantic contract untuk caller

Problem Details:
  body standar untuk error API
```

Rule utama:

```text id="dtzi9o"
Do not hide everything from audit.
Do not expose everything to client.
```

OWASP Error Handling Cheat Sheet menyarankan agar unexpected error mengembalikan response generic ke user, sementara detail error dicatat di server side untuk investigasi. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Error_Handling_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 3. Diagram Error Flow

```mermaid id="dz6hs9"
flowchart TD
    A[Request] --> B[Jersey Filter / Resource / Service]
    B --> C{Exception or Deny?}

    C -->|AuthN Failure| D[AuthenticationException]
    C -->|AuthZ Deny| E[AuthorizationDeniedException]
    C -->|Object Not Found| F[NotFoundException]
    C -->|Validation Failure| G[ValidationException]
    C -->|State Conflict| H[ConflictException]
    C -->|Unexpected Error| I[Throwable]

    D --> J[ExceptionMapper]
    E --> J
    F --> J
    G --> J
    H --> J
    I --> J

    J --> K[Audit / Security Log]
    J --> L[Problem Details Response]

    K --> M[(SIEM / Audit Store)]
    L --> N[Client]
```

---

# 4. HTTP Status Decision Table

| Case | Status | Client Meaning | Audit Detail |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Missing token | `401` | Authentication required | `missing_authorization_header` |
| Malformed token | `401` | Invalid credential | `malformed_token` |
| Expired token | `401` | Invalid/expired credential | `token_expired` |
| Invalid signature | `401` | Invalid credential | `invalid_signature`, safe kid/issuer |
| Wrong issuer/audience | `401` | Invalid credential | `invalid_issuer`, `invalid_audience` |
| Valid token, missing scope | `403` | Not enough authority | `missing_scope` |
| Valid token, missing permission | `403` | Forbidden | `missing_permission` |
| Valid permission, object denied | `403` | Forbidden | `object_access_denied` |
| Cross-tenant object | `404` or `403` | Usually hide existence | `cross_tenant_access_denied` |
| Object genuinely not found | `404` | Resource not found | optional |
| Invalid request body/schema | `400` | Bad request | validation reason |
| Bean validation failure | `400` or `422` | Field rule failed | field violations |
| Business state conflict | `409` | Resource state conflict | current/expected state |
| Same idempotency key, different payload | `422` | semantic mismatch | `idempotency_payload_mismatch` |
| Same idempotency key in progress | `409` | concurrent duplicate | `idempotency_in_progress` |
| Rate limit | `429` | retry later | client/rule |
| Unexpected server error | `500` | generic failure | stack trace internal only |
| Dependency unavailable | `503` | temporary unavailable | dependency/fallback mode |

---

# 5. 401 vs 403

## 401 Unauthorized

Pakai saat authentication gagal atau credential tidak valid.

```text id="sx3mt9"
- missing Authorization header
- invalid Authorization scheme
- malformed token
- expired token
- invalid signature
- wrong issuer
- wrong audience
- wrong token type
```

Untuk Bearer token, RFC 6750 mendefinisikan penggunaan `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer` dan error values seperti `invalid_token` dan `insufficient_scope`; `insufficient_scope` dipakai ketika request membutuhkan scope lebih tinggi daripada yang dimiliki token. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6750?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```http id="9e12d9"
HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized
WWW-Authenticate: Bearer realm="case-api", error="invalid_token"
Content-Type: application/problem+json
```

## 403 Forbidden

Pakai saat caller sudah authenticated, tetapi tidak authorized.

```text id="x1qy6j"
- valid token but missing permission
- valid user but wrong role/entitlement
- service token not allowed for internal endpoint
- partner not allowed for tenant
- impersonation grant missing
- object policy denies
```

Contoh:

```http id="05aei4"
HTTP/1.1 403 Forbidden
Content-Type: application/problem+json
```

---

# 6. 404 vs 403 untuk Object-Level Authorization

Untuk object ID yang berasal dari client, response perlu hati-hati.

```text id="zpvhd7"
GET /cases/CASE-789
```

Jika `CASE-789` ada tapi milik tenant lain, memberi `403` dapat mengungkap bahwa object itu ada. Dalam sistem multi-tenant/high-security, lebih aman mengembalikan `404` untuk cross-tenant access, sambil mencatat audit internal `CROSS_TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED`.

Pattern:

```text id="g5pqs4"
findByIdForTenant(caseId, actor.tenantId):
  empty -> 404

policy denies object in same tenant:
  403
```

Untuk kasus cross-tenant yang terdeteksi secara internal:

```text id="d1dqtn"
client response:
  404 Not Found

audit:
  CROSS_TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED
  actorTenant=tenant-a
  resourceId=CASE-789
```

---

# 7. RFC 9457 Problem Details

RFC 9457 mendefinisikan **problem detail** sebagai format standar untuk membawa detail error yang machine-readable dalam HTTP API response, supaya API tidak perlu menciptakan format error baru sendiri. RFC ini menggantikan RFC 7807. ([RFC Editor](https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc9457/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Struktur umum:

```json id="yvau52"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.com/problems/access-denied",
  "title": "Access denied",
  "status": 403,
  "detail": "You are not allowed to perform this action.",
  "instance": "/cases/CASE-789/approve",
  "code": "ACCESS_DENIED",
  "correlationId": "req-123"
}
```

Untuk security error, `detail` harus aman. Reason internal seperti `maker_checker_violation`, `tenant_mismatch`, atau `policy_version_stale` boleh masuk audit, tetapi tidak selalu boleh masuk response.

---

# 8. Error Body Standard

```java id="ulf0ru"
public record ApiProblem(
        URI type,
        String title,
        int status,
        String detail,
        String instance,
        String code,
        String correlationId,
        Map<String, Object> errors
) {}
```

Contoh generic forbidden:

```json id="bbglvz"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.com/problems/access-denied",
  "title": "Access denied",
  "status": 403,
  "detail": "You are not allowed to perform this action.",
  "instance": "/cases/CASE-789/approve",
  "code": "ACCESS_DENIED",
  "correlationId": "req-123"
}
```

Contoh validation error:

```json id="z84qyr"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.com/problems/validation-failed",
  "title": "Validation failed",
  "status": 400,
  "detail": "Request validation failed.",
  "instance": "/partner/orders",
  "code": "VALIDATION_FAILED",
  "correlationId": "req-124",
  "errors": {
    "externalTenantRef": "must not be blank",
    "orderId": "must not be blank"
  }
}
```

---

# 9. Public vs Internal Error Detail

| Error | Client Detail | Audit Detail |
|---|---|---|
| expired token | `Invalid or expired token.` | `token_expired`, exp, issuer |
| invalid signature | `Invalid token.` | `invalid_signature`, kid |
| wrong audience | `Invalid token.` | expected/actual audience |
| missing permission | `Access denied.` | `missing_permission: case.approve` |
| tenant mismatch | `Resource not found.` or `Access denied.` | actor tenant, resource tenant |
| PDP timeout | `Access denied.` or `Temporarily unavailable.` | `pdp_timeout_fail_closed` |
| SQL exception | `Internal server error.` | stack trace, SQL state |
| Bean validation | safe field details | raw input only if safe/minimized |

Principle:

```text id="vaolqk"
Client gets stable safe code.
Audit gets diagnostic reason.
```

---

# 10. Exception Hierarchy

```java id="fo1h55"
public abstract class ApiException extends RuntimeException {

    private final int status;
    private final String code;
    private final String safeDetail;
    private final String auditReason;

    protected ApiException(
            int status,
            String code,
            String safeDetail,
            String auditReason
    ) {
        super(auditReason);
        this.status = status;
        this.code = code;
        this.safeDetail = safeDetail;
        this.auditReason = auditReason;
    }

    public int status() {
        return status;
    }

    public String code() {
        return code;
    }

    public String safeDetail() {
        return safeDetail;
    }

    public String auditReason() {
        return auditReason;
    }
}
```

Specific exceptions:

```java id="d9n6cu"
public final class AuthenticationFailedException extends ApiException {
    public AuthenticationFailedException(String auditReason) {
        super(
                401,
                "UNAUTHORIZED",
                "Authentication is required or the credential is invalid.",
                auditReason
        );
    }
}
```

```java id="dcy2ip"
public final class AccessDeniedException extends ApiException {
    public AccessDeniedException(String auditReason) {
        super(
                403,
                "ACCESS_DENIED",
                "You are not allowed to perform this action.",
                auditReason
        );
    }
}
```

```java id="0ysv3t"
public final class ResourceConcealedException extends ApiException {
    public ResourceConcealedException(String auditReason) {
        super(
                404,
                "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND",
                "Resource not found.",
                auditReason
        );
    }
}
```

```java id="8jrgab"
public final class BusinessConflictException extends ApiException {
    public BusinessConflictException(String safeDetail, String auditReason) {
        super(
                409,
                "RESOURCE_CONFLICT",
                safeDetail,
                auditReason
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 11. Global ExceptionMapper

```java id="xtn7jd"
@Provider
public class ApiExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<ApiException> {

    @Context
    private HttpServletRequest request;

    private final SecurityAuditLogger auditLogger;

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(ApiException exception) {
        String correlationId = correlationId(request);

        auditLogger.logApiException(
                exception.status(),
                exception.code(),
                exception.auditReason(),
                request.getMethod(),
                request.getRequestURI(),
                correlationId
        );

        ApiProblem problem = new ApiProblem(
                problemType(exception.code()),
                titleFor(exception.status()),
                exception.status(),
                exception.safeDetail(),
                request.getRequestURI(),
                exception.code(),
                correlationId,
                Map.of()
        );

        Response.ResponseBuilder builder = Response
                .status(exception.status())
                .type("application/problem+json")
                .entity(problem);

        if (exception.status() == 401) {
            builder.header(
                    HttpHeaders.WWW_AUTHENTICATE,
                    "Bearer realm=\"case-api\", error=\"invalid_token\""
            );
        }

        return builder.build();
    }

    private URI problemType(String code) {
        return URI.create("https://api.company.com/problems/" + code.toLowerCase());
    }

    private String titleFor(int status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case 400 -> "Bad request";
            case 401 -> "Unauthorized";
            case 403 -> "Access denied";
            case 404 -> "Resource not found";
            case 409 -> "Conflict";
            case 422 -> "Unprocessable content";
            case 429 -> "Too many requests";
            default -> "Error";
        };
    }

    private String correlationId(HttpServletRequest request) {
        Object value = request.getAttribute("correlationId");
        return value == null ? null : value.toString();
    }
}
```

---

# 12. Throwable Fallback Mapper

Jangan biarkan stack trace keluar ke client.

```java id="o90w2u"
@Provider
public class UnhandledExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    @Context
    private HttpServletRequest request;

    private static final Logger LOG =
            LoggerFactory.getLogger(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class);

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        String correlationId = correlationId(request);

        LOG.error(
                "Unhandled exception correlationId={} method={} path={}",
                correlationId,
                request.getMethod(),
                request.getRequestURI(),
                exception
        );

        ApiProblem problem = new ApiProblem(
                URI.create("https://api.company.com/problems/internal-server-error"),
                "Internal server error",
                500,
                "An unexpected error occurred.",
                request.getRequestURI(),
                "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
                correlationId,
                Map.of()
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .type("application/problem+json")
                .entity(problem)
                .build();
    }

    private String correlationId(HttpServletRequest request) {
        Object value = request.getAttribute("correlationId");
        return value == null ? null : value.toString();
    }
}
```

---

# 13. Authentication Filter Error Mapping

Authentication filter biasanya belum masuk resource method, jadi bisa `abortWith`.

```java id="ly43or"
private void abortUnauthorized(
        ContainerRequestContext ctx,
        String auditReason
) {
    String correlationId = correlationId(ctx);

    auditLogger.logAuthenticationFailure(
            auditReason,
            ctx.getMethod(),
            ctx.getUriInfo().getPath(),
            correlationId
    );

    ApiProblem problem = new ApiProblem(
            URI.create("https://api.company.com/problems/unauthorized"),
            "Unauthorized",
            401,
            "Authentication is required or the credential is invalid.",
            ctx.getUriInfo().getPath(),
            "UNAUTHORIZED",
            correlationId,
            Map.of()
    );

    ctx.abortWith(Response.status(Response.Status.UNAUTHORIZED)
            .header(
                    HttpHeaders.WWW_AUTHENTICATE,
                    "Bearer realm=\"case-api\", error=\"invalid_token\""
            )
            .type("application/problem+json")
            .entity(problem)
            .build());
}
```

Jangan:

```json id="8o5uev"
{
  "error": "JWT signature invalid for kid abc; expected issuer https://idp-internal..."
}
```

---

# 14. Authorization Filter Error Mapping

```java id="jhmqfd"
private void deny(
        ContainerRequestContext ctx,
        SecurityActor actor,
        String action,
        AuthorizationDecision decision
) {
    auditLogger.logAuthorizationDecision(
            actor,
            action,
            decision,
            correlationId(ctx)
    );

    ApiProblem problem = new ApiProblem(
            URI.create("https://api.company.com/problems/access-denied"),
            "Access denied",
            403,
            "You are not allowed to perform this action.",
            ctx.getUriInfo().getPath(),
            "ACCESS_DENIED",
            correlationId(ctx),
            Map.of()
    );

    ctx.abortWith(Response.status(Response.Status.FORBIDDEN)
            .type("application/problem+json")
            .entity(problem)
            .build());
}
```

Audit reason:

```text id="kctqfj"
missing_permission:case.approve
service_not_allowed
impersonation_grant_expired
original_actor_not_authorized
pdp_timeout_fail_closed
```

Client tetap cukup:

```json id="9g8if5"
{
  "code": "ACCESS_DENIED",
  "detail": "You are not allowed to perform this action."
}
```

---

# 15. Bean Validation Error Mapping

Jakarta Bean Validation biasanya menghasilkan `ConstraintViolationException`.

```java id="g9c0xu"
@Provider
public class ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<ConstraintViolationException> {

    @Context
    private HttpServletRequest request;

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(ConstraintViolationException exception) {
        Map<String, Object> errors = exception.getConstraintViolations()
                .stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(
                        violation -> violation.getPropertyPath().toString(),
                        ConstraintViolation::getMessage,
                        (left, right) -> left
                ));

        ApiProblem problem = new ApiProblem(
                URI.create("https://api.company.com/problems/validation-failed"),
                "Validation failed",
                400,
                "Request validation failed.",
                request.getRequestURI(),
                "VALIDATION_FAILED",
                correlationId(request),
                errors
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
                .type("application/problem+json")
                .entity(problem)
                .build();
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="2clu66"
Field name dan validation message biasanya aman.
Raw submitted value belum tentu aman untuk response/log.
```

---

# 16. 400 vs 422

| Status | Gunakan Untuk | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| `400 Bad Request` | Request malformed/sintaks/format tidak valid | JSON rusak, missing required header |
| `422 Unprocessable Content` | Request sintaks valid tapi semantic invalid | idempotency key sama payload beda, business validation tertentu |

Untuk idempotency, draft IETF HTTPAPI menyebut reuse idempotency key dengan payload berbeda sebagai skenario yang dapat menghasilkan `422 Unprocessable Content`, sedangkan concurrent duplicate request dapat menghasilkan `409 Conflict`. ([RFC Editor](https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc9457/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh 422:

```json id="2xf8kh"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.com/problems/idempotency-payload-mismatch",
  "title": "Unprocessable content",
  "status": 422,
  "detail": "The idempotency key was already used with a different request payload.",
  "code": "IDEMPOTENCY_PAYLOAD_MISMATCH",
  "correlationId": "req-125"
}
```

---

# 17. 409 Conflict

Gunakan untuk conflict terhadap state resource.

Contoh:

```text id="rpmslz"
- approve case tapi status sudah CLOSED
- optimistic lock version mismatch
- idempotency key sedang IN_PROGRESS
- concurrent update conflict
- workflow task sudah completed
```

Response:

```json id="5p2eaq"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.com/problems/resource-conflict",
  "title": "Conflict",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "The resource state changed. Please refresh and try again.",
  "code": "RESOURCE_CONFLICT",
  "correlationId": "req-126"
}
```

Audit internal:

```json id="k5yfk3"
{
  "eventType": "BUSINESS_CONFLICT",
  "reason": "expected_status_submitted_actual_closed",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "CASE-789",
  "expectedStatus": "SUBMITTED",
  "actualStatus": "CLOSED"
}
```

---

# 18. WWW-Authenticate Header

Untuk `401`, sertakan `WWW-Authenticate`.

```http id="qsa4ul"
WWW-Authenticate: Bearer realm="case-api", error="invalid_token"
```

Untuk insufficient scope, RFC 6750 mendefinisikan error `insufficient_scope`, dan response dapat menyertakan atribut `scope` yang menunjukkan scope yang dibutuhkan. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6750?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```http id="u5u0gl"
HTTP/1.1 403 Forbidden
WWW-Authenticate: Bearer realm="case-api", error="insufficient_scope", scope="case:write"
Content-Type: application/problem+json
```

Praktik aman:

```text id="tzcldr"
401:
  invalid_token generic

403:
  insufficient_scope boleh jika scope memang bagian public API contract

Internal permission:
  jangan expose permission internal terlalu detail kecuali API contract mengizinkan
```

---

# 19. Error Code Taxonomy

Gunakan code stabil.

```text id="f7jljg"
UNAUTHORIZED
ACCESS_DENIED
RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND
VALIDATION_FAILED
RESOURCE_CONFLICT
IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED
IDEMPOTENCY_IN_PROGRESS
IDEMPOTENCY_PAYLOAD_MISMATCH
REPLAY_DETECTED
INVALID_SIGNATURE
RATE_LIMITED
SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE
INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR
```

Jangan jadikan `reason` internal sebagai public code jika terlalu detail:

```text id="mx7ud2"
Internal reason:
  maker_checker_violation
  tenant_mismatch
  pdp_timeout_fail_closed
  missing_permission:admin.role.manage

Public code:
  ACCESS_DENIED
```

---

# 20. Secure Error Mapping Matrix

| Internal Reason | Status | Public Code | Public Detail |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `missing_authorization_header` | 401 | `UNAUTHORIZED` | Authentication is required or invalid. |
| `token_expired` | 401 | `UNAUTHORIZED` | Authentication is required or invalid. |
| `invalid_audience` | 401 | `UNAUTHORIZED` | Authentication is required or invalid. |
| `missing_permission:case.approve` | 403 | `ACCESS_DENIED` | You are not allowed to perform this action. |
| `tenant_mismatch` | 404 | `RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND` | Resource not found. |
| `case_not_submitted` | 409 | `RESOURCE_CONFLICT` | The resource state does not allow this action. |
| `maker_checker_violation` | 403 | `ACCESS_DENIED` | You are not allowed to perform this action. |
| `impersonation_grant_expired` | 403 | `ACCESS_DENIED` | You are not allowed to perform this action. |
| `pdp_timeout_fail_closed` | 403/503 | `ACCESS_DENIED`/`SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE` | depends on policy |
| `same_key_different_payload` | 422 | `IDEMPOTENCY_PAYLOAD_MISMATCH` | Key used with different payload. |
| `nonce_already_seen` | 409/401 | `REPLAY_DETECTED` | Request cannot be processed. |

---

# 21. PDP Failure Error Semantics

Saat PDP/entitlement service down:

| Action Type | Client Status | Internal Reason |
|---|---:|---|
| Sensitive write/admin | `403` fail-closed or `503` if policy says unavailable | `pdp_timeout_fail_closed` |
| Low-risk read | maybe cached/degraded | `pdp_unavailable_cached_decision` |
| Break-glass | `503` or `403`, never allow | `pdp_unavailable_breakglass_denied` |

Decision guide:

```text id="9qaztm"
If caller can safely retry later:
  503 may be appropriate.

If revealing dependency failure is not desired:
  403 with audit reason fail-closed.

For admin/sensitive action:
  never fail-open.
```

---

# 22. Error Response and Audit Pairing

Every security error should have a matching audit event.

```java id="cn27wz"
public record ErrorMappingResult(
        Response response,
        SecurityAuditEvent auditEvent
) {}
```

Example client response:

```json id="8nxiyt"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.com/problems/access-denied",
  "title": "Access denied",
  "status": 403,
  "detail": "You are not allowed to perform this action.",
  "code": "ACCESS_DENIED",
  "correlationId": "req-abc"
}
```

Matching audit:

```json id="lopdst"
{
  "eventType": "AUTHORIZATION_DECISION",
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "missing_permission:case.approve",
  "subjectId": "user-123",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "action": "case.approve",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "CASE-789",
  "correlationId": "req-abc"
}
```

---

# 23. Avoid Information Disclosure

Jangan expose:

```text id="z6cxca"
- stack trace
- SQL query
- DB table/column name
- internal host/service name
- token content
- issuer internal details if not public
- expected audience details if sensitive
- permission catalog internal names
- tenant mismatch details
- object existence across tenants
- PDP dependency topology
```

Boleh expose:

```text id="rmzqod"
- stable public error code
- correlation ID
- safe validation messages
- retry hint when appropriate
- required public OAuth scope if part of API contract
```

---

# 24. Correlation ID in Error

Every error response:

```json id="chgjpy"
{
  "code": "ACCESS_DENIED",
  "correlationId": "req-abc"
}
```

Benefits:

```text id="awld7c"
Client can report correlationId.
Support/SRE can find internal audit/log.
Client does not need stack trace.
```

---

# 25. ExceptionMapper Registration Order

Specific mapper first conceptually:

```text id="r6yxam"
ApiExceptionMapper
ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper
JsonMappingExceptionMapper
NotFoundExceptionMapper
UnhandledExceptionMapper
```

In JAX-RS, providers implementing `ExceptionMapper` must be registered programmatically or annotated with `@Provider` for discovery. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/ext/exceptionmapper?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Avoid having only `ExceptionMapper<Throwable>` that swallows all exceptions before specific mappers can handle them.

---

# 26. JSON Parse Error Mapping

```java id="z8d5ke"
@Provider
public class JsonProcessingExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<JsonProcessingException> {

    @Context
    private HttpServletRequest request;

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(JsonProcessingException exception) {
        ApiProblem problem = new ApiProblem(
                URI.create("https://api.company.com/problems/malformed-json"),
                "Malformed JSON",
                400,
                "Request body is not valid JSON.",
                request.getRequestURI(),
                "MALFORMED_JSON",
                correlationId(request),
                Map.of()
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
                .type("application/problem+json")
                .entity(problem)
                .build();
    }
}
```

Audit internal boleh menyimpan location secara terbatas, tapi jangan expose payload besar.

---

# 27. MyBatis / PostgreSQL Error Mapping

Jangan expose SQL error detail.

| DB Error | Public Status | Internal Audit |
|---|---:|---|
| unique constraint business duplicate | `409` | constraint name |
| FK violation due invalid reference | `400` or `409` | SQL state |
| optimistic lock update count 0 | `409` | expected version/status |
| DB connection failure | `503` or `500` | dependency error |
| syntax/config bug | `500` | stack trace internal |

Example:

```java id="pq9v04"
public final class DatabaseExceptionTranslator {

    public ApiException translate(Exception e) {
        if (isUniqueViolation(e, "uk_order_external_id")) {
            return new BusinessConflictException(
                    "A resource with the same external id already exists.",
                    "unique_violation:uk_order_external_id"
            );
        }

        return new InternalServerApiException("database_error");
    }
}
```

---

# 28. Rate Limit Error

```http id="2bxkc1"
HTTP/1.1 429 Too Many Requests
Retry-After: 60
Content-Type: application/problem+json
```

```json id="tp8j7e"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.com/problems/rate-limited",
  "title": "Too many requests",
  "status": 429,
  "detail": "Too many requests. Please retry later.",
  "code": "RATE_LIMITED",
  "correlationId": "req-129"
}
```

Do not expose internal rate-limit bucket details unless API contract requires it.

---

# 29. Security Filter Failures

Filter errors happen before resource method. Handle consistently.

| Filter | Failure | Status |
|---|---|---:|
| Correlation filter | none/generate | continue |
| Raw body filter | body too large | `413` |
| HMAC filter | invalid signature | `401` |
| Replay filter | replay nonce | `401`/`409` |
| Authentication filter | invalid token | `401` |
| Endpoint classification filter | unclassified endpoint | `403` or `500` in prod |
| Authorization filter | missing permission | `403` |

For unclassified endpoint:

```text id="aeok5w"
In test/CI:
  fail build

In runtime:
  fail closed
  403 or 500 depending operational preference
  audit SECURITY_MISCONFIGURATION
```

---

# 30. Client-Retry Semantics

| Status | Client Retry? |
|---|---|
| `400` | No, fix request |
| `401` | Maybe refresh credential |
| `403` | No, unless permission changes |
| `404` | No, unless ID changes/eventual creation |
| `409` | Maybe refresh state/retry with new version |
| `422` | No, fix semantic issue |
| `429` | Yes after `Retry-After` |
| `500` | Maybe, with backoff |
| `503` | Yes, with backoff |

For idempotent retry, require idempotency key for unsafe operations.

---

# 31. Testing Matrix

```text id="j6wo4k"
[ ] Missing token returns 401 with WWW-Authenticate.
[ ] Expired token returns 401 generic response.
[ ] Invalid signature does not expose token detail.
[ ] Valid token missing permission returns 403.
[ ] Cross-tenant object returns 404 if concealment policy enabled.
[ ] Same-tenant object policy deny returns 403.
[ ] Invalid JSON returns 400.
[ ] Bean validation failure returns 400 with safe field errors.
[ ] State conflict returns 409.
[ ] Idempotency key same payload in progress returns 409.
[ ] Idempotency key different payload returns 422.
[ ] Unexpected exception returns 500 without stack trace.
[ ] Every error includes correlationId.
[ ] Security deny emits audit event with internal reason.
[ ] Raw token never appears in response/log.
[ ] SQL exception detail never appears in response.
[ ] Unclassified endpoint fails closed.
```

---

# 32. Test Example - Invalid Token Generic Response

```java id="sk4h1a"
@Test
void invalidTokenShouldReturnGeneric401AndAuditReason() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-1")
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer invalid.jwt.value")
            .get();

    assertEquals(401, response.getStatus());
    assertNotNull(response.getHeaderString(HttpHeaders.WWW_AUTHENTICATE));

    String body = response.readEntity(String.class);

    assertTrue(body.contains("UNAUTHORIZED"));
    assertFalse(body.contains("invalid.jwt.value"));
    assertFalse(body.contains("signature"));
    assertFalse(body.contains("kid"));

    SecurityAuditEvent event = auditSink.lastEvent();

    assertEquals("AUTHENTICATION_FAILURE", event.eventType());
    assertEquals("malformed_token", event.reason());
}
```

---

# 33. Test Example - Cross-Tenant Concealment

```java id="vtjhtu"
@Test
void crossTenantObjectShouldReturn404ButAuditCrossTenantDeny() {
    SecurityActor alice = PrincipalFixture.user()
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .subject("alice")
            .permission("case.read")
            .build();

    String tenantBCaseId = seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-b")
            .build()
            .id();

    Response response = target("/cases/" + tenantBCaseId)
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, tokenFor(alice))
            .get();

    assertEquals(404, response.getStatus());

    SecurityAuditEvent event = auditSink.lastEvent();

    assertEquals("CROSS_TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED", event.eventType());
    assertEquals("tenant_mismatch", event.reason());
}
```

---

# 34. Test Example - No Stack Trace

```java id="ngljvf"
@Test
void unexpectedExceptionShouldNotReturnStackTrace() {
    Response response = target("/test/throws-null-pointer")
            .request()
            .get();

    assertEquals(500, response.getStatus());

    String body = response.readEntity(String.class);

    assertTrue(body.contains("INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR"));
    assertFalse(body.contains("NullPointerException"));
    assertFalse(body.contains("java.lang"));
    assertFalse(body.contains("at com.company"));
}
```

---

# 35. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Semua error jadi 500 | Client contract buruk | status semantic mapping |
| Missing permission jadi 401 | AuthN/AuthZ kabur | 403 |
| Invalid token jadi 403 | Client tidak tahu refresh credential | 401 |
| Cross-tenant object jadi 403 selalu | existence leak | conceal as 404 jika perlu |
| Stack trace di response | information disclosure | generic 500 + server log |
| SQL detail di response | information disclosure | translate DB error |
| Raw token di error | credential leak | never echo credential |
| Public detail = audit reason | policy leakage | split safeDetail/auditReason |
| No correlation ID | support/debug sulit | include correlationId |
| No `WWW-Authenticate` for 401 | OAuth client behavior buruk | set header |
| ExceptionMapper terlalu generic | specific mapping hilang | specific mappers |
| Validation logs full payload | PII/secret leak | field allowlist |

---

# 36. Code Review Checklist

```text id="lpa5ik"
[ ] 401 only for authentication failure.
[ ] 403 for authenticated but unauthorized.
[ ] Cross-tenant response concealment policy is explicit.
[ ] 409 used for resource state/version conflict.
[ ] 422 used for semantic/idempotency mismatch when appropriate.
[ ] Error body follows Problem Details style.
[ ] Error response includes correlationId.
[ ] 401 includes WWW-Authenticate.
[ ] Response does not expose stack trace, SQL, token, internal policy detail.
[ ] Audit contains internal reason and evidence.
[ ] ExceptionMapper exists for ApiException.
[ ] Fallback Throwable mapper is generic.
[ ] Validation mapper does not echo unsafe values.
[ ] Filter abort responses use same error format.
[ ] Tests cover negative security cases.
```

---

# 37. Minimal Production Invariants

```text id="4kadzr"
1. Client error detail must be safe.
2. Audit detail must be diagnostic.
3. Authentication failure maps to 401.
4. Authorization failure maps to 403 unless concealment policy uses 404.
5. Cross-tenant object existence should not leak unintentionally.
6. Unexpected errors must not return stack traces.
7. Error response must include correlationId.
8. 401 Bearer errors should include WWW-Authenticate.
9. Security errors must emit audit events.
10. Exception mapping must be centralized and tested.
```

---

# 38. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | HTTP status semantics | 400/401/403/404/409/422 benar |
| 2 | Problem Details format | Error response stabil dan machine-readable |
| 3 | Authentication error mapping | Bearer 401 + safe body |
| 4 | Authorization error mapping | 403/404 concealment + audit reason |
| 5 | Validation error mapping | Field errors aman |
| 6 | Conflict/idempotency mapping | Retry semantics jelas |
| 7 | Global ExceptionMapper | Stack trace tidak bocor |
| 8 | Audit pairing | Response safe, audit detail |
| 9 | Security tests | Negative cases tertutup |
| 10 | Client contract | API consumers paham retry/fix behavior |

# Cheatsheet - Security Headers, CORS, CSRF, Cookies, Browser Clients, dan API Gateway Boundary untuk Jersey/JAX-RS Microservices

## 1. Tujuan

Cheatsheet ini fokus pada security boundary untuk API yang dipanggil dari browser, SPA, admin portal, partner dashboard, atau melalui API gateway.

Masalah yang ingin dicegah:

```text id="jk6d5o"
- API bisa dipanggil cross-origin tanpa kontrol.
- Cookie session dikirim dalam request cross-site.
- CORS dianggap sebagai authorization.
- CSRF tidak ditangani karena “API sudah pakai REST”.
- Browser menyimpan/menjalankan content dengan policy longgar.
- Security headers hanya dikonfigurasi di gateway, tapi service bypass masih terbuka.
- Admin/internal endpoint ikut terkena CORS public.
```

OWASP HTTP Headers Cheat Sheet menjelaskan bahwa security response headers dapat membantu mencegah vulnerability seperti XSS, clickjacking, dan information disclosure. OWASP REST Security Cheat Sheet juga menekankan bahwa non-public REST service harus menerapkan access control pada setiap endpoint. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/HTTP_Headers_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Big Picture

```mermaid id="sodnqp"
flowchart TD
    A[Browser / SPA / Admin Portal] --> B[API Gateway / Ingress]
    B --> C[CORS Policy]
    B --> D[TLS / HSTS]
    B --> E[Security Headers]
    B --> F[Rate Limit / WAF]

    C --> G[Jersey API]
    D --> G
    E --> G
    F --> G

    G --> H[Authentication Filter]
    H --> I{Credential Type}

    I -->|Bearer Token| J[JWT/OIDC Validation]
    I -->|Cookie Session| K[Session + CSRF Validation]
    I -->|API Key/HMAC| L[Partner/Internal Auth]

    J --> M[Authorization Filter]
    K --> M
    L --> M

    M --> N[Application Service Policy]
    N --> O[Object/Tenant Authorization]
    O --> P[MyBatis/PostgreSQL Guard]

    M --> Q[Audit]
    N --> Q
```

---

# 3. Core Mental Model

```text id="m8ih20"
CORS:
  Browser policy untuk cross-origin access.
  Bukan authorization.

CSRF:
  Attack yang menyalahgunakan browser user yang sudah authenticated.
  Relevan jika credential otomatis dikirim browser, terutama cookie.

Cookies:
  Browser-managed credential.
  Perlu Secure, HttpOnly, SameSite, domain/path/lifetime yang benar.

Security headers:
  Defense-in-depth untuk browser behavior.

API gateway:
  Edge control.
  Bukan pengganti service-layer authorization.
```

Rule paling penting:

```text id="nfm45x"
If browser automatically sends credentials, think CSRF.
If browser reads response cross-origin, think CORS.
If API mutates state, think authorization + CSRF/idempotency.
```

---

# 4. CORS - Cross-Origin Resource Sharing

CORS mengontrol apakah browser boleh membaca response dari origin berbeda. CORS bukan access control untuk server secara umum, karena non-browser client tetap bisa mengirim request.

## Origin

```text id="wzqj7f"
Origin = scheme + host + port

https://app.company.com
https://admin.company.com
https://app.company.com:8443
```

Origin berbeda:

```text id="29bg90"
https://app.company.com
https://api.company.com
```

## CORS Headers

| Header | Arti |
|---|---|
| `Access-Control-Allow-Origin` | Origin yang boleh membaca response |
| `Access-Control-Allow-Credentials` | Apakah browser boleh menyertakan credential |
| `Access-Control-Allow-Methods` | Method yang diizinkan saat preflight |
| `Access-Control-Allow-Headers` | Header request yang diizinkan saat preflight |
| `Access-Control-Max-Age` | Cache preflight |
| `Vary: Origin` | Cache proxy membedakan response per origin |

---

# 5. CORS Rules

## Public API tanpa credential

```http id="jlby3v"
Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *
```

Hanya untuk data yang benar-benar public.

## Credentialed API

Jika memakai cookie/session atau `Authorization` header dari browser:

```http id="d4cpl8"
Access-Control-Allow-Origin: https://app.company.com
Access-Control-Allow-Credentials: true
Vary: Origin
```

Jangan:

```http id="9otey3"
Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *
Access-Control-Allow-Credentials: true
```

Browser modern tidak mengizinkan wildcard origin bersama credentialed requests, dan secara desain ini juga buruk sebagai policy.

---

# 6. CORS Allowlist Strategy

```java id="c75211"
public final class CorsPolicy {

    private final Set<String> allowedOrigins = Set.of(
            "https://app.company.com",
            "https://admin.company.com"
    );

    public boolean isAllowedOrigin(String origin) {
        return allowedOrigins.contains(origin);
    }
}
```

Jangan pakai naive suffix check:

```java id="41vo49"
origin.endsWith(".company.com")
```

Karena rawan:

```text id="salq3f"
https://evilcompany.com
https://company.com.evil.org
https://attacker-controlled.company.com
```

Jika subdomain wildcard memang perlu, gunakan registry yang dikelola dan ownership jelas.

---

# 7. Jersey CORS Filter

```java id="r3jbu6"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR)
public class CorsFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    private static final String ORIGIN = "Origin";

    private final CorsPolicy corsPolicy;

    public CorsFilter(CorsPolicy corsPolicy) {
        this.corsPolicy = corsPolicy;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        if (isPreflight(requestContext)) {
            String origin = requestContext.getHeaderString(ORIGIN);

            if (!corsPolicy.isAllowedOrigin(origin)) {
                requestContext.abortWith(Response.status(Response.Status.FORBIDDEN)
                        .entity(Map.of("code", "CORS_ORIGIN_DENIED"))
                        .build());
                return;
            }

            requestContext.abortWith(Response.noContent()
                    .header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin)
                    .header("Access-Control-Allow-Credentials", "true")
                    .header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE, OPTIONS")
                    .header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers",
                            "Authorization, Content-Type, X-CSRF-Token, X-Requested-With, Idempotency-Key")
                    .header("Access-Control-Max-Age", "600")
                    .header("Vary", "Origin")
                    .build());
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
            ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) {
        String origin = requestContext.getHeaderString(ORIGIN);

        if (corsPolicy.isAllowedOrigin(origin)) {
            responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin);
            responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle("Access-Control-Allow-Credentials", "true");
            responseContext.getHeaders().add("Vary", "Origin");
        }
    }

    private boolean isPreflight(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        return "OPTIONS".equalsIgnoreCase(requestContext.getMethod())
                && requestContext.getHeaderString(ORIGIN) != null
                && requestContext.getHeaderString("Access-Control-Request-Method") != null;
    }
}
```

**Catatan:** pastikan filter authentication tidak memblokir preflight `OPTIONS`, tetapi preflight tetap harus divalidasi origin/method/header-nya.

---

# 8. CORS Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Reflect semua `Origin` | Semua website bisa baca response | explicit allowlist |
| `*` untuk credentialed API | policy invalid/berbahaya | origin spesifik |
| Allow semua headers | membuka surface tidak perlu | allowlist headers |
| Allow semua methods | method mutasi terbuka | method per API |
| CORS dianggap authorization | non-browser bypass | tetap authz di service |
| Admin API pakai CORS public | admin exposed ke browser asing | admin origin terpisah |
| Tidak pakai `Vary: Origin` | cache proxy salah response | set `Vary` |
| Preflight bypass semua logic | origin tidak dicek | validate preflight |

---

# 9. CSRF - Cross-Site Request Forgery

CSRF terjadi ketika browser user yang sudah authenticated dipaksa mengirim request ke situs tepercaya dari situs jahat. OWASP CSRF Cheat Sheet menjelaskan bahwa jika user sudah authenticated, situs rentan tidak dapat membedakan request sah dan forged request tanpa proteksi tambahan. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Cross-Site_Request_Forgery_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

CSRF relevan jika credential dikirim otomatis oleh browser:

```text id="ugvh0a"
- session cookie
- Basic auth
- client certificate
- some browser-managed credentials
```

CSRF biasanya kurang relevan jika API hanya menerima bearer token di `Authorization` header yang disimpan aman dan tidak otomatis dikirim browser. Tapi XSS tetap bisa mencuri/menyalahgunakan token jika token disimpan tidak aman.

---

# 10. CSRF Decision Table

| Client Credential | CSRF Risk | Mitigation |
|---|---:|---|
| Cookie session | High | CSRF token + SameSite + Origin check |
| HttpOnly cookie JWT | High | CSRF token + SameSite + Origin check |
| Authorization header bearer token | Lower | protect token from XSS; CORS still needed |
| Basic auth in browser | High | avoid or CSRF protection |
| mTLS browser cert | High | CSRF protection for state change |
| API key from server-to-server | Low browser CSRF | HMAC/replay/idempotency |
| Public unauthenticated GET | N/A | no state mutation |

---

# 11. CSRF Mitigation Layers

OWASP CSRF guidance includes synchronizer token pattern, signed double-submit cookie, custom request headers, SameSite cookie attribute, and Origin/Referer verification as defenses. It also notes that XSS can defeat CSRF mitigation. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Cross-Site_Request_Forgery_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Recommended for cookie-authenticated browser API:

```text id="2xn9wm"
1. SameSite cookie
2. CSRF token for unsafe methods
3. Verify Origin / Referer
4. Custom header requirement
5. No state change on GET
6. XSS prevention/CSP
```

Unsafe methods:

```text id="tt2awx"
POST
PUT
PATCH
DELETE
```

Safe-ish methods:

```text id="74egau"
GET
HEAD
OPTIONS
```

But do not mutate state in GET.

---

# 12. CSRF Token Pattern

## Synchronizer Token

Server stores token in session.

```text id="n3uk3r"
GET /csrf-token
  -> returns token

POST /cases/123/approve
  Cookie: SESSION=...
  X-CSRF-Token: token
```

Server validates:

```text id="1qds6v"
session exists
token matches session
method is unsafe
origin allowed
```

## Signed Double-Submit Cookie

Server sends CSRF cookie readable by JavaScript and validates signed token/header.

```http id="395x20"
Set-Cookie: CSRF-TOKEN=<random-or-signed-value>; Secure; SameSite=Lax; Path=/
```

Browser JS sends:

```http id="2di6la"
X-CSRF-Token: <same-token>
```

Server verifies token signature/binding to session.

---

# 13. CSRF Filter

```java id="aq2yh5"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION + 20)
public class CsrfProtectionFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private static final Set<String> SAFE_METHODS =
            Set.of("GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS", "TRACE");

    private final CsrfTokenService csrfTokenService;
    private final OriginPolicy originPolicy;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        if (SAFE_METHODS.contains(ctx.getMethod())) {
            return;
        }

        if (!isCookieAuthenticated(ctx)) {
            return;
        }

        String origin = ctx.getHeaderString("Origin");
        String referer = ctx.getHeaderString("Referer");

        if (!originPolicy.isTrusted(origin, referer)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("CSRF origin check failed");
        }

        String token = ctx.getHeaderString("X-CSRF-Token");

        if (token == null || token.isBlank()) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Missing CSRF token");
        }

        if (!csrfTokenService.isValidForRequest(ctx, token)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Invalid CSRF token");
        }
    }

    private boolean isCookieAuthenticated(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        return ctx.getCookies().containsKey("SESSION")
                || ctx.getCookies().containsKey("ACCESS_TOKEN");
    }
}
```

**Catatan:** jangan skip CSRF hanya karena endpoint punya JWT kalau JWT berada di cookie yang otomatis dikirim browser.

---

# 14. Origin / Referer Check

Origin check bagus untuk unsafe browser requests.

```java id="v5b85z"
public final class OriginPolicy {

    private final Set<String> trustedOrigins = Set.of(
            "https://app.company.com",
            "https://admin.company.com"
    );

    public boolean isTrusted(String origin, String referer) {
        if (origin != null && !origin.isBlank()) {
            return trustedOrigins.contains(origin);
        }

        if (referer != null && !referer.isBlank()) {
            return trustedOrigins.stream().anyMatch(referer::startsWith);
        }

        return false;
    }
}
```

Caveat:

```text id="zvxz8l"
Origin/Referer can be absent in some cases.
For sensitive cookie-authenticated mutation, missing Origin/Referer should usually deny.
```

---

# 15. SameSite Cookies

MDN menjelaskan `Set-Cookie` sebagai response header untuk mengirim cookie dari server ke browser, dan cookie dapat diberi attribute seperti `Secure`, `HttpOnly`, dan `SameSite`. `HttpOnly` mencegah JavaScript mengakses cookie, sedangkan `Secure` membatasi cookie agar dikirim hanya melalui HTTPS. ([MDN Web Docs](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Reference/Headers/Set-Cookie?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Cookie security baseline:

```http id="t66syg"
Set-Cookie: SESSION=abc;
  Path=/;
  Secure;
  HttpOnly;
  SameSite=Lax;
  Max-Age=1800
```

For cross-site embedded/login flows that require third-party cookie behavior:

```http id="nbrztb"
Set-Cookie: SESSION=abc;
  Path=/;
  Secure;
  HttpOnly;
  SameSite=None
```

`SameSite=None` requires `Secure` in modern browsers.

---

# 16. Cookie Attribute Guide

| Attribute | Recommendation | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `Secure` | Always for auth cookies | HTTPS only |
| `HttpOnly` | Always for session/access cookies | JS cannot read cookie |
| `SameSite=Lax` | Good default | reduces CSRF |
| `SameSite=Strict` | Stronger, may break flows | strong CSRF reduction |
| `SameSite=None` | Only if cross-site required | must use `Secure` |
| `Path` | Narrow if possible | limit cookie scope |
| `Domain` | avoid broad domain if possible | avoid subdomain leakage |
| `Max-Age`/`Expires` | explicit lifetime | session control |
| `__Host-` prefix | recommended for host-bound secure cookies | stronger scoping |

---

# 17. Cookie Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Auth cookie tanpa `Secure` | sent over HTTP | `Secure` |
| Auth cookie tanpa `HttpOnly` | JS theft via XSS | `HttpOnly` |
| `SameSite=None` tanpa alasan | CSRF risk | `Lax`/`Strict` |
| Cookie domain `.company.com` | subdomain takeover leak | host-only cookie |
| JWT in localStorage | XSS token theft | prefer HttpOnly cookie or secure BFF pattern |
| Long-lived session cookie | stolen cookie long usable | short idle/absolute timeout |
| GET mutates state | CSRF trivial | use POST + CSRF token |
| CSRF token in cookie only, not header | no proof of JS same-origin | header + signed token |

---

# 18. Security Headers Baseline

OWASP HTTP Headers Cheat Sheet recommends reviewing security-related HTTP response headers and gives guidance for headers such as HSTS, X-Content-Type-Options, X-Frame-Options, Referrer-Policy, and others. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/HTTP_Headers_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Recommended baseline for API/browser-facing responses:

```http id="tgaw0c"
Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains
X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff
X-Frame-Options: DENY
Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'
Referrer-Policy: no-referrer
Permissions-Policy: geolocation=(), microphone=(), camera=()
Cache-Control: no-store
```

Caveat: `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'` is safe for pure JSON APIs, but too strict for normal HTML frontend pages.

---

# 19. HSTS

HSTS tells browsers to access a site only over HTTPS for a period. Use it only when HTTPS is correctly deployed everywhere.

```http id="ksdc2h"
Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains
```

Add `preload` only when domain is ready for browser preload list requirements:

```http id="mg4ajl"
Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains; preload
```

Do not enable `includeSubDomains` if some subdomains still require HTTP.

---

# 20. X-Content-Type-Options

```http id="jbjlym"
X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff
```

Purpose:

```text id="knrmjc"
Prevent browser MIME sniffing from interpreting response as a different content type.
```

For JSON API:

```http id="ymykf4"
Content-Type: application/json
X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff
```

---

# 21. Clickjacking: X-Frame-Options / CSP frame-ancestors

MDN describes `X-Frame-Options` as a response header controlling whether a browser may render a page in `frame`, `iframe`, `embed`, or `object`, helping avoid clickjacking attacks. ([MDN Web Docs](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Reference/Headers/X-Frame-Options?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

For API and admin:

```http id="0tewd3"
X-Frame-Options: DENY
Content-Security-Policy: frame-ancestors 'none'
```

If your app intentionally embeds in a trusted parent, use CSP `frame-ancestors` with explicit origins instead of broad allow.

---

# 22. Content-Security-Policy

OWASP CSP Cheat Sheet explains CSP as a defense-in-depth mechanism, especially against XSS exploitation, while noting it does not remove the need to fix vulnerabilities. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Content_Security_Policy_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

For JSON API:

```http id="zuzlsd"
Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'
```

For frontend HTML, CSP must be designed around actual assets:

```http id="lyjb1i"
Content-Security-Policy:
  default-src 'self';
  script-src 'self' 'nonce-{random}';
  object-src 'none';
  base-uri 'self';
  frame-ancestors 'none';
```

Avoid:

```http id="v1yeef"
script-src 'unsafe-inline' *
```

unless truly transitional and risk-accepted.

---

# 23. Referrer-Policy

```http id="2wnu1c"
Referrer-Policy: no-referrer
```

Alternative for frontend pages:

```http id="f0cjb7"
Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin
```

For APIs handling sensitive paths/query params, `no-referrer` is safer.

---

# 24. Permissions-Policy

```http id="6t0gjh"
Permissions-Policy: geolocation=(), microphone=(), camera=(), payment=()
```

Purpose:

```text id="nua70m"
Disable browser features not needed by API/admin pages.
```

For pure APIs, deny most features.

---

# 25. Cache-Control

For sensitive API responses:

```http id="53528f"
Cache-Control: no-store
Pragma: no-cache
```

Use `no-store` for responses containing:

```text id="6qtbrt"
- user profile
- tenant data
- case/order/customer data
- tokens/session-related response
- admin data
```

For public static assets, use different caching policy at frontend/static CDN.

---

# 26. Jersey Security Headers Filter

```java id="7k1ypj"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR)
public class SecurityHeadersFilter implements ContainerResponseFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
            ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) {
        MultivaluedMap<String, Object> headers = responseContext.getHeaders();

        headers.putSingle(
                "Strict-Transport-Security",
                "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains"
        );

        headers.putSingle("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
        headers.putSingle("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");

        headers.putSingle(
                "Content-Security-Policy",
                "default-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'"
        );

        headers.putSingle("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer");

        headers.putSingle(
                "Permissions-Policy",
                "geolocation=(), microphone=(), camera=(), payment=()"
        );

        if (isSensitiveApiResponse(requestContext)) {
            headers.putSingle("Cache-Control", "no-store");
            headers.putSingle("Pragma", "no-cache");
        }
    }

    private boolean isSensitiveApiResponse(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        String path = requestContext.getUriInfo().getPath();
        return !path.startsWith("public/");
    }
}
```

---

# 27. Gateway vs Jersey Headers

| Header/Policy | Gateway | Jersey Service | Catatan |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| HSTS | Yes | Optional | best at edge |
| CORS | Yes | Optional | centralize if possible |
| CSP | Yes | Yes for direct service access | avoid inconsistent policy |
| Cache-Control | Both | Yes | service knows sensitivity |
| `X-Content-Type-Options` | Yes | Yes | safe duplicate if same |
| `X-Frame-Options` | Yes | Yes | service direct bypass safe |
| CSRF token validation | Usually app | Yes | needs session/app context |
| Authorization | Gateway coarse | Service final | object/domain auth in service |

Rule:

```text id="6k134i"
Gateway can add baseline headers.
Service should still enforce security-critical app semantics.
```

---

# 28. Browser Token Storage Decision

| Storage | Pros | Risks | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| HttpOnly Secure cookie | JS cannot read | CSRF risk | needs CSRF protection |
| Memory only | lower persistence | lost on refresh | common SPA pattern |
| sessionStorage | per-tab-ish | XSS readable | avoid for high risk |
| localStorage | persists | XSS readable | generally avoid for access tokens |
| BFF session | token stays server-side | backend complexity | strong browser architecture |

Practical recommendation:

```text id="zsagxb"
For high-risk enterprise apps:
  Browser -> BFF/session cookie
  BFF -> API with service token / token exchange

If SPA direct to API:
  Prefer short-lived tokens, strong CSP, no localStorage if possible, strict CORS.
```

---

# 29. BFF Pattern

Backend-for-Frontend:

```text id="9j9sol"
Browser
  -> BFF using secure HttpOnly SameSite cookie
  -> BFF calls APIs with server-side tokens
```

Benefits:

```text id="elxnb2"
- browser does not store API access token
- API tokens stay server-side
- CSRF can be centralized in BFF
- CORS surface smaller
```

Costs:

```text id="mz7kqn"
- extra service
- session management
- scaling
- careful CSRF/session handling still required
```

---

# 30. Fetch Metadata Headers

Modern browsers send Fetch Metadata request headers such as `Sec-Fetch-Site`, `Sec-Fetch-Mode`, and `Sec-Fetch-Dest`. They can be used as defense-in-depth to reject suspicious cross-site requests.

Example policy:

```java id="64q85d"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION - 50)
public class FetchMetadataFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private static final Set<String> SAFE_METHODS =
            Set.of("GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS");

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String site = ctx.getHeaderString("Sec-Fetch-Site");

        if (site == null) {
            return; // non-browser or older browser; do not rely solely on this
        }

        boolean unsafeMethod = !SAFE_METHODS.contains(ctx.getMethod());

        if (unsafeMethod && "cross-site".equalsIgnoreCase(site)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Cross-site state-changing request blocked");
        }
    }
}
```

Use as defense-in-depth, not as sole CSRF protection.

---

# 31. API Gateway Boundary

At gateway/ingress:

```text id="fo9478"
- enforce TLS
- redirect HTTP to HTTPS
- add HSTS
- route only public paths externally
- block /internal/*
- block /admin/* unless admin route/network/origin
- CORS per route
- WAF/rate limit
- request size limits
- strip spoofable headers
- add trusted forwarding headers
```

Headers to strip from untrusted external traffic:

```text id="srb733"
X-Forwarded-User
X-Forwarded-Email
X-MTLS-Verified
X-Client-Cert
X-SPIFFE-ID
X-Internal-Service
X-Original-User
```

Then trusted proxy may re-add verified versions.

---

# 32. Nginx Example

```nginx id="ne2d8o"
server {
    listen 443 ssl http2;
    server_name api.company.com;

    add_header Strict-Transport-Security "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains" always;
    add_header X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff" always;
    add_header X-Frame-Options "DENY" always;
    add_header Referrer-Policy "no-referrer" always;
    add_header Permissions-Policy "geolocation=(), microphone=(), camera=()" always;

    location /internal/ {
        return 404;
    }

    location /admin/ {
        # restrict by network/auth gateway policy
        proxy_pass http://case-api;
    }

    location / {
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto https;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Request-ID $request_id;

        # strip spoofable identity headers
        proxy_set_header X-MTLS-Verified "";
        proxy_set_header X-SPIFFE-ID "";
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-User "";

        proxy_pass http://case-api;
    }
}
```

---

# 33. Kubernetes Ingress / Network Boundary

Kubernetes NetworkPolicy defines how pods communicate with other network entities and can restrict ingress/egress for selected pods. It is useful for internal/ops endpoint isolation, but it is not a replacement for application-level authorization. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/HTTP_Headers_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical boundary:

```text id="g1662f"
Public ingress:
  /api/*
  /partner/*
  /health/live

Private ingress:
  /internal/*
  /metrics
  /admin/* if admin private

NetworkPolicy:
  only allowed namespace/service account can call internal service ports

Application:
  still checks actorType/service identity/permission
```

---

# 34. Admin Browser Clients

Admin frontend should be stricter:

```text id="gwcwot"
- separate origin: https://admin.company.com
- separate CORS allowlist
- stronger CSP
- SameSite=Strict or Lax where possible
- step-up auth for high-risk actions
- no embedding: frame-ancestors 'none'
- short session idle timeout
- audit all sensitive actions
```

Admin endpoint CORS:

```http id="onmdzf"
Access-Control-Allow-Origin: https://admin.company.com
Access-Control-Allow-Credentials: true
Vary: Origin
```

Never allow random customer/partner origins for admin APIs.

---

# 35. Partner Dashboard Browser Clients

Partner dashboard often needs per-partner origin management.

```text id="xsp2oq"
partner-acme dashboard:
  https://portal.acme.com

partner-beta dashboard:
  https://ops.beta.example
```

Use explicit registered origins per partner:

```sql id="4t69o9"
CREATE TABLE partner_allowed_origin (
    client_id  TEXT NOT NULL,
    origin     TEXT NOT NULL,
    active     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (client_id, origin)
);
```

CORS policy:

```text id="3r0y8s"
Origin must be registered for partner client.
Partner authentication still required.
Tenant mapping still required.
```

---

# 36. Error Handling for CORS/CSRF

| Failure | Status | Public Code |
|---|---:|---|
| Origin not allowed | `403` | `CORS_ORIGIN_DENIED` |
| Preflight method not allowed | `403` | `CORS_METHOD_DENIED` |
| Missing CSRF token | `403` | `CSRF_TOKEN_REQUIRED` |
| Invalid CSRF token | `403` | `CSRF_TOKEN_INVALID` |
| Origin/Referer missing for unsafe cookie request | `403` | `CSRF_ORIGIN_REQUIRED` |
| Cross-site unsafe request blocked by Fetch Metadata | `403` | `CROSS_SITE_REQUEST_BLOCKED` |

Audit internal should include origin, method, path, actor if available, and reason.

---

# 37. Testing Matrix

```text id="26b4bw"
[ ] Allowed origin receives Access-Control-Allow-Origin.
[ ] Disallowed origin does not receive allow header or gets 403.
[ ] Credentialed CORS never uses wildcard origin.
[ ] Vary: Origin exists for dynamic CORS.
[ ] Preflight OPTIONS works without auth token but validates origin/method/headers.
[ ] Admin API only allows admin origin.
[ ] Internal API not exposed through public gateway.
[ ] Cookie-authenticated POST without CSRF token is denied.
[ ] Cookie-authenticated POST with invalid Origin is denied.
[ ] Cookie-authenticated POST with valid CSRF token and Origin succeeds.
[ ] Bearer-token API is not incorrectly forced through CSRF if no cookies.
[ ] Auth cookies have Secure, HttpOnly, SameSite.
[ ] Security headers exist on normal and error responses.
[ ] Sensitive API responses have Cache-Control: no-store.
[ ] X-Frame-Options/CSP frame-ancestors deny framing.
[ ] Gateway strips spoofable identity headers.
```

---

# 38. Test Example - Credentialed CORS

```java id="3r0v76"
@Test
void credentialedCorsShouldEchoOnlyAllowedOrigin() {
    Response response = target("/cases")
            .request()
            .header("Origin", "https://app.company.com")
            .get();

    assertEquals(
            "https://app.company.com",
            response.getHeaderString("Access-Control-Allow-Origin")
    );
    assertEquals(
            "true",
            response.getHeaderString("Access-Control-Allow-Credentials")
    );
    assertTrue(response.getHeaders().get("Vary").contains("Origin"));
}
```

---

# 39. Test Example - Disallowed Origin

```java id="s7eiz4"
@Test
void corsShouldRejectDisallowedPreflightOrigin() {
    Response response = target("/cases")
            .request()
            .header("Origin", "https://evil.example")
            .header("Access-Control-Request-Method", "POST")
            .options();

    assertEquals(403, response.getStatus());
}
```

---

# 40. Test Example - CSRF Protection

```java id="jpyzvy"
@Test
void cookieAuthenticatedPostShouldRequireCsrfToken() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-1/approve")
            .request()
            .cookie("SESSION", "valid-session")
            .header("Origin", "https://app.company.com")
            .post(Entity.json("{}"));

    assertEquals(403, response.getStatus());
}
```

```java id="qvc5iw"
@Test
void cookieAuthenticatedPostShouldAllowValidCsrfToken() {
    String csrfToken = csrfTokenService.issueForSession("valid-session");

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-1/approve")
            .request()
            .cookie("SESSION", "valid-session")
            .header("Origin", "https://app.company.com")
            .header("X-CSRF-Token", csrfToken)
            .post(Entity.json("{}"));

    assertEquals(204, response.getStatus());
}
```

---

# 41. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` for authenticated API | cross-origin data exposure | explicit origin |
| Reflect any Origin | attacker site reads API response | allowlist |
| CORS as authz | curl/server clients bypass | real authz |
| Cookie auth without CSRF token | forged browser mutation | CSRF token + SameSite |
| JWT in localStorage | XSS token theft | BFF or memory/HttpOnly strategy |
| `SameSite=None` by default | higher CSRF surface | Lax/Strict unless needed |
| Missing `Vary: Origin` | cache poisoning/misdelivery | set Vary |
| Security headers only at gateway | bypass direct service route | also add in service |
| Admin CORS same as public app | admin attack surface | admin origin only |
| Internal path exposed publicly | lateral/public abuse | gateway block + app internal auth |

---

# 42. Code Review Checklist

```text id="78ig99"
[ ] CORS origin allowlist explicit.
[ ] Credentialed CORS never uses wildcard.
[ ] Preflight validates origin/method/headers.
[ ] `Vary: Origin` set for dynamic CORS.
[ ] Cookie-authenticated unsafe methods require CSRF token.
[ ] CSRF validates Origin/Referer.
[ ] SameSite/Secure/HttpOnly used for auth cookies.
[ ] No state mutation via GET.
[ ] Security headers present on success and error responses.
[ ] Sensitive responses use Cache-Control: no-store.
[ ] Admin and partner origins separated.
[ ] Internal/admin/ops paths not exposed publicly.
[ ] Gateway strips spoofable identity headers.
[ ] Service still enforces authorization independent of gateway.
[ ] Tests cover CORS, CSRF, cookies, security headers.
```

---

# 43. Minimal Production Invariants

```text id="ga02ds"
1. CORS is not authorization.
2. Credentialed CORS must use explicit allowed origins.
3. Dynamic CORS must send Vary: Origin.
4. Cookie-authenticated unsafe requests need CSRF protection.
5. Auth cookies must use Secure, HttpOnly, and appropriate SameSite.
6. State-changing actions must not use GET.
7. Security headers should be applied to both normal and error responses.
8. Admin/internal/ops endpoints must not share public CORS policy.
9. Gateway boundary must be backed by service-level enforcement.
10. Browser token storage decision must be explicit and threat-modeled.
```

---

# 44. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | CORS mental model | Tidak salah menganggap CORS sebagai authz |
| 2 | Origin allowlist | Browser cross-origin access terkendali |
| 3 | Cookie security | Secure/HttpOnly/SameSite benar |
| 4 | CSRF protection | Unsafe cookie-auth requests aman |
| 5 | Security headers | Browser defense-in-depth |
| 6 | Gateway boundary | Public/admin/internal route terpisah |
| 7 | Browser token storage | SPA/BFF strategy jelas |
| 8 | Admin/partner origins | Per-surface policy |
| 9 | Tests | CORS/CSRF/header regression tertutup |
| 10 | Audit/metrics | Origin/CSRF deny terlihat |
