## Response:
7/13/2026, 5:34:34 AM

Kita lanjut ke multi-tenant authorization deep dive. Fokusnya tenant boundary end-to-end: dari principal, tenant admin, org hierarchy, delegated tenant access, service tenant scope, sampai enforcement di MyBatis/PostgreSQL.

# Cheatsheet - Multi-Tenant Authorization Deep Dive di Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis/PostgreSQL

## 1. Tujuan

Multi-tenant authorization memastikan satu tenant tidak bisa membaca, mengubah, menghitung, mencari, mengekspor, atau memicu workflow milik tenant lain.

Problem utama:

```text
User valid ≠ boleh akses semua tenant.
Role admin ≠ admin semua tenant.
Permission case.read ≠ boleh baca semua case.
Service internal ≠ boleh akses semua tenant.
Tenant ID dari request body/header ≠ trusted tenant.
Object ID valid ≠ object milik actor.
```

OWASP API Security 2023 menempatkan **Broken Object Level Authorization** sebagai API1, dan menjelaskan bahwa attacker sering memanipulasi object ID di path, query, header, atau payload. Dalam multi-tenant system, object ID manipulation sering berubah menjadi cross-tenant leak jika tenant boundary tidak enforced di service dan SQL. ([OWASP Foundation](https://owasp.org/API-Security/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model

```text
Can <actor> perform <action> on <resource>
within <tenant boundary>
under <relationship/state/context>?
```

Multi-tenant authz selalu punya minimal 5 dimensi:

| Dimensi | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Actor | user, service, partner, admin, workflow worker |
| Tenant | tenant-a, tenant-b, org-x |
| Action | read, approve, assign, export, disable |
| Resource | case, order, task, user, file |
| Context | ownership, assignee, role, ACL, region, workflow state |

**Rule utama:**

```text
Tenant boundary is not a filter option.
Tenant boundary is a mandatory security predicate.
```

---

# 3. Diagram Multi-Tenant Authorization

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Request] --> B[Jersey Authentication Filter]
    B --> C[SecurityActor + TenantContext]
    C --> D[Endpoint Authorization Filter]
    D --> E[Application Service]

    E --> F{Tenant Source}
    F -->|User Token| G[TOKEN_CLAIM tenant]
    F -->|Service| H[SERVICE_REGISTRY allowedTenants]
    F -->|Partner| I[PARTNER_MAPPING externalTenantRef]
    F -->|Workflow Job| J[JOB_SCOPE tenant/process]
    F -->|Resource Lookup| K[RESOURCE tenant_id]

    G --> L[Object-Level Policy]
    H --> L
    I --> L
    J --> L
    K --> L

    L --> M[Visibility Criteria]
    M --> N[MyBatis Mapper]
    N --> O[(PostgreSQL tenant_id predicates)]
    O --> P[Optional PostgreSQL RLS]

    L --> Q[Audit Decision]
```

---

# 4. Tenant Isolation Model

| Model | Contoh | Pros | Cons |
|---|---|---|---|
| Shared DB, shared schema, `tenant_id` column | semua tenant di tabel yang sama | simple, cost-efficient | query harus selalu tenant-aware |
| Shared DB, schema per tenant | `tenant_a.cases`, `tenant_b.cases` | isolation lebih kuat | migration/schema management kompleks |
| DB per tenant | satu PostgreSQL database per tenant | isolation kuat, backup/restore per tenant | operational overhead tinggi |
| Hybrid | big tenant dedicated, small tenant shared | fleksibel | routing/data access lebih kompleks |
| Logical tenant + org hierarchy | tenant punya sub-org/department | cocok enterprise | authz model lebih kompleks |

Untuk stack Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL, model paling umum adalah:

```text
shared DB + shared schema + tenant_id mandatory
```

Karena itu, semua query tenant-scoped harus eksplisit membawa `tenant_id`.

---

# 5. TenantContext

Buat tenant sebagai first-class security context.

```java
public record TenantContext(
        String currentTenantId,
        TenantSource source,
        Set<String> allowedTenantIds,
        boolean platformWide
) {
    public boolean canAccessTenant(String targetTenantId) {
        if (platformWide) {
            return true;
        }

        return targetTenantId != null
                && allowedTenantIds != null
                && allowedTenantIds.contains(targetTenantId);
    }

    public String requireCurrentTenant() {
        if (currentTenantId == null || currentTenantId.isBlank()) {
            throw new AccessDeniedException("missing_tenant_context");
        }
        return currentTenantId;
    }
}
```

```java
public enum TenantSource {
    TOKEN_CLAIM,
    SERVICE_REGISTRY,
    PARTNER_MAPPING,
    JOB_SCOPE,
    RESOURCE_LOOKUP,
    PLATFORM_ADMIN_CONTEXT,
    NONE
}
```

---

# 6. Tenant Source Decision Table

| Caller | Trusted Tenant Source | Jangan Percaya |
|---|---|---|
| User | validated token claim / selected tenant session | request body `tenantId` |
| Tenant admin | token + tenant admin assignment | arbitrary tenant param |
| Platform admin | explicit platform admin context + step-up | hidden global default |
| Service | service registry allowed tenants | `X-Tenant-ID` from caller alone |
| Partner | partner tenant mapping | internal tenant ID from partner body |
| Workflow worker | process/job scope + business key lookup | taskId alone |
| Batch job | scheduled job scope | unbounded all-tenant loop |

**Rule:**

```text
Tenant ID may appear in request,
but must be validated against trusted tenant source.
```

---

# 7. Schema Baseline: Tenant-Scoped Tables

```sql
CREATE TABLE cases (
    tenant_id        TEXT NOT NULL,
    id               TEXT NOT NULL,
    title            TEXT NOT NULL,
    status           TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_by       TEXT NOT NULL,
    assigned_user_id TEXT NULL,
    risk_level       INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    version          BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, id)
);
```

Kenapa `PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, id)`?

```text
- Mencegah accidental global lookup mindset.
- Query by ID naturalnya harus include tenant_id.
- FK tenant-scoped bisa ikut membawa tenant_id.
- Index lookup tenant-aware lebih jelas.
```

Jika `id` harus global unique, tetap pertahankan index tenant-aware:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_cases_global_id ON cases(id);

CREATE INDEX idx_cases_tenant_status
ON cases (tenant_id, status, updated_at DESC);
```

---

# 8. Tenant-Scoped Foreign Key

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_comments (
    tenant_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    case_id     TEXT NOT NULL,
    comment_id  TEXT NOT NULL,
    body        TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_by  TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, case_id, comment_id),

    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, case_id)
        REFERENCES cases (tenant_id, id)
);
```

Benefit:

```text
Comment tenant_id must match parent case tenant_id.
Cross-tenant child-parent relation is structurally harder.
```

PostgreSQL constraints seperti foreign key dan check constraints membantu menjaga integritas data di level database, sehingga cocok dipakai sebagai defense-in-depth untuk tenant-scoped schema. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-constraints.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 9. Bad vs Good Tenant Query

## Buruk

```xml
<select id="findById" resultMap="CaseRecordMap">
    SELECT *
    FROM cases
    WHERE id = #{caseId}
</select>
```

Masalah:

```text
caseId dari path bisa dimanipulasi.
Jika id global atau bisa ditebak, tenant leak.
Jika id UUID sekalipun, BOLA tetap mungkin.
```

## Baik

```xml
<select id="findByIdForTenant" resultMap="CaseRecordMap">
    SELECT *
    FROM cases
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND id = #{caseId}
</select>
```

Interface:

```java
Optional<CaseRecord> findByIdForTenant(
        @Param("tenantId") String tenantId,
        @Param("caseId") String caseId
);
```

---

# 10. Service Pattern: Tenant-Aware Load

```java
public CaseDto getCase(SecurityActor actor, String caseId) {
    String tenantId = actor.tenantContext().requireCurrentTenant();

    CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findByIdForTenant(tenantId, caseId)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Case not found"));

    AuthorizationDecision decision = caseAccessPolicy.canRead(actor, caseRecord);

    auditLogger.logAuthorizationDecision(
            actor,
            "case.read",
            ResourceRef.caseRef(caseRecord),
            decision
    );

    if (!decision.allowed()) {
        throw new AccessDeniedException(decision.reason());
    }

    return CaseDto.from(caseRecord);
}
```

**Pattern penting:**

```text
Load by tenant first.
Then object policy.
Then audit.
Then return.
```

Untuk cross-tenant object, `findByIdForTenant` menghasilkan not found sehingga tidak membocorkan existence.

---

# 11. Tenant Admin ≠ Platform Admin

| Admin Type | Scope | Contoh Permission |
|---|---|---|
| Tenant admin | satu tenant | `tenant.user.invite`, `tenant.role.assign` |
| Org admin | beberapa tenant/sub-org | `org.tenant.manage`, `org.user.read` |
| Platform admin | seluruh platform | `platform.tenant.suspend`, `platform.audit.read` |
| Support admin | temporary/support | `support.impersonate`, read-only by grant |
| Break-glass admin | emergency | strict approval + alert |

Jangan:

```text
role ADMIN = semua tenant
```

Lebih aman:

```text
admin permission + tenant assignment + step-up + audit
```

---

# 12. Tenant Admin Assignment Schema

```sql
CREATE TABLE tenant_admin_assignment (
    tenant_id     TEXT NOT NULL,
    user_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    admin_role    TEXT NOT NULL,
    assigned_by   TEXT NOT NULL,
    assigned_at   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    revoked_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    reason        TEXT NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, user_id, admin_role)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_tenant_admin_active
ON tenant_admin_assignment (tenant_id, user_id)
WHERE revoked_at IS NULL;
```

Policy:

```java
public AuthorizationDecision canManageTenantUser(
        SecurityActor actor,
        String targetTenantId
) {
    if (!actor.hasPermission("tenant.user.manage")) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_tenant_user_manage");
    }

    if (!actor.tenantContext().canAccessTenant(targetTenantId)) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("tenant_not_allowed");
    }

    if (!tenantAdminRepository.isActiveTenantAdmin(
            targetTenantId,
            actor.effectiveActor().subject()
    )) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("not_tenant_admin");
    }

    return AuthorizationDecision.allow("tenant_admin_allowed");
}
```

---

# 13. Platform Admin Guard

Platform-wide access harus eksplisit dan sangat terbatas.

```java
public AuthorizationDecision canSuspendTenant(
        SecurityActor actor,
        String targetTenantId
) {
    if (!actor.hasPermission("platform.tenant.suspend")) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_platform_tenant_suspend");
    }

    if (!actor.tenantContext().platformWide()) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("not_platform_wide_context");
    }

    if (!actor.transportContext().strongAuthVerified()) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("step_up_required");
    }

    return AuthorizationDecision.allow("platform_tenant_suspend_allowed");
}
```

Audit:

```json
{
  "eventType": "PLATFORM_TENANT_ACTION",
  "decision": "ALLOW",
  "action": "platform.tenant.suspend",
  "targetTenantId": "tenant-b",
  "actor": "USER:platform-admin-1",
  "reason": "platform_tenant_suspend_allowed",
  "policyName": "platform-admin-policy",
  "policyVersion": "2026.07.1"
}
```

---

# 14. Org Hierarchy / Multi-Tenant Hierarchy

Beberapa enterprise punya struktur:

```text
Organization
  ├── Tenant A
  │   ├── Department A1
  │   └── Department A2
  └── Tenant B
      ├── Department B1
      └── Department B2
```

Schema sederhana:

```sql
CREATE TABLE tenant_node (
    node_id        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    parent_node_id TEXT NULL REFERENCES tenant_node(node_id),
    node_type      TEXT NOT NULL,
    tenant_id      TEXT NULL,
    name           TEXT NOT NULL,
    active         BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,

    CONSTRAINT chk_tenant_node_type
        CHECK (node_type IN ('ORG', 'TENANT', 'DEPARTMENT', 'TEAM'))
);
```

Closure table untuk lookup cepat:

```sql
CREATE TABLE tenant_node_closure (
    ancestor_node_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    descendant_node_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    depth              INT NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (ancestor_node_id, descendant_node_id)
);
```

Admin assignment:

```sql
CREATE TABLE tenant_scope_assignment (
    user_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    scope_node_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tenant_node(node_id),
    permission_code TEXT NOT NULL,
    assigned_by   TEXT NOT NULL,
    assigned_at   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    revoked_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (user_id, scope_node_id, permission_code)
);
```

---

# 15. Resolve Allowed Tenants from Org Scope

```sql
SELECT tn.tenant_id
FROM tenant_scope_assignment tsa
JOIN tenant_node_closure tnc
  ON tnc.ancestor_node_id = tsa.scope_node_id
JOIN tenant_node tn
  ON tn.node_id = tnc.descendant_node_id
WHERE tsa.user_id = :userId
  AND tsa.permission_code = :permissionCode
  AND tsa.revoked_at IS NULL
  AND (tsa.expires_at IS NULL OR tsa.expires_at > now())
  AND tn.node_type = 'TENANT'
  AND tn.active = true;
```

Use case:

```text
Org admin assigned at ORG node
  -> can manage all descendant tenants

Department admin assigned at department node
  -> can manage resources under that department only
```

---

# 16. Tenant Selection for User Sessions

Jika user bisa masuk ke beberapa tenant:

```text
Alice belongs to tenant-a and tenant-b.
Request must explicitly select active tenant.
```

Pattern:

```http
GET /cases
X-Active-Tenant: tenant-a
Authorization: Bearer ...
```

But validate:

```java
public TenantContext resolveActiveTenant(
        SecurityActor actor,
        String requestedTenantId
) {
    if (!actor.tenantContext().canAccessTenant(requestedTenantId)) {
        throw new AccessDeniedException("active_tenant_not_allowed");
    }

    return TenantContext.singleTenant(
            requestedTenantId,
            TenantSource.TOKEN_CLAIM
    );
}
```

Better for browser app:

```text
active tenant stored in server-side session/BFF context
or explicit tenant switch endpoint with audit
```

Audit tenant switch:

```json
{
  "eventType": "ACTIVE_TENANT_SWITCHED",
  "actor": "USER:alice",
  "fromTenantId": "tenant-a",
  "toTenantId": "tenant-b",
  "correlationId": "req-123"
}
```

---

# 17. Partner Tenant Mapping

Partner must not choose internal tenant ID directly.

```sql
CREATE TABLE partner_tenant_mapping (
    client_id           TEXT NOT NULL,
    external_tenant_ref TEXT NOT NULL,
    tenant_id           TEXT NOT NULL,
    active              BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (client_id, external_tenant_ref)
);
```

Service:

```java
public String resolvePartnerTenant(
        SecurityActor partner,
        String externalTenantRef
) {
    String clientId = partner.effectiveActor().clientId();

    return partnerTenantMapper.resolve(clientId, externalTenantRef)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new AccessDeniedException("partner_tenant_not_mapped"));
}
```

Request:

```json
{
  "externalTenantRef": "ACME-001",
  "externalOrderId": "ORD-123"
}
```

Internal tenant resolution:

```text
clientId=partner-acme + externalTenantRef=ACME-001
  -> tenant-a
```

---

# 18. Service Tenant Scope

Service internal bisa punya tenant scope berbeda.

| Service | Scope |
|---|---|
| case-worker-service | tenant-a, tenant-b |
| billing-reconciliation | all billing tenants |
| search-indexer | tenant-scoped per job |
| platform-migration-job | platform-wide with explicit job ID |
| partner-sync-service | mapped partner tenants |

Schema:

```sql
CREATE TABLE service_tenant_scope (
    client_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
    scope_type  TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_by  TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    revoked_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (client_id, tenant_id, scope_type)
);
```

Policy:

```java
public AuthorizationDecision canServiceAccessTenant(
        SecurityActor service,
        String targetTenantId,
        String action
) {
    if (service.effectiveActor().actorType() != ActorType.SERVICE) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("actor_not_service");
    }

    if (!service.hasPermission(action)) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_service_permission");
    }

    if (!serviceTenantScopeRepository.isAllowed(
            service.effectiveActor().clientId(),
            targetTenantId,
            action
    )) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("service_tenant_not_allowed");
    }

    return AuthorizationDecision.allow("service_tenant_allowed");
}
```

---

# 19. Multi-Tenant Search/List Visibility

List endpoint paling sering bocor karena query terlihat “normal”.

```java
public SearchResult<CaseDto> searchCases(
        SecurityActor actor,
        CaseSearchRequest request
) {
    CaseVisibilityCriteria criteria =
            CaseVisibilityCriteria.from(actor, request);

    List<CaseRecord> rows = caseMapper.searchVisibleCases(criteria);
    long total = caseMapper.countVisibleCases(criteria);

    return SearchResult.of(
            rows.stream().map(CaseDto::from).toList(),
            total
    );
}
```

Criteria:

```java
public record CaseVisibilityCriteria(
        String tenantId,
        String actorId,
        VisibilityMode visibilityMode,
        Set<String> allowedDepartments,
        Integer maxRiskLevel,
        String status,
        int limit,
        int offset
) {}
```

Visibility mode:

```java
public enum VisibilityMode {
    ALL_IN_TENANT,
    ASSIGNED,
    OWN,
    ASSIGNED_OR_OWN,
    DEPARTMENT,
    NONE
}
```

---

# 20. MyBatis Visibility Query

```xml
<select id="searchVisibleCases" resultMap="CaseRecordMap">
    SELECT *
    FROM cases c
    WHERE c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}

    <if test="status != null">
        AND c.status = #{status}
    </if>

    <choose>
        <when test="visibilityMode == 'ALL_IN_TENANT'">
            AND 1 = 1
        </when>

        <when test="visibilityMode == 'ASSIGNED'">
            AND c.assigned_user_id = #{actorId}
        </when>

        <when test="visibilityMode == 'OWN'">
            AND c.created_by = #{actorId}
        </when>

        <when test="visibilityMode == 'ASSIGNED_OR_OWN'">
            AND (
                c.assigned_user_id = #{actorId}
                OR c.created_by = #{actorId}
            )
        </when>

        <when test="visibilityMode == 'DEPARTMENT'">
            AND c.department_id IN
            <foreach collection="allowedDepartments"
                     item="departmentId"
                     open="("
                     separator=","
                     close=")">
                #{departmentId}
            </foreach>
        </when>

        <otherwise>
            AND 1 = 0
        </otherwise>
    </choose>

    ORDER BY c.updated_at DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
    OFFSET #{offset}
</select>
```

Count query must use the same criteria:

```xml
<select id="countVisibleCases" resultType="long">
    SELECT count(*)
    FROM cases c
    WHERE c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    <!-- same visibility predicates -->
</select>
```

---

# 21. Dynamic SQL Safety

Dangerous pattern:

```xml
<if test="tenantId != null">
    tenant_id = #{tenantId}
</if>
```

Safe pattern:

```xml
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
```

and validate in Java:

```java
if (criteria.tenantId() == null) {
    throw new AccessDeniedException("missing_tenant_id");
}
```

**Invariant:**

```text
tenant predicate must not be optional for tenant-scoped data.
```

---

# 22. Tenant-Aware Mutation

Bad:

```sql
UPDATE cases
SET status = 'APPROVED'
WHERE id = :caseId;
```

Good:

```xml
<update id="approveIfStillEligible">
    UPDATE cases
    SET status = 'APPROVED',
        approved_by = #{actorId},
        approved_at = now(),
        version = version + 1
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND id = #{caseId}
      AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
      AND created_by <> #{actorId}
      AND version = #{expectedVersion}
</update>
```

This protects:

```text
- cross-tenant mutation
- invalid state transition
- maker-checker violation
- stale read / race condition
```

---

# 23. Multi-Tenant Bulk Operation

Bad:

```sql
UPDATE cases
SET status = 'CLOSED'
WHERE id IN (:caseIds);
```

Better:

```xml
<update id="closeVisibleCases">
    UPDATE cases
    SET status = 'CLOSED',
        closed_by = #{actorId},
        closed_at = now(),
        version = version + 1
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND status = 'OPEN'
      AND id IN
      <foreach collection="caseIds"
               item="caseId"
               open="("
               separator=","
               close=")">
          #{caseId}
      </foreach>
      AND (
          #{canCloseAll} = true
          OR assigned_user_id = #{actorId}
          OR created_by = #{actorId}
      )
</update>
```

Service should compare requested vs updated count:

```java
if (updatedCount != requestedIds.size()) {
    auditLogger.logPartialBulkDeny(actor, requestedIds, updatedCount);
    throw new AccessDeniedException("bulk_operation_partially_denied");
}
```

Alternative: pre-load visible IDs and return per-item result.

---

# 24. PostgreSQL Row-Level Security as Defense-in-Depth

PostgreSQL Row-Level Security can restrict which rows are returned or modified by normal queries. When RLS is enabled on a table and no policy exists, PostgreSQL uses a default-deny policy so no rows are visible or modifiable; table owners and roles with `BYPASSRLS` can bypass it unless configured carefully. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-rowsecurity.html))

Example:

```sql
ALTER TABLE cases ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY cases_tenant_policy
ON cases
USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id'));
```

For writes:

```sql
CREATE POLICY cases_tenant_write_policy
ON cases
FOR UPDATE
USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id'))
WITH CHECK (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id'));
```

---

# 25. Setting Tenant for RLS

Use transaction-local setting.

```sql
SELECT set_config('app.tenant_id', 'tenant-a', true);
```

PostgreSQL `current_setting` returns a runtime setting value, and `set_config(..., is_local => true)` applies the setting only during the current transaction. This matters for connection pools because session-level tenant settings can leak across requests if not reset correctly. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/functions-admin.html))

Java transaction pattern:

```java
@Transactional
public <T> T withTenant(String tenantId, Supplier<T> work) {
    jdbcTemplate.update(
            "SELECT set_config('app.tenant_id', ?, true)",
            tenantId
    );

    return work.get();
}
```

**Important:**

```text
Use transaction boundary.
Use local transaction setting.
Do not use global SET with pooled connections.
```

---

# 26. App-Layer vs RLS Responsibility

| Layer | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| Jersey filter | authenticate actor |
| Authorization filter | endpoint permission/scope |
| Application service | object/state/workflow policy |
| MyBatis mapper | mandatory tenant/visibility predicate |
| PostgreSQL RLS | defense-in-depth row isolation |
| Audit | trace decision and tenant context |

RLS is powerful, but do not use it as the only place for business authorization. PostgreSQL docs also warn that row security policies using subqueries or other rows can create race-condition issues if not designed carefully. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-rowsecurity.html))

---

# 27. Tenant-Aware Cache Keys

Bad:

```text
case:{caseId}
visibleCases:{queryHash}
permission:{userId}
```

Good:

```text
case:{tenantId}:{caseId}
visibleCases:{tenantId}:{actorId}:{visibilityHash}:{queryHash}:v{entitlementVersion}
permission:{tenantId}:{userId}:v{entitlementVersion}
acl:{tenantId}:{resourceType}:{resourceId}:{subjectHash}:v{subjectVersion}:rv{resourceVersion}
```

Rule:

```text
If cache contains tenant-scoped data,
tenant ID must be in the key.
```

---

# 28. Tenant-Aware Audit

Audit every sensitive tenant decision.

```json
{
  "eventType": "AUTHORIZATION_DECISION",
  "decision": "DENY",
  "reason": "tenant_not_allowed",
  "actor": "USER:alice",
  "actorTenantId": "tenant-a",
  "targetTenantId": "tenant-b",
  "action": "case.read",
  "resourceType": "case",
  "resourceId": "CASE-789",
  "policyName": "case-access-policy",
  "policyVersion": "2026.07.1",
  "correlationId": "req-123"
}
```

For concealed cross-tenant access:

```text
Client:
  404 Resource not found

Audit:
  CROSS_TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED
```

---

# 29. Tenant Boundary in Error Semantics

| Case | Client Response | Audit |
|---|---:|---|
| tenant-scoped object not found | 404 | optional |
| object exists in other tenant | 404 or 403 | `cross_tenant_access_denied` |
| same-tenant but missing permission | 403 | `missing_permission` |
| same-tenant but not owner/assignee/ACL | 403 | `object_access_denied` |
| service not allowed tenant | 403 | `service_tenant_not_allowed` |
| partner tenant not mapped | 403 | `partner_tenant_not_mapped` |
| invalid tenant parameter | 400 or 403 | `invalid_tenant_context` |

For high-security multi-tenant systems, prefer 404 concealment for cross-tenant object ID probes.

---

# 30. Multi-Tenant File/Blob Access

Files often leak because metadata is tenant-aware but storage key is not.

Bad storage key:

```text
files/{fileId}
```

Better:

```text
tenants/{tenantId}/cases/{caseId}/files/{fileId}
```

File metadata table:

```sql
CREATE TABLE file_object (
    tenant_id     TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    owner_type    TEXT NOT NULL,
    owner_id      TEXT NOT NULL,
    storage_key   TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_by    TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, file_id)
);
```

Download flow:

```text
1. Load file metadata by tenant_id + file_id.
2. Check owner resource authorization.
3. Generate short-lived signed URL or stream file.
4. Audit download.
```

Never generate signed URL based only on `fileId`.

---

# 31. Tenant-Aware Export

Exports are high-risk.

Checklist:

```text
[ ] Export permission separate from read.
[ ] Tenant boundary mandatory.
[ ] Visibility criteria same as list/search.
[ ] Max row/page limit.
[ ] Async export job scoped to tenant.
[ ] Export file stored under tenant path.
[ ] Download requires same tenant/object auth.
[ ] Audit export request and download.
[ ] PII/minimization rules applied.
```

Permission examples:

```text
case.export.visible
case.export.all
admin.audit.export
```

---

# 32. Tenant-Aware Background Jobs

Bad:

```java
List<CaseRecord> cases = caseMapper.findPendingCases();
```

Good:

```java
for (String tenantId : jobScope.allowedTenantIds()) {
    List<CaseRecord> cases =
            caseMapper.findPendingCasesForTenant(tenantId);

    processTenantCases(jobActor, tenantId, cases);
}
```

Job scope:

```java
public record JobTenantScope(
        String jobId,
        String jobName,
        Set<String> allowedTenantIds,
        boolean platformWide,
        String reason
) {}
```

Audit:

```json
{
  "eventType": "TENANT_JOB_EXECUTED",
  "jobId": "job-123",
  "jobName": "case-timeout-handler",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "actorType": "JOB",
  "action": "case.timeout.handle"
}
```

---

# 33. Tenant-Aware Camunda/Workflow

Workflow auth must bind:

```text
processInstanceId
businessKey
tenantId
taskId
assignee/candidate
business object
```

Policy:

```java
public AuthorizationDecision canCompleteTask(
        SecurityActor actor,
        WorkflowTask task,
        CaseRecord caseRecord
) {
    if (!actor.tenantContext().canAccessTenant(task.tenantId())) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("task_tenant_not_allowed");
    }

    if (!task.tenantId().equals(caseRecord.tenantId())) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("task_case_tenant_mismatch");
    }

    if (!actor.hasPermission("workflow.task.complete")) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("missing_workflow_task_complete");
    }

    if (!task.isAssignedTo(actor.effectiveActor().subject())
            && !task.isCandidate(actor.effectiveActor())) {
        return AuthorizationDecision.deny("not_task_assignee_or_candidate");
    }

    return AuthorizationDecision.allow("task_complete_allowed");
}
```

Never complete task by `taskId` alone.

---

# 34. Multi-Tenant Data Model Checklist

```text
[ ] Every tenant-scoped table has tenant_id.
[ ] Primary/unique keys include tenant_id where practical.
[ ] Foreign keys include tenant_id for child-parent relation.
[ ] Indexes support tenant_id predicates.
[ ] Queries never make tenant_id optional.
[ ] Updates/deletes include tenant_id.
[ ] Bulk operations are tenant-scoped.
[ ] Cache keys include tenant_id.
[ ] Audit events include tenant_id.
[ ] Export/file/blob keys include tenant path.
```

---

# 35. Multi-Tenant Authorization Testing Matrix

```text
Authentication/Tenant Context
[ ] User with tenant-a cannot select tenant-b.
[ ] User without tenant context denied tenant-scoped endpoint.
[ ] Multi-tenant user can switch only to assigned tenant.

Object Access
[ ] tenant-a user cannot GET tenant-b case.
[ ] tenant-a user cannot approve tenant-b case.
[ ] same-tenant non-owner/non-assignee denied if policy requires.
[ ] assigned user allowed.

List/Search/Count
[ ] list returns only visible tenant rows.
[ ] count matches visible rows only.
[ ] search filter cannot override tenant predicate.
[ ] pagination does not leak total across tenants.

Mutation
[ ] update/delete wrong tenant affects 0 rows.
[ ] stale version returns conflict.
[ ] maker-checker works inside tenant.

Admin
[ ] tenant admin cannot manage other tenant.
[ ] platform admin requires platform permission + step-up.
[ ] tenant role assignment audited.

Service/Partner
[ ] user token rejected on internal tenant endpoint.
[ ] service not allowed tenant denied.
[ ] partner externalTenantRef maps to correct tenant.
[ ] partner cannot send internal tenantId to switch tenant.

Cache/RLS
[ ] cache key with tenant prevents cross-tenant response reuse.
[ ] RLS tenant setting is local to transaction.
[ ] connection pool does not leak tenant setting.
```

---

# 36. Example Test - Cross-Tenant Read

```java
@Test
void userShouldNotReadCaseFromAnotherTenant() {
    SecurityActor alice = PrincipalFixture.user()
            .subject("alice")
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .permission("case.read")
            .build();

    String tenantBCaseId = seed.caseRecord()
            .tenantId("tenant-b")
            .createdBy("bob")
            .build()
            .id();

    Response response = target("/cases/" + tenantBCaseId)
            .request()
            .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, tokenFor(alice))
            .get();

    assertEquals(404, response.getStatus());

    SecurityAuditEvent event = auditSink.lastEvent();
    assertEquals("CROSS_TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED", event.eventType());
}
```

---

# 37. Example Test - Tenant Admin Scope

```java
@Test
void tenantAdminShouldNotManageOtherTenantUser() {
    SecurityActor admin = PrincipalFixture.user()
            .subject("tenant-admin-a")
            .tenantId("tenant-a")
            .permission("tenant.user.disable")
            .build();

    assertThrows(
            AccessDeniedException.class,
            () -> tenantAdminService.disableUser(
                    admin,
                    "tenant-b",
                    "user-b"
            )
    );

    SecurityAuditEvent event = auditSink.lastEvent();
    assertEquals("tenant_not_allowed", event.reason());
}
```

---

# 38. Example Test - Service Tenant Scope

```java
@Test
void serviceShouldBeDeniedForTenantOutsideScope() {
    SecurityActor service = PrincipalFixture.service()
            .clientId("case-worker-service")
            .permission("internal.case.reindex")
            .allowedTenants(Set.of("tenant-a"))
            .build();

    assertThrows(
            AccessDeniedException.class,
            () -> internalCaseService.reindexCase(
                    service,
                    "tenant-b",
                    "CASE-1"
            )
    );
}
```

---

# 39. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Tenant ID dari body dipercaya | tenant spoofing | tenant resolver tepercaya |
| Query `WHERE id = ?` | cross-tenant leak | `WHERE tenant_id=? AND id=?` |
| `ADMIN` global | overprivilege | tenant/platform admin split |
| List tenant-aware tapi count global | count leakage | same criteria |
| Cache by resource ID only | cross-tenant cache leak | tenant+actor+version key |
| Service allowed all tenants by default | blast radius | explicit service tenant scope |
| Partner sends internal tenant ID | tenant spoofing | external ref mapping |
| RLS session setting global | tenant leak via pool | `SET LOCAL`/transaction-local |
| File key no tenant prefix | blob leak | tenant path + metadata auth |
| Workflow task by taskId only | cross-tenant workflow abuse | task + business object tenant |

---

# 40. Code Review Checklist

```text
[ ] Tenant source explicitly documented.
[ ] Tenant ID from client is validated, not trusted.
[ ] Tenant-scoped resource load uses tenant_id.
[ ] List/search/count use same visibility criteria.
[ ] Mutation SQL includes tenant_id and expected state/version.
[ ] Tenant admin cannot operate outside assigned tenant.
[ ] Platform admin requires separate permission and step-up.
[ ] Partner tenant comes from mapping table.
[ ] Service tenant scope is checked.
[ ] Cache key includes tenant_id.
[ ] Audit contains actorTenantId and targetTenantId where relevant.
[ ] Cross-tenant concealment policy is explicit.
[ ] File/export/job/workflow flows include tenant boundary.
[ ] Tests cover cross-tenant read/write/list/count/admin/service/partner.
```

---

# 41. Minimal Production Invariants

```text
1. Every tenant-scoped table has tenant_id.
2. Every tenant-scoped query includes tenant_id.
3. Every tenant-scoped update/delete includes tenant_id.
4. Tenant ID from request is never trusted without validation.
5. Tenant admin is not platform admin.
6. Service tenant scope is explicit.
7. Partner tenant is resolved through trusted mapping.
8. List/search/count use the same visibility rules.
9. Cache keys include tenant, actor, action/resource, and version.
10. Cross-tenant attempts are denied and audited.
```

---

# 42. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Tenant mental model | Tenant sebagai security boundary |
| 2 | TenantContext | Source dan allowed tenants eksplisit |
| 3 | Schema tenant-aware | `tenant_id`, FK, index, constraints |
| 4 | MyBatis tenant predicates | SELECT/UPDATE/DELETE aman |
| 5 | Tenant admin model | tenant admin vs platform admin |
| 6 | Partner/service tenant scope | non-user actor tidak bebas tenant |
| 7 | List/search/count visibility | tidak ada leakage agregat |
| 8 | RLS defense-in-depth | PostgreSQL guard tambahan |
| 9 | Files/export/jobs/workflow | tenant boundary di non-CRUD flows |
| 10 | Tests/audit | cross-tenant regression tertutup |

---

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjutkan ke: **Cheatsheet Workflow/Camunda Authorization Deep Dive - task assignee/candidate, tenant/process instance, business key, maker-checker, escalation, and service-worker authorization**.
